POST-KILL FLUSH PROOF (live 2026-11-18 improve ff-modal-stale-header-comment): after an external/SIGKILL teardown, tail the pair's log for `Client closed …` SSE lines (clients drained before the kill = no mid-write truncation) and re-confirm the disk baseline with bounded GETs (`change-data` `{}`, log `[]`) — a pair you relaunched under the brief's exact lock set is reclaimed cleanly the same way. **CAMPAIGN FILES ARE GITIGNORED — BYTE-RESTORE TRAP (live GGG 2026-11-21, witnessed self-inflicted):** `public/campaigns/**` (maps, chars, data JSON) are NOT in git, so `git show HEAD:public/campaigns/<c>/maps/<f>.json > <f>.json` FAILS with `exists on disk, but not in 'HEAD'` AND the redirect has ALREADY TRUNCATED THE FILE TO 0 BYTES before git errors — never attempt a git-redirect restore. SANCTIONED RECIPE (proved byte-exact, md5 860eda238 restored): keep the pre-edit `GET /maps/<slug>` body, strip exactly your inserted `placedItems`/`players` rows, and write `JSON.stringify(doc, null, 2)` with NO trailing newline (that is `maps.js`' own serializer, lines 565/654/870) — the md5 pin in the map doc confirms. Last-resort fallback: owner snapshot zips under `public/campaigns/.snapshots/` (earliest full test-campaign zip: 2026-11-01).
- **Hung child-run recovery (live e2e-improve 2026-11-18):** an implementer subagent can HANG on a silent model stream (session_message row has `created`+`firstToken` but no `completed`, zero parts, empty reasoning; disk edits stop). Detect via `sqlite3 -readonly ~/.local/share/opencode/opencode.db` on `session_v2`/`session_message` timestamps + `opencode api GET /api/session/active` (status "running" with stale times = hung). Follow-up messages only QUEUE as `steer` inbox rows — they never land while the stream is hung. Recover: `opencode api POST /api/session/<id>/interrupt` (returns `{"interrupted":true}`, harness reports `state="cancelled"`), then RESUME THE SAME sessionID with a "do not redo completed work" brief — disk edits and the child's context survive. No cancel/interrupt tool exists in the Code Mode catalog; the CLI `opencode api` is the lane.
- Related exit-hygiene facts from the same run: a campaign-SELECT alone bakes `combatSummary`+`__campaign__`+hydrated `hitPoints` into `character-change-data.json` within ~30 s with zero clicks (plus one `POST travel-dailyBudget` rides the re-select leg — live ZK 2026-11-17; closed-tab headless admin clear restores the file-absent baseline), and the EB load lane fires an effective-XP reconcile `PUT /encounters/<name>` on ANY loaded row whose `effectiveXP` ≠ computed (or key absent) — a zero-drift live ride needs pre-stamped rows (qty×xp × party-multiplier).
- Campaign-selection DOM probes: first `browser_evaluate` after `browser_navigate` can run BEFORE React's async campaign-folder fetch resolves — a `.new-campaign-button` count of 0 right after nav is a FALSE NEGATIVE (verified live 2026-10-24: 0 immediately, 1 after list renders). Always prove list rendered (`.campaign-button` count ≥ 1) before judging absence/presence of sibling affordances; the campaign-folder fetch can exceed ~4s after `browser_navigate` (live 2026-11-06) — SHARPENED live 2026-11-21 improve qms-outdoor-hide-token: a COLD reload can exceed 5 s to sidebar render — prefer a second bounded pass over the already-hydrated screen over one long timeout. wait for the button text, don't sample once. Quick-create modal DOM law (live 2026-11-12 OP): the New Campaign/Arena/Starter modal is `.modal-overlay` + `input[placeholder="Enter campaign name"]` (NOT the ct-* modal family), default name `Arena-/Starter-YYYYMMDD`, buttons `Create`/`Cancel` (Create never disabled — rename before it); campaign cards carry type badges ("qa-arena Arena Battle") in their textContent — match names with `includes`, and remember a `.campaign-button` label may be "<name> Arena Battle". CLICK-SCOPE LAW (live 2026-11-12 improve run): the quick-create buttons (New Campaign/Arena/Starter) sit among the FIRST `button` elements on the selection screen — a `querySelectorAll('button')[i]` index click can silently open the quick-create modal; scope every campaign click to `.campaign-button`. AUTO-WIZARD PROBE LAW (live 2026-11-12 QR; SHARPENED 2026-11-18 improve zero-char-wizard-offer-once): the zero-character select-callback wizard (App.jsx `loaded.length === 0` branch, gated by `campaignRef.current.isLocalhost && !isWizardOffered(name)` — SHARPENED 2026-11-20 fix zero-char-auto-wizard-traps-lan-visitor: visitor origins never auto-mount it and never burn the offer key (localhost gate first in the `&&`), and `FirstSessionChecklist` "Build your heroes" rides the same `isLocalhost` prop as its GM-only sibling rows; the effect CANNOT use the destructured `isLocalhost` binding — declared after the effect, TDZ-throws in deps — read it off `campaignRef.current`) mounts in a `[class*=wizard]` container, NOT `.modal-overlay` — a `.modal-overlay`-keyed 'wizard did not auto-open' reading is a FALSE NEGATIVE, and `evaluate`-dispatched `.click()` bypasses its backdrop (successful evaluate nav-clicks prove nothing about overlay presence). MOUNT CAN LAG SEVERAL SECONDS after a quick-create — live PD 2026-11-19: absent at +2.5 s post-create on both throwaways, `.character-creation-wizard-overlay` present ~+6 s; re-probe before judging absence. OFFER KEYS ARE PER-ORIGIN localStorage (live VN 2026-11-20): a fresh LAN-origin tab on a zero-char campaign DOES auto-mount the wizard and stamps `character-wizard-offered:<name>` on THAT origin even when the localhost profile is consumed — and that visitor mount is a dead-end trap (create POST is GM-only 403) — see `bug-zero-char-auto-wizard-traps-lan-visitor-creation-is-gm-only`. The auto-open is now OFFERED ONCE PER CAMPAIGN PER CLIENT: it fires only when localStorage `character-wizard-offered:<campaign>` is absent (facade `src/services/ui/wizardOfferedStore.js`, same TV-pref tier as `initiative-tv`) and stamps the key the moment it mounts — so a 'wizard did not auto-open' reading on a RE-select is the EXPECTED post-fix face (the old every-select re-arm law is DEAD; Cancel-first applies only to a campaign's first-ever select), and re-arming the probe means deleting the key or a clean profile — SHARPENED (UM 2026-11-20): the shared Playwright MCP profile PERSISTS these keys across runs and across campaign re-creation, so a re-created `qa-arena`/`qa-starter` honestly shows NO auto-wizard on entry; before judging "auto-open missing", census `Object.keys(localStorage).filter(k=>k.startsWith('character-wizard-offered'))` and `removeItem` the campaign's key. Re-creating a deleted throwaway under the SAME name lands straight on the consumed-offer face — no close-the-wizard dance needed to verify zero-character-stage faces (live improve zero-char-empty-state 2026-11-20). Related census artifact (CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-12 improve dice-tray-svg-glyph-leak): the sidebar dice tray USED to leak decorative-SVG digit glyphs into `textContent` (`4d4`, `8d8`, `10d10`, `12d12`, `%d100` concatenated right after the Admin button in body-text dumps) — the digits now live in component-scoped CSS `::after` on `.dice-glyph` wrappers (SVGs `aria-hidden focusable=false`), so every `.dice-btn` reads exactly `d4`…`d100`; a digit-leak sighting is stale evidence. SEPARATE LAW: tray rolls are NOT pure client UI — each roll writes a `roll` campaign-log row `Dice Tray dN` (pinned by `Sidebar.dice-tray-log.test.jsx`); clear the log after tray probing.
- **2024 class-feature row routing (grounded 2026-11-13 improve paladin-lay-on-hands-gap BLOCKED re-verify):** 2024 action rows derive LIVE via `src/services/rules/rules-actions.js getActions2024` ← `classRules2024.getFeatures` (flattens `class.class_levels`) ← `featureCategorizationUtils.categorizeFeatures`, whose PRIMARY router is the automation's `casting_time`, not a feature-name whitelist: a `healing_pool` feature with `"1 bonus action"` (e.g. 2024 Paladin `Lay On Hands`) lands in the **Bonus Actions** table (`CharBonusActions.jsx` has a dedicated `healing_pool` badge lane), passive features land in Special Actions. Consequence: innerText/action audits scoped to `Special Actions` FALSE-NEGATIVE rows that legitimately live under Bonus Actions (the DG backlog claim "no usable Lay On Hands row" was exactly this artifact — the row rides Bonus Actions and opens the HealingPoolModal chooser like CelestialWarlock's Healing Light). A `layOnHandsPool` key in change-data is PROOF-OF-COLLECTION (tracked-resource registration reads the automation), not evidence of a missing row. LIVE-PINNED (explore EH 2026-11-13): the EP sheet's `Lay On Hands:` row rides Bonus Actions and opens the `#healing-pool-target-modal-title` chooser; its amount path heals honestly (pool 100→90, bucket `currentHitPoints` 100→110 persisted). A Warrior-of-Mercy subclass swap on Disciplined_Monk (disk `class.subclass.name`, reload re-derives) puts `Hand of Healing:`/`Hand of Harm:`/`Hand of Ultimate Mercy:` on the sheet — swap+restore recipe re-proven byte-exact (md5 `ba4d456c`).
- **Create-family dismiss law (shipped 2026-11-13 DG-improve seeded-modal-escape-dismiss):** all three quick-create modals (New Campaign inline + Arena/Starter via `SeededCampaignModal`) now answer Escape and a pure-backdrop click, routed to the same inert `onCancel` lane as Cancel (no discard confirm — an un-submitted name is nothing to discard); wiring lives in `src/hooks/ui/useModalDismiss.js` (mirrors the `3f41dc16b` DeleteConfirmModal guard: dismiss fires only when NEITHER click target NOR recorded mousedown was in `.modal-content`; `{disabled}` keeps dismiss inert mid-create/mid-seeding). Backdrop probes on this family target `.modal-overlay` (create tier), NOT `.modal-shell-overlay` — the DG-era "SeededCampaignModal has no Escape/backdrop dismiss" claim is DEAD — ALL THREE FACES LIVE-VERIFIED (explore EH 2026-11-13 on the shipped build): Escape closes the Arena modal with zero materialization, a pure backdrop (mousedown+mouseup both on `.modal-overlay`) closes the Starter modal, and the drag-guard holds (content-mousedown → backdrop-mouseup = NO dismiss).
- **Racing a transient render window (live 2026-11-12 improve initiative-empty-state):** a short-lived face (e.g. the pre-authoring empty state before the Initiative mount-merge settles) can last under one render frame warm (~16–30 ms) — `waitForFunction` raf-polling and `setInterval` samplers MISS the press opportunity (3 attempts failed). Deterministic pattern: `page.addInitScript` a MutationObserver that stashes the target copy AND dispatches the button click inside the observer callback — the microtask runs on the very commit that mounts the face and reliably precedes the settle macrotask.
- **Initiative HP settle gate (live 2026-11-16 improve initiative-hp-settle-flash):** HP-flash REPRO SEED LAW — the seeded board must CARRY PLAYER ROWS (the creation-bake face): a goblin-only board never flashes (no player rows → no mount-merge defer, cards paint real values). The XB-era "cosmetic flash" claim under-read it: uncontrolled `defaultValue` inputs never repaint after the bake lands, so the fabricated `0` was PERSISTENT, not transient — any fix must force a remount (shipped gate flips `key='hp-settling'|'hp-live'`, the presence-keyed `InitiativeControl` idiom) alongside the `disabled` placeholder. Card activation seam law: card-body click does NOT activate (`CreatureCard` has no `onActivate`) — GM turn activation rides Next/Prev/`+`/arrows; and `:has-text("<Monster>")` card scoping strict-violates (the target dropdown re-names every monster on every card) — keep `:has(input[aria-label="<Name> current HP"])`.
- **Quest-board + planner lanes and the zero-char empty-state card (all lived VN 2026-11-20, qa-starter throwaway fixture):** player board read = `GET /quests/board` — OPEN to every origin, rows whitelisted to `id,name,status,description,rewards` (GM `notes` never leave the server — `GMONLYSECRET` census-zero in visitor text), `draft:true` rows dropped SERVER-SIDE for all origins (the GM planner still lists them with a draft badge); grouping ladder Active→Completed→Failed via `groupQuestsForBoard` (empty groups hidden; unknown statuses normalize to active — `archived` pill reads `active`); Show-more earned only above 140 chars — collapsed face computes `-webkit-line-clamp:3`, expanded `none`, `aria-expanded`+label flip; empty board = `No quests on the board yet.` and the Faction Standings strip hides when `factions/standings` (`{factions:[]}`, origin-open) is empty; a MOUNTED board live-refreshes on the `quests-list-<c>` broadcast (add/delete/status-flip all re-group without reload). PLANNER WRITE LAW: `POST /quests {quests:[…]}` is a whole-array write with the jsonEntityCrud blind-overwrite tripwire — intentionally DROPPING a live row through the array honestly 409s `Quest "X" was created or deleted…`; the sanctioned removal lane is `DELETE /quests/<id>`; New Quest form ids `#quest-name/#quest-status(active|completed|failed)/#quest-description/#quest-rewards/#quest-notes`; LAN visitor POST/GET on the planner lane 403 `[quests] BLOCKED` (board lane stays open). ZERO-CHAR EMPTY STATE (`src/components/campaign-empty-state/`): card `.empty-campaign-state` owns the blank stage ONLY while `!activeView && characters.length===0 && no wizard`; buttons `.empty-campaign-add-btn` (isLocalhost-gated — absent for visitors) + `.empty-campaign-checklist-btn` (renders iff starter-seed GET carries `entities` — arena face is add-button-only, `/starter-seed` answers 200 `{seed:null}` for non-starters); the checklist button REVIVES a dismissed checklist via `checklistEpoch` remount (App.jsx:957 `key={checklistEpoch}`). `qa-arena` prunes Quest Board/Quests from the sidebar (campaign-only views).
- **EffectAdder complexity ceiling + registry membership laws (live improve zone-manual-add-inert-feedback 2026-11-19):** `EffectAdder` sits AT the lint complexity cap (15) — adding a new inline `{flag && …}` JSX branch tips it to a warning; extract a module-level presentational micro-component (e.g. `CastArmedSavesHint`) so the modal's branch count is unchanged. Registry law: `TARGET_EFFECT_DEFINITIONS` carries ONLY `Grease (Zone)` — there is NO plain `Grease` spell row; an exact-`Grease` browse click stalls the locator. Zone te lane: `onApply('effects',…)` settles fast — ~1.2 s then GET the TOP-LEVEL `targetEffects` key (the monster bucket stays empty); caster `_<prefix>_<source>` tracking keys never appear from manual adds (hint-only by owner design, flag `castArmedSaves` on grease/sleet_storm/stinking_cloud/web). SAME-CEILING FAMILY (lived fix c70d5cf65): `MonsterCardModal`'s async chip press-handler sits AT cap 15 too — even an `&&` inside a `const` initializer tips the warning; hoist discriminators to module-level helpers (precedents `isMeleePressRange` in MonsterCardModal.jsx, `foldFiveFootRangeRiders` in conditionEffects.js).
- **EffectAdder (`.ea-overlay`) config-selector laws (live improve leaked-saveAbility 2026-11-19):** the Effects-tab def search is SUBSTRING-FUZZY — a `hasText` regex like `/^.*Save Disadv\s*$/` silently captures `DEX Save Disadv` (icon-prefixed labels defeat `^` anchoring); reliable walk: click the row, then assert `.ea-config-header` textContent before filling any field, and disambiguate rows with `getByText(label, { exact: true })`. The zone dc field is `input[type=number]` (React native-setter + input event) and the source `select` lists the full roster INCLUDING monsters (bare `Goblin` present). Badge remove-lane: `button.creature-badge-remove` (`title="Remove effect"`) is NESTED inside the badge button — the label text lives on the PARENT's `textContent`, so filter removes by `parentElement.textContent`, not by a `.creature-badge` class match (misses silently). Headless GM admin clears answer 200 with NO native dialog and hold `{}`/[] once the last tab is closed — campaign-scoped paths `/api/campaigns/:c/admin/clear-change-data` + `/…/admin/clear-log` (a BARE `POST /admin/clear-change-data` 404s `Cannot POST` — corrected live RF 2026-11-19; canonical form in the Admin-clears bullet). CONFIG-PANE NAV (live QE 2026-11-19): selecting a def SWAPS the browse list for `.ea-config` (subsequent row clicks no-op — the list is gone); `Back` restores browse, typing into `.ea-search` also resets the selection. The castArmedSaves hint renders as `.ea-config .ea-dc-hint` inside the config pane ONLY for the four zone defs (byte copy `Recurring saves only when cast — GM manual add marks the zone.`; non-zone defs render zero `.ea-dc-hint` inside `.ea-config`). POST-`ffb985151` a manual Grease (Zone) apply persists te `{target,effect,source,saveDc}` with NO `saveAbility` — a saveAbility presence-probe on fresh manual adds is honestly ABSENT. Condition-tab Apply lands in the target's `activeConditions` CHAR KEY (a cs-row `conditions` read is a false negative).
- Playwright MCP required keys (silent-arg-rejection noise): `browser_console_messages` needs `level`; `browser_network_requests` needs `static`; `browser_take_screenshot` needs `scale` ("css"|"device" — missing key is a hard arg error, live ZK 2026-11-17). `browser_click` takes `target` (ref-or-selector), NOT a bare `ref` key (older `element`+`ref` shape errors `target: Missing key`). `browser_run_code_unsafe` code MUST be an `async (page) => {...}` function expression — a bare `await page…` body throws `SyntaxError: Unexpected identifier 'page'` (live 2026-11-08 improve). Set `page.setDefaultTimeout(5000)` at the top of each run — an unqualified `locator.click()` burns MCP's 30s default on a miss, and per-call inline `{timeout}` overrides are NOT dependable (live 2026-11-21 improve attack-log-cover-ac: a wait with an explicit override still burned the 30 s default) — the run-start `setDefaultTimeout` is the binding guard. EXACT-LABEL LAW (live 2026-11-13 BE): `locator('button',{hasText:/^Reject$/})` timed out on a button whose trimmed text is exactly `Reject` (raw `textContent` matching), while `getByRole('button',{name:'Reject',exact:true})` resolved first try — anchor exact button labels with the ROLE matcher, not a `hasText` regex. SIDEBAR-HEADER TAG LAW (live MA 2026-11-18; corrected in place by improve sidebar-header-keyboard 2026-11-18): view headers are UNIFIED on the single `button.sidebar-section-header` tier — the headless div face (Characters; its real class was `sidebar-section-header-static`, no stat readout ever lived there) is gone, censuses show zero `div.sidebar-section-header`; `button.`-scoped locators now match ALL headers (the class-anchor idiom still works and stays safe). NEW CAUTION: the Characters header is a click/keyboard-TOGGLEABLE disclosure (per-client `useState`, default EXPANDED, never persisted) — a stray press collapses the roster submenu until pressed again, so roster-link probes must assume expanded or re-press the header. ARIA-IS-NOT-TEXT corollary (live 2026-11-13 improve heal-modal-live-hp-echo): `aria-label` values never enter `getByText`/`wait_for(text:)` matching — probe an `aria-label` adoption (e.g. heal-picker radio `Select X, N of M HP`) with `getAttribute('aria-label')` polling; a text-keyed waiter false-negatives on the aria lane while the visible row span updates in lockstep. PITFALL (live 2026-11-12 improve ai-dress-vocab-hint): `page.fill` on a React-19 controlled textarea can silently SKIP onChange (DOM value set, state empty, submit stays disabled) — focus-click then `page.keyboard.type` instead. PITFALL (2026-11-08 improve music-dupe-label-echo): inside `browser_run_code_unsafe`, nested backtick template literals in the code string (e.g. building `[title="Add ${label}…"]` with a template literal) can throw `ReferenceError: Unknown identifier 'title'` in the MCP code runner — build such selectors by plain-quote string concatenation instead. Same family (live 2026-11-11 improve map3d-census-avatars): a literal `\n` two-char escape in the code string throws `SyntaxError` through the MCP runner — build newlines with `String.fromCharCode(10)`. Same family again (live 2026-11-11 improve hidden-vs-fogged-dim): some bodies bounced `SyntaxError: Unterminated template` with NO template literal present — don't re-run blind, fall back to browser_snapshot ref + browser_click. Sidebar view nav buttons are `button.sidebar-section-header` (text match; not `<a>`; textContent carries a LEADING space — " Maps" — so a `^Maps$` strict regex misses, use substring match; live 2026-11-10 improve). `browser_take_screenshot` requires the `scale` key in this MCP build, and with `filename` it writes to the WORKSPACE ROOT — dirties `git status`; delete the artifact at exit (live 2026-11-11 improve map3d-inline-imageurl) — pass filenames with a `.playwright-mcp/` prefix to keep the tree clean (live 2026-11-12 improve ai-dress-audit-description). PITFALL (EN 2026-11-08): `fetch('/api/…')` at the OUTER scope of `browser_run_code_unsafe` throws `TypeError: Failed to parse URL` (Node context has no base URL) — always use absolute `http://localhost:5173/api/…` or fetch inside `page.evaluate`. `browser_tabs {action:'close'}` WITHOUT an index closes the CURRENT tab — a blind follow-up close can wipe the page you needed; recover with `browser_navigate`. Slice before returning big DOM censuses (an unscoped `querySelectorAll('input')` dump on Encounters returned 500+ monster-checkbox rows in one tool result). TWO-CONTEXT gotcha (live 2026-11-08 improve map-advisory-view-new-button): with an `isolatedContext:'lan'` tab open, `browser_run_code_unsafe`'s `page` binds to the CURRENT tab and `page.context().pages()` sees ONLY that context — the localhost GM tab lives in a sibling context reachable via `page.context().browser().contexts()[i].pages()[0]`; a GM-targeted locator against the wrong context silently times out. `browser_tabs {action:'select', index}` changes which context the next run binds to (per-tab `browser_console_messages` depends on it). SHARPENED (live XC 2026-11-16): the escape ban covers EVERY `\uXXXX`/`\x`/`\n`-style escape in the code string, not just the two-char `\n` — `/\u000d|\n/g` and `split('\n')` both bounced SyntaxError while all other bytes were identical; build the characters instead (`String.fromCharCode(10)` for newline-in-body-text, `(215)` for ×, `(39)/(34)` for quote-bearing CSS attribute values) or pass values through `page.evaluate(fn, arg)`. A SyntaxError bounce is CLEAN (code never runs, zero side-effects land — distinct from the land-then-throw family): fix the string and re-fire safely. REF-PREFIX DRIFT (live XX 2026-11-20): after navigations/new tabs, snapshot refs gain a frame prefix (`f4e16`) — a `\[ref=(e\d+)\]` regex silently MISSES and the click throws `Ref … not found`; match with `\[ref=([^\]]+)\]`. `browser_evaluate`'s `function` param must be a complete function-expression STRING (`async () => {…}` is fine; a stray key beside `function:` in the call object is a pre-exec SyntaxError). `+ Add music category` (and any sidebar toggle-button ride) is TOGGLE-semantics — a failed mid-script click leaves the form OPEN and the next click silently closes it; re-probe `.music-category-form` existence before filling. NEW SIBLING (live GGG 2026-11-21): `page.waitForFunction(…)` INSIDE a `browser_run_code_unsafe` code string bounces `SyntaxError: Unexpected identifier 'Function'` from the MCP runner's source transform — the code never runs; poll with `page.waitForTimeout` + `page.evaluate` loops instead (every GGG ride passed that way). Same-run CLEAN bounce family witness: an evaluate body referencing bare `document` OUTSIDE `page.evaluate` (e.g. building a return object) throws `ReferenceError: document is not defined` after its side-effecting calls already LANDED — judge state by re-probe, never assume the error aborted the ride.
- **Sheet feature-row name-prefix trap (live improve 2026-11-14):** the tracked-resource DISPLAY row (`Lay On Hands Pool: 100/100 (cur/max)`) precedes the activation row (`Lay On Hands:`) in DOM order — a `startsWith('Lay On Hands')` row click silently hits the display row (inert, no modal); match exact `textContent.trim() === 'Lay On Hands:'`. Also: a dice-pool row with an unseeded current value (`Healing Light: /21`) click-opens nothing — the tracked-resource bucket must exist first. TAG FACE (live 2026-11-14 improve houm-sheet-target-chooser; SHARPENED live LA 2026-11-18): sheet FEATURE activation rows are `b.clickable` elements — a `td`/`div`/`span`-scoped leaf probe for the exact row text silently finds NOTHING and the first press no-ops; include `b` in the probe selector. WEAPON ATTACK rows are the OPPOSITE shape: `div.left.clickable`/`span.clickable` whose textContent starts with the weapon name — a `b.clickable`-scoped `/^Glaive/` locator matches nothing (Playwright `locator('b.clickable',{hasText:/^Glaive/})` timed out live); walk attacks via `page.evaluate` textContent over `.clickable`, and press with the same evaluate dispatch (or unscoped `locator('.clickable')`). MCP-tab survival face (same run): the shared MCP `page` handle can be navigated OFF-localhost by an external actor BETWEEN `browser_run_code_unsafe` calls (witnessed on a google.com search tab) — re-probe `page.url()` at the top of every call and re-`goto` localhost; never follow the foreign URL. SHARED-BROWSER CONTENTION (live MA 2026-11-18, witnessed hard): a SECOND live opencode session sharing the same playwright-mcp browser drives the SAME default page — its attack presses/chooser cancels land inside YOUR log attribution (attribute by log timestamps vs your call windows, never trust page-local cues alone), and its `browser_close` destroys your auxiliary `browser.newContext()` tabs and parks the default page on about:blank. Mitigation: a marker-tagged own context (`localStorage.__runMA` + find via `browser.contexts()` each call) isolates your rides until the other session's browser_close, and with one shared server store the only trustworthy judge is the server-side GET.
- **HoH picker re-anchor + divergent-dwell rig (live MM 2026-11-14, post-`9c3ec68ea`; SHARPENED NP 2026-11-14 post-`1d6cc8e2c`+`3c369ae84`):** the picker stage re-renders target rows every render through `resolveLiveTargetHp` — **N = bucket `currentHitPoints` canonical; M = bucket `hitPoints` FIRST, live cs-row `maxHp` only as FALLBACK** (NP false-negative face: after a fresh select+settle the mirror carries `hitPoints`=real max, so a scoped row PATCH is honestly INVISIBLE to the picker until the mirror is thinned — two "echo is dead" misdiagnoses before the bucket-first law was read in `liveTargetHp.js`). Divergent-dwell recipe (the negative-heal bug door, now a GREEN fix-verify face): (1) full-store `POST /<Victim> {value:{currentHitPoints:60}}` (thin payload — the echo REPLACE evicts `hitPoints` from the loaded tab's mirror, restoring row-fallback), (2) force the cs row below it (`PATCH /combatSummary {creatures:[{name,maxHp:1,currentHp:1}]}`), (3) open the picker — inverted pair now renders the BARE `Select <Name>` aria (post-`3c369ae84`; the MM-era `60 of 1` face is DEAD), (4) mid-dwell `PATCH` row maxHp above the bucket → aria re-anchors to the honest `60 of 112` within one echo (distinguishing form: bare→full, NP-proved), commit applies against the SAME live row the gate resolved (`1d6cc8e2c`): positive heal capped at live headroom, `hp_change` positive with live max, `(Regained N HP)` audit, Focus spent FOR the heal; an inverted-pair commit WITHOUT raising the row REFUSES inline (gate reads max<current ⇒ full) with zero requests — never a drain. FORENSIC LAW: log `hp_change sourceName:'sheet editor'` is written ONLY by CharHitPoints (grep-unique) and requires a typed commit on some client — an unexplained such row rules out app-internal lanes and points at another human/tab (MM witnessed one it could not author; single-context census via `page.context().browser().contexts()` first). DISPLAY LAW (shipped 2026-11-14 improve hoh-picker-mount-inverted-fraction): `SecondaryTargetModal` `hasHpValues` now requires `currentHp <= maxHp` — an inverted pair hides the HP chip, percent lane and aria HP segment (no fabricated max; aria reads bare `Select <Name>`); honest pairs (`60 of 112`) render byte-identically, pinned by `SecondaryTargetModal.inverted-hp.test.jsx`.
- **Heal-audit sign-stamp law + mass-heal chooser faces (shipped 2026-11-14 improve heal-audit-sign-stamp):** EVERY `hp_change` writer in the heal families stamps `isHealing: <written delta> > 0` (never literal `true`) — healingRoll `logHealingToSSE` + zero-call-site `rollHealingForAction` (whose `delta: result.delta` undefined-byte landmine became `result.actualHeal`), massHealUtils/massHealHandler/massHealSpellFactory/prayerOfHealingService, and the Flurry HoH rider in bonusAttacksHandler; the delta-0 over-log quirk rows stay but read `isHealing:false`. CHOOSER FACES: the mass-heal/Prayer chooser rides `.sp-overlay` chrome with NO `role=dialog` and unlabelled checkboxes (match by `<label>` text) — a `[role=dialog]`-keyed "chooser never opened" probe false-negatives while it silently dwells and intercepts sheet clicks; nothing writes until the dynamic `Heal (N)` confirm (slot charges at cast). PITFALL: the HoH picker footer `Heal` button's textContent carries a leading icon space (`" Heal"`) — `hasText:/^Heal$/` times out, use `getByRole('button',{name:'Heal',exact:true})`. ACCNAME-TRIM REFINEMENT (live improve popup-footer-leading-space 2026-11-18): `getByRole({name,exact})` trims accessible-name whitespace in the Chromium lane, so role matchers NEVER see a leading-space artifact — raw-text matchers (`hasText`/`textContent`) are what break; the durable guarantee is byte-exact `textContent`, which a role-locator test alone cannot pin. The `.popup-modal` footer family is now byte-exact (staged-Done label = ONE expression child after the icon, gap CSS-owned via `.dice-roll-done-btn i`, OA-chip precedent); RESIDUAL (unfixed, other chrome tier — same one-line recipe applies if a future ride needs those names): `HandOfHealingModal`, `HealingPoolModal`, `EyebiteEffectModal`, `BastionOfLawSpendModal` footers still carry JSX-space `</i> Done` leading spaces (`.sp-overlay`/modal tier, not `.popup-modal`).
- **Mirror/bucket-shape + headless Admin laws (live 2026-11-14 improve hoh-picker-mount-inverted-fraction, sharpened same-day improve heal-audit-sign-stamp):** (a) player combatSummary rows bake ONCE at campaign-select ONLY when the server has NO board, at `maxHp: computedStats?.hp?.max || hp?.max || 1` (App.jsx:456-466) — the char-file fixtures carry NEITHER key, so the bake lands 1/1 placeholder rows, and they are PERMANENT, not transient (CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-15 explore run PW, PT#2 closed code+live: `mergeCombatSummaryWithCharacters` (initiativeService.js:231) is keep-and-additive — the player-row branch preserves HP bytes and normalizes only initiative/targetName/concentration; appended skeleton rows carry NO HP fields; live mount kept nine 1/1 rows byte-exact through five real resurrections riding on the buckets). Bucket-canonical is the whole story; a divergent-dwell rig must FORCE the split via a scoped row-PATCH (the creation-bake makes the 1/1 starting point, never a settle-time writer updates it later). (b) `setRuntimeValue` POSTs the WRITING tab's whole local mirror, and mirrors gain `hitPoints` only via `seedCharacterRuntimeData` (App.jsx ~291/542) which runs AFTER async party-stats settle — a third-party `currentHitPoints` write from a pre-settle tab, or a CharSheet statsLoadError lane, leaves a THIN bucket `{currentHitPoints}` with no max, so bucket-first HP resolution must tolerate a missing max (this is what makes the picker's inverted first paint real-lane, not rig-only).
- **Heal-modal DWELL mechanics (live run JA 2026-11-14):** a picker/result modal opened from a sheet feature row (`b.clickable`) STAYS OPEN across separate `browser_run_code_unsafe` CALLS (GENERALIZED live 2026-11-21 improve qms-outdoor-hide-token: the overlay family incl. the quick-spawn picker dwells across calls too — a between-calls `press('Escape')`/click can land on the wrong layer or re-dwell it; DRAIN any open overlay at the START of the next run before arming probes/spies, not after) — a subsequent row-click is silently intercepted by `.sp-overlay` (Playwright retries to timeout: `… <div class="sp-overlay">…</div> … intercepts pointer events`); either finish the open lane in the same call or press the dismiss control first (`Done`; `Cancel` on the HoH picker, `Skip` on LoH/Healing-Light choosers) and confirm the overlay is gone. HoH lane selectors all proved stable: picker root `[role=dialog][aria-labelledby=hand-of-healing-target-modal-title]`, result root `[role=dialog][aria-labelledby=hand-of-healing-modal-title]`, LoH roots `#healing-pool-modal-title` / chooser `#healing-pool-target-modal-title`; mid-dwell writes ride full-store `POST /<Victim> {value:{…}}` (replaces the whole bucket — keep the victim's other seeded keys in the body). DICE-LANE FACE (live 2026-11-14 improve healing-modal-inline-styles): `.healing-roll-details` renders ONLY on the dice-pool lane — ride CelestialWarlock's `Healing Light:` (isDicePool, d6, max=CHA mod); the LoH amount lane never renders it, and the roll details are visible only BETWEEN `Roll` and `Done`; the healing modal's loading row is uncatchable by ≤10 ms sampling (the combat-context fetch resolves faster) — CSSOM rule presence + correct modal render is the practical proof. HARNESS FACE (same run): when the MCP tool set lacks `browser_run_code_unsafe`, ONE async `browser_evaluate` call can drive a whole dwell lane (row-click → picker → commit) inside the page context, sidestepping the cross-call `.sp-overlay` interception law entirely.
- Badge-remove probe (2026-11-09 improve st-card-badge-parity): `CreatureBadge`'s × is a SIBLING of `.creature-badge` inside `.creature-badge-wrapper` — `badge.querySelector('button[title=…]')` returns null; use `badge.closest('.creature-badge-wrapper').querySelector('.creature-badge-remove')`. te rows live in the TOP-LEVEL change-data `targetEffects` bucket (not under creature keys). Named-badge × one-shot (live 2026-11-09 fix run): `browser_click` target `.creature-badge-wrapper:has(:text("Poisoned")) .creature-badge-remove` removes a named badge without an evaluate round-trip — but `:has-text()`/`:text()` are Playwright-engine selectors that THROW in `browser_evaluate`'s `querySelector`; inside evaluate anchor via `:has(input[aria-label="<Name> current HP"])` + textContent filtering instead. dc-gate DOM fact (live 2026-11-09 fix dad80f67b): a blank-DC condition badge now renders `span.creature-badge` (inert, description tooltip only — no save roll offered); only DC-bearing badges are `button.creature-badge` — locators anchoring a blank-DC badge by `button.` are obsolete post-fix.
- PlanEditor unit-test pitfall (live 2026-11-14 improve designer-plan-aria-labels): `Propose build plan` is `disabled={busy || messages.length === 0}` — a test opening the PlanEditor must rehydrate ≥1 conversation message first, and `fireEvent.click` on a disabled button silently no-ops (looks like "plan never opened"). As a11y anchors the plan card now exposes stable `aria-label`s on all 11 controls (`Scope`, `Levels (min)`, `Levels (max)`, `Story spine`, `<kind>s` per PLAN_KINDS row) — resolve by accessible name (getByRole `{ name, exact: true }` — default name matching is case-insensitive substring), not nth().
- Endpoints: spell-overlay is `/spell-overlay?campaign=<c>` — NO `/api` prefix (Vite-proxied; `/api/spell-overlay` → 404 Cannot GET, corrected live 2026-11-05). Map-with-tokens one-shot is `POST /api/campaigns/:c/maps` (the `/maps` shorthand 404s on :80 — corrected live 2026-11-05). Log `/api/campaigns/:name/log`; change-data `/api/campaigns/:name/change-data` (keys NESTED `d['<Name>'].<key>`; top-level specials `combatSummary`,`lastAttack`,`pendingSavePrompts`,`__map__`); campaign JSON PUT `/api/campaigns/:c/<Char>.json`; full-store POST `/api/campaigns/:c/<CharName>` and `/combatSummary` WRapping in `{value:{...}}` — unwrapped silently no-ops; `/changes/:key` REPLACES whole char object; change-data object buckets ALSO accept scoped `PATCH /:campaign/:key` `{value:{…}}` (2026-11-04 write-lane improvement): server merges fields — name-keyed record arrays (cs `creatures`) merge per record by `name`, unmatched records warn+skip — and SSE-echoes the FULL merged bucket; 404 if no object stored (creation stays on POST — a heal-rig seed for a NEVER-OPENED character honestly refuses with 404 `no object stored… create via POST`, even while a loaded tab's in-memory bucket exists — so seed via full-store `POST /<Char> {value:{…}}` first; status corrected in place 2026-11-21, an earlier note said 400). COUNTER LAW (shipped 2026-11-21 improve applied-counter-honest-fields): the 200 body reports `applied` (name-keyed record-array merges ONLY — honest record-merge truth, = 0 for a char bucket) AND `appliedFields` (top-level patch fields actually WRITTEN, write-not-diff counting, each key once at its shallowest level); a landed char-bucket patch answers `{applied:0, appliedFields:n}` — the old counter-lie workaround is RETIRED, though GET remains the cheap truth witness. SHARPENED (2026-11-10 PFEG-fix run): the flip side is real too — ARRAY fields (e.g. `activeBuffs`) silently DO NOT merge on a scoped PATCH (200 returned, GET shows the array unchanged; a `currentHitPoints` scalar in the SAME PATCH landed); replace arrays via full-store POST, and to affect a LOADED tab's mirror you still need reload + re-select (§49 law — a pre-select curl seed is the only mid-load-session-safe buff write; the save lanes read the mirror) — SHARPENED (live 2026-11-14 improve cure-echo-live-conditions): the full-store POST's `change-<c>-<Char>` echo REPLACES the whole bucket and mounted store SUBSCRIBERS (e.g. the heal modal's `activeConditions`/HP echo lane) adopt it mid-dwell with NO reload — the reload+re-select law stands only for NON-reactive `getRuntimeValue` readers and pre-existing settled state; - **Rejected change-data writes log a dedicated `write_rejected` audit row** `{type:'write_rejected', origin:'server-validator', key, reason, description:'Server rejected write to <key>: <reason>'}` (`campaigns-changedata.js` validator-refusal branch, pinned by `campaigns-changedata-l5-validation.test.js` P4-d; improve 2026-11-10) — the gameplay lane's `{type:'enforcement_denied', creature, bucket, source, reason}` rows carry NO origin and render via the generic row; `write_rejected` renders via the dedicated `LogWriteRejectedEntry` (`log-write-rejected`) and appears as its own `Write Rejected` Type-filter option, so filtering `Enforcement Denied` now shows ONLY gameplay denials (this REPLACES the GC-era "distinguish by origin; log-type filters mix the two" note). The 400 body and `denied-<c>-<key>` SSE are unchanged (no client consumer of denied-* exists — 400s surface via the write's rejection, e.g. the Admin `Server refused:` inline notice). Admin: POST `/api/campaigns/:c/admin/clear-change-data` + `/admin/clear-log` (the native `window.confirm` is a UI-BUTTON behavior only — headless exit ritual: close the tab, POST the routes, GET-verify `{}`/`[]`; live 2026-11-07 clock-expiry improve).
- **Planner lanes (notes/sessions/quests/npcs/factions/settlements — deep live 2026-11-12 run OP, guard matrix LIVE 2026-11-16 run WK via `curl -H 'Host: <LAN-IP>'` on :80 + LAN tab; every gate re-verified from a real en0:5173 tab 2026-11-12 QR; pre-fix faces kept for history → archive):** GUARD MATRIX (current law): npcs GET 200 — read stays open for the Initiative/attack pipeline (`contextBuilder-map.js`/`damageRoll.js` loadNPCs; a 403 would silently strip map-aware combat math; live LAN tab: Initiative mounts clean, zero pageerrors) but non-GET 403 `Access denied: GM-only feature` + `[npcs] BLOCKED` dev-log (`8ef8bae63`, pinned `npcs-localhost-guard.test.js`); quests/sessions whole-lane 403 on EVERY method incl. GET (`eced35cf6` quest GET = hidden-title-leak fix; `2081e3a65` sessions; `8d93705a5` notes reads survive but the sidebar is localhostOnly + writes gated) (path-scoped `refuseUnlessGm` BEFORE the conflict guard, so the private/hidden-label 409 leak is unreachable; quest GET = hidden-title-leak fix; dev-logs `[quests] BLOCKED non-localhost …`/`[sessions] BLOCKED non-localhost …`; pinned `quests-localhost-guard.test.js`/`sessions-localhost-guard.test.js`); factions+settlements whole-lane 403 on every method (dev-logs `[factions]`/`[settlements] BLOCKED`, pinned `factions-localhost-guard.test.js`/`settlements-localhost-guard.test.js`); notes GM-only WRITES (`notes.js` wraps the factory router, non-GET → 403 `Access denied: GM-only feature` + `[notes] BLOCKED non-localhost …` dev-log, pinned `notes-write-localhost-guard.test.js`); notes READS unchanged (list filters isPrivate, GET private id → 403 `Access denied: private note`); sidebar mirrors it — visitor LAN tab shows Drafts/Encounters/Factions/Maps-manager/Notes/NPCs/Quests/Sessions/Settlements/Admin all ABSENT, roster + Initiative/Inventory/Log/Map/Music/Rules only. NPC bestiary-name law is ALSO client-gated (`a8337dbbb`, live XB 2026-11-16: typing a published-monster name disables Save and renders the server copy VERBATIM inline with zero network — trim+lowercase matching, "  goblin " gates; the gate is fail-open while `/data/monsters.json` loads, server 400 stays the backstop for the never-typed face); both Save buttons disable on empty AND whitespace-only names. QUESTS GM law: modal `#quest-name`/`#quest-status`/`#quest-description`/`#quest-rewards`/`#quest-notes`, row `.ct-list-item[aria-label="Edit quest: <name>"]`, whole-array POST `{quests:[…]}` + per-row DELETE by id, NO PUT lane; delete is modal-footer `.ct-btn-danger` behind NATIVE `Delete quest "<name>"?`; dup-names blocked CLIENT-side in-form ("A quest with that name already exists", zero network). NOTES: `GET/POST /api/campaigns/:c/notes` ⇄ `data/notes.json` — the POST takes `{notes:[…]}` WRAPPED (a raw array 400s `Expected an array for notes`); rows `{id,description,isPrivate,partyLevel,partyLocation,dateCreated,dateModified,updatedAt}`; the "No location" row label is a LEGACY-ROW RENDER FALLBACK only — Notes DISABLES Save on empty description OR empty/whitespace partyLocation (server stays permissive), and a save whose row wouldn't match the armed "Search notes" filter auto-clears that filter (matching save leaves it; SEARCH MATCHES `description` + `partyLocation`; the filter and the post-save visibility check share the one NOTE_SEARCH_FIELDS constant); zero-match copy `No notes found matching “<q>”`. Editor is a `.ct-modal-overlay` MODAL (`#note-description`/`#note-location`/`#note-private`; PreviewToggle renders `.markdown-preview`; row `.ct-list-item[aria-label="Edit note: <title>"]` click-to-edit). Confirms byte-pinned: `Discard changes to "<title>"?`, `Delete Note "<location>"?`, `Reload the note list and discard your unsaved edits?`. 409 copy family: `Note "<label>" was modified in another tab or session since you loaded it — reload the list and re-apply your edit — it was NOT saved.` (+`Reload list` btn) vs the whole-array drop leg `… was created or deleted …`; SSE `notes-list-<c>` re-fetches mounted views both directions (paused while own modal open). SESSIONS: `…/sessions` ⇄ `data/sessions.json` — create/edit ride PER-ROW PUT `/sessions/<name>` upsert (case-insensitive dup → 400 `A session with that name already exists` IN-FORM banner; empty-Name gates Save; UI create seeds auto-checklist `auto-baseline-chars`/`auto-baseline-init` → row "PLANNED 0/2 ready"; API-seeded rows show 0/0 — checklist seeds client-side only); `Delete Session Plan "<name>"?` NATIVE confirm fired by the modal footer's `.ct-modal-footer .ct-btn-danger` (row click re-opens the planner prefilled); `Mark as Played` recap is `window.prompt`. WRITE-AUTHORITY RIDE LAW: an `evaluate`-click on `.ct-new-btn` opens the modal on React's NEXT tick — a synchronous field read in the same evaluate sees an empty form, and the still-mounting `.ct-modal-overlay` then intercepts the next trusted click (locator.click stalls on "intercepts pointer events"); recover by filling the already-open modal, or use trusted click + `waitFor('#session-name')`. GM UI ride law for npc/faction/settlement: `.ct-new-btn` "New NPC|Faction|Settlement" → `.ct-modal-overlay` with `#npc-name`/`#faction-name`/`#settlement-name`, Save `button.ct-btn-primary`, rows `[aria-label="Edit NPC|faction|settlement: <name>"]`, delete via modal-footer `.ct-btn-danger` — NPC delete NATIVE `Delete NPC "<name>"?` (byte-pinned era as `Delete NPC|Faction|Settlement "<name>"?` before Faction/Settlement migrated) (confirm-override pattern, byte-pinned) while Faction/Settlement delete migrated to the shared role=dialog `DeleteConfirmModal` tier (see the Faction/Settlement lane note). Inventory authority: GET 200 open; the old whole-doc POST lane is RETIRED 404 (see the Party-Inventory bullets). The sidebar `Rules` button is an EXTERNAL launchpad (opens github.io dnd-tools in a new tab, not a view); `openRules` (`Sidebar.jsx`) CAPTURES `window.open`'s return — falsy (browser blocked) fires the app toast lane (`.sidebar-toast` `role="status"`, copy `Popup blocked — allow popups for this site to open the Rules reference`, 2500ms auto-hide, pinned by `Sidebar.rules-popup.test.jsx`); truthy → byte-identical launch, no toast (stub `window.open = () => null` in-page to test the blocked face without spawning an external tab; there is NO shared toast utility — every component owns the local state + role=status + auto-hide idiom). Lane-file RESTORE recipe: these route files persist `JSON.stringify(arr,null,2)` with NO trailing newline — a heredoc restore adds one and md5-mismatches; strip via `printf '%s' "$(cat f)" > f` (canonical pins: sessions baseline = bare `[]`, md5 d751713…; notes fixture md5 836a3fb6…; inventory md5 7638da67…). Routes read fs per request (no memory cache), so a quiet-state direct file write IS authoritative.
- **Music lane (data + editor + editor-error laws; live DH/MB, post-8dd70a511; category/Save/Reset faces re-lived DG descent 2026-11-13 post-`1a9d12c74`; EM trio + DG rig walk → archive):** truth = TOP-LEVEL change-data `music` key `{mood,playing,volume,overrides,customCategories}` + `type:'music'` log rows — rows carry `{action:'set_ambience'|'pause'|'stop', mood?, message}`; probe the MESSAGE field (a `description` read returns null). View button `Music` → `.music-player` panel, mood buttons `.music-mood-btn` (active class = live state), icon buttons ONLY by `[title=Pause]`/`[title=Stop]`/`[title=Edit tracks for this mood]` (empty textContent). First play mounts a `.yt-music-frame` YouTube iframe (lazy — idle sidebar: no frame, no iframe_api script); the component lives OUTSIDE the view outlet (App, hidden via `visible` prop) so navigating away unmounts the PANEL but keeps iframe + playback; Pause keeps `mood` + `playing:false`, Stop nulls mood (`Stopped ambience`), leaving the campaign = real teardown (`Stopped ambience (left campaign)` + `{mood:null,playing:false}`). OWNER-TEARDOWN law (fixed 2026-11-20 bug-music-owner-window-gone-claims-playing-elsewhere): the frame-owning tab while `playing` releases the shared claim on `pagehide` via two keepalive POSTs (`/music {value: stopCommit(music)}` + the `Stopped ambience (left campaign)` row to `/log`, savePrompt liveness precedent, pinned by `MusicPlayer.pagehide-release.test.jsx`), so owner RELOAD and CLOSE both land stopped truth and recovery is ONE chip click; while a live owner exists the survivor note `Playing in another window` still renders honestly; the hard-crash ghost (no pagehide) is the documented accepted residual — do NOT file one unless pagehide itself failed (WW pre-fix era → archive). Ownership probe: `document.querySelectorAll('.yt-music-frame').length` + the note, not the chip highlight (which stays ON through the honest stand-down face). FRAME-COUNT LAW (corrected in place 2026-11-20 `improve music-local-stop-unmount`): frame-count IS a trustworthy ownership witness on the LAST ACTOR tab again — the XX-era face (local Stop / delete-active kept the silent `.yt-music-frame` mounted while only the remote stand-down lane unmounted) is DEAD: both local lanes now mirror the remote-release bytes (`setPlayerMounted(false)` → mount-effect cleanup disposes), so your own Stop or delete-while-playing drops the frame to 0 immediately; a reload after a local stop writes ZERO release rows (the `playerMounted && playing` guard sees the already-released claim) and re-arming fresh-lazy-mounts exactly one frame + one iframe_api script. And a reload while PAUSED keeps the mood armed: `{mood, playing:false}` survives the reload with ZERO release rows (the `playing` guard is honest); dev-mode reload lands on Select-a-Campaign (the campaign does not auto-reselect). QUEUE-EXHAUSTION LANE (FIXED — `fix(music-queue-exhausted-leaves-dead-frame-mounted)` 91b8488ef, live-verified ZZ 2026-11-20): the exhaustion un-arm (all tracks dead → `music-queue-exhausted` → stopCommit + toast + `no_playable_tracks` row) NOW releases the frame like Stop does — `handleQueueExhausted` calls `setPlayerMounted(false)` before the commit, so `frames` drops 1→0 the same moment `.music-toast music-toast-error` renders, bytes `{mood:null,playing:false}`; the YY-era residual (frames stayed 1) is DEAD. Rig: `POST /api/campaigns/:c/music {"overrides":{"<mood>":[{"videoId":"zzBogusDeadID","title":"…"}]}}` (forged overrides are legal bytes, the lane's merge stores them; YT answers error 150 in ~3–5 s) → click the mood chip and poll toast+frames in the SAME call (straddle law) → after verifying, the whole `music` key evicts via the admin change-data clear. Stop-lane regression re-lived in the same ride: real-track arm mounts exactly 1 frame, `[title=Stop]` → 0, one `stop|Stopped ambience` row. Delete-while-playing serialization witness: a window.fetch spy recording start/end stamps shows the evict PATCH starting at the stop POST's end ms — order, not just bytes. Also lived: the LAN Music view is fully visitor-writable (arm/stop included, rows unattributed). FIXED LAWS (all three shipped 2026-11-20 improves): an already-playing active chip is INERT (`selectMood` early-returns reading the synchronously-stamped `localMusicRef` — no POST, no duplicate `Set ambience:` row, no playlist restart; paused-same-chip stays a real restart), `POST /music` validates the transport trio when present (see the next bullet's transport hardening), and a custom category CANNOT share any catalogue label — the in-form duplicate guard scans built-in + custom labels trim/case-insensitively and echoes the canonical label. Headless caveat: audible audio unverifiable (autoplay policy) and the embed emits blocked doubleclick/pagead CONSOLE ERRORS — third-party noise, not app faults; sheet-lane `spellValidation: dropping non-spell token "two"` console.errors on caster loads are FIXED (9a82ec4ef): stoplisted quantifiers drop at console.debug; console.error stays for real catalog misses. CAMPAIGN CATEGORIES (post-c9ddca4e3): the top-level `music` key gains `customCategories:{custom-<slug>:{label,icon}}` (definitions only — tracks keep living in `overrides[key]`, an empty category = no overrides row); writes ride the DEDICATED `POST /api/campaigns/:c/music` lane (intent-split merge, guards 400 with verbatim copy: key must match `custom-<slug>` so built-in shadowing is API-impossible, label non-empty ≤40, ≤20 categories); `.music-add-category` → `#music-category-label` + 9-preset icon picker + Create (inline error "Category name must include letters or numbers" for junk labels; duplicate custom labels blocked IN-FORM with `A category named "X" already exists.` where X is the EXISTING entry’s canonical `customCategories[*].label`, not the typed variant (corrected in-place 2026-11-08 improve music-dupe-label-echo); the EM re-ride observed the echo printing the NORMALIZED QUERY, not the existing label (backlog) — client-side trim/case-insensitive, the server `-2` suffix remains only as API fallback); clicking an EMPTY custom mood seeds the track editor instead of playing; Save resolves oEmbed titles into overrides rows; invalid-URL saves are console.debug-tier (inline error only, no console.error mirror). Category deletes + editor discard ride the shared role=dialog DeleteConfirmModal (2026-11-08 confirm-modal-migration): discard modal `Discard Changes?`/`Yes, Discard` (Cancel keeps editor+draft; confirm closes zero-write); delete modal `Delete Music Category`/`Yes, Delete Category` — confirm evicts the customCategories row AND overrides[key] together (toast `Category <Label> deleted`, built-ins untouched) and deleting the ACTIVE mood stops the player first; REOPEN then reads `#music-editor-mood` back on the catalogue default so a typed Save files the right key (the dangling-label discard dialog reads e.g. `Discard changes to the Town track list?`). PITFALL: while the editor is open on a mood, `button[title="Delete <Label> category"]` matches 2 elements (chip `.music-mood-delete` + in-editor `button.music-btn`) — strict-mode violation; scope the chip × with `button.music-mood-delete`. STAY-OPEN SAVE (live 2026-11-08 improve music-save-stay-open): Save with a valid id NO LONGER closes the editor — toast copy `"<Category> tracks saved — keep editing"`, textarea persists, dirty guard re-baselines on the saved text (Cancel right after Save closes silently; further edits still pay the "Discard Changes?" discard dialog); Invalid Save unchanged (inline `No valid YouTube URLs or video ids entered`, stays open, zero write). Walk-script PITFALL: once a category has saved tracks its chip title flips `Add <X> tracks` → `Play <X> ambience` — a locator that opens the editor mid-walk must use the pencil `[title="Edit tracks for this mood"]` (re-seeds the last-edited mood) or re-derive the chip title; clicking the Play chip would START playback. DANGLING-MOOD-KEY LAWS (fixed 2026-11-13 music-deleted-category-orphan-overrides, BOTH legs live-verified): deleting a custom category resets `editMood` to the catalogue default in the same commit, and Save/Reset refuse with inline `.music-editor-error` copy (zero writes) when the editor mood is missing from the merged `moods` list (covers remote-tab deletes) — orphan `overrides[custom-<deleted>]` rows no longer occur; remote-delete SIM without a second tab: API-create via `POST /api/campaigns/:c/music {"customCategories":{"custom-<slug>":{label,icon}}}` (a SHALLOW `{...base,...incoming}` merge — replacing `customCategories` wholesale is the cheapest remote-delete SIM, it SSE-broadcasts `change-<c>-music` and shrinks the mounted editor's catalogue), then Save → inline `Mood "custom-<slug>" no longer exists — pick a mood before saving`, Reset → the same copy, ZERO `/music` POSTs, zero toasts, GET shows no orphan row. MIXED-LIST WARNING (shipped 2026-11-13 improve music-mixed-line-warning): a MIXED track list saves the parseable subset AND announces the drop on the same `.music-editor-error` seam, `N line(s) are not valid YouTube URLs or video ids and were not saved` (cleared on the next keystroke; count = `enteredLineCount − parsedTracks.length` sharing the `TRACK_SEPARATORS` `/[\n,]+/` split — whitespace-only lines never counted, a comma-joined id pair on one line parses as two tracks and pays no warning; drops leave only a `console.debug`, pin tests in `MusicPlayer.mixed-save-warning.test.jsx`). GENERAL PITFALL: catalogue-derived helpers like `isCustomMood()` return FALSE for a dangling key (absent from the merged list) — existence checks must test membership in `moods`, never "custom AND missing". Probe law: reading `sel.value` on a desynced controlled `<select>` is ambiguous (a value with no matching option reads back the first option, indistinguishable from a genuine selection) — the change-data GET after Save is the authoritative display-vs-target proof.
- **Music POST /music transport hardening (improve 2026-11-20):** the route now REJECTS (honest 400, `{error}`, zero write) forged transport bytes: `music.volume` must be a finite number in [0,1] (inclusive), `music.mood` must be `null` or a key in the client `MOODS` catalogue (server imports it directly from `src/services/ui/musicConfig.js` — the maps.js→outdoorConfig.js shared-module pattern, no server-side mirror constant) ∪ the EFFECTIVE `customCategories` map (payload's own wholesale replacement when carried — so create-category+play in one POST lands; else the stored map — so a mood naming a retired key 400s), `music.playing` must be a boolean. All three validated ONLY when PRESENT — absent keys remain the editor PATCH intent (overrides/customCategories-only bodies keep the merge + oEmbed lane unchanged). Forged-write 400 witnesses need a throwaway :8123 (the owned :80 has no watcher and keeps running pre-fix code all session); replaying captured live UI POST bodies (fetch-hook over `window.fetch` recording `args[1].body`) through the throwaway proves the validator never fires on real traffic. Pinned `campaigns-music.test.js` "transport field validation".
- **Same-key `/music` write-serialization law (live 2026-11-20 improve music-local-stop-unmount):** any lane that commits FULL transport bytes and a follow-up `/music` PATCH back-to-back MUST await the first POST before dispatching the second — the route merge is `{...base, ...incoming}` last-write-wins and the full transport body carries the not-yet-evicted `overrides`/`customCategories`, so concurrently-dispatched writes land out of order and RESURRECT the evicted maps (6/6 witness replaying the lane's exact bytes at pure API level; the shipped race fix chains stopCommit → evict PATCH deterministically). Bytes alone are an insufficient order witness — pin the ORDER with a slow-response fake recording dispatch/landed tokens (see `MusicPlayer.local-stop-unmount.test.jsx` timeline pin); client enabler: `useSyncedState` `setValueSynced` returns the store POST promise (additive — every other caller ignores it).
- **TV header music transport + cross-window play ownership (shipped 2026-11-08 improve tv-music-controls; faces re-lived MB 2026-11-11, pref law corrected TA 2026-11-16):** `initiative/TvMusicTransport.jsx` mounts in `.combat-header` only while TV ON AND `music.mood` armed (zero chrome when stopped): chip + `[title=Pause]`(playing only) + `[title=Stop]`; LAN read-only visitors get a chip-only `.music-remote-note` face, no buttons. Commits ride the shared `services/ui/musicTransport.js` builders — byte-identical to the sidebar lanes (`Paused X ambience` / `Stopped ambience` rows, Pause keeps mood, Stop nulls it; stop nulls mood AND strips all transport chrome; the visitor note retires live on the owner's stop echo while the visitor's own TV stays ON). A non-owner window NEVER calls youtubePlayer: the COMMITTING window mounts the sole `.yt-music-frame` and every other window's player+iframe tears down (remote stand-down — "no second frame" really means "exactly one, in the committer"); later transitions committed by a stood-down window drive the committer's frame remotely through MusicPlayer's remote-adoption lane (store/SSE echo) with byte-identical rows (GM-TV Pause on a visitor-committed mood → `playing:false` + committer's active-chip highlight retires, frame persists; GM-TV Stop unmounts the remote frame + nulls mood); resume is deliberately absent (a cross-window play commit stands every other tab's player down). The TV transport FACE is origin-gated, not committer-gated: localhost TV windows get chip+Pause+Stop on ANY mood; LAN TV windows get the chip-only `.music-remote-note` even for their own commit. WRITE law BOTH directions: `POST /api/campaigns/:c/music` is CAMPAIGN_LOCK-only (NO GM gate, `campaigns-changedata.js`), the LAN Music view is visitor-writable. TV↔music interplay: TV-ON keeps playback (`.yt-music-frame` stays mounted, `music` key `{mood,playing:true}` persists across the view swap and TV exit) but renders ZERO music affordance on the TV screen (panel `visible=false`, sidebar unmounted) — `button.tv-exit-button` restores controls; a second window without a local frame shows the active mood + `.music-remote-note` "Playing in another window" and never auto-plays; its Pause ADOPTS across windows (server `playing:false`, mood kept) even when the owner tab sits on a non-music view. TV PREF law (corrected in place TA): mounting Initiative does NOT stamp localStorage — a LAN visitor context that mounted Initiative TWICE still reads `localStorage.getItem('initiative-tv') === null` (the mount-time write is not persisted per-context); only a toggle persists `'1'`/`'0'` — never assert the cold-stamp, judge TV state only after an actual toggle (GM tab: toggle ON → `'1'` + sidebar hidden + zoom 1.875; `tv-exit-button` → `'0'` + sidebar back). TV-ON HIDES the sidebar in both contexts — exit via the `Exit TV mode` pin before any view navigation. XX 2026-11-20 re-lived the transport face whole on both origins: the LAN TV visitor note flips `playing ⇄ paused` LIVE off a GM-TV Pause commit and the entire `.tv-music-transport` UNMOUNTS on Stop while the visitor's TV stays ON; GM-TV chip reads `Tavern` / `Tavern (paused)` and its rows are byte-identical to the sidebar's.
- **Native-confirm inventory + headless Admin clears:** Admin "Clear Change Data"/"Clear Campaign Log"/"Full Reset" STILL fire native `window.confirm` (as does the character-wizard discard confirm and Initiative's remove-NPC/+NPC placeholder lanes); NPC/quest/session/note deletes and the Party-Inventory give/move/delete are native confirms too (byte-pinned copy in the Planner + Party-Inventory bullets); Initiative's Clear and the music lane's category-delete + editor "Discard changes?" MIGRATED to the shared `DeleteConfirmModal` role=dialog tier 2026-11-08 (improve confirm-modal-migration; `ClearCombatConfirmModal` wrapper + fail-closed setter bridge in `createCreatureHandlers`); Maps-manager row Delete is ALSO the role=dialog tier, never native (live 2026-11-11 improve hidden-vs-fogged-dim). CLEANEST RIDE + COPY WITNESS (live 2026-11-11 improve visitor-initiative-plain-text): inside `page.evaluate`, temporarily override `window.confirm` to `return true` while recording the prompt copy — rides the Admin native-confirm clears in one call with no MCP abort, and the recorded bytes double as copy witnesses (returning `false` is the zero-risk way to WITNESS native-confirm copy on owner fixtures without any dismiss race; in-run `page.on('dialog')` + `d.dismiss()` also preempts cleanly — post-state probe still the judge); MCP dialog-abort handling lives in the §12 dialogs bullet. HEADLESS CLEANS (cleaner than the UI walk at exit): `POST /api/campaigns/:c/admin/clear-change-data` + `…/admin/clear-log` with `Host: localhost` (GM-guarded, `server/routes/campaigns-admin.js`, no dialogs/overlays; curl form `curl -X POST localhost:80/api/campaigns/:c/admin/clear-change-data` and `/admin/clear-log`) — use the FULL `/admin/clear-*` paths: a bare `POST /:c/clear-log` is swallowed by the `:key` change-data wildcard and 500s `Cannot use 'in' operator…` (SHARPENED live 2026-11-16 improve party-inventory-modal-affordances); closed-tab two-pass clear + GET verifies the `{}`/`[]` baseline; clear-change-data WIPES combatSummary, concentration, and `BattleMasterManeuvers_selection` mid-fight — re-cast/re-pick after. Route-shape notes: `GET /change-data/combatSummary` may 404→HTML while `POST /combatSummary {value:cs}` works; whole-store change-data GET returns the object directly; `/change-data/campaign` 404s to HTML (JSON parse SyntaxError). Related probe law: a synthetic `dispatchEvent(new Event('blur'))` does NOT fire React 19's `onBlur` (delegated via focusout) — a hand-blurred initiative edit silently no-commits; use trusted `locator.fill(...)+press('Enter')` instead. ADMIN-BUTTON LOCATOR FACE (live 2026-11-17 fix run): the Admin view carries a DISABLED decoy `Clear All` button (an enforcement sub-flag reset) — the state clears are `Clear Change Data`/`Clear Campaign Log`; their native confirms self-accept when a `page.on('dialog', accept)` listener is registered before the click in the same call (the MCP "Modal state" report can lag an already-accepted dialog — judge by API GET, per the §12 dialogs bullet).

WIZARD HEADER TITLE LAW (live improve edit-wizard-named-header 2026-11-20): the edit-mode title reads `Edit "<name>"` off the CAPTURED `characterData` (renaming the draft never retitles), create face `Create New Character`; `getByRole('heading', { level: 2 })` is AMBIGUOUS inside a mounted wizard (step content carries its own h2s) — scope title pins to `.wizard-header h2` via container query.
