- **Campaign data is git-IGNORED — md5 is the only byte-proof (live YG 2026-11-16):** `.gitignore:2 public/campaigns/*` covers EVERY runtime file (maps, data, char JSON) — `git status --porcelain public/campaigns/` reads EMPTY no matter what you wrote, so NEVER cite porcelain as map/char innocence; take md5 censuses at run START and EXIT (and record the start values — a pin you didn't capture can't be distinguished from drift; poi-showcase drift 7561e4bf→b5dbad11 at YG was only explainable via the map's recorded pin). Related one-shot RESOLVED (2026-11-16 improve, fitVersion law): opening a stamp-less legacy OUTDOOR map's board used to REWRITE the stored zoom/panX/panY to viewport-derived floats on first mount (mtime bump within ~10 s; viewport-dependent — baselines shifted by whichever window opened first). Now the mount-fit is gated by the doc's `fitVersion` stamp (see the §Hex-board mount law bullet): the stamping pass writes ONCE, and every later mount — any window — is byte-neutral; an exit census that still sees a map mtime bump after a board open means the doc was UNSTAMPED, not that the lane regressed (record the new pin with a narrative; poi-showcase migrated cold-disk to `a0d190d1…`).
- **Popup focus-retention tier (`efc0696ae`, all faces LIVE-YG 2026-11-16):** a dismissable shared `Popup` installs a document `focusin` reclaimer that pulls focus back onto its own panel whenever it lands OUTSIDE every `[data-testid=popup-overlay]` layer — so a card re-key that steals focus to `MonsterNameAutocomplete` no longer makes Escape inert (the exact XF bug face: roll-RESULT popup over an open `.mc-overlay`), while stacked popups never ping-pong (inside-any-layer reads as a pass) and an already-auto-focused child keeps its caret. `dismissable={false}` (staged-damage/HIT face) attaches NOTHING: Escape inert, backdrop inert, focus stays on the trigger input — assert `activeElement` to tell "guarded by design" from "trap failed", never Escape-inert alone. CLOSE-RESTORE TWIN (`4b5fb7e10`, GK explore 2026-11-17 live): the dismissable Popup now also restores the pre-open anchor after EVERY close path — MISS popup over an open `.mc-overlay`: Escape, pure backdrop AND Done all land focus back on the opening `span.mc-dice-link` chip (the FI-era BODY strand is DEAD, stat modal survives); the guarded (staged-damage) face still moves NO focus on open (chip keeps it) and its Cancel-attack close lands on BODY by the documented no-anchor design — keyboard post-close ergonomics there is a backlog note, not a trap failure.
- **Initiative avatar zoom + keyboard tier + scrim laws (`8ebbffb8f` + `963f569d9` + `19f7540fb`; all faces LIVE YG 2026-11-16 + run AB 2026-11-17):** three distinct surfaces. (1) GM/controlled-monster card avatar → stat modal, whose `.mc-header-avatar` (60px, `role=button`, only clickable when an image resolves) opens the shared `AvatarModal` INSIDE `.mc-card` — × hittable, Escape closes zoom only (focus restores to `avatar-wrapper`), `.mc-close` tears down both (no orphan). (2) VISITOR (non-localhost) uncontrolled monster/NPC avatar → `CardAvatar` `AvatarModal`, which now `createPortal`s to `document.body` (FIXED 2026-11-16, was BROKEN as `bug-visitor-avatar-zoom-trapped-in-card-stacking-context`: the card's base `transform: scale(1.0)` had made `.creature-card` the fixed containing block); `AvatarModal` carries a backdrop-dismiss lane via the shared `useModalDismiss` (panel-start drags and body clicks inert; Escape stays on the document listener — no focus trap). (3) Monster with no image on either lane is a silent no-op BY DESIGN. SCRIM LAW (`19f7540fb`): `.avatar-modal-overlay` = fixed inset-0, z-index 1000, `var(--background-color-overlay)` on EVERY consumer — live rects [0,0,viewport]; the card/sheet hosts (`.mc-card`, `.no-print`) are transform-free so they are viewport-anchored WITHOUT a portal (only the visitor CardAvatar path portals); ×/backdrop/Escape/sidebar-region click all close and the drag guard holds. KEYBOARD TIER (`963f569d9`): `NpcAvatar` renders `role=button` `tabIndex=0` + Enter/Space (`preventDefault`, page scroll prevented) whenever `onClick` is wired (both GM and visitor initiative cards); Enter on a GM monster card OPENS THE STAT MODAL (`MonsterCardModal`); Space opens the visitor's portaled `AvatarModal`; accessible name = img `alt` ("Goblin"). FOCUS-RESTORE LAW: every dismissal lane (×, pure backdrop, Escape, sidebar-region click) restores `activeElement` to the `.npc-avatar` div — the ZK-era body stand-down is DEAD on this surface (GM header-avatar/sheet hosts still restore to `avatar-wrapper`). SHEET-STALE-FLAG FACE (FIXED 2026-11-17 fix sheet-imageless-avatar-arms-stale-zoom-modal, was FILED, lived run AB): `CharSummary.jsx` used to pass `onClick` to the sheet `AvatarImage` UNCONDITIONALLY while guarding the modal render with `&& playerStats.imagePath` and never resetting `showAvatarModal` on character switch — Enter on a no-image sheet avatar armed a hidden flag that SELF-OPENED the zoom on the next image-bearing character's sheet (once; Escape consumed it; controls without an activation press stay closed). The fix gates the sheet avatar's button tier behind `playerStats.imagePath` and resets `showAvatarModal` on character change (pinned `CharSummary-AvatarZoomArming.test.jsx`; live: imageless avatar now `role=null tabindex=null`, image-bearing avatar keeps Enter-opens/Escape-restores-focus). AVATAR CONDITIONAL-TIER LAW: `onClick={path ? handler : undefined}` is the app-wide avatar gate — consumers CharSummary (post-fix), `NPCFormModal:151`, `MonsterCardBody:205`, and `NpcAvatar` gates at its own wrapper; audit all four for any future avatar face. Control probe to isolate causes: the DELEGATED visitor path (`Assign who controls <M>` select → stat modal → in-card zoom) is healthy in the SAME tab/build — a visitor-leg failure is a mounting bug, not an image/permission failure. `useMonsterAvatarZoom` precedence is combatSummary-row `imagePath` > host `imageUrl`, with form rows (wildShape/polymorph/animalShapes/shapechange sources) skipping the row image so the form's own image surfaces. CAROUSEL-SETTLE LAW (live 2026-11-16 fix run): the board is a `.carousel-container` — after a programmatic `scrollLeft` change, wait for the scroll animation to settle before a trusted `page.mouse.click`; a rect computed pre-settle silently misses the avatar, and `scrollIntoView` does nothing horizontally — set `scrollLeft` directly. AVATAR-OPEN PROBE LAW (live 2026-11-16 mc-header fix run): opening the stat modal from the card avatar needs a TRUSTED `page.mouse.click` at the avatar rect centre — an `evaluate`-dispatched `el.click()` on `.creature-avatar` silently no-ops (`.mc-overlay` count stays 0). The EB search field is `input.search-input` (placeholder "Search by name, type, or subtype..."), distinct from Initiative's `input.monster-autocomplete-input`. VISITOR-CARD SCOPING: visitor Initiative cards carry NO HP input (`input[aria-label="<Name> current HP"]` is a GM-only affordance) — scope monster cards there via `.creature-card.npc` or `.creature-avatar img` presence. STATLESS-NPC RIG: `+ NPC` spawns a statless "NPC N" card writing ONLY change-data (never npcs.json — md5 `db6666c1` re-proven byte-exact) — the cheap imageless-card producer for visitor no-op legs, swept by the admin clear.
- **Party-Inventory UI + purse laws (live YG/ZK + shipped 2026-11-17 fix player-currency-give-is-one-way-invisible + improve transfer-contribution-audit; clobber eras + AB ride chronology → archive; test-campaign pins `inventory.json 7638da67` / `AasimarTest.json 3bfe7eca` SELF-RESTORE via a full round-trip with the −1 chip as final leg — the AasimarTest cp-restore rig is retired):** the GM currency card is one labelled row per coin (`Copper`/`Silver`/`Gold`/`Platinum` text on a `span`, not a leaf) whose wrapper holds the steppers — walk `label → closest button-bearing ancestor` and match `+1`/`−1` by TRIMMED text (`+1` also matches `+10`/`+100`, so never substring-match); stepper clicks write the PARTY pool through a 400 ms client trailing debounce (`CURRENCY_PERSIST_DEBOUNCE_MS`) that flushes the burst's SUMMED SIGNED per-coin DELTA through the atomic `/inventory/adjust` lane (server clamps each coin at 0 and adds onto ITS ledger; two windows pressing inside the window now BOTH land) + one `inventory_transfer` audit row per accepted batch (`Party currency adjusted: +100 gp, -1 cp`). A burst that NETS TO ZERO inside one debounce window POSTS NOTHING and writes NO audit row (display already true — CE 2026-11-17; never expect a +1/−1 pair of rows). NAME-QUERY LAW SHARPENED (shipped `dc1bf7b29`, aria census lived CE): every stepper/chip now carries intent title+aria pairs (`Add 1 cp`/`Remove 100 gp`, `Increase quantity of <item>`, decrease DISABLED at qty 1) and From-Players `To Party` buttons carry the exact move copy (minus "?") as title+aria — the ancestor-walk/+1-substring recipe above is legacy; the four `Give` chips are the one remaining title-only gap (backlog). POOL-ZERO face: the party pool can sit ALL-ZERO, making every Give chip legitimately `disabled` (`currency[key] <= 0`) — seed with a `+1 cp`/`−1 cp` round trip and ALWAYS GET-verify `/inventory` after the final chip leg (a lone chip click can silently no-op, live 2026-11-17 improve sheet-purse-display: pool stayed cp 1 — press again if the server disagrees); `.pi-currency-card .pi-currency-row:last-of-type` = Copper (CURRENCIES order pp/gp/sp/cp). The row's `Give` chip opens a `.pi-modal-overlay` TRANSFER modal (`Give Currency to Player`, player `<select>` + per-coin amounts — 4× `input[type=number]` in pp/gp/sp/cp order, React 19 needs the native-setter `input` event since `page.fill` can skip onChange, submit is the modal's own `Give` button disabled at 0, native confirm `Give N cp to <Char>?` (pinned live as `Give 1 gp to AasimarTest?`) — an evaluate-dispatched click's confirm auto-answers when a `page.once('dialog', accept)` handler is armed, the harness "modal state" notice is informational) that TRANSFERS OUT of the party pool and edits CHARACTER disk files (both `inventory.gold` and `inventory.currency.gp` when present — back up char files pre-ride); modal rows name live balances (`Gold (party has 10)`), amount inputs clamp [0,balance]. SHARP DWELL PITFALL: that modal stays open across `run_code_unsafe` calls and its overlay silently intercepts every other Inventory click (`<div class="pi-modal-overlay"> … intercepts pointer events`) — press its Cancel (`button` text `Cancel` inside the overlay) before any other Inventory interaction. FROM PLAYERS: one purse row per non-zero coin above the backpack rows (reuses `.pi-player-items` classes, zero new CSS) with a `To Party` button riding the existing atomic `player-to-party` `transferInventory` lane — native confirm `Move N <coin> from <Char> to party inventory?` + directional `inventory_transfer` audit; item rows render `<item>×1 To Party`, press fires NATIVE `Move 1 × <item> from <Char> to party inventory?`, landing an atomic char-backpack debit + party-items credit + one audit row (log persists at `<campaign>/data/campaign-log.json`); the row vanishes live via the SSE `character-*` echo; the debounced pool-chip snapshot is drained first (`beforeTransfer` → `flushCurrencyPersist`) so a pending stepper write can't clobber the credit. CANONICAL CURRENCY RULE: player currency resolves via the server's `characterCurrency()` rule — `inventory.currency` `{pp,gp,sp,cp}` block when present, else positive `inventory.gold` as gp (10/13 test-campaign chars are gold-only; writes keep `gold = gp` synced) — the ONE client mirror lives in `src/services/campaign/characterCurrency.js` (extracted from PartyInventory’s former local `playerCurrency` copy) (imported by BOTH the From Players pane and the sheet's read-only `Purse:` row in `char-sheet/CharInventory.jsx`: `Purse: 10 gp, 1 cp`, one segment per non-zero coin, no row at zero, zero editing affordances, updates live on the SSE `character-*` echo with no reload). LOCATOR PITFALL: `getByRole('button', { name: /To Party/ })` in From-Players tests DOUBLE-matches for any gold-carrying fixture (Gold purse row + item row) — scope item locators via `within(document.querySelector('.pi-players-panel'))` (the party table re-names items too). ITEM UI: `Add Item` → `#pi-item-name`/`#pi-item-qty`/`#pi-item-desc` + `Save` (type, don't `fill`, per the React-19 law); catalog autofill needs a DROPDOWN-SUGGESTION (≤8, case-insensitive substring) or EXACT-normalized pick — typing a plural guess (`Hempen Ropes`) passes through the exact catalog name mid-keystroke so the description autofill fires and sticks (`!description` guard) and clearing a prefilled description re-fires it on the very next change, so a text-bearing catalog row can only be stored blank by editing `inventory.json` on COLD disk; free-typing `Longsword` leaves the description empty and the row renders a BLANK-ish popup `<b>name</b>` because equipment.json's Longsword row has no `description` key at all; the popup's plural trial only runs when `endsWith('s')`, so a catalog name like `Acid (vials)` never matches the trial — pick plural probes whose last char is the `s`. Item rows are `span.clickable.pi-item-name[role=button]` (ENTER key also opens — `td.pi-item-name` matches nothing); the details popup is the shared `Popup` (focus lands `DIV.popup-modal`, Escape / any click-in-overlay / backdrop all dismiss — the `click to dismiss` hint only renders when the face carries reroll chrome); an un-escaped shared `Popup` survives across `run_code_unsafe` calls and intercepts row clicks for the full default timeout — press Escape / verify detached BEFORE the next row click. Row Actions are `Decrease quantity`/`Increase quantity`/`Edit item`/`Move`/`Delete item` title-labelled icons; delete fires NATIVE `Delete "<item>" from party inventory?` + ONE `inventory_transfer` log row. Visitors see the read face (no stepper chips, no Actions column, `No shared items yet.` copy). BACKGROUND-INERT LAW (shipped improve party-inventory-modal-affordances): there is NO app-wide background-inert pattern — the minimum-viable treatment is the `inert` attribute on background sections + one component-scoped `.pi-container [inert] { opacity: 0.4 }` veil (browsers paint nothing for inert, so the veil is what makes it READ as disabled); all `.pi-modal-overlay` modals ride `useModalDismiss` (Escape/pure-backdrop → Cancel lane, drag-off guard, `disabled` mid-save).
- **Party-Inventory server lanes + authority matrix (clobber FIXED + ITEM face CLOSED 2026-11-17 fix runs, were FILED `bug-inventory-chip-cross-tab-full-doc-clobber` + `bug-inventory-item-save-concurrent-snapshot-last-writer-wins`; the whole-doc-snapshot era, its live race outcomes and the race-forcing rigs → archive):** the whole-doc `POST /api/campaigns/:c/inventory` lane is RETIRED at the router (no alias — pinned by `inventory.item-intent.test.js`) BUT the composition face is NOT 404 (CORRECTED IN PLACE by CD explore 2026-11-17, filed `bug-retired-inventory-post-shadowed-by-change-data-wildcard.md`): with the full `server.js` stack mounted, the retired path falls through to the change-data wildcard `POST /:campaign/:key` and answers **200**, storing the raw snapshot verbatim as a phantom top-level `inventory` change-data key (GET /change-data + disk proof; broadcast `change-<c>-inventory`; the wildcard's own DELETE is the only eviction) — the router test mounts in isolation so it never sees the shadow; the old `/deposit` lane is gone (no alias — canonical name `/adjust`). Coin movement is additive on two atomic lanes: `/inventory/adjust` (GM-only; signed whole-number per-coin deltas, server re-reads the ledger and clamps each coin at 0; the burst-merged chip flush AND the loot Award `adjustCurrency` ride it; each accepted batch writes `Party currency adjusted: ±N coin…`) and `/inventory/transfer` (party↔player; since the 2026-11-17 improve transfer-contribution-audit every ACCEPTED POST appends exactly ONE server-side `inventory_transfer` row via the `log.js` `addEntry` lane — the client's addEntry writes for all four /transfer lanes were REMOVED, so exactly one row per transfer either origin; message copy `<movement> moved from <A> to <B>` with `contributedBy` = the optional client-asserted actor string, whitespace-collapsed + 80-char capped, never identity, falling back to the debited/credited character name; an all-zero/negative `currency` body 400s `currency must contain a positive whole-number amount`, aligning with /adjust's zero-delta refusal — pinned by `inventory.transfer-audit.test.js`; a SHORT player→party debit 409s `<Char> has only N gp`). SSE `inventory-<c>` publishes the merged doc. Every ITEM write is ONE atomic intent on GM-only `POST /inventory/item` — `add {item:{id?,name,quantity,description}}` (client-minted id KEPT server-side → retried adds are idempotent and follow-up intents resolve the temp id before ack; server generates a guid only if absent), `update {item:{id,…}}`, `setQty {id,quantity,name?}`, `delete {id,name?}`; the server re-reads its ledger, mutates only the named row, re-sorts, `writeFileSync`s and `publish(inventory-<c>)` — currency structurally untouchable; a stale `update/setQty/delete` whose row vanished elsewhere 409s with the SERVER family copy `Party item "X" was deleted in another tab or session since you loaded it — this response body carries the current merged document — it was NOT saved.` (display-name-labelled, never raw id; fallback to id when name blank; CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-17 — the old "reload the list and re-apply your edit" clause was DEAD ADVICE once `8ce25f1dc` made the client auto-adopt the doc, dropped for API-consumer honesty) plus the merged doc, which the client ADOPTS (no ghost row, no resurrection); SINCE `8ce25f1dc` (fix-verified live EG 2026-11-17, BOTH legs) the GM client does NOT quote that server sentence on a doc-carrying 409 — dedicated alert copy: setQty/update leg `Party item "X" was deleted elsewhere — this window now shows the current list; your edit was NOT saved.`, delete leg `Party item "X" was already deleted elsewhere — this window now shows the current list.`; the `Save failed:`/`Delete failed:` prefixes ride ONLY non-409 (or no-doc) failures; no intent can re-add rows it doesn't name. Client: serialized FIFO intent queue in PartyInventory (one in-flight POST, rev-guarded acks, delete audit row still post-ack, rejection rollback + 409-adoption); add with a name matching a stored row posts `update` on that row (historic modal merge UX kept); a modal Save whose edit-id row already vanished locally posts `update` → loud 409 instead of silent append. AUTHORITY MATRIX (`server/routes/inventory.js`, re-lived curl + route code, re-stamped by the item-intent fix): GET open (200 LAN, wraps `{inventory:{currency,items}}`); POST /inventory 200 WILDCARD SHADOW (NOT 404 in the composed server — see correction above; the router-level 404 pin holds only in isolation); POST /inventory/item 403 GM-only; POST /adjust 403 GM-only; POST /transfer party→player 403; POST /transfer player→party OPEN to every client (audited exception: the server debits the named char atomically, 409 short, NO session↔character binding — any LAN tab may drain ANY character's purse/items; traceable since 2026-11-17 via the `contributedBy` audit row). PITFALLS: the +/− currency chips are `disabled` while ANY item intent is in flight (`saving`) — a scripted chip click fired immediately after a stepper is silently swallowed by the DOM gate (zero POST, zero row — honest, by design; never misread as loss); a direct curl write on the /transfer lane DOES log server-side (the old "currency-only writes log NOTHING by design" asymmetry is dead — CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-17 fix run) while curl /adjust and curl /inventory/item writes still log nothing, those rows living in the UI lanes; probe copy-capture — override `window.confirm/alert` inside `page.evaluate` to record byte-exact dialogs in the same call; on a :8123 throwaway the cross-tab echo is rig-invisible (ack + reload prove convergence; the publish itself is curl-provable on `:8123/subscribe?campaign=`).
- **CD harness laws for the Inventory + multi-tab lanes (live explore CD 2026-11-17):** (1) DIALOG-HANDLER RESIDUE DANGER — a `page.waitForEvent('dialog').then(d => d.accept())` or `page.once('dialog', accept)` registered inside a `run_code_unsafe` call that MCP later ABORTS (native confirm pauses the call; the harness re-surfaces it as "Modal state") STAYS ARMED on the page object and silently auto-ACCEPTS the NEXT dialog (witnessed: an unauthorized `Move 10 gp…` transfer landed and audited while I was aiming a Copper-row press); safe pattern = plain click, let MCP return the pending "Modal state", then a separate `browser_handle_dialog({accept})` call, and VERIFY the message text you accepted — the tool errors "no related modal state" if your inline handler already consumed it (then judge by server GET, never assume refusal). (2) STALE-VIEW RIG: `page.route('**/subscribe**', () => {})` BEFORE `goto` yields a permanently stale tab (never receives SSE) — perfect for stale-write/409 faces; close the page when done (EG 2026-11-17 variant that works cleanly per-tab: build the stale tab as its OWN `browser.newContext()` + `p.route('**/subscribe*', () => {})` — a bare handler hangs the request; find those pages in LATER `run_code_unsafe` calls only via `browser.contexts()` enumeration, `page.context().pages()` cannot see them). (3) After the extra tabs close, `run_code_unsafe` can throw `Failed to parse URL from /api/...` (no current page to anchor relative fetches) — `browser_navigate` first or switch to `browser_evaluate`. (4) Sidebar view buttons are `button.sidebar-section-header` with a LEADING-SPACE textContent (`" Inventory"`) — `getByRole(name exact)` and `hasText:/^X$/` both miss; loop and match `textContent.trim()`. (5) A second default-context tab may STALL at Select a Campaign (CD re-proof) — always verify the header and click `.campaign-button` explicitly; the "auto-enters" claim is downgraded. (6) An evaluate crash AFTER a locator click still LANDED the click — after any mid-call error, judge by server state before retrying (stepper counted twice for one visible error).
- **Road entry shape (live DG 2026-11-13):** a whole-doc PUT whose `roads` rows lack the key 400s plain-English `Map save rejected: road entry 0 must carry "hexes" — a list of "q,r" hex-key strings like "7,2".` — rig roads as `{id, hexes:["12,5","12,6","12,7"], fromPoiId, toPoiId}` (the POI-delete cascade filters `fromPoiId`/`toPoiId`, so include both for the cascade face). Outdoor POIs ride the same PUT: `{id,q,r,label,type,visible,linkedMap?}` (truth field is `label`).
- **Map whole-doc `PUT /api/campaigns/:c/maps/:slug` takes the map object UNWRAPPED with a top-level `players` array — the `{map:{…}}` wrapper is rejected (live-proved 2026-11-05 aoe-overlay-lane rig). PUT IS REPLACE, NOT MERGE (live-proved the hard way, WY descent 2026-11-12): a PARTIAL body silently FLATTENS the stored doc to your keys (only server-managed `name`/`displayName` survive) — an outdoor doc flattened to `{travelState,pois}` lost `type`, the LIST builder then classifies `type||'indoor'` and the INDOOR editor mounts the outdoor doc (SVG NaN spam on `{q,r}`-shape POIs; no app crash — garbage-in honest); ALWAYS GET the full doc, merge your fields, PUT the WHOLE doc back. PLAYERS GUARD: once the stored doc carries `players` (even `[]`), a PUT whose body omits `players` 400s plain-English `Map save rejected: … must include the "players" field — without it every player token would be erased from the map.` (a doc without the key passes — that asymmetry is why an early flatten PUT 200'd and its redo 400'd). MERGED 2026-11-21 (improve erasure-guard-all-fields): players is now a member of the indoor guarded set inside `wholeMapErasureError` — ONE refusal lists EVERY missing field together (`must include the "players" and "walls" fields — without them every player token…; every wall…`) instead of the old short-circuit branch teaching `players` one round-trip ahead; players keeps ARRAY-SHAPE strictness (unlike the general fields, `players: null` also refuses — explicit null is NOT the sanctioned clear for tokens). HONEST SAVE ECHO (shipped 2026-11-21 improve map-save-clamp-echo, LLL-lived): the whole-doc PUT 200 body is now `{message, gridSize: <STORED post-clamp>, clamped: <bool>}` — PUT 110 answers `gridSize:100, clamped:true`, 2→5, passthrough 37 answers `clamped:false`, string '80' stores numeric 80 with `clamped:false` (the flag polices NUMBERS only), explicit null echoes `gridSize:null clamped:false` (and GET witnesses null — sanctioned clear); the POST /maps CREATE lane echoes the SAME law (shipped 2026-11-21 improve map-create-clamp-echo): 201 body is `{message:'Map created successfully', gridSize:<stored>, clamped, map:{name,fileName,slug}}` — create always canonicalizes (absent gridSize stores+echoes the default 20 with `clamped:false`, unlike PUT's never-invent omission), explicit null stores+echoes null on BOTH builders, outdoor party pin centres on the CLAMPED grid. NOTE: the `players`-only short-circuit refusal COPY survives verbatim when players is the sole offender. GENERALIZED (shipped 2026-11-12 improve map-put-erasure-guard, `wholeMapErasureError` in `server/routes/maps.js`): the same omission-vs-null asymmetry now covers `type`+`gridSize` on both types plus structural fields per type (outdoor `terrain`/`pois`, indoor `walls`/`placedItems`) — a partial flatten PUT now 400s plain-English per-field copy and writes ZERO (live matrix XA 2026-11-13: each of the five omissions 400s with its own verbatim per-field sentence, omitting the UNGUARDED indoor `paintCells` legitimately 200s and drops the key); fields builders legitimately omit (outdoor roads/marchingOrder/weather/travelState, indoor items/paintCells/fog, view zoom/pan) stay unguarded, and create-via-PUT (no stored doc) is unpoliced. CLIENT-CONTRACT VICTIM + FIX (live 2026-11-13 XA, fixed 2026-11-14 commit 43e94bdad): `seededCampaign.js persistDungeonMap()` used to PUT the raw `generateDungeon()`/`carveArena()` output with NO `type` key → the generalized guard 400'd New Arena/New-Starter quick-create mid-seed (folder + empty shell map orphaned, raw copy inline in the modal) and Admin Regenerate-adventure too; the seed client now PUTs `{ ...dungeon, type: 'indoor' }` and `seededCampaign.erasure-guard.test.js` pins the seed PUT body against `wholeMapErasureError` IMPORTED CROSS-LANE — any future required-key addition that breaks the seed client fails that test (still check the generate-modal create bodies by hand). Arena/starter/regenerate all re-lived clean post-fix (thrown-away qa-* names are the sanctioned repro lane under a `test-campaign,qa-arena,qa-starter` lock; API-delete them at exit). AND UNMOUNT mounted boards before curl doc surgery: a board open across your mutation wins the last-write race with its own debounced settle/autosave flush (same family as the sandbox-switch law). MAP-DATA SSE ECHO IS PER-FIELD MERGE (live 2026-11-11 improve hexmap3d-census-weather):** `useHexMapSSESync.js` only sets fields where `data[field] !== undefined` — a PUT that OMITS a key (e.g. to "remove" weather) leaves a mounted board's live state untouched; removal must be expressed as an explicit `null` value in the doc. Applies to every echoed map field (terrain/roads/weather/…). OUTDOOR CREATE STAMPS CENTRE partyPosition (CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-11 improve outdoor-create-partyposition): `POST /maps {name,type:'outdoor',gridSize:N}` stores `partyPosition {q:floor(N×GRID_COLS_MULTIPLIER/2), r:floor(N/2)}` (gridSize 20 → {q:20,r:10}) at writeFileSync-create — the create-then-PUT-partyPosition dance is RETIRED (GET-verify needs no wait); explicit `body.partyPosition` wins verbatim; indoor docs never carry the key (body-carried is dropped); `marchingOrder` stays trip-lane/client — still needs the trip write. Client `useMapLoader.js` already ADOPTS a stored partyPosition (editor-open never clobbers the stamp).
- **Indoor-map dock/probe laws (shipped 2026-11-13 improve item-details-dock-dialog-role):** the `item-details-dock` is OUT of the dialog tier — it renders `role="complementary"` + aria-label `Selected item: <label>`; while it is open with no confirm, `[role="dialog"]` matches ZERO in the editor DOM, and the shared `DeleteFromMapModal` (`.modal-shell`, `aria-modal=true`) is the EXACTLY-ONE match even dock-behind-modal. Probe law: a synthetic `dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('click'))` on `.item-hit-area` silently NO-OPS for select — a trusted `page.mouse.down()/up()` at the rect centre (or real press+click) is required; the dock's own aria-labelled buttons DO respond to `el.click()` via evaluate. MCP pitfall: `getByRole(...,{name})` with embedded quotes intermittently times out resolving an existing element — match the aria-label inside `page.evaluate` using `String.fromCharCode(34)` and click there. Between separate `run_code_unsafe` CALLS the SPA view can drift (board unmounts/partial-renders) — bundle a full ride into ONE call. **Indoor-map notice seam (shipped 2026-11-13 improve map-token-eviction-toast):** the shared `.map-toast` ephemeral channel in `Map.jsx` (`useEphemeralToast` → `MapNoticeToast`) carries BOTH the rejected-load and the token-eviction notices, copy built in `src/services/maps/mapLoadNotice.js` (`buildMapLoadRejectedNotice`/`buildTokenEvictionNotice`). Mount reconcile that evicts roster-less doc `players` toasts `Removed N token(s) with no matching character` + a `console.info` listing the names — future map-load feedback rides this seam; eviction probes read `.map-toast` textContent.
- **Indoor fog/door probe laws (live XC 2026-11-16):** fog rects carry `data-cell="x,y"` but BARE `[data-cell]` also matches the grid cells — scope `.fog-cell[data-cell="x,y"]` (GM veil) / `.fog-cell-player` (visitor veil) or every cell reads "fogged". `[class*=door]` substring-matches `indoor-poi-layer` (inDOOR); anchor a CLOSED door by `image[href*=door.png]` and walk to `g.placed-item` → `rect.placed-hit-area` — but OPEN doors honestly render NO sprite (two `#8B5A2B` frame rects, `PlacedItems.renderDoor`), so a doorVisible=false read under an open door is correct, not a leak. Trusted `page.mouse` right-click at the hit-rect centre used to open no menu — FIXED 2026-11-16 (improve item-context-menu-a11y): the root cause was `useSelectMove`/`useWallDrawing` calling `svg.setPointerCapture()` on EVERY button, and an active capture retargets the trusted `contextmenu` to the svg root, away from the child hit-rect handler; with the button-0 guards a trusted right-click opens ItemContextMenu under Default, Select AND Paint tools (synthetic `dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('contextmenu',{bubbles:true}))` on the rect still works). Entries are now `g[role=menuitem][tabindex=0]` wrappers (shared `src/components/common/MenuOption.jsx` seam, same one the hex POI menu uses) around the `text.menu-option` labels: dispatch-click still answers ONLY on the `text` leaf (wrapper-`g` dispatch no-ops — the :104 svg-root pointer-capture family), while Enter/Space on a focused wrapper fires the same action; opening focuses the first entry, Arrow/Home/End rove, Escape routes to the same `onClose` seam as the ✕ with zero `PUT /maps` writes. RIG FACE: the indoor visibility toggle keeps the menu OPEN (label flips Hide↔Show) — unlike the hex POI lane that closes — so a rig waiting for menu-absence after activating Hide false-times. Fog arithmetic: veil = gridSize² − |revealed ∪ visible|; `computeClosedDoors` = `type==='door' && !open` cells blocking LOS as bresenham INTERIORS (endpoints never block, so every wall cell stays seeable and the door cell itself is visible while closed); closing a door after reveal does NOT re-veil (memory law), `Reset Fog` (toolbar aria-label, also resetView) := revealed shrinks to current LOS and persists via the debounced doc PUT. Visitor (LAN-origin tab) mounted with the veil count matching GM exactly and issued ZERO map-doc writes over a 16 s dwell — and a GM fog-reset while a stale visitor tab is mounted stays reset (resurrection guard `Map.jsx:372` + server gate hold).
- Map create honors `displayName` (shipped 2026-11-09 improve map-create-displayname): `POST /api/campaigns/:c/maps` with non-blank string `body.displayName` stores it verbatim (trimmed) on indoor AND outdoor docs — the create-then-PUT displayName dance is retired; blank/absent/non-string falls back to `name`. Maps-manager create form: the collapsed toggle and expanded submit share the label `Create Map` — a `^Create$` matcher silently no-ops; take the LAST `Create Map` button in DOM order. Map files are `writeFileSync` at create (no 10s debounce) — disk GET-verify is immediate. Maps-manager row Delete is the role=dialog tier (`Delete Map` dialog; confirm button is `Yes, Delete Permanently` — an exact `=== "Yes, Delete"` matcher false-negatives, use `startsWith("Yes, Delete")`; corrected in place 2026-11-11 improve active-map-split-brain-activate) — NOT a native confirm; Admin clears still are (live 2026-11-11 improve hidden-vs-fogged-dim). Row Delete aria-labels use the displayName for authored rows and the file slug for slug-named rows (`Delete "Travel Encounter Stage"` vs `Delete "qa-split-staging"`) — probe by the row's visible label. PITFALL: map create CLAMPS `gridSize` ≥5 (requested 4, stored 5) — size census expectations accordingly. CREATE-BUILDER WHITELIST LAW (live 2026-11-12 improve gen-modal-seed-validation): the POST `/maps` builders `buildIndoorMapData`/`buildOutdoorMapData` in `server/routes/maps.js` are field WHITELISTS — a new per-map authoring field carried in the create body evaporates before `writeFileSync` unless it is added to the builder (unlike the whole-doc PUT); outdoor docs previously could never persist a `seed` (the field was dropped) — now passed through mirroring indoor. GENERATE-MODAL SEED LAW (shipped 2026-11-12 improve gen-modal-seed-validation): both twin modals validate the seed field through shared `src/services/maps/seedInput.js parseSeedInput` BEFORE generating — non-whole-number input blocks with inline `Seed must be a whole number` (zero POST, modal stays open; the Terrain `parseInt||undefined` silent-random is RETIRED), `"0"` is honoured end-to-end, empty stays random, and the real effective seed lands in the map doc (Terrain now routes `generateHexTerrain`'s new `seed` return into the createMap payload; `dungeonGenerator` normalizes non-integer seeds to random instead of mulberry32(NaN)≡seed-0 + `seed:null` on disk). ALL LIVE-VERIFIED end-to-end (TE descent 2026-11-12, 16 sandbox generations, disk-hashed): empty-seed effective ranges DIFFER per modal — dungeon client-random ≤2^31 (saw 448296883), terrain generator-internal ≤100000 (saw 1836 — small terrain seeds are honest, not truncation); same seed+grid ⇒ walls/placedItems/terrain/fog BYTE-identical (normalize away name/displayName/createdAt before hashing). GENERATE-MODAL GRID LAW (corrected in place 2026-11-12 improve terrain-grid-clamp-block): BOTH twins now BLOCK an out-of-range/non-integer grid through shared `src/services/maps/gridInput.js parseGridInput(raw, min, max)` BEFORE generating — persistent inline `.maps-manager-error` naming the modal's REAL bounds (`Grid size must be between 7 and 100` dungeon / `…between 30 and 100` terrain), modal stays open, zero POSTs; a CLEARED number field reads `Number('')===0` → below-min gate (React-19 law). The Terrain silent `Math.max(30,Math.min(100,…))` clamp is RETIRED (pre-fix face kept for history: typed 20 → hint promised 800 hexes, map landed 30-grid/1800-hex, modal closed silently). Editing name/grid/seed now `setError(null)`s the banner on both twins — a stale error never lingers next to now-valid inputs. ROOM-ADJACENT CONTROLS (corrected in place 2026-11-12: originally FILED as bug-adjacent-room-size-corridor-controls-inert after TE's byte-identical probe — FIXED in `83037fc91`, the generator now consumes `opts.minRoom/maxRoom/corridorLength` and same-seed generations differing only in Room Size or Corridor Length produce distinct maps, +6-test regression pin; LIVE-RE-RIDDEN by the UF descent (all pairs differ, walls monotone cramped>std>spc, doors live only at Compact — Moderate/Sprawling carve halls, 0 door items; grid-7 + Spacious + Sprawling generates clean)). ENGINE-DETERMINISM LAW (UF descent 2026-11-12; corrected in place 2026-11-12 fix run): bug-adjacent-generator-random-sort-irreproducible is FIXED — the three `sort(() => rng()-0.5)` sites in `adjacentDungeonGenerator.js` (comparator-call counts drove RNG consumption, so the same opts gave node 233 / Chromium-cold 237 / Chromium-warm 213 walls with a mid-session V8-tier-up flip) now use `shuffle(arr, rng)` seeded Fisher-Yates added to `src/services/maps/rng.js` (exactly `length-1` draws per call, engine-independent); post-fix anchor seed 42 grid 20 Standard/Compact = 199 walls/42 items in node AND Chromium (12-call in-page ladder stable, fresh UI pair byte-identical), old seed outputs intentionally rebaked; INDEPENDENTLY RE-PROVEN BY VG DESCENT 2026-11-12 (normalized-SHA identity node==in-page `d4cd577f35031f1d`; vg-pair-a/b created in two separate page sessions → disk docs byte-identical 5267 normalized chars; UF's "walls monotone cramped>std>spc" reading RETIRED post-rebake — seed 42/standard = cramped 247/std 199/spacious 212, pairs still ALL differ and items 53/42/28 + doors 7/6/3 stay monotone; layoutStyle is consumed — balanced 199/linear 242/forking 227/winding 243). RANDOM-SORT HUNT uses grep `rng() - 0.5` (BOTH forms) — the arrow-only grep `sort(() => rng` MISSED `furniturePlacer.js` L102/238/511 (BSP lane, `sort(function(){return rng()-0.5})`): that BSP cross-engine divergence (seed 42 grid 20: walls agreed, items node 32 vs Chromium 28) is ALSO FIXED (2026-11-12 fix run, same shuffle pattern) — post-fix anchors 256 walls/34 items node == Chromium byte-identical, old BSP seed outputs intentionally rebaked; ZERO live random-comparator sites remain in `src/` (grep `rng() - 0.5` BOTH forms) and each generator lane carries a seeded-shuffle spy/stability test as the structural lock. Pin-rebake gotcha (BSP fix run): on any stream, entrance stairs can land on a door cell and vanish via the pre-existing last-wins dedupe — generator tests must flood from a deterministic row-major open cell, never assume stairs exist. Terrain stays random-sort-free — the byte-identical same-seed law stands for it. LAW: a same-seed reproducibility probe is engine-honest ONLY when it compares node vs browser — same-page pairs pass straight through engine-dependent consumption (that is how the BSP face hid). In-page module-cache bust: `import('/src/.../gen.js?v=N')`; durable structural lock against reintroducing random comparators: the two-probe `Array.prototype.sort` comparator-equality spy test in `adjacentDungeonGenerator.seeded-shuffle.test.js`. Grid hint `gridSize×FEET_PER_CELL ft/side, N² squares` correct in BOTH dungeon branches (`6bb468839`); adjacent grid-7 minimum generates clean (`3d81a8e6e`).
- **Maps-manager ROW-INLINE rename law (live 2026-11-12 RS; ACCESSIBLE NAME ADDED 2026-11-17 improve):** the open rename `<input>` carries `aria-label="New name for \"<displayName>\""` (mirrors the row-control idiom; label keys off the CURRENT name, stable while typing) — `getByRole('textbox', { name: 'New name for "x"' })` resolves first try and `fill` + evaluate-`focus()` + trusted Enter commits race-free (the old `input[value=…]` attr-locator `.press()` race is moot for discovery). The row `Rename "<name>"` button swaps the name badge for an inline input that AUTOSAVES ON BLUR (`onBlur→handleRenameSave`) — click-away commits; Enter commits, Escape cancels, empty/whitespace on blur cancels SILENTLY (zero write, zero error); same-name commit cancels silently. Client gates: kebab-junk inline error `Map name must contain letters or numbers`, duplicate `A map with that name already exists` (UNIFIED 2026-11-12 improve maps-dup-name-copy-drift: the server create/rename 400s now return the byte-identical `A map with that name already exists` — one canonical copy client+server, pinned in `server/routes/maps*.test.js` + the client mocks); a successful rename CLEARS a stale error banner. Zero-risk server-lane dup proof: curl `PUT …/maps/<slug>/rename` with an existing map's display name → 400 canonical body, zero disk write, no cleanup. Cancel-clears-banner law (shipped 2026-11-12 improve maps-rename-cancel-error-clear): `handleStartRename`, the Escape branch and the empty/whitespace-blur branch each `setError(null)` — a cancel never leaves a stale banner; probe caveat: the dup/kebab gate branches CLOSE the input as they set the banner, so live-sequentially a banner is only seen alongside an OPEN rename input via async-lane errors (list-reload failure) — unit-pinning the Escape/blur cancel legs in isolation needs a deferred-promise race rig (pending activate + rejecting reload), else the start-rename clear masks them. A committed rename flips the FILE slug on disk immediately (create/rename are writeFileSync — GET `<new-slug>` 200 with `name`=kebab + `displayName`=trimmed input, old slug ghosts 404; response `{map:{name,fileName,slug}}`). Create button is `disabled={!createName.trim()}` — the empty-name face is button-gated, never reaches the server. Admin typed-name `Delete Campaign` WHILE the campaign is selected lands the tab cleanly on the selection screen with the `✅ <name> deleted successfully.` toast (auto-deselect, no limbo, no wizard artifact). Selector law (live XC 2026-11-16, GATE RE-LIVED XD 2026-11-16): dialog input `#delete-campaign-title-typed-input`, confirm `.delete-confirm-btn` labeled `Delete "<name>"` (always the TRUE name); disabled until the typed value matches TRIMMED + CASE-INSENSITIVE (raw-`===` case-sensitive law RETIRED by `8bafa42af` improve admin-delete-case-insensitive — XC's era claim): live matrix `wrong-name`→disabled, `QA-ARENA`→enabled, `  qa-arena  `→enabled, whitespace-only/empty→disabled; a caps-confirmed delete DELETEs the true name (folder gone, NO variant folder materialized, ghost 404 for qa-arena/QA-ARENA/Qa-Arena). Probe the ID — a `[role=dialog] input`-scoped enabled-read fired a false positive once, the isolated ID-scoped leg read correctly (wrong-name → disabled TRUE).
- **Generate-modal opts-stamp law (shipped 2026-11-12 improve generator-opts-persistence):** the create body carries `generationOpts` and both create builders pass it through VERBATIM like seed (undefined drops, old docs honestly lack it). Stamped effective opts — adjacent: mode/roomSize/gridSize/density/seed/roomCount/corridorLength/layoutStyle/derived minRoom/maxRoom; bsp: mode/gridSize/density/seed; terrain: mode/gridSize/seed — a blank seed field stamps the generator's ACTUAL random draw. KNOB FACE LAW: Room-Adjacent mode has NO density slider (effective density is always the 0.5 state default) — a non-default-density face must be taken on BSP or Terrain. LIVE END-TO-END by VG DESCENT 2026-11-12 (6 UI-created docs disk-hashed): adjacent stamps all 10 keys incl. derived minRoom/maxRoom, bsp 4, terrain 3; doc top-level `seed` mirrors `generationOpts.seed`; blank-seed faces stamp the actual draw (terrain 21860 ≤100k, dungeon 1810107569 ≤2^31); legacy docs (battle-arena/test-map) honestly lack the key. Cleanup-probe laws (same run): a maps-list GET immediately after bulk DELETEs can briefly serve stale rows — the per-slug ghost 404 is the authoritative delete proof; quoted `aria-label` values (`Open "Battle Arena"`) are unusable as CSS attribute selectors through the MCP runner — use `getByRole('button', { name: 'Open "…"' })`.
- **3D scene census handles (shipped 2026-11-10 improve map3d-scene-census-handle):** dev-build-only `globalThis.__map3d.census` / `globalThis.__hexmap3d.census` — mounted-only, detached on unmount; getter traverses the LIVE scene graph (`Map3DScene`/`Hex3DScene.getCensus()`), so patch-lane writes show through WITHOUT remount (overlay `add` POST → `overlays:1`, `clear` → `0`; whole-doc `weather` PUT → `weather:"rain"`). Indoor census: gridSize/walls/floor/grid/players/npcs/monsters/avatars/textured/fallback/torchLights/fogCells/overlays + per-type `items{}` (unregistered prop type → the `fallback` bucket); hex census: hexes/ground/terrain/trees/peaks/rivers/riverHexes/roads/per-type `pois{}`/party/travelPath/weather + `travelPathSegments`/`travelPathTravelled` (added 2026-11-11 improve hex3d-census-travel-counts: patch-lane stamp on `pathGroup.userData.travelPath`, no remount, echo-proved; N+1 axial pts → N segments, travelled = `travel-pathIndex` clamped to [0,segments]; arrival/cancel face is 0/0 PRESENT, never absent — harnesses can branch on travelled===segments for arrival; a path failing the <2-pts guard leaves no stamp → 0/0 beside travelPath:0). This RETIRES screenshot-diff and `.glb` network-URL-grep mesh proofs. Prod bundles are grep-zero for the handles (`import.meta.env.DEV` guard, pinned by `vi.stubEnv('DEV', false)` tests). AVATAR DISCS (sharpened 2026-11-11 improve map3d-census-avatars): discs are token CHILDREN added async when the texture resolves, but they now carry `userData.census={kind:'avatar'}` and `getCensus()` recurses the whole graph — `avatars` (disc count) and `textured` (discs whose live `material.map.image` resolves) answer art-presence directly, no capture needed. The prototype capture is now owed ONLY for the URL values: in-page `const S=(await import('/src/components/map/Map3D/map3dScene.js')).Map3DScene; const ob=S.prototype.buildMap; S.prototype.buildMap=function(d){globalThis.__scene=this;return ob.call(this,d)};` then remount (back→maps→Open→3D) and traverse `__scene.mapGroup.children` reading `children[].material.map.image.src`. Creature art law (corrected in place 2026-11-11 improve map3d-inline-imageurl): 2D AND 3D render alike as `npcImages[item.name] || item.imageUrl` — the name-keyed bestiary lane wins, inline `imageUrl` is the off-catalog fallback, neither → plain disc. `useNpcImageCache.js` filters `type==='npc'`, so `type:'monster'` tokens reach name-keyed art only when a same-named npc-type token hydrates the map; otherwise monsters carry art exclusively via inline `imageUrl` (the IE-era "both types get bestiary art" reading was imprecise). 2D probe: `getAttribute('xlink:href')` — `href` returns null there. **PAREIDOLIA LAW: never adjudicate 3D art/camera motion from mid-zoom screenshots — zoomed 512×128 LABEL sprites read as "portraits" (IE burned 3 false leads); camera law is LEFT=pan, RIGHT=orbit, WHEEL=dolly (OrbitControls mouseButtons map).** Hex: `travelPath` renders from the campaign change-data key `travel-path` (`useTravelManagement.js:135-137` — NOT planning-client-only as the FZ-era note claimed): seed `POST /:c/travel-path {value:[{q,r},{q,r},…]}` (bare array, ≥2 axial pts) then a fresh campaign-select adopts it and `__hexmap3d.census.travelPath` = 1 (tube+pin live without any console walk).

- Full-suite runs: `npx vitest run` is ~2 min on this box — foreground caps (≤120s) WILL time it out; run `> /tmp/<name>.log 2>&1` with `background: true` + `echo EXIT:$?` trailer, then read the `Test Files`/`Tests`/`EXIT` lines once. PITFALL (2026-11-10 fix run): `--reporter=basic` no longer exists in vitest 4.1.11 — it dies at startup with a `loadCustomReporterModule` error and exit 1 with NO test summary; use the default reporter. NEVER conclude green from a silent tail — grep the summary lines + EXIT. PITFALL (2026-11-11 e2e-improve exit): a background full-suite run can be SIGKILLed (exit 137, `(no output)`, log only holds the `RUN` header + a few jsdom `Window's confirm() not implemented` lines and NO `Test Files`/`EXIT`) while the box is contended (dev pair + concurrent probes) — this is a resource kill, NOT a test failure; memory_pressure showed 25% free. Re-run ONCE cleanly (`npm run test:run`, bounded) — the confirmed-green run is authoritative; judge ONLY by the `Test Files`/`Tests`/`EXIT` lines, never by a 137 with no summary (it says nothing about the suite). CONTENTION-TIMEOUT FACE (live 2026-11-13 improve victory-banner-zero-attack-clear): a full suite run while the dev pair + other probes contend can spuriously fail the heavy `adjacentDungeonGenerator` sweep tests at the 20 s per-test cap (g100 leg took 7.7 s uncontented — under load it crosses the cap); before believing any full-suite failure, re-run ONLY that file isolated — a clean isolated pass means environmental contention, not a regression. **Full vitest suite ~165 s (live 2026-11-14):** exceeds the 120 s foreground shell cap — background `npm run test:run` and poll the log bounded; foreground runs die at the tool cap. (b) The FULL vitest suite exceeds a 120s foreground bound (~147s cold) — run `npm run test:run` with `background: true` and output redirected, probe the file in bounded follow-ups. Probe pitfall: `npx vitest run --reporter=basic` does not exist in vitest 4.1.11 (`ERR_LOAD_URL` at load) — use the default reporter. (c) MID-EDIT FULL-SUITE TRAP: a `test:run` started while sources are still being edited can collect stale module state and report phantom failures — re-run once files are stable before believing them.
- **SSE instrumentation poisons the observation (live 2026-11-10 sheet-open-rebake fix):** wrapping `EventSource` and redefining `onmessage` via `Object.defineProperty` on the INSTANCE swallows the app's own handler (the IDL slot is shadowed) — the app then receives ZERO SSE events and every echo-dependent UI silently stalls; a second `addInitScript` double-patches and crashes React with "Cannot redefine property: onmessage". Instrument with `addEventListener('message')` ONLY, add each init script exactly once per context, and use a fresh `isolatedContext` per ride. **Definitive producer tooling for suspicious bucket writes (live 2026-11-10 sheet-open-rebake fix):** from the page, `await import('/src/hooks/runtime/useRuntimeState.js')` and monkey-patch `.set`/`.delete` on the STORE INSTANCE returned by `getStore(name)` — gives stack-traced logs of every local bucket mutation and names the producing component/effect exactly. Related timing fact: the sheet-switch stale-state eviction write lands ~31 ms after the sidebar click when sheet content is warm (the "2–8 s" face is the rules-engine-warm-up variant of the same write); SSE connect-replay lands ~70 ms after campaign-select.
- ONE SSE per campaign (`subscribeToSSE`, `skipSync=true` on echoes). RELOAD BEHAVIOR (CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-12 improve run, live-proved twice + code census): there is NO general last-campaign auto-restore — after a reload the selection screen renders and STAYS until you click `.campaign-button` (an 8 s 500 ms-cadence sample never flipped the title `CharSheets`); selecting a campaign persists NOTHING (localStorage census at campaign-state = theme, activeCreatureName, encounterFilter-2024, __wlog, initiative-victory-dismissed, initiative-tv; zero campaign-name key, zero sessionStorage writer outside postRestore/postDelete/travelSession). The ONLY boot-time auto-select lane is the rollback-only `usePostRestoreNotice` (one-shot sessionStorage `postRestoreNotice`). A run that "observes auto-restore within ~1 s" is riding a pending rollback notice or a harness race — verify the notice/store census before believing a restore affordance exists. Change-data buckets SEEDED via curl BEFORE campaign-select load correctly into the client mirror on selection (no reload needed — live 2026-11-07 clock-expiry improve; contrast the curl-written-keys caveat below: keys written AFTER load don't refresh a settled tab). DOCUMENT-TITLE ORACLE (shipped 2026-11-21 improve browser-title-campaign, LLL-lived): `document.title` is now a trustworthy campaign+view probe via `getDocumentTitle` (`src/routes/config.js`): neutral `CharSheets` on the select screen AND while the campaign-select overlay shows (stale-view guard), `<campaign> — <characterName>` on the sheet view, `<campaign> — <sidebar label>` elsewhere (labels derive from SIDEBAR_BUTTONS — every SIDEBAR_VIEWS entry has one; note Encounter is singular), BARE `<campaign>` when activeView has no label (witnessed: fresh quick-create's zero-char empty-state lands titleless — `qa-arena`), and activeCharacter persists client-side across re-selects (a re-select titles `test-campaign — AasimarTest` with no click).
- **SSE snapshot replay (live 2026-11-07 duplicate-overlay improve):** `GET /subscribe` replays ALL current change-data keys as `change-<c>-<key>` events on EVERY connect and EventSource auto-reconnect (`server/routes/sse.js:27-34`) — stale undrained `savePrompt-*`/prompt keys remount as overlays on tab load ("identical twin overlays, load-dependent"). Server does exactly ONE publish() per POST (`campaigns-changedata.js:293`) — diagnose double-mounts by counting publishes (bounded `curl -sN --max-time 4 /subscribe` while POSTing = 1 per write) vs startup replay; and ALWAYS drain/DELETE prompt keys before finishing a run (the subagent's own leftover probe key reproduced the bug live). Mount-side identity law now pinned: prompt queue entries are keyed by promptId when both sides carry one, else by ARRIVAL KEY (one server key = one live prompt) — `SavePromptModal.jsx isSamePrompt`.
- Keys consumed NON-reactively via standalone `getRuntimeValue` (e.g. `lastAttack` feature gates) do NOT refresh mid-session — reload/re-select required before those readers see them. SHARPENED (live run KV 2026-11-11): keys written by ANY origin (curl or another client) that a mounted `useSyncedState`/store subscriber owns DO adopt live via the per-key SSE echo (travel-* hook keys, foreign combatSummary PATCHes, whole-doc map PUTs all re-render settled tabs within ~ms) — the "curl doesn't refresh a loaded tab" law is about the non-reactive readers, not a property of the writer. 2024 subclass-feature rows auto-derive from the char JSON's `class.subclass.name` (classes.json at the right level) — disk subclass swap + reload arms the row, no fixture authoring (live-proved Beguiling Twist lv7 Fey Wanderer). PROBE LAW (live 2026-11-16 improve monster-modal-target-select): `lastAttack` carries NO `timestamp` field — probes reading `.timestamp` false-negative as "cleared"; compare `attackerName`/`targetName`/`total` instead.
- HP truth: PC = runtime `currentHitPoints`; MONSTER = combatSummary `currentHp` ONLY (runtime monster HP keys never exist). Card HP input `input[aria-label="<Name> current HP"]` fill+Enter writes cs (revive/adjust; needs TRUSTED fill; dead lock → merged full-store POST `currentHitPoints:0`+reload). GM HP display lies — judge by `hp_change` log delta. Rig-face (live 2026-11-09 sheet-OA damage fix): a victim whose change-data bucket lacks the `currentHitPoints` key (curl-seeded bucket or sheet never loaded) makes the damage pipeline compute raw damage then console.error `currentHitPoints not found` with ZERO hp_change row — top up via scoped `PATCH /:campaign/<Victim> {value:{currentHitPoints:N, hitPoints:N}}` (GET-verify; `applied:0` on a plain bucket is honest row-merge truth since the `appliedFields` counter shipped — see the §2 PATCH counter law) before judging an application "broken".
- **Sandbox-map switch law (live 2026-11-09 fix run, cost a prod-fixture scare):** with a board tab already mounted on map A, `POST /maps` + whole-doc PUT + API `PUT …/activate` can be stamped BACK to A by the mounted board's MapContextSync — your drags then silently mutate A (it happened to owner fixture battle-arena; recovered because un-snapped cell-step drags land exactly Δcells (verify: billed feet = Δcells×5) so an on-disk digit revert restored md5 94fcd0bf). LAW: switch sandbox maps ONLY via the Maps-manager row `Activate` (app-native click) AFTER leaving the board view, and GET change-data `__map__` to confirm the active slug BEFORE every drag rig (sharpened 2026-11-10 improve: with NO board tab mounted, curl `PUT …/activate` is safe — nothing exists to re-stamp — and the one-shot `POST /maps {name,type,players:[…]}` auto-ids tokens; GET-confirm `__map__` before dragging regardless). SHARPENED (live 2026-11-11 improve map3d-census-avatars): the fresh-board mount-flush clobber bites the ONE-SHOT POST `players[]` seed too — first mount of a never-opened map flushed one of two seeded players server-side, twice; only the Open→settle(~2.5s)→PUT sequence survives remounts. Related: a mid-rig `page.goto` on a stale-empty board flushes a save that CLOBBERS a just-landed curl PUT (same law as the CREATE-drops-players finding in the OA-ride map recipe bullet) — drive token seeds while the Maps-manager view is open and let a settled board adopt via echo. SHARPENED both-directions (live 2026-11-09 improve clamp-toast-copy): mounting the board on a freshly-PUT sandbox map flushed an empty-`players` save over the landed token seed TOO (reproduced twice; a mis-row `Activate`/`Open` click briefly stamped owner fixture battle-arena active — file untouched, md5-proved). Reliable sequence: create → `Activate` → **Open the board and let it settle FIRST**, *then* whole-doc PUT the tokens (the settled board adopts via the map-data SSE echo). Row-rect lookups must anchor on the row ancestor whose normalized `innerText` **startsWith** the exact row label — an `includes('ACTIVE')` OR-clause matched a table-wide ancestor and clicked the wrong row's `Open`. RE-LIVED BOTH WAYS (HHH 2026-11-21): walking UP from the button looking for an ancestor containing the slug has the same trap (hit the whole-table ancestor, force-stamped `__map__` to battle-arena — file md5-untouched, zero-write mount law) — while walking, ABORT as soon as the ancestor contains >1 `Open` button. Sharpened (2026-11-10 improve fade-buried-tokens): token drags on a board materialize a `combatSummary` change-data MIRROR even with enforcement/economy OFF — include it in the exit admin-clear expectation (closed-tab clears GET-verify `{}` covers it). Stacking render fact updated in place: buried (non-zero-offset) token groups now carry `.token-buried` (opacity 0.55, Map.css) on BOTH lanes; stack tops/singles keep plain `creature-group`/`npc-group` byte-exact — probes matching those groups by EXACT class-string must use substring or account for the second class.
- **Sheet spell-cast chain + MATERIAL gate (live GB 2026-11-10):** spell row `td.left.spell-name.clickable` → SpellDetailPopup (`Cast Spell`) → cast runs; a spell in `materialComponents.js MATERIAL_REGISTRY` whose `itemName` is NOT an EXACT string in `playerStats.inventory.backpack` refuses with an explicit refusal popup ("<Spell> refused: material component <items> is not in <caster>'s backpack. Nothing spent." + RAW sentence as support) AND one `{type:'automation', automationType:'material_refused', reason:'material_missing'}` log row, zero slot spend (copy+row shipped 2026-11-10 improve material-refusal-log, pinned by `useSpellMetamagicGates.material-refused.test.js`; the material gate runs FIRST — before the enforcement gate and every payment lane). Rider faces: the wizard's registry rows are Animate Dead (modal-confirm face) and Divination (non-modal face) — Dispel Magic is a permanent material-gate CONTROL, NOT a face (V/S-only both rulesets, registry-absent). The detail popup shows the hint `(not found in backpack)` next to the Material row pre-cast. Grant path = disk JSON `inventory.backpack` += exact itemName string, reload + re-select (playerStats come from disk); a legit cast then writes consumption to DISK (`material_consumed` row + backpack edit). Denial-order law (fixed 2026-11-10): the material gate still runs BEFORE the enforcement gate (missing material = silent zero-cost refusal), but an enforcement turnlock denial now pays NOTHING — `gateMetamagic` carries an entry `assertCanAct` (`useSpellMetamagicGates.js`) before slot payment/material/chooser, so a row-click denial logs `enforcement_denied` with source `useSpellMetamagicGates` (the `useSpellCastExecutor` source survives only on confirm-seam-race denials: chooser opened on-turn, turn advanced before confirm); attack-lane denials unchanged-clean (1 `enforcement_denied` row, zero deltas). Animate Dead success face: modal is `.sp-overlay` with `Animate Dead (N)` confirm → Skeleton/Zombie rows join at caster-init (blank ⇒ fresh roll), `summons` + `spell` rows, board adoption via SSE only (summon lanes dispatch `combat-summary-updated`, never `initiative-rolled`, since 0349d9ddd — zero pendingOAs/round-flag/buff wipes verified).
- **Change-data row shapes & visitor DELETE-capture law (live GB 2026-11-10):** runtime `activeConditions` rows must be BARE STRING keys — the GB-era face where an object entry (`{key,name}`) POSTed via API threw `key.charAt is not a function` and CRASHED the whole Initiative view to cards=0 in every tab is CLOSED on both legs (2026-11-10 improve runtime-conditions-string-guard): server 400s non-string entries on all three write lanes (direct `POST /:c/activeConditions`, full-store `POST /:c/<Name>`, scoped `PATCH` merged truth — `keyValidators.js` `validateActiveConditionsArray`, null/absent stays an honest clear) AND `buildRuntimeConditions` renders sanitized `String(key)` labels without throwing; so a live malformed face can only be disk-seeded + API-restarted (route bypassed), and it now shows badges like `[object Object]`/`42` beside the good ones with all cards rendering. **Visitor DELETE law (FIXED 2026-11-10, corrected in place):** `pendingOAs` was never specially gated — the 403 was a WILDCARD route capture: `server/routes/campaigns-character.js` DELETE `/:campaign/:file` ran `refuseUnlessGm` BEFORE its non-`.json` `next()` fallthrough (mounted ahead of the change-data router), so every visitor-origin change-data DELETE 403'd while POST landed — stranding OA markers for a double-resolve and silently no-op'ing every `deleteRuntimeKey` drain-to-evict lane. The guard reorder (fall-through FIRST, GM gate only for real `.json` files) shipped with regression pins (`campaigns-character-delete-changedata-fallthrough.test.js`); live face now: fresh key POST 200 / DELETE 200 from the LAN origin, key ABSENT in the aggregate (visitor `savePrompt-*` drain-to-evict GET-proved), delegated visitor OA press evicts the marker atomically (GM tab shows no re-armed chip, exactly one automation+roll pair); visitor DELETE of a `.json` character file STILL 403s GM-only (existence-blind). Fail-loudly law: `deleteRuntimeKey` checks `res.ok` — a 403 refusal broadcasts `runtime-evict-failed` (Initiative renders an `EvictFailureToast` banner) and console.errors; a 404 honestly means a concurrent tab won the evict — CONFIRMED ABSENCE, SILENT on both lanes (NB 2026-11-18 fix `bug-cross-tab-evict-404-false-survives-console-error`: the false "survives server-side" console.error on the race-lost 404 is DEAD, same 2xx/404-confirm doctrine as `clearDeathSavePrompt`, pinned `useRuntimeState.deleteRuntimeKey.test.js`; non-403 refusals like 5xx keep console-only loud; the loser tab's two browser-level "Failed to load resource: 404" network lines are the browser's own log, not app output). The OA chip renders `.mc-overlay [data-kind="oa"]` with mover-pinned tooltip and live-mounts/un-mounts in settled tabs via the marker echoes; visitor monster cards need `img.avatar-image[alt="<Name>"]` + `scrollIntoViewIfNeeded()` + a TRUSTED click (off-screen synthetic clicks no-op; `browser_run_code_unsafe`'s `page` binds the CURRENT tab — `browser_tabs select` the witness tab first, GB ate two wrong-tab probes). **Audit asymmetry on the string-guard lane (live differential 2026-11-11 run LZ):** a malformed-conditions 400 from the wildcard POST leaves a persistent `write_rejected` log row (+ `denied-*` SSE, rendered by `log-write-rejected`), but the SAME refusal from the scoped PATCH lane is console-only (`BLOCKED PATCH` line, zero log rows) — FILED `bug-write-rejected-audit-missing-on-patch-lane`; judge PATCH denials from the 400 body/server console until fixed, not from the Log.
- **Maps-manager row selection + placedItems `visible` default (live 2026-11-11 run IF):** every Maps-manager row button carries an EXACT aria-label — `Open "<displayName>"`, `Activate "<name>"`, `Delete "<name>"` etc. — use it; a row-context probe that CLIMBS ANCESTORS from the button reaches the whole list wrapper and its text match picks the FIRST row (opened Battle Arena twice while meaning if-3d — a wrong-map rig ride + a false "create ignores gridSize" lead; `clampGridSize` at `maps.js:148` DOES honor POST `gridSize` ≥5≤100). Escaped-quote selectors (`'[aria-label="Open \\"x\\""]'`) mangle through the MCP code runner — build them inside `page.evaluate` with `String.fromCharCode(34/39)`, and when constructing the attr selector as a string use OUTER single quotes with the inner `"` raw (live 2026-11-11 improve map3d-chip-token-split: outer-double-quote construction throws `Invalid selector`). A settled mounted board ADOPTS whole-doc PUTs live via the map-data echo (the §56 clobber fears don't apply post-settle). RE-PROVED hard (live 2026-11-11 improve-run PT2 re-verify): whole-doc `players[]` add/move/remove adopts on the SAME mounted tab in ~400 ms with the map ACTIVE and with it merely Opened (non-active) — the "indoor players PUT stale-until-remount" backlog lead was a settle-timing/3D-census-race artifact, not an echo-lane gap; wait Open→settle ~2.5 s then PUT, and poll DOM/census VALUES not one-shot reads. RIG LAW: placedItems OMITTING the `visible` key = hidden tier (`isHidden/isVisible/itemOpacity` all read `!item.visible`) — probes wanting a fogged-but-VISIBLE or plain-visible item must write `visible:true` explicitly or the amber `hidden-item-ring` (73bb60655) fires and the item is stripped from LAN views.
- **Popup families + attack-hit Done-gate (live 2026-11-11 run IF, backdrop-void claim CORRECTED by same-day improve hit-popup-backdrop-void-guard):** three distinct popup DOM families — save prompts = `.sp-modal` (the LIVE one uniquely carries `data-testid="active-save-prompt"`, single-active through Roll Save and Done stages; a disabled AoE picker stays mounted behind it inside a `.sp-overlay` and carries NONE); attack/modals = `.sp-overlay`; monster-card attack popups = `.popup-overlay > .popup-modal` ("✓ HIT (n vs AC m)"). The hit popup STAGES damage only when `Done` is pressed. CORRECTION (improve re-ride, live-proved): the shared `Popup` backdrop has been INERT since 57d68ff80 (no overlay click handler; pinned by popup.test.jsx "no click dismissal") — a backdrop click with damage staged leaves the popup mounted and adds ZERO rows (IF's "backdrop SILENTLY VOIDs" claim does not reproduce; their voided damage was the popup merely being abandoned, the trap being the unconditional `click to dismiss` hint + Done-only affordance). Post-improve staged face: hint suppressed, buttons `["Done","Cancel attack"]` — `Cancel attack` (`.popup-close-btn` in `popup-close-row`, `AttackResultPopup.jsx`) logs `{type:'automation', automationType:'attack_cancelled', reason:'staged_damage_cancelled'}` naming attack+target and closes with zero dice-roll-done/hp_change rows; nothing-staged popups keep the old chrome (footer Done, hint, no Cancel). SHARPENED (live 2026-11-16 improve monster-modal-target-select): `Cancel attack` exists ONLY on the HIT-staged-damage branch — a MISS-staged popup carries just `Done` + the "click to dismiss" hint, so a cancel-leg probe on a MISS honestly finds no button (the cancel audit row still names attack+target and voids nothing on a MISS since no damage was staged). A card-wide popup probe MUST enumerate all three families (`.sp-overlay, .popup-modal`) or reads "no popup" while one is mounted. Results popups ("X — Results … Close") can linger behind later ones — drain `.popup-modal` first when adjudicating a fresh press.
- **Synthetic travel-event + enforcement seeding law (live 2026-11-11 explore run KW):** the event dialog can be armed WITHOUT advance-hunting: POST change-data `travel-pendingEvent` = `{type:'combat', title, description, terrain, encounter:{monsters:[{index,name,qty}], difficultyLabel, totalXP}}` — a mounted tab adopts it via the per-key SSE echo and `.event-dialog-overlay` renders immediately. The `terrain` field is MANDATORY in a synthetic seed: `EventDialog` prints `event.terrain` verbatim (a blank "Terrain: " row on a seeded event is fixture shorthand, not an app bug — `generateRandomEvent` always stamps it for natural events). The dialog's `.event-btn-accept` runs the FULL accept chain. Enforcement arm shape: `POST change-data enforcement {mode:'on', <subFlags>}` — the validator 400s without `mode` ("enforcement.mode must be 'off' or 'on'"); the stored bucket reads back FULLY MERGED (all 12 default flags) — branch on the sub-flag you armed. Enforcement-ON combat-accept face (autoEncounters:true): exactly three rows — `event_accept` (battleMap label), `session_phase` exploration→encounter changedBy `auto-encounter`, `automation/auto_encounter_started` (roster + tracker count + battle map name); engine rolls NPC initiative ONLY (PC initiative cells stay blank until rolled), `activeCreatureName` = top NPC, `sessionState.phase`='encounter', `__map__` moves to the reused "Travel Encounter Stage", `travel-pendingEvent` nulls, travel-mode returns to 'planning'.
- **Throwaway-seeded lane laws (re-lived 2026-11-11 run LZ; all three post-KW improves verified live):** quick-create buttons are `New Arena Battle`/`New Starter Adventure`; modal default name is date-stamped `Arena-YYYYMMDD`/`Starter-YYYYMMDD`, input carries `aria-label="New campaign name"` + placeholder `Enter campaign name` (label added 2026-11-18 improve campaign-name-input-labels, live-verified starter side MA 2026-11-18), submit is the LAST `Create` button — under `CAMPAIGN_LOCK` the off-allowlist default 403s now render INLINE in the modal with the typed name preserved (verified live 2026-11-11, 10d3866ad; the LZ-era full-page "Campaign locked to …" + `Reload Page` screen is RETIRED for quick-create — it survives only for load/select failures; see the §Lock-probe bullet for the verbatim denial copy); fill the canonical `qa-arena`/`qa-starter` name for a success-path create. Starter header carries a `STARTER ADVENTURE` badge and the zero-char wizard auto-opens — its footer has NO `Cancel` button (LZ walked all buttons: only `×` + the step tabs `1 Ruleset`…`9 Feature Choices`, and `×` on an untouched form closes with zero native confirm); its backdrop INTERCEPTS checklist/sidebar clicks (LZ's deep-link click timed out on it) — click `×` first or `page.goto('/')`, then drive steps; `getByText(name,{exact:true})` misses campaign rows — filter containers by `hasText` and `.last().click()`. FirstSessionChecklist steps deep-link OUTDOOR-view targets directly ("Meet the folk" → Settlements row), the regenerate step carries `data-testid="starter-step-regenerate"` and is a NAVIGATOR (its copy says `Admin → Regenerate` truthfully — it lands on Admin — Campaign Management, it does NOT open the dialog itself; the real control is `Regenerate Adventure` / `data-testid=regenerate-adventure-btn` there; note the copy DOES contain the word "Regenerate", TA 2026-11-16 — the "no longer contains" split at §74-era refers to role-query scoping, re-clicking the step body just re-navigates); the checklist re-derives generated NAMES live (post-regenerate it read the new dungeon without reload). Regenerate: role=dialog `Yes, Regenerate` → new dungeon slug replaces the old file (old GONE), `data/starter-seed.json` (campaign-ROOT path is wrong) `layer` +1, `regeneratedAt` stamped, `entities.pastTitles` keeps the retired title, countryside portal POI `linkedMap` + main-quest description + settlement `rumors` all re-pointed; the campaign log gains EXACTLY ONE `adventure_regenerated` audit row naming retired title(s) → new + layer ("Adventure regenerated: Old → New (adventure layer N)"; first-gen seeds log honest "initial generation — <new>" wording) — written by the client orchestrator `regenerateAdventure()` in `src/services/campaign/seededCampaign.js` via the shared POST /log lane (there is NO server-side regenerate route; corrected in place 2026-11-11 improve adventure-regen-log-row, which retired the old "zero rows" face). Encounters deep state: saved waves live in `data/encounters.json`; the starter checklist's "Join <wave>" step fires `requestEncounterLoad()` (`src/services/ui/encounterLoadSignal.js`: single-shot pending flag consumed on the builder's next mount + sync window event when already mounted — improve 2026-11-11) so the builder's Load modal opens AUTOMATICALLY with rows visible (manual `Load` button still the sidebar lane); rows live in `.encounter-modal-load > .encounter-list`, click target is the `Load "<name>"` aria button — a bare `text=Dungeon Wave 1` click hits the label and silently no-ops. Typed-name Admin delete (CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-16 improve `8bafa42af`, re-lived XD same day): the confirm button (`Delete "<true name>"`) renders natively DISABLED until the typed input matches TRIMMED + CASE-INSENSITIVE (the 2026-11-11 raw-`===` case-sensitive law is retired; shared `typedNameMatches` gates button AND handler so they never disagree); the handler guard + inline copy "Campaign name did not match. Deletion cancelled." remain an unreachable belt-and-braces fallback; judge the gate from `isDisabled()`; a caps/padded match DELETEs the true name (never the typed variant); the typed input is `<label htmlFor>`-bound AND (since 2026-11-18 improve campaign-name-input-labels) carries a name-echo `placeholder="<campaign>"` (live MA: `placeholder="qa-starter"`, aria stays null — §314 corollary); starter-seed GETs are ENVELOPE-shaped — `/settlements`→`{settlements:[…]}`, `/encounters`→`{encounters:[…]}` (a bare-array parse false-negatives the seed census, live MA). Shared `DeleteConfirmModal` gained an optional `confirmDisabled` prop (omitted consumers byte-identical). success → reload onto selection with one-shot banner "<name> deleted successfully."; API-lane delete `DELETE /api/campaigns/<name>` 200-equivalent, folder gone, no re-materialization. Arena throwaway: sidebar prunes to Campaigns/Encounters/Initiative/Log/Maps/Music/NPCs/Rules/Admin (campaign-only views hidden), 2 seeded halls + 3-encounter pool via Load. Starter-Admin a11y anchors (2026-11-04 improve): primary button `data-testid="regenerate-adventure-btn"`; checklist deep-link step `data-testid="starter-step-regenerate"` with aria-label "Open Campaign Admin to re-roll the adventure (keeps <village>)" — its accessible name no longer contains "Regenerate", so `/Regenerate Adventure/i` and `/Regenerate/i` role queries resolve to exactly the ct-btn.
- **AI Dress structural-smuggle law (live 2026-11-14 fix ai-dress-wall-as-item):** (a) SNAKE_CASE SMUGGLE PITFALL: `\b` counts `_` as a word char — the model re-smuggled `type:'stone_wall'` past `\bwall\b` mid-fix; structural-noun scans must use `(?<![A-Za-z0-9])…(?![A-Za-z0-9])` boundaries (camelCase sprites like `arrowSlitWall` stay correctly glued). Server lane now refuses structural-noun item TYPES fail-closed (all-or-nothing error lane; registered prop sprites door/stairs/arrowSlitWall stay placeable); a success audit row with `attempts: 2` beside a clean preview = fail-closed refusal + repair fired (refusal detail is NOT stored on the success row).

- **Active-map TWO-SOURCE law + ghost eviction FIXED (live explore FI 2026-11-17 filed; `c5e4b020c` fix-VERIFIED explore GK 2026-11-17):** the server's `GET /api/campaigns/:c/active-map` (in-memory `activeMaps`, written ONLY by the activate route) and the change-data `__map__` key are DIFFERENT truths: a GM row-**Open** force-stamps `__map__` via `MapContextSync` (client POST `{value:{activeMapName}}`) without touching `activeMaps` — live face after Open (no Activate): active-map `null` while `__map__` names the slug. Ghost era CLOSED by `c5e4b020c` (GK explore 2026-11-17 fix-verify 4/4): Map DELETE now reconciles BOTH sources — deleting a merely-Opened map converges `__map__` to `{activeMapName:null}` (UI-row AND API-origin deletes re-lived; a board tab still mounted ON the deleted map adopts the new `map-activate` null publish and never resurrects the stamp — 5 s watch), and RENAME re-points a stamped-but-not-active old slug to the new one (Open → inline-rename → `__map__` names the new slug, old file ghosts 404). Remaining eviction lanes: wildcard `DELETE /api/campaigns/:c/__map__` (200), a later Open/Activate overwrite, or the admin change-data clear. PROBE LAW: never judge "map is active" from `__map__` alone — GET `/active-map` is the activation truth; sandbox maps Opened for editor rides DO stamp `__map__`, so clean it at exit or the census shows a phantom.
- **EB join/load initiative-row honesty law (shipped 2026-11-18 improve eb-join-honest-roll-log):** `logRoll` in `src/services/encounters/encounterToInitiative.js` (sole join/load seam initiative-row writer — `loadEncounterToInitiative` and `addMonstersToInitiative` share it) stamps every join roll row `description:"<Name> rolled initiative: d20 <die> ± <bonus> = <total>"` (negative bonuses sign-formatted, qty-expanded instances described by their numbered name). DANGLING-CENSUS PROBE LAW: judge a bare roll row by `description == null && message == null && targetName == null` TOGETHER — absence of targetName alone false-positives (initiative rows legitimately have no target). OUT-OF-SCOPE SIBLINGS (untouched): `npcCombatService.logInitiativeRoll` (+NPC Add-to-Initiative lane) still writes a target-less initiative row WITHOUT a description — consistency-improve candidate; `combatLoggingService.logInitiativeRoll` has zero non-test callers.
- **curl `-G` body-move probe trap (live 2026-11-18 improve sheet-attack-inflight-guard):** `curl -G` relocates `-d` data into the QUERY STRING — the route receives an EMPTY body, and the spell-overlay lane answers `Unknown spell-overlay action: undefined` (looks like an app defect; is a harness defect). Clear bodies: `POST /spell-overlay?campaign=<c>` with body `{"action":"clear"}` (NOT `clearAll`), campaign ONLY in the query; never combine that POST with `-G`.
- **Sheet attack-chain DWELL lane laws (live + shipped 2026-11-18 improve sheet-attack-inflight-guard):** (a) DRAIN-RACE LAW: a staged HIT popup's `Cancel attack` does NOT end the chain — the pending rider step (e.g. Devious Strikes) mounts its `.sp-overlay` chooser AFTER the popup closes, and the chooser's Cancel resumes into a SECOND damage popup (`click to dismiss` + Done); multi-face dwell lanes must drain popup→chooser→popup, and a momentary `!.popup-modal && !.sp-overlay` observation is a race, not the chain's end. (b) DWELL SIGNAL (shipped): `CharActions` derives `attackChainDwelling = popupHtml != null || Object.values(mergedModalState).some(v => v != null)` — per-sheet (each CharSheet mounts its own DiceRollContext.Provider; popup is NOT a global singleton), purely local UI state; during dwell the three weapon activation cells get class `attack-dwell-disabled` (opacity 0.45, cursor not-allowed — distinct from the 0.35 `.disabled-attack` idiom) + `aria-disabled="true"`. (c) UNIT HARNESS: `modalState` is local `useState`, so chooser-face dwell is driven by passing `spellModalState={{ attackRiderModal: {...} }}` into CharActions (the merged channel sees it) — no need to mock `useCharActionModals` (pinned `CharActions.attackDwell.test.jsx`).
- **Indoor ItemContextMenu Rename lane (FI rig; viewport-clamp `be6604015` fix-VERIFIED GK explore 2026-11-17):** the Rename entry renders ONLY for placedItems with `type==='npc'` (`showRenameOption = item.type === 'npc'`); `character:<Name>` tokens do NOT get it.
