The FI-era BELOW-viewport overflow (Rename row y≈966 in a 941-px viewport) is DEAD: the menu now clamps its origin into the visible world rect (npc token at gridY 18, hit-rect y 838-881 → menu rows render y 798-890, ALL inside view, Rename directly hittable). DOM correction: a `type:'npc'` placedItem renders as `g.npc-group > rect.placed-hit-area` — a `g.placed-item` probe false-negatives (0 matches while the token is mounted; anchor `rect.placed-hit-area`). The FI keyboard lane (focus `g[role=menuitem]` + trusted Enter) still works and is belt-and-braces. The token-rename popover (MonsterNameAutocomplete with explicit `initialFocus`) mounts WITH the caret and pre-fills the stored name; Escape only drops the suggestion list (popover stays), an outside click closes popover+menu zero-write (doc md5 byte-exact); commit posts on Enter/blur.
- **Rider-chain sheet-attack probe prerequisites (live improve charger-chooser-radio-aria-labels 2026-11-18):** a GM-tab sheet weapon attack REFUSES with a `.popup-modal` "No Target — … cannot use <W> — no target is armed on …" until the Initiative card's `select[aria-label="<Char> target"]` is armed (`locator.selectOption({label:'<Monster>'})`) — every rider-chain rig needs this arm step first. Chain-seam timing: after `Roll Save` on an armed STR prompt the SAVE-FAILURE result face DWELLS as the top `.sp-overlay` and the Shield-Bash fail chooser mounts only after its `Done` — a chooser probe <~2s after Roll reads the result face, not the chooser. Aria anchors now stable: EVERY `AttackRiderModal` option input carries `aria-label="<Option> <target>"` (target segment dropped on no-target mounts, ShieldBashChoiceModal convention) — role/accessible-name locators resolve rider rows first try.

- **Damage-popup Savage Attacker chip face law (live improve savage-attacker-chip 2026-11-18, chip now raw byte-exact `'Savage Attacker'`):** the chip rides ONLY the post-Done damage-result DWELL face (`type:'damage'`, `savageAttacker` flag still set in popupData, same-round suppressed 2nd HIT keeps the dwell-with-Done face) — the staged HIT face shows only raw `Done`/`Cancel attack`; a chip locator on the staged face always misses. Cheap dwell rig: EB-join one Goblin → arm `select[aria-label="<Char> target"]` → press `.attacks div.left.clickable` (Glaive) → `Done` → consume the rider chain (Charge Cancel → STR Roll Save → result Done) → a FRESH same-round press re-mounts the dwell face with raw `[Savage Attacker] [Done]` buttons. Locator nuance once raw-exact: page-wide exact-text `'Savage Attacker'` matches TWO legitimate owners (sheet `span.feat-name` + chip) — scope to `.popup-modal` or use `getByRole('button',{name:'Savage Attacker',exact:true})`. SCOPE RESIDUAL (not a defect, family sweep candidate): the `:702` reroll-result row still carries the analogous leading space (live raw `" Savage Attacker: 4 → 3 — Original kept"`), as do `Keep First/Keep Reroll` icon-adjacent text nodes.

- **Lapsed-copy liveness law + rescue-rig sharpeners (shipped improve save-lapsed-copy-live-peer 2026-11-18):** `pendingSavePrompts` rows now carry an `ownerTab` token (`tab-<random per page-load>`, stamped by `createSaveListener`); the owning page STRIPS only its own markers via a `keepalive:true` whole-bucket wildcard POST on `pagehide` — event-driven, zero wall-clock. So `save_lapsed` copy branches truthfully: foreign live token → "resolved/dismissed from another window — … lapsed" (asserts NEITHER "after a page reload" NOR "with the lost page"); absent token (reload/close stripped it; hard-crash stale-marker is the documented accepted limit) → the byte-identical reload copy. Rig sharpeners lived: (1) a MISS-stage popup face renders `Done` + "click to dismiss" ONLY — a retry loop keying on `Cancel attack` dead-times out, dismiss via `Done`; (2) after Done on a live-chain save FAIL the Shield-Bash fail chooser mounts ~1–2 s LATER than the overlay-drain census — an immediate `sps:0` read FALSE-reports drain, re-probe; (3) sharpened two-tab law: when the ANSWERING tab rolls a FAIL, the PARKED producer tab's resumed chain additionally mounts the fail chooser and stamps a `Choose an effect.` roll row — the live-peer face must drain that chooser (Skip/Apply) before a both-tabs-zero census; (4) Initiative `Clear` by locator intermittently dead-times right after a re-select though visible+enabled — an evaluate-dispatch `.click()` lands reliably. (5) OC 2026-11-19 re-verify (independent, SUCCESS path): the `ownerTab` token is visible on the `pendingSavePrompts` row at ARM time (GET-able — no need to wait for resolution to prove the stamp); the live-peer description landed byte-verbatim "…was resolved from another window — the Shield Bash awaiting it lapsed (effect not applied, no chooser available)." with `reason:"save resolved from another live window — awaiting effect chain parked on that page"`; dup `save_result` pair (3 ms apart) + one outcome `roll` row, tab-A app console zero errors (only the 2 browser network-404 lines). RIG SHARPENER: the FIRST Roll Save press on a replay-mounted prompt CAN silently drop (Playwright reports the click OK, zero rows, prompt stays parked — witnessed 1×; a press gated on button-VISIBLE inside `[data-testid=active-save-prompt]` landed first try) — after answering, always VERIFY a `SAVE SUCCESS/FAILURE` result face mounted before pressing Done, never trust the click alone. Also: single-key scoped GETs `/<Char>/_Latch_round` 404 even when the keys are PRESENT — latch censuses must enumerate `/change-data` `d[<Char>]` (NB's "GET-probe" shorthand means the bucket census).

- **Campaign-zone + sweep-seam laws (lived improve grease-sleet-area-save-wiring 2026-11-19):** (1) the campaign `targetEffects` store hydrates from the change-data TOP-LEVEL `targetEffects` key — seed zone tes via `POST /{campaign}/targetEffects {value:[…]}`; writing the `value` bucket feeds a DIFFERENT doc that campaign-store reads silently miss (one false "sweep dead" diagnosis burned on this). (2) `expireStaleEffects` Phase 1/2 (queue expiry) precede the zone phases (3 sleet caster-side, 4 stinking-cloud, 5 web, 6 grease carrier-side `processZoneTurnStartSaves`) in ONE sweep — a clock seeded `appliedRound+expiryRounds <= currentRound` matures in the same sweep that would arm the zone prompt, so separate the turn seams to observe both legs. (3) The sheet spell-row cast lane does NOT dispatch `HANDLER_MAP` handlers for array-automation spells (`handleGenericAutomation` screens on `automation.type`) — zone-spell cast dispatch lives in `spellGates.js`/`SIMPLE_SPELL_SPECS`; Grease's `handleGreaseAreaSave` dispatch row (`index.js:556`) is unit-pinned but UI-unfed on either ruleset (known cast-producer residual, proven around by seeding the handler's exact write shape via scoped PATCH). (4) The recurring-zone carrier te needs a `targetEffectDefinitions` registry entry (GM EffectAdder reads it); the GM-initiated `initiate-zone-save` lane gates on it. (5) PRODUCTION-SEAM RIDE LAWS (live explore run PD 2026-11-19, Phase 6 armed organically from the pressing tab): the caster tracking must carry the FULL handle() shape — `mapName` is a HARD silent gate (`processGreaseAreaSave` `if (!_mapName) return null` — a center/saveDc/radius-only seed arms NOTHING) and `condition` is required too (`greaseTargetIsImmune` does `tracking.condition.toLowerCase()` and its TypeError is swallowed by the per-te catch — the console.error did NOT surface in the MCP console listing, judge the seam by `pendingSavePrompts` GET / prompt mount, never by console absence); no active map ⇒ `isWithinRange` true (line 17 law) so NO map rig is needed; the GM turn-advance control on the board row is `Next →` (arrow in the text — a `'Next'` exact match misses) and `.end-turn-button` renders ONLY under enforcement (off ⇒ absent, not disabled); EffectAdder manual-add of a zone effect (Grease (Zone) census'd live in the Effects tab with source+dc fields) writes ONLY the campaign te — it never seeds the caster `_<prefix>_<caster>` key, so a manual-only zone is an inert marker by the same lane-parity as sleet/web/stinking (badge + removal only, no recurring save).

- **Death-state clear race + roll-to-DEAD loop pitfalls (live improve death-save-remove-copy 2026-11-19; race FIXED 2026-11-19 improve remove-dead-bucket-write-race):** (1) WRITE RACE RESOLVED: `handleRemoveDead` (DeathSavingThrows.jsx) used to fire 3 SEQUENTIAL `setRuntimeValue` calls, each a whole-bucket replace POST that could interleave so a stale body resurrected the DEAD badge via the SSE echo (witnessed 1/2 cycles; same family as the resolved `bug-clear-latch-eviction-resurrects-stamps`) — it is now ONE `setRuntimeBatch` clearing isDead + both tracks in a single POST, pinned by `DeathSavingThrows.clear-single-write.test.jsx` (wire-shape vs the real useRuntimeState module + fetch spy). (2) Roll-to-DEAD loop law: an organic NAT 20 during the loop restores HP>0 and UNMOUNTS the whole panel — the loop needs the HP-editor re-drop leg (works from any cur value). (3) MCP string-layer escape mangling: a `waitForFunction` regex embedding `\uXXXX` escapes arrives mangled through `browser_run_code_unsafe` — probe `.textContent` with literal glyphs (✓/✗) or class anchors instead. (4) TERMINAL-ROLL CHIP LAW + remove-fix re-verify (live explore run PD 2026-11-19, 28-roll farm): a NON-terminal roll's result chip mounts ~1 ms after the press (MutationObserver `add@1`) and 60 ms polling sees it — but a LIFE-ENDING roll's chip NEVER enters the DOM at all (3/3 nat20s + fatal 3rd-failure rounds: no add, no rm): the optimistic `isDead`/`currentHitPoints` writes batch into the SAME React commit that swaps to the DEAD-badge branch / unmounts the block via CharHitPoints' `currentHitPoints <= 0` gate, so the 2 s dwell face (incl. the `NAT 20` span) renders zero frames — filed `bug-death-save-terminal-roll-chip-never-renders.md`; an organic NAT 1 that is NOT fatal DOES render (`(1)NAT 1Failure`, honest double-fail track 0→2 in one row's totalFailures). Chip farming rig that works: per-press = press → 60 ms chip poll → verify a new `death_save` row via log GET (row carries `isNatural1/isNatural20`, immune to the chip race) → reset via scoped PATCH `{currentHitPoints:0, isDead:0, deathSaves:[f,f,f], deathFailures:[f,f,f]}` (adopted live; the re-drop leg doubles as the nat20-remount). `1daee7fee` title fix live-verified solo 3/3: DEAD badge × `title="Clear death state"` → badge gone, Roll area re-arms, HP stays 0 (honest rename), `removed|0/0` row lands; the §115(1) write race did NOT re-arm the badge in any of 3 solo cycles.

- **Log POST schema-gate law (shipped 2026-11-20 improve log-post-schema-gate, owner ruling):** `POST /api/campaigns/:c/log` is shape-gated, origin-neutral (visitor-writable shared-table doctrine like /spell-overlay — never 403): body must be a JSON object; `type` must be a string in `ALLOWED_LOG_TYPES` (exported from `server/routes/log.js`, derived from every real producer — grep evidence in the code comment); and non-empty human-readable text is required in at least ONE of `message`/`description`/`noteText`/`summary` (log-utils `directAuditText` display fields) or `text` (info-lane). Refusals 400 `{error:<plain-English>}` with ZERO side effects (validation runs strictly before `addEntry` — no row, no `log-<c>` publish). PITFALLS: Express 5 `express.json()` strict mode itself 400s JSON primitives (`null`, bare strings) as `entity.parse.failed` BEFORE any route runs — a shape-gate test must not expect the route's own copy for those bodies (the array body is the primitive-ish shape that reaches the route); and `Log.jsx` `NoteEntry` renders ONLY `entry.noteText` — a `{type:'note',message}` row is VALID server-side but renders with an empty note body, so browser-visible note probes must use `noteText`. FIXED 2026-11-20 (`fix log-schema-gate-rejects-app-own-combat-rows`, resolved): the gate stays byte-identical/origin-neutral — instead the app's own textless rows now carry derived text: the single client POST funnel `src/services/ui/logService.js addEntry` stamps a plain-English `message` on rows lacking canonical text (`stampAuditText`/`deriveAuditText`, per-type builders mirroring the Log renderers' family voice: HIT/MISS/Critical Hit!/Critical Miss!, Defeated/Bloodied, Has Perished!, Encounter Joined…); text-bearing rows pass byte-identically. WIRE INVARIANT: every row on the wire carries readable text — any NEW direct `POST /log` lane that bypasses logService must stamp its own `message` or it honestly 400s (the sole exception lived: `npcCombatService.logInitiativeRoll`'s direct fetch, now self-stamping). Attack-row `total` is the RAW d20 (bonus separate), so the stamped line reads the popup equation — `16 +4 (+4 to hit) vs AC 19 — HIT` — not a sum. The `ALLOWED_LOG_TYPES` producer-drift backlog concern remains open.

## 3. Rule data is truth
- Verify from `public/data/monsters.json` (+ `/data/2024/` twins) FIRST; the bug file's "Expected" may itself be wrong. EB join names must match monsters.json exactly; EB search top hit may be wrong species — read autocomplete rows; first join after autocomplete can join WRONG monster (verify cs after every join).
- monsters.json edits are NOT visible in a loaded tab even after reload — browser HTTP cache serves stale /data. Proof: `fetch(cache:'reload')`; fix display = close card × → Campaigns → re-select → reopen (the old `combat-ui-viewingMonster` key-DELETE step is DEAD: the `*-viewingMonster` modal-open keys were retired 2026-11-07 by 579aca8d2 — modal state is per-client useState in Initiative/Map/EncounterBuilder and the stat block never syncs; TV screens never show it); EB-joined combatants keep stale snapshots — remove+re-join.
- monsters.json prose anchors NOT monster-unique (dragons share Pounce/Rend text; "Stone spikes sprout" ×4; gynosphinx byte-identical block; soft-hyphen U+00AD embedded) — anchor edits on monster-unique neighbors + indentation, JSON.parse + full `git diff` after.
- OCR typo family "1dlO"→"1d10" (Silver, Ancient Bronze, yochlol, lizardfolk): `canRollExpression("1dlO")=false` → MV-12 chip suppression; author explicit dice on fix.
- 2024 classes.json Devious Strikes options carry `saveAbility:"DEX"` on CON-save options (Poison/Daze/KO) — NOT a mislabel: `buildSaveDc` reads `saveAbility` as the DC-SOURCE ability and 2024 Cunning Strike DC = 8+PB+DEX by RAW for every option (save TYPE stays CON via `saveType`); flipping it breaks the primary DC (proved 2026-11-07, DS-Poison repeat-save fix).
- D20-conversion features diverge by ruleset in canonical data: 2024 Stroke of Luck = "turn the roll into a **20**" (attack conversions ARE natural-20 crits) while the 5e twin = "turn the **miss into a hit**" (never a 20, never crits); Boon of Combat Prowess hits without becoming a 20 in both (non-crit by design). Gate conversion-crit logic on `playerStats.rules` + `rollType==='attack'`. FT-007 conversion latch folds into the roll CONTEXT, so a spent-boon roll ships `popupHtml` WITHOUT `autoRerollForAttack` (button absent — tests pinning the locked face model flag-off, not a hidden button; live 2026-11-06).

## 4. Live monster-card rig
- **Chip-lane geometry exemption rig (lived both directions, GGG 2026-11-21, PT#3 CLOSED):** census — `MonsterCardModal.computeMapRangeState` measures DISTANCE only and `computeCoverState` reads ONLY granted Bulwark/Sanctuary/Smite; consumers of `checkLineOfSight` are the PC-sheet handlers (`useCharActionsAttackHandlers.js`, `useSpell*`), never the monster chip. LIVE RIG: active indoor map + whole-doc PUT staging — attacker token in `placedItems` `{type:'npc',name:'<exact cs name>',gridX,gridY,visible:true}` (QMS `#qms-place-token` stamps one at the party anchor, reposition via the PUT — every press refetches the doc, no reload needed), target PC in `players[]`, and `{'type':'door','open':false}` on the in-between cell (`coverService.buildClosedDoors` treats it as a FULL-cover/LoS blocker); enforcement seeded `POST /enforcement {value:{mode:'on',zones:true,lineOfSight:true}}`. FACES: monster chip ranged press rolls CLEAN through the closed door (`✓ HIT (21 vs AC 12)` — unbumped AC, zero cover clause, zero refusal; row `… vs AC 12 — HIT`), while the sheet-lane control on the SAME geometry refuses `Goblin is behind a wall at (5,10) — no line of sight.` + lands `enforcement_denied` (since `improve(enforcement-denied-log-row)` 2026-11-21 the row renders ATTRIBUTED via the dedicated `LogEnforcementDeniedEntry` — `creature → target: reason` + one category label + bucket chip; the bare `message:'Enforcement Denied'` stamp remains but is not what the Log shows). Lane-split is DOCUMENTED-INTENTIONAL (mirrors FFF PT#1) — not a bug. MODAL-OPEN IDIOMS re-lived: a hand-POSTed cs creature (bare `npc`, no bestiary link) NEVER opens `.mc-overlay` (avatar click honest no-op) — use a QMS exact-name `.qms-row-name === 'Goblin'` spawn; player-card scope idiom `.creature-card` with `textContent.trim().startsWith('F<Name>HP')` beats hasText (target dropdowns contaminate every card); sheet ranged press without an armed INITIATIVE-card target refuses verbatim (`… no target is armed on <name>'s initiative card. Arm a target with the Target select on the initiative card. No attack rolled, nothing spent.`) — arm via the card select, NOT the modal one; `Cancel attack` on a staged hit writes an `automation` row `staged_damage_cancelled` (zero hp delta).
- **Quick-spawn token stamp seam (shipped `improve(quick-spawn-map-token)` 2026-11-21):** the `+ Monster` picker carries `#qms-place-token` — an opt-in "Place a token near the party on the map" checkbox, DEFAULT OFF, rendered ONLY when a map is active (OFF/no-map = byte-identical historical flow, zero map traffic). ON stamps one `placedItems` row `{id:'quick-spawn-<slug>-<x>-<y>', type:'npc', name:<EXACT board name incl. series suffix>, gridX, gridY, visible:true}` per instance via the ONE GM map-doc PUT; anchor = rounded centroid of `players[]` tokens (grid centre when none), preferred cell +(2,0), deterministic Chebyshev-ring free-cell scan (blocked by players[]/placedItems AND by same-batch earlier draws, radius 4, clamped) — pure seam `src/services/maps/spawnTokenPlacement.js`. SERIES-RENUMBER FOLLOW: a plain 'Goblin' token renames IN PLACE (same id/cell) to 'Goblin 1' in the same PUT as the new row's stamp, so renumbered boards keep measuring (§4 token-name law). OUTDOOR maps carry hex `{q,r}` `partyPosition` and NO grid-token lane — since `improve(qms-outdoor-hide-token)` 081944a36 the checkbox is HIDDEN there entirely (`useActiveMapType` resolves the type from the maps LIST lane — one `GET /maps` per picker mount, entry match `slug===mapName||name===mapName`, unresolved/failed fetch fails CLOSED to absent with a loud `Error loading maps:` console.error), so the producer never sees stamp intent; the producer-side named-skip console.error remains only as the defensive backstop lane. APPEND-ORDER LAW (live 2026-11-21 improve enforcement-denied-log-row): the spawn APPENDS its token row AFTER any staged doc rows — it never overwrites the staged doc — and the LoS gate resolves `tokens.find(name)` on the FIRST name match, so for exact staged geometry either re-PUT the doc AFTER spawning (staged cell wins) or stage the token you want measured earlier in `placedItems`; the spawn button label is `Spawn 1× Goblin` with U+00D7 (regex around the ×, scope to `.qms-modal`).
- **Stat-modal target select is SEPARATE from the board card's (live 2026-11-17 fix bi-defense-prompt-producer-orphaned):** the monster stat modal (`.mc-overlay`) carries its OWN Target `<select>` — arming the board card's `[aria-label="<Name> target"]` does NOT arm the modal's copy; a chip rolled from the modal resolves against the modal's (un-armed) select. Set BOTH (or arm after opening the modal) before rolling a targeted attack. Related popup-face reminder: staged attack popups root at `.popup-overlay` with their `button.dice-roll-reroll-btn`/`.popup-close-btn` "Done" — NOT `.sp-overlay` (see Popup Done classes law in §12).
- **`+ NPC` is BOARD-ONLY (live ZK 2026-11-17):** the Initiative toolbar `+ NPC` button (exact accname `+ NPC` with space; title-only, no aria-label — sidebar `NPCs` catches loose `hasText:'NPC'` locators and NAVIGATES) adds a board-only placeholder behind native confirm `Add NPC with placeholder AC 10 / HP 10? Edit on the card.` — `data/npcs.json` stays byte-exact `db6666c1` through the add; removal is the card's `button[title="Remove NPC"]` + native confirm, and the admin clear evicts the row anyway. Its avatar is a non-focusable initial-div → honest no-op as a visitor zoom target (no-image leg).
- **Attack-result popup blocks the board (live CF 2026-11-13):** a monster-card dice-chip press leaves `.popup-overlay` mounted showing the roll face (`…click to dismiss Done`); it INTERCEPTS every subsequent click in the Initiative view — a `force:true` press of the Clear button silently lands on the popup layer and the confirm dialog never arms (no error). Dismiss with `page.locator(".popup-overlay button", {hasText:"Done"}).click()` (or click the popup body) before any board control; audit `!!document.querySelector(".popup-overlay")` after any roll-driven ride.
- EB: Encounters page → search → checkbox → "Join Encounter" (only path into initiative; table resets after; joins mid-round keep `.mc-overlay` open; join can auto-land monster at cs idx 0). EB search top hit may be wrong species — read autocomplete rows; first join after autocomplete can join WRONG monster (verify cs after every join). EXACT locator law (live 2026-11-09 ER): every bestiary checkbox carries `aria-label="Select <exact name>"` — click `input.monster-checkbox[aria-label="Select Goblin"]` instead of the EQ-era name+CR concat index-matching dance (`Goblin0.25` defeating `^Goblin\b` is obsolete; `Select Hobgoblin` can never collide with `Select Goblin`). Join auto-navigates to the Initiative VIEW (`App.jsx onJoinEncounter -> setActiveView('initiative')`) — a side effect with teeth (see Initiative mount law below). Initiative-page "+ NPC" = BARE statless NPC, not a real join. REST witness for a join: cs lives at change-data key `combatSummary` (`GET /api/campaigns/:c/change-data`) — NOT `__initiative__` (gate stamp, reads null there); joined creature carries `monsterIndex` + `size`/`armorClass`/`maxHitPoints` for name/size verification; pre-join baseline can read `{}` until the 2s debounce flushes — poll the aggregate key (live 2026-11-08 improve eb-monster-list-perf). Bestiary first paint is BOUNDED since that improve: empty/un-filtered search renders ≤50 `input.monster-checkbox` rows + `.monster-table-cap-notice` ("Showing first N of M — type to narrow"); any active filter/search renders the full match set (census probes: un-narrowed 50/636). The cap BITES JOIN LOCATORS too (live 2026-11-10 improve gc-oa-chip-leading-space): `input.monster-checkbox[aria-label="Select Goblin"]` is simply ABSENT on an un-narrowed table (alphabetical first-50) — always type into `input[aria-label="Search monsters"]` before the exact-aria checkbox click. EB "one-tab monster attack" live rig (live 2026-11-13 improve victory-banner-zero-attack-clear): `Select <monster>` checkbox → `Join Encounter` (auto-navigates to Initiative, auto-rolls initiative) → arm via row `<select aria-label="<Name> target">` → click avatar `img[alt="<Name>"]` → click the action's `.mc-dice-link` → the result popup `[data-testid="popup-overlay"]` exits via its `Done`; a STAGED hit face (own Done + Cancel attack) is Done/Cancel-attack ONLY (`dismissable={false}`), while every non-staged face now also answers Escape/pure backdrop with zero writes (shared-Popup modal-family law, improve popup-dismiss-safe-faces 2026-11-16) — the CARD still needs its `aria-label="Close"` button (Escape dismisses neither the `.mc-overlay` nor the board) before `button.clear-button` is clickable again. RIG SHARPENINGS (live 2026-11-16 improve hide-target-select-uncontrolled): tick `input.monster-checkbox` and click `Join Encounter` in the SAME `browser_run_code_unsafe` call (a search re-fill between calls silently drops the selection); the bestiary search is substring-matched alphabetically (`Skeleton` hits Flaming first) — anchor the exact `td` name; the Clear-board confirm is the IN-APP `Clear Initiative Board` modal (`Yes, Clear Board`), no native dialog fires.
- **EB Load-picker DOM law (live-proved 2026-11-13 improve arena-load-affordance on test-campaign pool rows POSTed through the identical lane the arena seeder uses):** `button[aria-label="Load encounter"]` opens the picker on its OWN overlay family — `.encounter-modal-overlay` (role=presentation) + `.encounter-modal`, header `Load Encounter` — with ZERO `[role=dialog]`, zero `.modal-overlay`/`.modal-shell-overlay`/`.ct-modal-overlay`, no dropdown/select: generic dialog/dropdown probes read a mounted picker as a "dead affordance" (the EH backlog premise was exactly this locator blind; the lane itself is live, no code change shipped). Rows = `li.encounter-list-item`, name on `.encounter-list-name`; row buttons carry exact aria-labels `Load "<name>"`/`Rename "<name>"`/`Delete "<name>"` — the escaped-quote selector mangles through the MCP runner, build `button[aria-label='Load "…"']` inside evaluate with String.fromCharCode(39/34). Row Load click (evaluate `.click()` works on these buttons) → GET `/encounters/<name>` → modal closes, title/description/Selected Monsters hydrate, `Encounter loaded` toast; an EXTRA `PUT /encounters/<name>` riding right after a load is the effective-XP reconcile write-back BY DESIGN, not harness damage. Save round-trips: a saved row appears in the picker; the toolbar button flips `Save`→`Update` once a row is loaded and Update fires the NATIVE confirm `Overwrite encounter "<name>"? This replaces the saved encounter.` (handle via browser_handle_dialog in the same ride). FIRM-leg + Cancel-leg BOTH lived (XD 2026-11-16): accept → exactly ONE `PUT /encounters/<name>` 200 + toast `Encounter updated`; refuse → zero writes, draft untouched; row delete from the Load picker = native `Delete "<name>"?`; a create save is ONE `POST /encounters` 201 (xd-probe row create→overwrite→delete round-tripped the pool byte-exact to dce8d33a). PROBE LAWS (same run): (a) the bestiary checkbox is a NATIVE `input.monster-checkbox` — a CSS `[role=checkbox]` probe finds NOTHING (a11y-tree role ≠ attribute selector; the WK-ledger "(role=checkbox)" wording means the ACCESSIBILITY role only) — click `input.monster-checkbox[aria-label="Select <Name>"]`; qty stepper buttons carry `aria-label="Increase quantity of <Name>"`, qty cell `td[data-testid="qty-<rowIdx>"]`. (b) Re-arming a `window.fetch` monkey-patch in the SAME context NESTS the wrappers and DOUBLE-LOGS every request (two "PUT /encounters/XD Probe" lines for one real PUT) — arm the monitor EXACTLY ONCE per context and treat `browser_network_requests` as ground truth for write counts. (c) The monster-card modal opens ONLY from a TRUSTED click on `[class*=avatar]` inside the creature-card (evaluate-click silently no-ops); the arm-target select now lives on BOTH surfaces (SHARPENED IN PLACE 2026-11-16 improve monster-modal-target-select: `TargetSelect.jsx` is shared — the card select AND a mirrored `select[aria-label="<Name> target">` in the `.mc-overlay` header slot `.mc-header-arm`, both riding the SAME `handleTargetChange` instance, live-sync both directions via the cs echo, modal arm never closes the overlay via stopPropagation; the delegation select (`Assign who controls X`) STAYS card-only); chip rows match by row-text (chip textContent leads with the modifier, e.g. `+4`). **EB toolbar-Save gate + label law (live 2026-11-16 fix encounter-modal-rename-target-sticky):** the toolbar Save is NOT clickable-into-a-modal on an empty draft — the empty-draft gate shows a builder-level `.encounter-modal-error` banner OUTSIDE any modal and refuses to open the form; and once a Load row arms the draft the toolbar button relabels **Save→Update**, so blind-clicking it means an overwrite-confirm save. A save-form rig must read the current button label and the gate state BEFORE waiting for `#encounter-name`.
- Card: avatar `img.avatar-image.click()` opens `.mc-overlay`; monster alt=name, PC alt=initials. AVATAR-ASYNC TRAP (live 2026-11-16 improve popup-dismiss-safe-faces): remote bestiary avatar imgs resolve ASYNC after an EB join — a broad `img.avatar-image` census/click right after join can hit the WRONG (already-painted) avatar and silently navigate the tab to a character sheet; wait for `img[alt="<Name>"]` visible before the click. Scope card by `:has(input[aria-label="<Name> current HP"])`, never hasText (matches every target-dropdown). Attack chips = `span.mc-dice-link` (text often ONLY "+N" or "3d6" — locate via `.mc-action` container `<strong>` startsWith match, then inner link). Spell chips `.mc-dice-link-spell` (leading space in textContent — startsWith/nth). Legendary gated chip `.mc-dice-link-legendary`; lair chip `span.mc-dice-link-lair[role=button]`. Locate links by row-text + `page.mouse.click` at FRESH boundingClientRect (scrollIntoView first — stale rects silently miss / hit backdrop closing the card). CHIP ANCHOR (live 2026-11-08 improve monster-card-chip-data-kind): every monster-card dice-link chip carries `data-kind` (attack/save/skill/spell/legendary/lair/ability/initiative/damage/reaction/aura/summon/selfbuff/grantreaction/shapeshift/zone/advisory) — anchor automation by `[data-kind=…]`, never by `.mc-dice-link` index. Census myth fixed: plain-`.mc-dice-link` indices 0–5 on a monster card are the SIX ability-check cells (`div.mc-ability-mod`, handleAbilityCheck), NOT saves; save chips render ONLY when `saving_throws` is authored (Goblin/Bugbear/Hobgoblin author none → 0 save chips) — the EO "0–5 saves" claim was ability cells. EB "Join Encounter" mounts only AFTER a checkbox is ticked (wait_for before ticking always times out); `browser_evaluate` sandbox has no `setTimeout` — bounded waits go in `browser_run_code_unsafe` + `page.waitForTimeout`. **Monster-card DOM markers (live 2026-11-08 EQ; aria-label lane shipped 2026-11-08 improve monster-card-name-a11y):** BESTIARY monster cards have NO `[data-kind="initiative"]` CHIP — initiative is a bare card input (placeholder `Init`); manual initiative edits write cs but log NOTHING, so post-rename/attribution proofs must ride a `[data-kind="attack"]` chip (arm the card's target-select first) or a join initiative roll (live 2026-11-09 improve eb-series-renumber). PC names render `.creature-name span`; BESTIARY-joined monster cards render the name as `.creature-name > .monster-autocomplete input` and the GM-editable lane now carries `aria-label="<Name> name"` — ONE stable census selector for every initiative card type (PC spans, GM editable monster/NPC inputs, visitor read-only spans, polymorph form spans) is `.creature-name span, .creature-name input[aria-label$=" name"]` (bare `.creature-name span` alone still silently misses every GM-tab monster; LAN tabs render those rows as spans). Stable anchors: card = `.creature-card:has(img.avatar-image[alt="Goblin"])` (alt = exact bestiary name); +NPC placeholder row on the GM tab = `.creature-card:has(.creature-name input[aria-label="NPC 1 name"])`; attack chips = `[data-kind=attack]`; OA chip = `.mc-overlay [data-kind="oa"]` whose textContent is BYTE-EXACT "Opportunity Attack on <mover>" (normalized at source 2026-11-10 improve gc-oa-chip-leading-space — the icon-adjacent JSX text node became ONE expression child, icon gap stays CSS-owned via `.mc-dice-link i` margin; `^Opportunity Attack on <mover>$` strict match now passes — spell chips' leading space is unchanged, §67 trim law still stands for THEM); delegation picker = `[data-testid=control-select]` (aria "Assign who controls <Name>"; stamps cs row `controller`, visitor face renders NO control-select, initiative is a PLAIN-TEXT span `[data-testid="initiative-value"]` (e71405e8f — the permanently-disabled input is retired; `—` placeholder when unrolled, value live-adopts GM edits via the combatSummary echo, holds in TV mode too, verified live 2026-11-11; AWAY rows keep the disabled-input face with its rejoin tooltip), target-select ENABLED). The `mc-overlay` card SURVIVES popup drains — arm once, then chip→Done→chip loops without re-opening (a "blocked by mc-overlay intercepts" avatar click means the card is already open); monster HIT popups are 1-stage (Done applies + closes). EB "Join Encounter" PRESERVES away flags + parked-last placement through its full-replace merge.
- Arm target via initiative-card `[data-testid="target-select"]` Playwright `selectOption` BEFORE clicking chips — SHARPENED IN PLACE (live XE 2026-11-16, post-`49a6c4716`): the SAME shared TargetSelect now ALSO mirrors into the `.mc-overlay` stat-modal header (`div.mc-header-arm select[aria-label="<Name> target"]`); both surfaces read/write ONE combatSummary `targetName` and repaint live via SSE echo in BOTH directions (card-arm → open modal pre-synced; visitor-tab modal-arm → GM card follows). Weapon rows are `div.mc-action` (NOT `tr` — a `.mc-overlay tr` scoping finds nothing). CORRECTED IN PLACE (shipped 2026-11-16 improve unarmed-chip-loud-refusal): monster-card attack chips pressed UNARMED no longer roll at all — they refuse loud via `buildNoTargetRefusalPopup(rollNoun:'attack')` in the `[data-testid=popup-overlay]`+Done lane ("No Target — X cannot use <Action> — no target is armed…"), zero roll, zero log rows, zero runtime writes (the old "unarmed rolls with `targetName:null` roll forever, no vs AC, no damage" law is DEAD; the PC-sheet lane keeps its own separate behavior). After re-join arm on the ATTACKER's OWN card select — generic first-empty-select arms wrong combatant → null-target rolls, popup lacks Done, center-click abandons attack (MA-0320). EB melee auto-miss >8 ft — place tokens adjacent (manual mouse.down/move/up; cell px `p.x=gx*40+20` matrixTransform).
- Popups: SHARED-Popup MODAL-FAMILY DISMISSAL LAW (improve popup-dismiss-safe-faces 2026-11-16): `common/popup.jsx` routes Escape AND a pure backdrop click (mousedown+click both on `.popup-overlay`; drag guard: mousedown inside `.popup-modal` → backdrop mouseup never dismisses) to `onClickOrKeyDown` — the same close-only handler every plain consumer's footer Done uses — and a dismissable face pulls focus into its panel on open (`tabIndex=-1` container, Escape works even when the opening chip keeps focus). Consumers gating a pending apply pass `dismissable={false}` → Escape/backdrop fully inert AND no focus steal. FOCUS-PULL RACE (live XF 2026-11-16, FIXED 2026-11-16 fix run): the once-at-mount pull used to LOSE to `MonsterNameAutocomplete`'s refocus-on-remount on roll-RESULT faces opened over the stat modal (the roll commit re-keys the card AFTER the popup mounts → activeElement snapped back to `INPUT.monster-autocomplete-input`, trusted Escape INERT on that face). Now the dismissable Popup runs a `document` `focusin` RETENTION trap: focus landing outside the whole popup-overlay layer is reclaimed into the panel (stacked popups never ping-pong; guarded `dismissable={false}` faces install no listener and move no focus — HIT-staged face keeps activeElement on the autocomplete by design). Pinned by `popup.focus-retention.test.jsx` (jsdom `focus()` dispatches real `focusin`); roll-RESULT Escape re-lived exact. HIT popup "Done" = `button.dice-roll-reroll-btn` (has child `<i>` — leaf-text search MISSES it; Done applies damage; the STAGED hit face is Done-only — `AttackResultPopup` threads `dismissable={!showsOwnDone}` so the hit-popup-backdrop-void-guard covers Escape too, pinned by popup.test.jsx guarded faces + AttackResultPopup.staged-cancel; reroll loops must click Done which APPLIES damage — top up victim HP via scoped PATCH between rolls). TWO-STAGE — CORRECTED IN PLACE (RZ 2026-11-18): the post-9-commit sheet staged-HIT lane is SINGLE-STEP for the COMMIT — one Done lands damage and any rider pause (Charge chooser/STR prompt) mounts an `.sp-overlay` above the sheet; a parked rider chain intercepts the next row click until resolved; the old "repeat Done ≤6 to walk a stage-2 damage popup before the commit" shape did NOT reproduce for a weapon row (a cosmetic APPLIED-damage result popup can mount AFTER the commit per MA 2026-11-18 — it never gates damage; retiring it cleanly is a backlog item); MISS popups close with one root click (live 2026-11-06); plain-damage MISS popup (no reroll stage) renders "Done" as `button.popup-close-btn` instead (KB chip, live 2026-11-05) — probe both. Buttonless second-stage `popup-overlay` survives and intercepts the next chip click — flush via its own click `[data-testid="popup-overlay"] el.click()`. Blocked Playwright clicks RETRY and can leave several stacked overlays; one click per pass may re-surface the next — flush loop: click root AND all descendants of every `[data-testid="popup-overlay"]` until the query is empty (live 2026-11-06). NEVER `element.remove()` any overlay — corrupts React until reload. `offsetParent===null` is FALSE for fixed overlays — poll computedStyle/getBoundingClientRect + count log deltas. Results `.sp-overlay` can linger ~2min intercepting — poll unfiltered, own Close. AoE-stack law (live 2026-11-11 run IE): after a monster AoE confirm the picker `.sp-overlay` STAYS MOUNTED with its button disabled (`<action> (N)(dis)`) and the real save prompt stacks BEHIND it — a `.sp-modal`/`.sp-overlay` FIRST-MATCH probe reads the dead picker (false "no Roll Save leaf" twice; every click before the throw already LANDED — judge by log delta); drain loops must enumerate ALL `.sp-overlay`s per pass and match the leaf button per overlay, and the save result reuses the same `.sp-modal` (Roll Save → Done stage in-place).
- Feature rows with a queued save can emit TWO channels at once (Stunning Strike live 2026-11-06): an `automation_info` popup ("Target X must make a CON saving throw (DC N). / Done") AND the `.sp-overlay` prompt — close the popup's Done to reach Roll Save/Dismiss. Save prompts `.sp-modal` "Saving Throw Required (n of N)": Roll Save / Next Save / Done; queues behind result popups; NPC saves often AUTO-ROLL inline (no prompt). One roll per prompt; success rigs need high-stat victims, ≤3 loops. STABLE ANCHOR (shipped 2026-11-11 improve active-save-prompt-testid): the ACTIVE prompt's `.sp-modal` carries `data-testid="active-save-prompt"` — single-active (queue front only), both stages (Roll Save → Done) render through the SAME element, never present on picker/results/evasion overlays, zero matches with no prompt mounted; `[data-testid=active-save-prompt]` RETIRES the `.sp-modal` first-match archaeology and per-overlay leaf enumeration for the prompt stage. A throw in probe code still queues the cast's prompt — judge casts by log delta. Leading-Evasion chooser re-stacks above the prompt ONCE PER SUBSEQUENT SAVE (not just the first) — drain loops must re-check `.sp-overlay--evasion` every iteration (live 2026-11-05). AoE picker confirm now DISABLES at dispatch (SaveAttackAoeModal `dispatched` latch, 2026-11-05) — the CY-era "live confirm until Results closes" double-cast hazard window is closed on the picker lane.
- AoE monster-card SAVE chip picker (live 2026-11-10 PFEG-fix run): pressing a radius-row save chip (Performer Maestro Beguiling Song) opens a `.sp-overlay` picker layer ("<N>-ft Radius … Select creatures", creature checkboxes as `<label>Name(<pct>% HP)`, confirm button labeled `<action> (n)` when ≥1 ticked, decline = `Skip`); after confirm the picker layer STAYS STACKED BELOW the target's Roll Save prompt — `document.querySelector('.sp-overlay')` then grabs the stale picker, so prompt waiters/drain loops must iterate ALL `.sp-overlay` nodes, and the picker's disabled confirm must never be re-clicked. STACK DEPTH (live 2026-11-11 improve active-save-prompt-testid): after Done a third overlay `"<action> — Results"` `.sp-overlay` LINGERS and silently intercepts the next chip click — drain loops must also press its `Close` leaf before the next chip; and Maestro's SECOND save chip (`save|DC 15 Wisdom`) re-opens the SAME AoE picker, so a single-target sanity leg needs a different monster (Succubus Charm). PFEG multi-condition ward live rig (canonical faces, live 2026-11-10; seed-corrected in place run HD): seed target bucket PRE-select via full-store POST `{activeBuffs:[{name:'Protection from Evil and Good', effect:'protection_from_evil_and_good'} (BOTH keys REQUIRED — the gate `isProtectionFromEvilAndGoodActive` reads `b.name===SPELL_NAME && b.effect===…`, a row without `name` leaves the ward honestly INERT and all conditions "legitimately" land — false-bug trap), {effect:'warding_bond', saveBonus:-19 (guaranteed-fail dial, folds ALL saves; -30 = auto-fail face incl. nat 20)}], protectionFromEvilAndGoodWardedTypes:[Aberration,Celestial,Elemental,Fey,Fiend,Undead]}`; multi-condition Fiend row = **Lamia Corrupting Touch** (WIS DC 13; live prompt carries saveConditions `['charmed','cursed','poisoned']` — the row's "cursed for 1 hour" clause extracts as its own condition) → post-fix activeConditions `['cursed','poisoned']` + ONE `automation blocked` per warded condition + `condition applied` for the survivors; single-condition warded face = Succubus Charm (Fiend, `save:DC 15 Charisma` chip); non-warded humanoid face = Performer Maestro (Humanoid — charmed lands WITH the ward active). CAVEAT (live 2026-11-11 run IE): the Maestro carrier is Beguiling Song (`save` chip `3d10 + 4`, opens the AoE picker — tick one label, confirm; the dispatched picker stays mounted disabled BEHIND the save prompt) and its charmed face: the IE-era self-strip (`condition removed … took damage (Friends)` — the row's own save damage stripping the just-granted Charmed, filed as `bug-beguiling-song-own-damage-strips-its-charm`) was FIXED 2026-11-11 (58f6e86c3, now in resolved/; LIVE-VERIFIED 3/3 run IF 2026-11-11: the humanoid Charmed LANDS and persists — conds `['charmed']` + badge + log order hp_change→roll→condition — a LATER separate damage source still strips it, and the strip purges the `activeConditionMeta` row in the merged write, no orphan); the GATE face is still best judged by the absence of the `automation blocked` row. Cheapest IF rig for the persistence legs: pre-select full-store POST `{activeBuffs:[{effect:'warding_bond',saveBonus:-30}], activeConditions:[], currentHitPoints:143, hitPoints:143}` (HP keys REQUIRED or the damage lane console.errors `currentHitPoints not found` with zero hp_change) + EB-join Performer Maestro & Goblin + the §"popup families" Done-gate law below. Note: the hag-attribution in rules prose is wrong for THIS data — "Charmed and Poisoned" lives on the Lamia (monsters.json:40929), Green Hag carries no such row. **Lion MEASURED-grid exempt face (the GC-owed leg, run HD CLOSED live):** active indoor map + tokens via `placedItems` `{type:'npc',name:'Goblin',gridX,gridY}` for the attacker and `players[]` for PC bearers/targets; a target carrying its own `optionName:'Lion'` stamp stays `mode:"normal"`/1 die/zero aura rows even with tokens placed (pre-loop exemption runs BEFORE measurement); the adjacent un-stamped control target takes the dis AND the `lion_aura_disadvantage` row switches copy to MEASURED prose "…within 5 feet of raging ElderPaladin (Power of the Wilds — Lion)." — the gridless "proximity unmeasured (gridless) — GM-enforced" tail is ABSENT on this face (copy discriminator for measured vs lenient). EB-joined monsters do NOT auto-token: the 3D/map header "0 npcs" with a joined monster on the board is expected (token = separate placedItems entry).
- Initiative "Next →" walks the CREATURES-ARRAY — the mount merge RE-SORTS the array it writes (`initiativeService.js:264` → `sortCreaturesForBoard`: present-first, initiative DESC, blank=−1, ties name-asc), so boards walk initiative-desc regardless of the POSTed order. DOM-vs-walker divergence CLOSED 2026-11-09 (improve board-order-walk-parity): the old FW fingerprint (settled tab adopting a full-store cs POST rendered POST order in the DOM while mirrors walked DESC) was two ingress seams writing two orders — NOW `handleCombatSummaryChange` (sseHandlers.js) also runs `sortCreaturesForBoard` at adoption, so EVERY ingress writes board-sorted order and the card row renders exactly the walk sequence (pointer sweeps left-to-right incl. wrap; pinned by `sseHandlers.board-order.test.js` + `Initiative.board-order.test.jsx`). Seed init values to place rows adjacent in DESC order when walk sequence matters — POST order controls neither display nor walk anymore — top-level `activeCreatureName` is truth (cs mirror lags ~10s) — poll between every click. EB Join mid-round advances activeCreatureName onto the board head (never onto an AWAY row — join re-anchors away-active off the board, live EQ). "Clear" (law RE-STATED 2026-11-18 improve placeholder-foes-victory-bank): removes EVERY monster row including bare `+NPC` placeholders (the old keep-and-re-seed law is DEAD — see §9), resets initiative/round, and DROPS split-party away flags (live EQ 2026-11-08 for the away-flag leg). Banner law: bare placeholders are NOT foes and never hide the fresh victory banner — only real (non-placeholder) `type:'npc'` rows do. `getCurrentCombatRound` MUST thread campaignName (omitted → round pinned 1). Reaching a specific ACTIVE creature via "Next →": budget up to N presses and poll `combatSummary.activeCreatureName` between every press (GB 2026-11-10: an EB board walk stepped Wolf→AasimarTest past the Goblin row once — never assume one press = one expected row). SHARPENED (live 2026-11-19 improve consolidate-turn-start-gate-key): a blank-initiative 13-PC board WALKS FINE via native `Next →` clicks — the "deadlock" law applies to card-commit UX, not the array-order walker (`getNextCreatureName` walks insertion order; first Next from active:None lands idx0). Scoped PATCH `combatSummary {round:N+1}` echo-updates a mounted GM tab's UI round within ~1s — the clean gate re-arm rig, no UI surgery. SINGLE-HOME LAW post-sweep: the turn-start gate persists ONLY in `__initiative__` (the loose top-level `lastAppliedTurnStartCreature` key's writer is DELETED, zero readers ever); the `combatSummary.lastAppliedTurnStartCreature` mirror byte can be ABSENT on disk while the bucket holds the gate (later cs POSTs re-bake without it) — bucket-only hydration is the steady state, judge walk truth from `__initiative__` alone.
- Recipe (live 2026-11-09 fix run): ride a Thief's-Reflexes `(Second Turn)` row WITHOUT the EF Thief disk-swap — POST full-store `combatSummary` `[holder, {name:'<H> (Second Turn)', secondTurn:true, holderName:'<H>'}]` before the Initiative mount; the remount merge law keeps the synthetic twin while `holderName` is a campaign character. Sharpened (2026-11-09 improve st-rejoin-title): adding `away:true` to the twin row in that seed renders a REJOIN-state ST card on first mount (greyed, Rejoin toggle showing) — no away-click round-trip to ride rejoin-copy lanes; the mount merge re-adds the other campaign characters around the seed, and a live away→present toggle on a normal PC renders the non-ST rejoin face in the same pass. ST-row lane authority (live 2026-11-09 FU, post-c5883e4a4): EffectAdder ADD opens (all 3 tabs) remap to `holderName` — modal titles the holder, writes+audits land on the holder, synthetic bucket stays GET-absent; display resolves the holder's HP/conditions AND — since 2026-11-09 improve st-card-badge-parity — te + concentration badges too (`CreatureCard.jsx` `effectSubjectName = isSecondTurnEntry && holderName ? holderName : name` feeds the te filter, ConditionEffectBadges `creatureName`, and the concentration badge/×; × on the ST card acts on and audits the HOLDER, both cards show the same badges). FIX-VERIFIED 2026-11-09 FV (fixes 583d078ff + 930393775 both PASS: × on the ST card clears the HOLDER's condition + audits the holder; allies editor titles the holder, EXCLUDES second-turn rows from candidates, writes holder `selectedAllies` + holder audit — phantom bucket gained zero keys; concentration badge on the ST card also rides the holder correctly — click rolled +11 with holder CON bonus + aura and audited `EvasiveFighter`). CLICK LANE FIXED 2026-11-09 (d55d4160a, `bug-secondturn-badge-click-synthetic-name`): the remap lives at the `createRollConditionSaveHandler.js` `handleRollConditionSave` chokepoint (holder row feeds roll/remove/audit, mirroring the × precedent — the prop still passes `creature.name`), pinned by `createRollConditionSaveHandler.second-turn.test.js`; face proof: ST-click +11 (holder CON + aura), holder audit, holder cleared, synthetic bucket GET-absent; holder-card click byte-unchanged. Delegation control-select is absent on ST cards and target lists exclude them (guards held). Modal-header provenance hint (2026-11-09 improve st-card-modal-name-mismatch): twin-opened modals append display-only " (second turn)" after the holder name — EffectAdder header via `titleSuffix` from the `openedFromSecondTurn` flag stamped at open (rides the open-target state — LOCAL `React.useState` since the 2026-11-19 demotion, unit-pinned by `Initiative.effectAdder-second-turn.test.jsx`), allies editor via local `fromSecondTurn` state; holder/normal opens are byte-identical, and a twin without `holderName` gets NO suffix (its raw name already carries the marker).
- **Badge click lane truths (live 2026-11-09 FV; dc-gate CLOSED dad80f67b):** a condition added with the DC input BLANK used to get `dc` coerced to **0** by `buildRuntimeConditions` (displayCreatureUtils.js:16) with `ConditionBadgeRow` wiring the click ungated, so clicking ALWAYS printed “SAVE SUCCESSFUL (DC 0)” and removed the condition (players too — `canRoll = player||localhost`) — a silent rules bypass, now fixed per owner ruling “gate the click — no DC, no roll”: blank-DC badges render as inert `span.creature-badge` (description tooltip only, zero popup/log/change-data on click, pinned by `CreatureCard.noDcBadgeClick.test.jsx`); removal is via × or consumer lanes only. The old dc=0 auto-success face is NO LONGER a deterministic-SUCCESS rig — for guaranteed save-success test faces use the §7 ±19 `warding_bond` dial with a real DC (DC-bearing badges remain `button` and roll honestly, incl. the ST holder-remap).
- **TV up-next ST-seam laws (live 2026-11-09 FV, useTvPresentation.js:114-135):** selector family `.tv-next-up` + `.tv-next-up-label` + `.tv-next-chip`; with TWO `(Second Turn)` twins adjacent in display order (e.g. inits 10 & 11) and a normal active row before them, the strip skips BOTH consecutive ineligible rows and yields the 4 next eligible creatures; with an ST row ACTIVE, the strip never lists it or its twin (eligible-list excludes all secondTurn, ST-anchor cap min(4,len) behaves identically). Curl-POSTing `combatSummary` (full store, `{value}`) onto a SETTLED mounted Initiative tab is adopted live (echo law; the appended twin appeared without remount) — and adoption is a REPLACEMENT: rows the POST omits are evicted from the live board too (a 2-row twin-seed dropped an unlisted ElderPaladin card without remount; a fresh view-navigation re-merges all campaign characters back), so seed via full-store POST only when you intend the whole board. `activeCreatureName` is a top-level key POSTable as `{value:'<Name>'}` and drives the TV anchor immediately.
- **Enforcement/turn-lock ride (live 2026-11-09 FV2; ST-seam hole CLOSED c076dfdf1):** arm with `POST /:c/enforcement {value:{mode:'on',turnLock:true}}` — the route merges and GETs back the FULL default blob (all L5 flags present, false); gate reads `mode==='on'` AND the flag. Denial witnesses: popup `<reason> Done` + top-level log row `{type:'enforcement_denied',creature,bucket,source,reason}` + ZERO roll rows (cleanest no-side-effect refusal probe). `activeCreatureName` truth for the gate = the combatSummary mirror's field (assertCanAct `readActiveCreature` — renamed from `readActiveCreatureName` by the fix; it resolves a `secondTurn:true` row's seam once and exposes `{name, holderName}`), so flip the acting turn by re-POSTing the WHOLE cs `{value:{…,activeCreatureName:'<other>'}}` — a settled sheet tab adopts it live, no reload (see the full-store REPLACEMENT law — omitting rows evicts them). Face-switch rig for the four ST-lock faces (holder-under-twin allow / stranger-under-twin deny / holder-normal / stranger-normal) is pure full-store re-POST, no reloads; the denial popup drains with the §64 root+descendants `[data-testid=popup-overlay]` click loop. Re-bake pitfall (live GC 2026-11-10): a SHEET mount after your flip re-bakes `activeCreatureName` back to the tab's previously-selected character via its §5 full-store snapshot — re-POST + GET-confirm the flip after EVERY sheet open before trusting which name the denial copy prints. Post-fix law: holder AND `companionOf=holder` rows act while their twin is active (action + bonus buckets); every other creature still denies with copy truthfully naming the active twin row. Sheet attack roll face = first `.attacks div.clickable` whose text starts with `+` (hit chip; `.base-action-info` spans are informational text, clicks there are inert).
- Away→Rejoin initiative law (updated in place 2026-11-09 improve st-rejoin-initiative-anchor): NON-second-turn player rows still re-roll to the end-of-round slot on every rejoin (PC drift like `''→3` remains app law — never trust a row's initiative across an away round-trip); second-turn twins instead RE-ANCHOR to the holder's CURRENT initiative − 10 (`creaturePresence.js` `secondTurnAnchorInitiative`, grant-law arithmetic; holder absent/blank ⇒ end-of-round fallback). Audit copy distinguishes them (`re-anchored to <holder>'s second-turn slot (initiative N)` vs `(end of the round)`). MOCK CAVEAT: tests factory-mocking `thiefsReflexesService` that exercise a rejoin must spread `await importOriginal()` (or supply `isSecondTurnEntry`) — `creaturePresence` now imports the predicate. SHIPPED-COPY LIVE-VERIFIED (FW 2026-11-09): the ST card's away→Rejoin TOOLTIP now reads "Rejoin <ST> to the encounter (initiative re-anchors to <holder>'s second-turn slot)" (3f2acaa9f — the FV-era stale "(end of the round)" copy is gone); the twin-opened allies modal titles "Allies of <holder> (second turn)" (219985867) with the ST row excluded from candidates.
- **Economy-on ST matrix in ONE load (verified 2026-11-09 fix-verify 4dc24883b + full-matrix live re-ride FX):** clear → `POST /:c/enforcement {mode:'on',turnLock:true,actionEconomy:true}` → select campaign → Initiative settle (~4s, 13-PC merge) → full-store cs re-POST with holder init 20 / twin init 19 / next init 15 (DESC order so walk adjacency is holder→twin→next) → top-level `POST /activeCreatureName {value:'<holder>'}`; denial/roll popups drain with the §64 root+descendants loop **plus the .sp-overlay rider stack (§255 FX face) — a popup-only drain strands stage-2 and FALSE-DIAGNOSES "damage dropped"**. FX closed every face live: spent-holder pre-seam DENIES (control); holder→twin seam RE-ARMS (actionsUsed 1→0, movement 30→0 from a REAL drag charge, hasMoved false) and spent-holder then ROLLS with the full attack→rider→damage→hp_change chain honest (crit 1d10*2+3+6 HWM = −29, non-crit −11), zero `enforcement_denied`; re-arm ≠ bypass (post-seam second press denies honestly — 2nd row); twin→X seam CLOSES the holder (turnActive:false, spent face) and opens next fresh; Prev→twin rewind re-opens the HOLDER key with NO refund and zero synthetic keys; `movementBudget` ON: token drag while twin-active charges `movementUsedFeet` on the HOLDER key, over-budget drag CLAMPS at the budget edge AND fires the `.map-toast` clamp notice (toast-copy law updated in-place 2026-11-09 improve clamp-toast-copy, live-verified: partial clamp = "X moved only N ft of M requested."; this move charged the ENTIRE per-turn budget = "X's movement used up at S ft." (total-at-cap, S = speedFeet); the old ambiguous "snapped back to N ft of movement" frame is RETIRED — a bare toast number never reads as remaining; log rows unchanged by that improve) (corrected in-place 2026-11-09 improve run: the FX "no toast on the clamp leg" claim was a probe artifact — `usePlayerDragging.js:339` toasts every `budget.snapped` drop, shipped 37dc2c313; the toast dies at 2500 ms so arm a `MutationObserver` on `.map-toast` BEFORE the drag, never query after mouse.up; SHARPENED FY 2026-11-09: a SECOND enforcement advisory REPLACES the mounted sticky toast's textContent IN PLACE — addedNodes never fires again, so observe childList AND live-query `.map-toast` after each leg; a full-block drag (0 ft remaining) honestly toasts "X cannot move — no movement left this turn." (toast copy shipped a3d5d9947 — diverges from the log row BY DESIGN: the log keeps "(0 ft this move)" bytes on every leg) + logs `enforcement_denied` bucket:movement "Out of movement — snapped to the remaining budget (0 ft this move)."); repeat-save te on the holder prompts exactly ONCE at the holder seam ("save-repeat", c:te.source, fail keeps te+cond byte) and ZERO at the twin seam (skips by design; producer has no round latch — fires every genuine turn-end seam). - **Drag-rig laws (GA 2026-11-10):** (1) cell-stepped drags BILL PER LANDING — N single-cell down/up gestures = N charged moves (5 ft each, and OA triggers evaluate start-vs-end per event: a 1-cell APPROACH provokes nothing, the NEXT departure provokes); one burst gesture = one move; (2) SAME-CELL token overlap is DETERMINISTIC since 7cce4cb73 (live-verified GB 2026-11-10): every token sharing a cell fans diagonally top-left by 10px×steps (paint order players-array → monster items → npc items; stack TOP stays pinned at the exact cell centre, buried shift −1/−2 steps, singles render byte-unchanged); the pointer grabs by paint order at the visible pixel — the token you SEE under the cursor is the token that MOVES (cell centre = stack top forever; buried tokens grab on their visible crescent, which can sit just OUTSIDE the cell border; stacked creature tokens swap the 40×40 rect hit for a circle matching the art; a drag started off-centre preserves the grab offset, so the landing cell = pointer-cell + round(offset/50%-of-cell) — a −22px grab landed +1/+1 off); the old GA mover-alternation trap is CLOSED — two consecutive cell-center drags off an occupied cell moved the SAME token each time; (3) the map EDGE clamps silently BEFORE the movement budget does — a "clamp at speed" face needs ≥ speed/5 spare cells along the drag axis (a 50-ft request upward from y=3 "clamped" at the edge after 15 ft, zero toast, log stays clean); (4) an NPC placedItems-token drag PROVOKES PC defenders honestly (markers `mover:<Monster>`, reactions charged, joined toast — no defector flip needed, npc movers are always hostile) but writes NO turnState entry — owner-ratified DESIGN LAW "GM moves are free by design" (1982383ab, header comment in `useItemDragging.js`; the movementBudget clamp is player-token-only by design — do NOT add a charge lane without an owner ruling); non-player rows' movement badges carry the advisory tooltip `GM-driven moves are not charged against this budget — …` (verified verbatim on card strips + the header strip when the ACTIVE row is non-player; player rows keep `<N> ft of movement remaining this turn`; a11y lanes shipped 2026-11-10 improve gm-move-advisory-a11y — every GM movement badge mirrors the full advisory into `aria-label` ("<used/ft> — <advisory>") and the CARD face also renders a visible `(GM-driven)` span `.turn-economy-gm-hint` (header stays compact, hint omitted), player faces keep `aria-label` absent + no hint byte-unchanged; `CreatureBadge` gained the optional `ariaLabel` prop (undefined = attribute omitted, DOM byte-identical for every consumer); the clamp lane resolves speed from the CHAR SHEET, so a mid-session feat grant needs a tab reload before the new speed clamps — GB rode a stale tab honestly clamping at 30); (5) a defector flip (`selectedAllies:[self]`) on the MOVER makes every PC defender react to its moves; the mover itself never appears as a DEFENDER key in `pendingOAs` for its own move (no self-marker witnessed GA). **Turn-economy (turnState) ride + ST seam (live FW 2026-11-09):** enforcement arm for economy = `POST /:c/enforcement {value:{mode:'on',turnLock:true,actionEconomy:true}}`. `turnState` surfaces as a TOP-LEVEL change-data key (GET cd.turnState; cd.campaign is null) and accepts full-store `POST /:c/turnState {value:{…}}` (REPLACES the bucket — the cheapest phantom-evict + companionOf stamp: `AasimarTest:{companionOf:'EvasiveFighter', …}` passed the turn-lock while the twin was active, closing the companion face ER/FV deferred). Sheet attacks CHARGE on allow (before popup resolution), and the gate never reads the `turnActive` flag — denials come only from turnLock + bucket exhaustion ("No action left this turn." popup + `enforcement_denied` row). FIXED 2026-11-09 (4dc24883b, `bug-secondturn-seam-starves-holder-economy-phantom-turnstate` in `resolved/`): `applyTurnStateTransition` + `markRewoundTurnActive` (navigationHandlers.js) now resolve the twin via `economyNameFor(summary,name)` (mirrors the c076dfdf1 gate law) — the HOLDER is re-armed at the holder→twin seam (fresh action/bonus budget even with actionEconomy ON, reactions preserved), the holder CLOSES at twin→X, and Prev-rewind-into-twin opens the HOLDER without refund; `expireStaleEffects` is `!isSecondTurnEntry`-guarded in BOTH navigation handlers AND the SSE echo lane (see §16 echo-producer pitfall). Post-fix twin walk leaves ZERO `*Second Turn*` keys anywhere in change-data; pinned by `navigationHandlers.second-turn.test.js`. Still never ridden: movementBudget token-drag under twin-active (`movementUsedFeet` zeroes correctly at the seam, the drag lane itself unridden). Face-switch law unchanged: holder-pass/stranger-deny/companion-pass all switchable by full-store cs re-POST + top-level `activeCreatureName` POST — but the POSTed cs creatures adoption does NOT move a settled tab's turn pointer (that follows the top-level `activeCreatureName` POST), and a FRESH mount merge WIPES the seeded `activeCreatureName` and re-highlights from per-client localStorage `activeCreatureName`. FIXED 2026-11-09 (improve mount-merge-pointer-fidelity): `mergeCombatSummaryWithCharacters` now PRESERVES cs `activeCreatureName` when the named row survives the merge (ST twin counts via its own name), and Initiative mount resolution is `merged.activeCreatureName → getActiveCreatureName() → creatures[0] → null` — a seeded board's pointer survives select→mount→debounce-flush end-to-end; dangling pointers drop and the per-client fallback wins. Correction to the same claim: the fallback is NOT localStorage — `getActiveCreatureName()` reads the in-memory `cachedCombatSummaries` Map in `services/encounters/combatData.js` (per-client, campaign-keyed); no localStorage read for `activeCreatureName` exists anywhere.
- **Initiative mount-test harness recipe (live 2026-11-09 improve mount-merge-pointer-fidelity):** an Initiative mount test can use the REAL `initiativeService` (do NOT mock it) — jsdom imports clean given the standard zero-creature harness mock block, so mount tests exercise the merge-eviction legs for free (see `Initiative.mount-active-creature.test.jsx`). - `saveResult-<Target>` change-data key = machine truth `{saveBonus,rawRolls,mode,success}`; mode is the ONLY adv/dis truth; crit nat20 doubles DICE only (flat mod not doubled; secondary shows `secondaryTotal=2×sum`). §82 ledger law RESTORED EVERYWHERE (fix 2026-11-10, `bug-monster-attacks-log-phantom-second-die` resolved): every shared-lane roll row (`useLoggedDiceRollAttack.js buildRollLogEntry`) satisfies `mode==='normal' ? rolls.length===1 : rolls.length===2` — the pre-fix phantom second die hit BOTH monster-card AND PC-sheet presses (GB's "sheet lane logs one die" contrast was stale), plus the EB-join initiative row (`encounterToInitiative.js`, now single-die); `rolls.length` is again a valid adv/dis discriminator. Popup payload intentionally KEEPS the 2-die pool on normal (GM adv/dis toggle + roll-time Lucky/Feats-of-Chaos folds consume it). Genuine monster-lane adv/dis faces proven live: restraint on the ATTACKER's CS ROW (`conditions:[{key:'restrained','name':'Restrained'}]` via scoped PATCH combatSummary) ⇒ `[a,b] disadvantage` keep-low + "Disadv (conditions)" chip; same on the TARGET's cs row ⇒ `[a,b] advantage` keep-high + "Adv (conditions)" chip (the cs-row feed is `extractConditionKeys` reading `creature.conditions` OBJECTS, not the `activeConditions` string list). OA chip press with a curl-seeded `pendingOAs` marker + clean mover ⇒ `rolls:[n] mode:"normal"`, marker drains to absent. Log truth: `roll.total` = raw d20 (bonus separate); popup total = +bonus; breath logs' `rolls` can diverge from applied `total` — decisive: `total==finalDamage==|hp_change|` vs authored max. `/half/i` scans false-positive on names; "/hit/i" on "(+N to hit)" — match ✓/✗ glyphs. 0-HP victim = death-clamp `finalDamage:0` — never use for half-damage proofs.
- **Forced crit/fumble chip-commit rig (live BBB 2026-11-20; PRIMARY lane SHIPPED 2026-11-20 improve dev-test-roll-seam):** the app now carries its own DEV-only seam in `src/services/dice/diceRoller.js` — `window.__forceNextD20(n)` (integer 1..20 arms ONE-SHOT, `null` disarms, bad values loudly console.error-rejected and leave it unarmed) or the `?testRoll=<n>` URL param (arms the same one-shot at module load, so a fresh dev page needs zero injection); the next `rollD20()` returns the forced face verbatim, consumes ZERO Math.random draws (no `guid()`/other-consumer bleed), auto-disarms (the follow-up commit must roll natural — always prove it), and rides ONLY the d20 primitive (damage dice stay honest). `import.meta.env.DEV`-gated: the production bundle is grep-zero for `__forceNextD20`/`testRoll` (dead-code eliminated) and `typeof window.__forceNextD20 === 'undefined'` on :80 — prove that leg only after `npm run build`. Lived faces: `__forceNextD20(20)` → staged popup `d20 20 … CRITICAL HIT! — DAMAGE DICE DOUBLED ✓ HIT (24 vs AC 12)`; `?testRoll=1` reload → `d20 1 … CRITICAL MISS! ✗ MISS (5 vs AC 12)` + attack-row-only wire. LEGACY fallback for pinning NON-d20 dice (damage pools etc., where the seam does not reach): page-side value-slot Math.random wrapper (`value: () => window.__randomOverrideOn ? window.__randomVal : orig()`, `Object.defineProperty` configurable); restore the pin before judging any later roll. QUICK-SPAWN PICKER TRAP (lived improve 2026-11-20): `.qms-search` matches name OR type OR subtype (`monsterBestiarySearch.js:41-43`) and rows sort name-asc, so querying `Goblin` lists Bugbear/Bugbear Chief/… BEFORE the real `Goblin` — click the `.qms-row` whose `.qms-row-name` textContent is EXACTLY the target, never `first()`. Wire faces: crit trio = attack row (`isCrit:true`, message ends `— Critical Hit!`) + damage row (`formula "1d6*2+2 (6)"`, total = die×2+mod) + hp_change row; fumble = attack row ONLY (`isNatural1:true`, `hit:false`, message ends `— Critical Miss!`, zero damage/hp rows by design). Play-spy names EXACTLY ONE cue per commit (`crit.wav` / `fumble.wav` — damage rows answer null, audioCues.test:143). Log-view badges: rows carry classes `log-nat20`/`log-nat1` with tokens `Critical Hit!`+`NAT 20` / `Critical Miss!`+`FUMBLE` (badge probe must target `.log-entry` innerText, not the content-area slice — AAA's MISS/HIT-token census shape re-lived). KNOWN RENDER DEFECT filed and FIXED 2026-11-20 (crit damage row doubled the dice — `(6) (6)` — from baked-roll formula + inline rolls span, bug-log-crit-damage-row-duplicate-dice → resolved): the renderer (`RollDiceValues`, LogRollEntry.jsx) now suppresses the inline `.log-dice-values-inline` span when the stored formula already ends with that exact `(<rolls>)` annotation; baked formula wire shape unchanged (still canonical), pinned by the crit pin in `LogRoll.test.jsx`. Dice-display law for future log-lane work: dice display belongs to the RENDERER; baked-roll crit formulas stay as stored bytes — the exact-tail guard is the pinned owner.
- **Crit-rig probe casing + draw-order pitfalls (live 2026-11-20 fix log-crit-damage-row-duplicate-dice; SHARPENED live CCC 2026-11-20):** the VERDICT line is UPPERCASED via CSS `text-transform` — `innerText` probes must match `CRITICAL HIT!`, not `Critical Hit!` (a casing miss reads as a popup wait timeout, not a missing popup) — but the authored `d20 <n>` token stays LOWERCASE in innerText (it is not transform-styled; CCC burned one popup-wait matching `/D20\s+20/`): probe the verdict uppercased, the die token lowercase (or `/i`); the value-slot Math.random pin applies to EVERY draw in the window, so a pinned 0.5 d20 can surface as `d20 11` (a consumer draws before the d20) — match ✓/✗ HIT/MISS tokens, not exact die values. SEAM RE-LIVE (CCC): every face of the `__forceNextD20`/`?testRoll` seam re-derived independently — rejection table (0/21/-3/1.5/'20'/NaN/undefined → `false` + one verbatim `[diceRoller] forced-d20 seam rejected…` console.error each, stays unarmed), null-disarm true, arm 20 → real Scimitar commit CRIT trio with honest damage dice (die 2 → `1d6*2+2 (2)` total 6), follow-up commit natural (auto-disarm), `?testRoll=1` reload → CRITICAL MISS attack-row-only wire; prod leg: after `npm run build`, `grep` on `dist/assets/*.js` is zero for both symbols AND `typeof window.__forceNextD20 === 'undefined'` on `http://localhost/?testRoll=20`.
- **Sandbox-map range-band rig + monster-chip range law (live CCC 2026-11-20):** range/cover measurement on the monster-card chip lane is MAP-TOKEN-based: `computeMapRangeState` (MonsterCardModal.jsx ~1093) needs the ATTACKER as a `placedItems` token with the monster's exact board name AND the TARGET as a `players[]` token (else `resolveTargetGridPos` falls to nearest-npc) — missing either → LENIENT no-band roll (line 1098 early-return). Quickest rig: `POST /api/campaigns/<c>/maps` with `{name, gridSize, players:[{id,name,gridX,gridY}], placedItems:[{id,type:'npc',name,gridX,gridY,visible:true}]}` (GM curl) + `PUT …/activate`, then RELOAD+re-select the tab to arm the `mapName` prop chain (activation SSE arms too, but reload proves it); distance = EUCLIDEAN cells × 5 ft (`FEET_PER_CELL 5`, `MELEE_RANGE_FT 8`). Lived faces: ≤normal single-die normal; >normal ≤2× → popup chip `Disadv (Beyond normal range (35 ft > 30 ft))` + two-dice keep-low + roll-row `mode:'disadvantage', rangeReason:'…'`; >2× → `✗ AUTO-MISS (Out of range (65 ft > 60 ft))` + row `isAutoMiss:true` (the d20 still rolls and stamps, e.g. a nat-1 surfaces `Critical Miss!` alongside AUTO-MISS). RANGE-INPUT law CORRECTED IN PLACE and SHIPPED (`fix(monster-ranged-attack-wrong-range-bands)` c70d5cf65, re-lived 2026-11-20): the 30/60 faces above were the BUG, now gone — `resolveAttackRange` (moved to `MonsterCardHelpers.js`, exported) derives structured reach → structured range → authored PROSE (`/breach|brange\s+N[/M] ft\./i` → RAW `"N/M ft."` token) → **null = LENIENT, never an invented band** (42 prose-only bestiary actions incl. Goblin Shortbow 80/320, Hobgoblin Longbow 150/600); `rangePairToFeet` parses dual notation (79 authored fields, unit-less "30/120" too) to the TRUE `{normal, long}` and `computeRangeEffect` adjudicates against the AUTHORED long figure (single values keep the RAW ×2; `rangeMultiplier` scales both bands; `rangeToFeet` scalars answer the NORMAL figure — the sheet-weapon display lane included). Post-fix lived faces: 35/65 ft Shortbow single-die `mode:'normal'` `rangeReason:null`; 170 ft Longbow `Disadv (Beyond normal range (170 ft > 150 ft))` keep-low, `isAutoMiss:false`. LONG-BAND DISCRIMINATOR RECIPE: a gridSize-30 sandbox tops out ~212 ft diagonal and can NEVER reach an authored long band — author a **gridSize-100** map (create honors it, clamp ≥5≤100) for ~665 ft across: Longbow `AUTO-MISS (Out of range (665 ft > 600 ft))` proves the real 600 figure (the retired ×2 model would print 300). BY-DESIGN LENIENT faces: two no-structured-range "ranged" actions carry NO prose range either (Spectator Confusion Ray via `rays[]`, Yuan-Ti Malison Multiattack) — probes must expect no-band lenient rolls there, not a missing band.
- **Modal first-press map gate (CCC race CLOSED by `improve(modal-first-press-map-gate)` 6e1381fc2):** the modal's active-map fetch is tracked (`useActiveMapFetch` in MonsterCardModal.jsx, lazy-init `mapPending` true from the FIRST render whenever mapName is set, settles resolve-OR-reject) — while pending the attack chip renders inert (`aria-busy`+`aria-disabled`, no onClick, muted `mc-dice-link-spell-spent` class) and `handleAttack` silently refuses any indirect spatial press (zero roll/log/write); pending-OA presses stay trigger-lenient (OA_TRIGGER_SPATIAL_STATE law); NO active map ⇒ nothing awaited ⇒ never gated. The old "dwell ~1.5 s and never count a first-press" workaround is DEAD — a post-fix first press either commits nothing (pending) or measures honestly (settled). PENDING-WINDOW DETERMINISM recipe: `page.route('**/api/campaigns/<c>/maps/<slug>', async r => { await page.waitForTimeout(3000); r.continue(); })` freezes the window at will (far more reliable than racing the natural ~0.6–1.2 s gap); `unroute` before map teardown. Spinner idiom SHIPPED (200ac4cc7, EEE re-lived): the pending chip's dice glyph swaps to `fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin` (aria-hidden) and re-reads `fa-dice-d20` on settle — probe `chip.querySelector('i').className` for the gate state.
- Crit damage lane log shape (live 2026-11-06): the doubled pool logs as `formula "1d6*2+1 (N)"` + `isCrit:true` — a single roll × dice multiplier with the flat modifier NOT doubled — not a two-dice `rolls` array; pin this shape for crit proofs (attack and converted-nat-20 faces). **Crit shape two-lane UNIFIED (2026-11-06 improve, crit-ledger-shape-sheet-lane):** the sheet lane now stamps the same per-dice-group `*2` marker as the monster lane on the base+sneak doubled groups (`buildRollBaseDamageStep`/`buildSneakAttackStep` → `markCritDoubledGroups` in attackRollDamageCalc.js, idempotent via `(?!\*2)` guard, fired only when `wasCrit && !isOverchannel`). Display-string only: `total` stays 2×dice+flat, `rolls` stays single-set. ORIGIN of the old split: `buildPlainDamageLogData` (handlePlainDamage.js) routes sheet crit rows through `formatDamageFormula`, but MULTI-GROUP formulas (`X [a] + Y [b]`) fail its `parseExpression` and fell through unmarked — that was the whole bug; a source-marked formula also fails that parse and passes through verbatim (label-preserving), so the log lane needed no change. Spell lane (`directSpellDamageSteps.js`) deliberately NOT source-marked: single-group formulas already get marked at log time and source-marking would strip the `(N)` roll list. RE-PIN CENSUS TRAP: the only test running attackRollDamageCalc end-to-end against the hook is `char-sheet/useAttackDamageResolution.test.jsx` (real-pipeline lane) — a steps-folder-only run misses it.
- **Monster-modal header target-arm laws (live XE 2026-11-16, `49a6c4716` fix-verify):** the avatar-opened `.mc-overlay` header mounts the SHARED `TargetSelect` (`src/components/initiative/TargetSelect.jsx`) in `div.mc-header-arm` for GM tabs and for CONTROLLED monsters on visitor tabs (`isPlayerSummoned = controller !== null` hoisted into `GmStatBlockModal`); hosts without the initiative lane (EB bestiary `details-btn` preview, Map) render NO arm (probe: `hasArmWrap:false`, zero selects — the pre-commit "arm ABSENT inside the modal" law survives only for those board-less hosts). Arm-wrapper click NEVER closes the modal (stopPropagation guard; the rest of `.mc-header` still closes via `onClick=onClose`, × = `.mc-close`). Unarmed monster shows NO overlay option group (monsters are fed `overlays:[]` — `hasOptgroup:false` is exact). Unarmed chip press inside the modal = loud `.mc-no-target-refusal` string popup inside the shared `.popup-overlay` ("X cannot use Y — no target is armed on X's initiative card. Arm a target with the Target select at the top of this card (or on the initiative card)… No attack rolled, nothing spent.") — CORRECTED IN PLACE (improve popup-dismiss-safe-faces 2026-11-16): the refusal face now answers Escape AND a pure backdrop click (zero writes, same Done-equivalent close; drag guard holds) — the XE-era "persistent: Escape AND click-away are inert (`Popup.jsx` wires `onClickOrKeyDown` ONLY to its `.popup-close-btn` Done)" law is DEAD (Done still exits too); drain the popup before any other click regardless (the fixed overlay intercepts the whole viewport while open). Full delegated attack from the modal commits honest (visitor nat20 crit, `1d6*2+2 (1)` formula shape, hp_change pair, no FF dialog for unlisted PC).
- **Devious Strikes sheet-lane chooser law (live 2026-11-06 DH, post-e81bdd985):** the DS chooser mounts ONLY when sneak dice are available for the round — a sneak-LATCHED hit-cycle honestly shows no chooser and lands base `1d6+1` (never call that a jam; re-arm via scoped cs `round` PATCH). Tick options via `.sp-overlay label` startingWith the option name → inner `input.click()`; costs Poison/Trip/Withdraw 1d6, Daze 2d6, Obscure 3d6, Knock Out 6d6; max-2 stacks queue two prompts. A declined round's sneak-eligible repeat hits land base damage straight to the popup — to force a sneak-ELIGIBLE same-round repeat (the pre-fix re-offer face), null `_SneakAttack_usedRound` in-page via `setRuntimeValue` (the mounted tab's own module instance: `await import('/src/hooks/runtime/useRuntimeState.js')`). - **Initiative page re-seeds hand-rolled boards (live 2026-11-07 fix-verify):** opening the Initiative view while a hand-rolled (e.g. 3-creature type:player) combatSummary is live appends EVERY campaign character as a board member (blank initiatives) — the walk order/round-wrap then needs a full 13-creature cycle to reach an anchor creature's NEXT turn. Plan anchor-leg verification either with ≥13 bounded Next clicks (poll `activeCreatureName` between clicks, drain per step) or re-POST the small board AFTER the Initiative page has settled (re-POST while it mounts races the seed). Turn boundaries during the walk can open other creatures' prompts (Stunning-style strikes on player turns) — always run the drain pass inside the walk loop. SHARPENED BOTH LEGS (live 2026-11-09 ER, IN PLACE post-fix 7e5ffc2b9): the mount merge (`initiativeService.mergeCombatSummaryWithCharacters`) DELETES every `type:'player'` row whose name matches no campaign character EXCEPT `secondTurn:true` rows, which are kept while their `holderName` is a campaign character and evicted as zombies otherwise, and normalize through the player leg (never the monster HP-10 defaults) — the old silent eviction of Thief's-Reflexes `(Second Turn)` rows is FIXED (`bug-initiative-remount-drops-second-turn-row`, pinned by `initiativeService.second-turn.test.js`); the UNMOUNT writes nothing (row survives a 14s+ away window incl. debounce); so any lane that leaves and re-enters Initiative re-runs the merge — seed boards via curl right before the mount, not after.
