WALK LAW (fully live-verified FT 2026-11-09): Next steps INTO the row (single `.creature-card.active`, no double-highlight with the holder card), the row card resolves the HOLDER's runtime HP via `holderName` (input showed 103), its target-select lists the holder and excludes secondTurn rows globally, Prev rewinds INTO the row fine, and the row RETIRES at the round-1 wrap (Next past the last creature → round 2, cards−1, zero audit rows for the row). RE-GRANT RIG: a settled tab RE-BAKES `round` from its local mirror on every cs write — a curl scoped-PATCH `{round:1}` alone never re-arms the grant (the next card commit POSTs round 2 back; grant honestly refuses, use stays unspent). To re-ride round 1: Initiative `Clear` → role=dialog Yes (resets round to 1, strips monsters, re-seeds the 13 PCs) → re-arm the use via scoped PATCH `{"thief'sreflexesUses":1}` → card-init commit grants fresh. And the trap is now closed regardless: an app-level `TvExitButton` (bottom-right, z-1400) renders on EVERY view while the pref is ON — click = pref OFF + fullscreen exit + presentation restored (same face as the toggle's OFF); `tvModeStore` gained the reactive pref slot (`getTvPref`/`subscribeTvPref`/`saveTvPref`/`exitTvPresentation`, pure localStorage facade) consumed via `useSyncExternalStore`. Clear `initiative-tv`/`initiative-victory-dismissed` keys when a run leaves TV state. Spotlight = `zoom` (1.875 / 1.44 media-query), NOT transform — read `getComputedStyle().zoom` for the face, and flex `order` is gone (3a527a30e: order is authoritative, active card scales IN PLACE). Stat-block privacy (579aca8d2): MonsterCardModal open state is per-client useState — TV ON means avatar click = ZERO overlays even for localhost GM; the retired `*-viewingMonster` keys must never be asserted in change-data (campaignKeys comment pins the retirement). Delegation: GM-only `[data-testid="control-select"]` (aria `Assign who controls X`) on type:npc cards stamps cs row `controller` + audit "…handed to X — their device can run its card (roll, target, saves) until released." / "…returned to the GM."; release = selectOption('') → controller null. LAN device sim = `browser_tabs {action:'new', isolatedContext:'lan', url:'http://<en0-ip>:5173'}` — isLocalhost follows the HOSTNAME (context isolation doesn't matter); visitor sees read-only nav ("Map" not "Maps", no Encounters/Admin), no `.combat-controls`/control-selects/HP inputs, `YourTurnCue` ("Your turn!") on viewer-controlled active cards, unassigned monster avatar = inert, ASSIGNED card opens + arms via its own row `[data-testid="target-select"]` + rolls through the identical monster lanes (log attrs the monster). Open-gate is `controller!==null` WITHOUT per-device identity check (creatureControl.js LAN-trust D2, accepted): any visitor tab may run any delegated card. FLAKES (2026-11-08 improve tv-music-controls): a LAN dev-host tab's first load can die mid-module-graph with `ERR_NETWORK_CHANGED` (blank body) — bounded reload fixes; and a visitor's first `.tv-toggle-button` OFF click was observed ineffective (pref stayed '1', second click worked) — re-probe pref after every toggle, EK-era face worth re-riding (NOT reproduced live 2026-11-11 run LZ: visitor-context first ON and first OFF clicks both worked from a null-start pref). LZ 2026-11-11 full ride (all PASS except the filed bug): picker stamps `controller` + both audit strings verbatim; visitor census = 14 cards, 0 `.combat-controls`/control-selects/HP-inputs/Next/Clear, ONLY a permanently-disabled `Init` number input on monster cards (backlog), name span (not input); `YourTurnCue` mounts/drops live with the controller stamp; ledger 1:1 (one roll row per press — MISS carries `hit:false` + `targetName`, HIT row + separate damage roll row + one `hp_change`; distinct dice, no replay); cross-client floater re-proven (GM-TV MutationObserver caught `hp-floater-damage::−7` from a visitor hit); RELEASE revokes the open-gate (re-open inert) and the cue, but a card opened BEFORE release stays live and keeps rolling damage ⇒ FILED `bug-released-delegation-stale-open-card-keeps-rolling`; manual GM card HP edit (`input[aria-label="<Name> current HP"]` fill+Enter) DOES write an `hp_change` log row (§89's "logs nothing" is initiative-input-only). **EN TV-faces / transport / allies / friendly-fire selectors (live 2026-11-08):** Spotlight zoom BOTH faces — tall viewport: active card `getComputedStyle().zoom` = 1.875, base 1.5; `browser_resize` window height ≤900 flips to 1.44/1.15 via the `@media (max-height:900px)` lane (Initiative.css ~:1615) — probe `.initiative--tv .creature-card.active` and restore size after. Floaters are PURE render HP-diffs (useTvPresentation over displayCreatures): with TV ON, scoped-`PATCH` a victim's `currentHitPoints` and probe `.hp-floater` within ~900ms — classes `hp-floater-heal|hp-floater-damage` + `hp-floater-big` when |delta| ≥ max(10, 25%·maxHp), damage text uses the U+2212 glyph (`−90`), the node self-retires on animationend (zero residue); BLOODIED badge = `.status-badge.bloodied` at HP ≤ half, reads live from runtime `currentHitPoints` seeded before select. TV header transport (23a8adc45) proven: chip mounts in `.combat-header` ONLY while TV ON AND mood armed; buttons `.combat-header button[title="Pause"]`/`[title="Stop"]`; Pause drops itself from the roster (chip+Stop) keeping `mood`, Stop nulls mood → zero chrome; audit rows byte-identical to sidebar lanes (`Paused Tavern ambience`/`Stopped ambience`). Allies editor: `.allies-edit-btn` (aria `Edit allies of <X>`, one per card, GM-only) was once reported carousel-clipped → evaluate-click — SHARPENED IN PLACE (live 2026-11-13 BE): on a settled 14-row board a plain `getByRole('button',{name:'Edit allies of Goblin'})` trusted click LANDS with zero evaluate, so the clip only bites in genuine carousel-overflow states; the modal MOUNTS AS `.sp-modal`/`.sp-overlay` (save-prompt chrome reuse; the controls cluster resolves as `.ally-selection-controls`); checkboxes aria `Select <name>`, header buttons `Select All`/`Clear All`/`Self only (hostile)` (fa-user-slash one-click preset — 7e517015d, verified live 2026-11-11 incl. monster subjects: draft-only, selects exactly `[selfName]`, nothing written until Confirm), confirm label `Confirm Allies (N)` DISABLED at 0 (default/current selection = self-only, the minimum); confirm writes `<Char>.selectedAllies` (self kept) + `ability_use`/`Ally Selection` audit `<X>'s allies set to: <joined>`. Stable arm selector: `[aria-label="<Name> target"]` (PC avatar alts are INITIALS — `:has(img[alt="<PCName>"])` misses). Arming a LISTED ally fires the Friendly-Fire confirm (`Cancel`/`Yes, Arm Target`, FAIL-CLOSED — targetName stays null until accept; shell selectors are `.modal-shell-overlay`/`.modal-shell`/`.modal-shell-actions` since the 2026-11-08 improve modal-shell-css factor — the shared `DeleteConfirmModal` tier rides the same neutral global shell, `maps-manager-modal-*` remains ONLY on Maps-manager-native modals (Description, Generate Dungeon/Terrain); PITFALL: judging the accept leg's `targetName` from an in-page `fetch` fired in the SAME tick as the button click races the un-awaited scoped PATCH (the log row lands first) — re-GET via curl to confirm the arm; accept logs `Target Selection` "…armed X as a target — …"); flipping the list back self-only = the next arm fires NO confirm (engine follows the edit immediately, no reload). Victory banner is click-transparent outside its card since 2026-11-08 (improve victory-banner-click-block): the `.victory-banner` scrim is `pointer-events:none`, only `.victory-card` captures (card click/Continue dismiss) — a post-Clear walk needs NO banner dismiss first, first `Next →` lands; `document.elementFromPoint` on the Next-button center is a one-shot live proof of overlay interception mirroring Playwright's hit-test. Delegation re-ridden post-TV-batch, all PASS: assign audit `control_assigned` "…handed to X — their device can run its card (roll, target, saves) until released."; release `selectOption('')` → `control_released` "…returned to the GM."; the controller stamp survives a GM reload server-side; LAN visitor (isolatedContext tab on `http://<en0-ip>:5173` — vite needs `--host`, plain `npx vite` = ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED on the LAN IP) auto-adopts identity from the first character, sees read-only nav + 0 control-selects/HP inputs/Next/Clear, but on the CONTROLLED monster its card target-select renders and arms, and a chip press rolls honestly (attributed row `characterName:"Goblin"`, raw `total`, `result:false`).
- **Duplicate-overlay identity gate (verified eca20cc5c, EK 2026-11-08):** seed one `savePrompt-<Target>-<pid>` POST → exactly 1 `.sp-overlay`; re-POST identical key/value → still 1; reload + campaign re-select (connect replays all keys) → still exactly 1; DELETE key → unmounts. Exit ritual still: delete every prompt key you mint (§39 replay law).
- **`.npc-avatar` provenance + keyboard tier (live 2026-11-17 improve visitor-avatar-keyboard-zoom):** the uncontrolled-card `.npc-avatar` renders from `initiative/NpcAvatar.jsx` — the `handleAvatarClick` div at `CreatureCard.jsx` ~:126 is the polymorphObject icon lane (different surface, still pointer-only), so a card-DOM probe anchored on that line's markup targets the wrong component. The visitor-zoom opener now carries the full tier (role=button, tabIndex=0, Enter/Space with Space preventDefault); accessible name is content-derived (img `alt` = creature name / placeholder initial span), matching the GM header `.mc-header-avatar` convention — no separate aria-label. AvatarModal's opener focus-restore works unchanged once the opener is focusable (all three close lanes return focus to the avatar).
- **Friendly-fire through the DELEGATION lane (PT2 CLOSED live 2026-11-11 run MA):** the subject monster's ally list is GM-editable like a PC's (`.allies-edit-btn` exists on EB-joined monster cards, aria `Edit allies of <Monster>`, writes `<Monster>.selectedAllies` + `Ally Selection` audit); an arm of a LISTED ally by the VISITOR's tab fires the same `.modal-shell` Friendly-Fire confirm on the visitor context (copy "Friendly fire: <X> is on <Monster>'s ally list. Arm them as the target anyway?", `Cancel`/`Yes, Arm Target`), FAIL-CLOSED both tabs (target-select value stays "" and cs `targetName` null server-side while open); Cancel = zero write + zero audit; Accept = arm lands + `friendlyFire:true` "Target Selection" row attributed `characterName:"<Monster>"` (the SUBJECT, not the visitor identity). Re-arming the same listed ally again after a Cancel re-fires the confirm (no silent latch).

## 5. Fix conventions (code layer)
- **Promise-settle microtask law for fetch effects whose tests drain with `await act(async …)` (live 2026-11-20 improve modal-first-press-map-gate):** settle state via the two-callback form `.then(onOk, onErr)` — both branches land in ONE microtask so the established `await act(async () => {})` flush recipe still drains them; a `.catch(…)`/`.finally(…)` tail after the `.then(setState)` adds a SECOND microtask those tests don't drain and the leg flakily reads still-pending.
- **Roll-face copy law (fixed 2026-11-18 improve roll-face-copy-separator):** the popup roll line is owned by `DiceRollResult.jsx` `RollSummary` (feeds sheet CharActions, `AttackResultPopup` host AND the monster-chip `MonsterCardModal` — one change covers all three; verdict copy `✓ HIT (n vs AC m)` is a separate `HitMissLine`, untouched by roll-line edits). The fused-`17d20` reading was a block-adjacent `textContent` artifact (JSX stacks the divs; concatenated text fuses them) — the fix is literal `{' '}` text nodes bracketing the breakdown div (invisible visually, the only separators that survive textContent) + a truth-gated equation tail `d20 8 +9 (+9 to hit) → 17` printed ONLY when shown die + shown modifier === shown total (floor/Homing/WIS-replace faces keep their own resolution line). PROBE LAW: key readability assertions on `container.textContent`/`.popup-modal` text, not innerText; assert `not.toMatch(/\dd20/)`.
- **Byte-pin-safe relocation of pure builders (live 2026-11-18 improve sheet-no-target-loud-refusal):** when a copy-shaping pure function with byte-pinning usage tests (e.g. `buildNoTargetRefusalPopup`/`buildNoTargetRefusalLog` pinned by `MonsterCardHelpers.usage.test.js`) must move to a shared owner so a second lane re-uses it verbatim, MOVE the body and leave a `export { … } from` re-export at the old home — the byte-pins and existing consumers ride the same lane with zero drift (pinned again by the new owner's test, `services/combat/noTargetRefusal.js`).
- **React-19 `autoFocus` commits BEFORE the parent's passive focus effect (live 2026-11-16 fix hex-poi-menu-keyboard-rove):** an open-focus-into-first effect on a menu/panel steals the caret from a child input that mounts with `autoFocus` (the editor is armed, focus yanked back to the row). The focus effect must SKIP while an inline editor is armed — null the hook's `openKey`/trigger while the editor state is set (`useMenuRoving` idiom) rather than racing it.
- **App-wide z-index tier map (live 2026-11-08, `bug-victory-banner-above-confirm-dialogs` FIXED):** page chrome ≤100 (fixed sidebar/party-inventory) · ambient-celebration slot 150 (`.victory-banner` — was 1300, which swallowed dialog buttons) · ambient dialog tier 200 (`popup-overlay` dice results, sheet sub-modals, dice-tray drawer) · primary modal tier 1000 (`.modal-shell-overlay` + all feature modals, inner layers 1100) · `TvExitButton` 1400 (intentional always-reachable corner pin) · adjudication prompts 2000–2100 (save/concentration/death-save, CampaignReloadNotice). Never place a transient/ambient overlay above 200. Cleanest pre/post witness for "overlay swallows dialog button" bugs: `document.elementFromPoint` at the dialog button's rect center (identical to Playwright's hit-test and a human's dead click).
- te registry: register EVERY new te key in `src/services/combat/conditions/targetEffectDefinitions.js` (fields whitelist pinned by test; alphabetical/Defensive ordering enforced); GM add-UI reads it only.
- **Ephemeral UI flags are LOCAL; genuinely-SHARED keys must EVICT on close, never POST null (demoted 2026-11-19 improve effectadder-target-local-state, owner decision):** `combat-ui-effectAdderTarget` is GONE — the EffectAdder open-target is plain per-GM `React.useState(null)` in `Initiative.jsx` (zero network across the open/close/Apply/Escape/view-switch/reload lifecycle, fetch-census-proved), the `useEffectAdderTarget.js` hook + `Initiative.effectAdder-evict.test.jsx` are DELETED, the `changeDataKeys.js` allowlist entry is retired (the admin census test now fixtures the key as an intentional PHANTOM) and pins live in `Initiative.effectAdder-local.test.jsx` (zero store traffic, oracle = any `effectAdder`-shaped key; intentionally does NOT restore after remount, never shared cross-tab). The 2026-11-08 ghost-key doctrine STANDS for keys that genuinely live in the shared store (prompt books, savePrompt drain lane): a `useSyncedState` close that sets `null` persists a GHOST key (`key: null`) which replays on every connect/re-select; an unmount-while-open orphans the full `{value}` — evict via `deleteRuntimeKey` (server DELETE + null echo, `has()` guard makes repeat deletes idempotent, no 404 churn), and evict on component detach so a view-switch while open can't orphan. APPLY chain (live 2026-11-09 ER, still current): a successful Conditions-tab Apply closes the modal — plus the full honest chain `condition applied` (type:condition, ability stamped) → `creature-badge effect-condition` on the card → badge × sibling `button[title="Remove effect"]` → `activeConditions:[]` + a `condition broken` audit row (action:"broken"). Anchor the affected card via `:has(input[aria-label="<Name> current HP"])` — every card's Target dropdown lists ALL names, so any card-text/hasText probe hits card #0 first (live ER trap). GATE SHIPPED (2026-11-10 owner ruling, corrects the prior face in place): AWAY cards render NO card-level `Add condition, effect…` affordance (absent, not disabled; away→Rejoin restores without reload); `EffectAdder.jsx` filters its candidate list through `isCreaturePresent` INSIDE the component (law lives in the flow for every opener); `Initiative.jsx openEffectAdder` + `createEffectAdderHandlers.js handleApplyEffect` refuse explicitly-away targets (stale/shared-flag backstop — absent rows keep prior semantics); badge-× REMOVAL stays permitted on away cards (cleanup lane). Unit-test recipe (CORRECTED IN PLACE with the demotion): the away-refusal + present-open pins ride `Initiative.effectAdder-away-gate.test.jsx` asserting ZERO store traffic; the old stale-modal-away race recipe (seeding fake sync store key `combat-ui-effectAdderTarget` as a plain property name — real `useSyncedState` read `getStore().has(prop)`, not the mock composite) DIED with the synced lane.
- **`deleteRuntimeKey` URL law — only top-level `<name>-<key>` campaign keys are evictable (pitfall, 2026-11-17 fix bi-prompt-skip-never-evicts-server-key):** the helper composes its DELETE URL from the propertyName only (`/api/campaigns/<c>/<propertyName>`) and is built for `characterKey='campaign'` callers — it CANNOT evict a key nested inside a character bucket (and a bare nested name like `biPrompt` would collide across creatures). Any per-creature one-shot/prompt state that needs the drain-to-evict contract must therefore live on a top-level `<name>-<key>` wildcard key with the dynamic suffix registered in `src/services/automation/common/campaignKeys.js` `isCampaignKey` (value echoes mirror; null evict echo = REMOVE on every tab) — mirror the save/concentration/death-save prompt lanes (`savePromptService.js`), not a character-bucket shape. BI adjudication no longer rides this lane at all: the ENTIRE BI-prompt key/modal lane (`<attacker>-biPrompt` producer + campaignKeys registration + BardicInspirationReactionModal + prompt utils) was DELETED 2026-11-18 (fix bi-offense-modal-rider-gate-arg-shape, owner decision — the rider's gate call was mis-shaped so the modal lane was zero-reach; mirrors the 2026-11-17 defense-half deletion `1a308c928`) — the popup-inline `Bardic Inspiration - Offense/Defense (dN)` buttons (hitResolution.js flags → `handlePlainDamage.js`/`useLoggedDiceRollAttack.js`) are the ONLY BI adjudication lane (§17 retire note).
- **Dice-popup payload keys land on `DiceRollResult` with zero wiring (live 2026-11-08, awayNote fix 99c3843a9):** initiative/sheet d20 popups flow producer payload → `AttackResultPopup`'s `DiceRollPopupContent` → `{...popupHtml}` spread → `DiceRollResult` props — a new producer-side key already reaches the renderer, so a render fix is just a file-local notice component mirroring the `HolyAuraSaveNotice`/amber-family idiom (e.g. `awayNote` → `.dice-roll-away-note`, icon `fa-person-walking-arrow-right`), no plumbing. Also: `away` on combatSummary rows survives a full reload + campaign re-select (two-phase pre/post-fix sessions need no re-arming). **Shield Bash has TWO byte-twin save-outcome producers (fixed 2026-11-08, bug-attack-rider-log-claims-applied-on-declined-or-saved):** the sheet-hit pipeline step `services/combat/steps/features/shieldBash.js logShieldBashSaveResult` (auto-fires on sheet attacks — the LIVE producer of the observed rows) and the feature-row/router lane `automation/handlers/combat/attackRiderHandler.js logShieldBashSaveOutcome` — duplicate log copy there means fix/verify BOTH lanes. Shipped honest copy: pre-chooser fail leg "X failed the STR save (DC N). Choose an effect." (chooser's commit/decline rows are the only effect claims); rider-save `ability_use` lead branches `attempted on` (success) vs `applied to` (fail) in attackRiderHandler.handleRiderSave AND versatileTricksterHandler secondary row; pinned by `attackRiderHandler.log-honesty.test.js` + save-flow/eviction/shieldBash/VT test pins. EM RE-RIDE LIVE-PASS 2026-11-08: canonical-EF sheet cycle (3-creature board + warding_bond −19/+19 dial) — fail leg row bytes `ElderPaladin failed the STR save (DC 17). Choose an effect.` paired with `Shield Bash: skipped (no effect, use not consumed).`, success leg `ElderPaladin succeeded the STR save (DC 17).` (×2), and the DS Trip save-SUCCESS lead `Trip attempted on ElderPaladin — ElderPaladin rolled 6 on DEX save (DC 15), succeeded — no effect` with cost-deducted sneak `1d6+3 [slashing] + 9d6 [Sneak Attack]`, te evicted, VT picker honest success copy — every face byte-honest; rider prompts carry `Source: Shield Bash` on the shared `.sp-source` lane (the DS Trip DEX prompt rendered no source line).
- Rider save-success eviction (2026-11-06 improve): save-gated rider legs pre-append a descriptor row to campaign-root `targetEffects` (applyRiderEffect / versatileTricksterHandler); on strict `success===true` resolution both lanes now EVICT that row (tuple-scoped target+source+option+effect) — saved Trip/Daze/etc legs leave no root residue, fail legs keep the row byte-identical and grant the condition (`attackRiderHandler.save-success-eviction.test.js`, `versatileTricksterHandler.test.js`). Audit truth is the `ability_use` row, never the te row: descriptor keys (prone/poisoned/daze/…) are UNREGISTERED in the te registry so badges skip them and `conditionToApply` folds are dead outside conditionEffects.js (census 2026-11-06). Dismissed (un-resolved) legs still park the createSaveListener promise → row kept (§5 save-dismiss lane). Proven clause trio: parser in `MonsterCardHelpers.js` (armed in `buildAbilitySaveRollContext`) → forwarded prop → `SaveAttackAoeModal` `resolveSaveFailGrant`/`saveProcessing` grant → registered te + ONE `addExpiration` clock (`rounds`: minutes×10, hours×600, seconds×10) + grant log. Single-target rows byte-unchanged (props default undefined = inert). Expiry: `addExpiration(..., rounds=undefined, expireOnCreatureName=anchor)` → `expiryRounds:null` (Infinity); anchor leg fires at anchor's NEXT turn-start (`currentRound > appliedRound`) — same-round expiry never fires; long buffs need rounds + DROP the anchor. TWO sequential addExpiration calls RACE (one clock survives) — merge into one write. Clock-fired CONDITION legs now log ONE `condition removed` row each with provenance copy (live 2026-11-07 improve, `clearExpirationEffects` 5th arg = the fired entry): `expireOnCreatureName` set → "round-anchor clock expired at X's turn start", finite `expiryRounds` → "N-round clock expired", no entry (legacy 4-arg caller) → "duration has expired"; absent-condition strips stay silent, te/buff-only clocks write nothing; hand-crafted clock + small board via curl is the cheapest live ride.
- **Rider-condition seam-clear lane truth (sheet-lane race FIXED 2026-11-10, `resolved/bug-sheet-initiative-seam-resurrects-rider-conditions.md`; per-creature law FIXED 2026-11-10, `resolved/bug-combat-seam-clears-board-wide-violate-per-creature-ruling.md`)**: **PER-CREATURE SEAM LAW (owner ruling, Paul 2026-11-10, verbatim: "EB join is not a board wide new initiative roll. EB join can be used in the middle of combat… So an initiative roll, short rest, or long rest signals old combat ending and new combat beginning for the character making the initiative roll, not for everyone")** — an initiative roll / short rest / long rest clears ONLY the actor's own conditions/expirations/self-buffs and NEVER any other creature's state; EB Join is never a clear seam at all (patrols/reinforcements join mid-combat; joiners are fresh, present creatures untouched). Mechanism: roll producers carry `detail.characterName` and every `initiative-rolled` consumer (Initiative.jsx listener, CharSummary wild-magic-surge lane, useInitiativeEffects, BlindnessDeafnessModal) clears ONLY the roller; join lanes (EB Join both branches, saved-encounter load / auto-encounter, Add-NPC-to-Initiative) dispatch DETAIL-LESS events and the per-creature lanes clear nobody on detail-less — only the campaign-level laws still ride any roll (WM-008 `_Vex_appliedTarget` latch + the b570aa359 pendingOAs drop). The board-wide `sweepNewCombatClocks` survives ONLY behind the explicit Initiative "Clear" board wipe (log copy "Combat board cleared: N combat-scoped effects ended (enforcement: zones)"); rests sweep ONLY the resting creature's own store (`sweepRestClocks(campaign, restKind, name)` → ownerName-scoped, log copy "Short rest: X's N combat-scoped effect ended"), and every rest/roll helper touching shared buckets filters to rows INVOLVING the actor (te source OR target): rest te-key clears, summon/globe/polymorph clears (`summonedBy`/`polymorphSource === actor`), Regenerate flag+restore, Wild Shape end, hp_max_reduce restore, and `clearAllExpirationEffects`'s Sanctuary strip; useInitiativeEffects' INITIATIVE_CLEARED_EFFECT_KEYS sweep + regenerate restore are roller-scoped too. Victim-side truth: clock-carried conditions (save-margin riders etc.) genuinely evict at the VICTIM'S OWN seams — short rest (restRules-shortRest atomic batch: `activeConditions:[]` + meta {} + incoming-clock drained) and long rest, and the victim's OWN roll: `clearAllExpirationEffects`'s `clearIncomingExpirations` leg correctly drains clocks living in OTHER stores that TARGET the actor (that inbound leg is victim-scoped, not board-wide); finite-round rider clocks persist across ANOTHER creature's roll and across every EB join (by law now, not merely by code accident). THE SHEET-LANE initiative re-roll (`processInitiativeRoll` → `clearAllExpirationEffects`) previously fired the clock honestly (removed rows + attacker-queue trim) while its same-tick un-awaited full-store sibling writes (clearSelfBuffs/outgoing-stamp snapshots taken pre-strip) resurrected the pre-strip `activeConditions` — MA-0809 §5 race, live-forced 1/1 via `page.route` stale-snapshot delay. Fix = short-rest idiom: the clock sweeps (incoming + self/outgoing) run FIRST so every later snapshot of the victim bucket already carries the stripped state; the condition strip itself is ONE merged `setRuntimeObject` (conditions + `activeConditionMeta` purge together — the meta row previously survived every clock expiry); all self-bucket changes (buffs, flags, queue stamp) ride ONE final merged write; pinned by `clearAllExpirationEffects.write-race.test.js` (snapshot-issue-order harness). Judge eviction by settled state, not the removed rows alone. PC-victim deterministic deep-fail at the monster-chip prompt seam: `warding_bond saveBonus:-25` in the victim's `activeBuffs` (bucket POST BEFORE campaign-select, §49 law) folds into the prompt breakdown (+5 CON +5 aura −25 = −15 shown) — guarantees margin ≥5 in one press even aura-padded. PITFALL: the seam's clearSelfBuffs CONSUMES the dial buff — re-stamp mid-session with scoped `PATCH /:campaign/<Victim> {value:{activeBuffs:[…]}}` (a settled tab adopts it live, the breakdown re-renders −25, proved ×2). NPC victims take the cs `saveBonuses` −15 stamp (inline auto-roll folds, margin ≥5 at DC 11). Per-creature pins: `encounterToInitiative.per-creature-seam.test.js` (join lanes sweep nobody + non-vacuous real-sweep contrast + rest sweep owner-scoping), `initiative-init-rolled-handler.test.jsx` (roller-only clears; detail-less join clears nobody; roller absent from board clears nobody), `CharSummary-EventHandlers.test.jsx` (foreign/join dispatches keep the surge badge), `useInitiativeEffects.effectClearing.test.js` + `.invisibilityRegen.test.js` (actor-involving te rows + roller-only regen restore), rest-suite actor-scope pins. In-view GM-card face: a monster with authored `initiative_details` shows `[data-kind="initiative"]` on its stat card (test-campaign fixtures: Wolf "+2 (12)" HAS it, Goblin has none) — pressing it is the ONLY roll producer that fires `initiative-rolled` WHILE the Initiative view is mounted, and it now clears only the roller.
- **SSE turn-start GM-authority guard (FIXED 2026-11-08, `bug-turn-start-automation-double-fires-with-second-client`): `createSseEventHandler` takes `isLocalhost` (fail-closed default false) and `applyTurnStartPass` early-returns for non-GM clients — the name echo ALWAYS precedes the executor's stamped cs (GM POSTs `activeCreatureName` at navigationHandlers:290 before the stamps), so a mounted visitor's own round gate can never dedupe and must simply not execute; visitors keep mirror reads. Visitor-tab network baseline for this lane: exactly ONE by-design `POST /__campaign__` (MapContextSync mount stamp, pinned in App.state-transitions.test.jsx) — any `/__initiative__`/`/<Char>`/`/log` POST from a visitor IS a regression witness.** SHARPENED 2026-11-08 (`bug-visitor-drains-pending-save-listener-bucket` FIXED): the `/<Char>`-POST-witness law has a pinned EXCEPTION — `expireForCreature` (expirationQueue.js) seeds a missing `pendingExpirations:[]` on every client's turn-start mirror read, visitor included (deliberate mirror-read lane); cheapest witness: seed a char bucket WITHOUT `pendingExpirations`, next seam each mounted visitor POSTs it (200). And for page-singleton `window` handler lanes whose hosts are prop-less (e.g. `useLoggedDiceRollEventHandlers.syncListenerPromptFilters`), gate with the shared `isGmHost()` (`src/services/ui/gmHost.js` — per-origin hostname law, fail-closed) instead of prop-threading: first-installer-wins makes a fail-closed prop default silently kill the GM lane.
- Runtime writes: full-store snapshot per POST — captured AT CALL TIME by setRuntimeValue/setRuntimeObject (the POST body is `Object.fromEntries(store)` at that instant), so multiple un-awaited `setRuntimeValue` in one tick LOSE all but last (MA-0809 sharpen: same-ENDPOINT `/changes/<Name>` replace-route can ALSO reorder network-side, dropping the earlier key's meta while the later key's snapshot lands — zero console errors; the sheet-initiative-seam bug 2/2 hit exactly this with ~12 pre-strip clearSelfBuffs snapshots). Fix = single merged `setRuntimeObject` or sequential awaits, latch stamp awaited BEFORE consumers read; at CLEAR seams order the state-stripping writes FIRST so every sibling snapshot taken afterwards is already honest (short-rest batch idiom, now mirrored by clearAllExpirationEffects). Deterministic race-riding harness: `page.route` the racing endpoint and DELAY every POST whose body carries the STALE content so it lands last — the reorder fires 1/1 that way (live-proved 2026-11-10; natural arrival order in the same session was 3/3 issuance-order, so never conclude "no race" from clean settles — ride the harness). Complementary burst-replay recipe when the UI-level seam lands in order on your machine: from the live tab fire the seam's EXACT staged bodies as a synchronous un-awaited `fetch` loop, then poll the final bucket — repeated rounds eventually invert (1/8 witnessed, 2026-11-18 clear-latch fix). RACE-PAIR AUDIT LAW (2026-11-18 clear-latch fix): when fixing a multi-POST same-bucket race in a handler, audit EVERY same-bucket POST in that handler tick — `handleClear`'s Dread Ambush loop POSTed whole buckets carrying live stamps BEFORE the eviction, so collapsing only the eviction would still have left a resurrection partner; order the authoritative eviction FIRST (runtime-store writers mutate the mirror synchronously, so every body dispatched afterwards already carries the nulls ⇒ an order-independent seam). Mock-surface corollary: routing a lane through `setRuntimeObject` breaks every partial `vi.mock` of `useRuntimeState.js` in the graph — a MISSING export surfaces as an UNHANDLED REJECTION (vitest exit 1 with "3187 passed / Errors 2", zero failed tests) — grep the mock factories, not just the failures. In-place array splice/push mutates store ref → dirty-check `new===old` skips POST — always spread new arrays. Same species client-side (live 2026-11-08 TV-escape improve): NEVER persist/notify an external store (`useSyncExternalStore` source or localStorage+listeners) from INSIDE a `setState` updater — React runs updaters during render and sibling subscribers throw "Cannot update a component while rendering a different component"; move side-effects into the click/handler phase.
- **Save-grant vs damage-strip sequencing race (FIXED 2026-11-11, `resolved/bug-beguiling-song-own-damage-strips-its-charm.md`):** `applyDamageToTarget` (applyDamage.js) awaits internally SEVERAL times before `removeCombatConditionsOnDamage` reads `activeConditions` — any caller that applies a save-row's damage and then grants conditions MUST await the damage legs first (SaveAttackAoeModal was calling it un-awaited while the grant ran synchronously, so the engine's Charmed-ends-on-damage strip deterministically deleted the same effect's just-granted charm; the `hp_change`-LAST log order is the fingerprint of this race — the correct order is hp_change → save-damage roll → condition applied). Canonical pattern to mirror: `saveProcessing.applySaveDamage` (awaits damage before dispatching the grant). Test idiom that pins it deterministically in jsdom: mock `applyDamageToTarget` to YIELD, then strip from a store-faithful runtime stub, asserting the grant survives; reusable for sibling grant-order bugs. Side law shipped same pass: `removeCombatConditionsOnDamage` purges the stripped condition's `activeConditionMeta[key]` row in ONE merged `setRuntimeObject` write (stripConditionsMerged idiom) — condition strips never orphan meta rows.
- Round latches `_<Feature>_usedRound` on `playerStats.name`/monster name, round from FRESH `getCombatContext`; clear at round-wrap in BOTH initiative.jsx + navigationHandlers beside `_Slow_Fall_usedRound`; monster latches must NOT rely on PLAYER_ROUND_LATCH_KEYS (player-only clear).
- Log conventions: every resolution logs — spends `ability_use` (characterName+abilityName+description), refusals `automation` + `<feature>_refused` (+reason token) zero-spend, `save_result`, `roll attack/roll damage`, `condition applied` with source meta, `automation blocked`. Special-Actions grid NEVER flushes handler `logEntries` — `addEntry` DIRECTLY in handler. `{type:'modal'}` returns bypass the flusher too — modal's own confirm handler must flush. `isWithinRange` (rangeCheck.js) + `rangeToFeet` (rangeValidation.js) for every range gate; gridless/positionless = LENIENT (consulted-and-passes is enough); phantom/tokenless targets CANNOT be gated — record advisory distance in log.
- DCs: `dc_success:"none"` consumed by MonsterCardModal getSaveDcSuccess (default `half` hardcode MV-20); `buildSaveDc` accepts `saveDc:'ability'` token — never bake numeric DC before abilities computed; saveBonuses keys LOWERCASE; EB join strips monsters.json immunities (cs.immunities partial) — look up monsters.json by cs name. Picker `computeNpcSave` reads `saveBonuses[saveType.toLowerCase()]` = `'dexterity'` while live cs keys are `'dex'` → NPC-picker saves roll raw-d20 vs DC; PC-prompt path is the live seam; harness mocks must key `'dexterity'` (MA-0303). Re-validate hit/AC honestly: popup may print BASE AC while resolver used buffed `effectiveAc` — judge HIT/MISS boundary + log `targetAc/coverLevel/coverAcBonus`; converted MISS popups still print "✓ HIT" — log `hit:false` decides.
- Complexity ceiling 15 is real (zero warnings) — move parsing to helpers/child components; SHARPENED (live 2026-11-16 improve monster-modal-target-select): the rule counts PARAMETER DEFAULTS as decision points — two `= false` defaults pushed the already-at-cap `MonsterCardModal` to 17; pass undefined (falsy) at the call site or hoist branching into a module-level helper (`buildTargetArm`) instead; a null-when-idle file-local wrapper component (friendly-fire/MusicToast idiom) clears the cost of adding inline modal render branches. Initiative.jsx additionally sits at the max-statements cap (60) — pay for a new statement by deleting a redundant one (confirm-bridge factories should BE the useState setter, payload = pending value, not a pass-through wrapper); App.jsx is capped the same way (live 2026-11-11 improve back-to-maps-label: statements inside nested callbacks/effect bodies count against the CALLBACK, only direct-body declarations cost against `App` — consolidating a ref+state mirror into one hook call reclaimed budget; GATE-COMPONENT PRECEDENT (live improve zero-char-empty-state 2026-11-20): a multi-clause render condition belongs in a module-level null-gate component above App — precedents `MapsAreaView`/`EmptyCampaignStage`); even one `?.` added inside its JSX tips the complexity-15 ceiling — hoist such predicates to module scope (precedent: `isActiveEconomyRowGmDriven`, 2026-11-10); SHARPENED (live 2026-11-14 improve fanatical-focus-latch-gate): the rule counts `?.` AND `||` as decision points, and `CharSheetContent` also sits at the 15 ceiling — a gate added there must be decision-neutral vs what it replaces (dropping a redundant `!playerStats` guard paid for the new branch). While a DeleteConfirmModal is open over an editor there are TWO "Cancel" buttons — always scope dialog clicks via `within(getByRole('dialog'))`. Adding an export to a heavily-mocked util breaks ~12 partial `vi.mock`s — extend every partial mock (`importActual`); `vi.clearAllMocks()` keeps implementations — use `mockReset`; leaked `mockResolvedValue(false)` kills later tests — re-stub per-key.
- Legendary economy (reusable, MA-0021/0070/0218): header dict must author numeric `uses:N` → `monsterLegendaryUses.js` expend/refuse/regain via turnStartEffects; children auto-gate; prose weapon child `delegates_to:"<Weapon>"`; prose spellcast child needs NUMERIC spell fields copied (delegates_to:"Spellcasting" misfires through save leg); header+children MUST be fixed SAME pass (else "Expend Legendary" chip silently BURNS uses on inert rows, console.error "no resolvable mechanic"). Lair: structured dicts with `name` clickable; raw-string/nameless = inert; zone dict shape `{name,zone:{radius_ft,no_save,effect_key,noun,advisory},duration}`; advisory `{name,advisory:"<snake_key>",description}` → record-only log "initiative 20 (GM-enforced)". No initiative-20/lair-recurring consumer app-wide.
- **Save-dismiss outcome (fixed 2026-11-05)**: the un-rolled branch of SavePromptModal `handleDismiss` dispatches a dedicated `save-dismissed` window event carrying `promptId` — NOT a `save-result` (~40 `createSaveListener().promise` consumers read falsy `success` as "failed → apply damage"; resolving them on dismiss would over-deal damage). `createSaveListener` detaches its window listener on dismissal. Producers opt in by listening for `save-dismissed` (DivineSparkModal template: route to its `refuse()` helper → refusal view + working Done + `<slug>_refused` log row; charge stays spent — §9 pay-early family). Dismiss is the ONLY un-rolled footer button (post-roll swaps to Done/Next Save — `handleDone`'s no-result branch is unreachable). FIXED SAME PASS 2026-11-05: CharReactions beguiling-twist + saveOnlyHandler produce `*_refused`/`save_dismissed` rows and detach both listeners on dismiss. Census: all remaining raw `save-result` producers are leak-only (guid/promptId-gated, can never fire wrong — no wrong-state producers found); modal-lane ones (StepsOfTheFeyTaunt, AreaEffectTargetModalBase, MassSuggestion, SaveAttackAoe, Eyebite family) SWEPT 2026-11-05 (improve save-result-producer-census): full 28-file census classified, 15 modal-parking sites opted into `save-dismissed` via the DivineSparkModal pattern + shared helpers `logSaveDismissed` (savePrompt.js) and `useSaveDismissedListener`/`dismissedNoEffectResult` (AreaEffectSaveFlow.utils.js) — neutral `{dismissed:true, success:true, total:0}` rows drain AoE queues instead of freezing; per-site `.save-dismissed.test.jsx` ledgers guard promptId gating. Test doctrine: listener-ledger tests MUST mint unique promptIds per test — shared mock promptIds poison later ledgers (stale listeners remove/count on later dispatches). **createSaveListener ledger-test guid monotonicity (live 2026-11-08, save-result-attribution fix):** a `createSaveListener` test that never dispatches leaves its window handler attached forever — mock `guid()` ids must be **monotonic across the whole file** (resetting the counter in `beforeEach` makes a later dispatch resolve the stale listener and double-log). Sharpens the §save-dismiss promptId-uniqueness doctrine.
- **Second-turn (ST) phantom-bucket law — chokepoints and pinned-bug test audit (fixed 2026-11-09, `bug-secondturn-allies-editor-phantom-bucket` + `bug-secondturn-badge-removal-synthetic-name`):** every per-card lane on a `(Second Turn)` row must remap `isSecondTurnEntry(creature) && creature.holderName → holderName` at its chokepoint before any change-data write or audit (precedents: `openEffectAdder` c5883e4a4; allies editor fixed in `useAllyEditor.js` `openAllyEditor` — one edit there covers modal title, `currentAllies` read, `setAllyList` write AND audit name simultaneously, not `createCreatureHandlers`; condition-badge × fixed in `createAutoBreakConditionHandler.js` — the LIVE × lane is `CreatureCard.jsx ConditionBadgeRow` → `handleAutoBreakCondition(creature.name, cond)` which must remap via the cs row it already looks up before `removeCondition` + `logConditionEvent`; the `ConditionEffectBadges.jsx REMOVE_HANDLERS.condition` branch is DEAD code — grep-zero `removeKey` producers app-wide, don't fix there), and candidate lists must `.filter(c => !isSecondTurnEntry(c))` (law exists in `CreatureCard.jsx` target list / `useTvPresentation.js`). When fixing any ST-law bug, first grep `src/components/initiative/` tests for assertions that ENshRINE synthetic-name behavior — a green suite can mean the buggy face is pinned (the pre-fix `useAllyEditor.test.js` roster test actively asserted "FULL roster including … second-turn entries"). Adjacent lane FIXED same run 2026-11-09 (d55d4160a): the same badge's CLICK (condition-save reroll) now remaps inside `handleRollConditionSave` (`createRollConditionSaveHandler.js`) — see the ST-row lane authority bullet for the face proof. **Modal-family dismiss lane + spell-grant rig (live-proved 2026-11-06 run DC, BlindnessDeafnessModal)**: AreaEffectTargetModalBase consumers receive the un-rolled Dismiss through the base's `save-dismissed` listener (attached only while `processing:true`); the consumer's neutral `dismissedNoEffectResult` row satisfies the all-resolved gate → Done appears (no "Resolving…" freeze). LANE-TRANSPORT LAW: spell-modal lanes queue via DIRECT `sendSavePrompt` — only the `savePrompt-<Target>-<promptId>` arrival key materializes; `pendingSavePrompts`/`pendingSaveListenerPrompts` buckets are NEVER armed there (drain proof = arrival-key DELETE; the bucket-evict doctrine is `createSaveListener`-lane only) and the rolled verdict lands as the RESOLVED `roll`/`save-damage` row's `saveResult:"success|failure"`+total+bonus+formula (NO `save_result` row, NO `saveResult-<T>` key — those belong to `sendSaveResult`/`createSaveListener` writers). Double-fire probe: re-dispatch a queue-shaped `save-dismissed` with a non-matching promptId while the resolved modal is open AND after Done — both inert (count refused rows across casts). GRANT RECIPE (sanctioned, e2e-explore pitfall 10): append the spell-name string to the fixture's disk `spells[]` (surgical str.replace + json.loads validate), wait past the ~2s debounce, GET `/<Char>.json` for disk truth, hard reload + campaign re-select → the `td.left.spell-name.clickable` sheet row auto-derives for a 2024 prepared cleric (zero preparedSpells authoring); slot pays PRE-modal at "Cast Spell" (GET the char bucket's `spell_slots_level_N` mid-chooser); the modal's own target picker (`label.abjure-target-row` input → `.sp-roll-btn` "(N target)") means NO cs.targetName arming is needed and gridless = lenient; seed caster AND victim `activeConditions:[]` before first cast post-clear (victim bucket needs full-store POST to CREATE before scoped PATCH works); restore = cp backup over the file + md5-equality (public/campaigns is GITIGNORED — git porcelain there is a weak check) + closed-tab clears restore the paid slots with the bucket.
- **Popup fallback-host LIFETIME law (live-proved 2026-11-17 improve guarded-popup-close-fallback-host):** `Popup`'s close-restore tier lands a stranded close onto the stamped `tabindex="-1"` host at `overlay.parentElement` — that host is only viable if the CONSUMER keeps it mounted past the popup's own unmount. A `{popupHtml && <Wrapper>}` outer gate detaches the stamped host in the same commit the popup unmounts, the `isConnected` guard honestly stands down, and focus strands BODY anyway (this is why the guarded BODY-strand survived even after the tier shipped; the fix moved the gate INSIDE `MonsterAttackPopup` so the wrapper/host survives while the stat modal is open). Guarded faces now capture zero anchor + never pull while open but participate in the close side (stamped-host landing). Unit-lane corollary: jsdom never moves focus on click — a guarded-close test must model the real browser by leaving activeElement BODY pre-close (stranded lane) or pre-focusing a `tabIndex=0` chip (stand-down lane); `fireEvent.click` alone false-pins the stand-down face.
- **Shared-modal family-convention changes (live 2026-11-13 improve modal-escape-dismiss):** the real work is STALE PINS — consumer wrapper tests may pin the INVERSE convention (`FriendlyFireConfirmModal`/`ClearCombatConfirmModal`/`MapsManager` each asserted "backdrop/Escape do NOT cancel" before the Escape/backdrop ship); grep the overlay class + `Escape` across ALL consumer test folders before declaring a shared-modal change done. Reusable backdrop-drag guard: overlay `onMouseDown` stores `target.closest('.modal-shell')` in a ref, overlay `onClick` dismisses only when NEITHER the click target NOR the recorded mousedown was inside the panel, resetting the flag on every bubbled click (synthetic `fireEvent.click`-only probes still dismiss). Probe gotcha: `expect(screen.getByRole(...)).not.toBeInTheDocument()` THROWS before the assertion can pass — dismissed-state probes need `queryByRole`. LIVE INHERITANCE PROVED (BE descent 2026-11-13): all five ridden consumers answer Escape + a pure backdrop click at (15,15) with zero-write dismiss — reject-draft (row stays `draft:true`, no audit row), Delete Map (row survives), Clear Initiative Board (13-row board intact), Friendly-Fire (arm DISARMS: the subject's `<select aria-label="<Name> target">` value reverts to `''`, cs `targetName` absent), typed-name campaign delete (confirm already gated-disabled; meta stays 200) — while every confirm leg (`Yes, Delete Permanently`/`Yes, Clear Board`/`Yes, Arm Target`) lands normally; drag-guard live rig: `mouse.move` onto the dialog `<p>` → down → move ~30px outside the panel with steps → up — dialog STAYS open; a browser viewport-null `page.viewportSize()` doesn't block the corner-click face.

- **CSS specificity dead-rule pitfall (live 2026-11-21 fix session-planner-buttons-outside-modal):** a lane stylesheet overriding a shared `.ct-container .X` property with a bare `.lane-X` selector (0-1-0 < 0-2-0) silently does NOTHING — the lane CSS reads correct but the rule is dead code; measure the APPLIED rect (getBoundingClientRect / getComputedStyle), never trust the lane CSS text. Here the dead `.sessions-modal { width: 720px }` masked the footer-overflow bug's severity (the lane WAS meant to be wide enough); fix idiom = match the shared selector's tier (`.ct-container .ct-modal.sessions-modal`) + responsive cap `min(720px, 92vw)`, pinned via the repo's CSS-contract test idiom (`App.css.test.jsx` asserts on rule text — jsdom does no layout, so rect-pinning is impossible in vitest).

## 6. Fingerprints (pre-diagnosis — confirm live before fixing)
- Flat integer damage ("Hit: 1 Slashing", no `damage_dice_primary`) → zero damage silently (MA-0322/0364/0332) — FIXED MA-0322 app-wide: `extractFlatHitDamage` (MonsterCardHelpers, dice-paren guard `\(\s*\d+d\d+` — non-dice parentheticals must not block) → autoDamageFormula "N" → `resolveAutoDamageResult` (dice-less log, flat NEVER doubles on crit); constants "N" remain unrollable by rollExpression — Route A data "damage_dice_primary:\"1\"" hits MA-0014 block; siblings just verify live. "Varies (WIS,CON,…)" save_type / multi-dice strings → unparseable save-shell, mod-0 "VAR" (MA-0374/0383 family) — all such rows FAIL.
- Unparseable damage chip ("+spell level"): `canRollExpression` gate suppresses; chip w/o parse = click dies silently (MA-0014). Damageless saves: `applyFailedSaveConditions` once stranded no-damage rows (MA-0014/0090 family) — NOW damage+condition rows apply fine (MV-27); damageless spellcasting save-legs still route advisory when `spellHasDamage` false (MA-0348); save_effect prose must byte-carry canonical condition word else `extractConditionsFromSaveEffect` → [] (MV-31).
- Non-canonical prose clauses (cursed/fortify/haunted…) → no te unless a parser exists; MA-0090 = FAIL class: failed save leaves ZERO state; proof = `saveResult-<T>` success:false + no te + log holds only `ability_use`. Prose-only save DC ("spell save DC 23" in description, no numeric `save_dc`) → prompt "DC Unknown", damage abandoned; prose fallback exists only for attack bonus "+N to hit" (`monsterSpellAttackBonus`) — DATA fix (MA-0237/0318/0328/0362).
- Spell ATTACK misroutes to save path when 5e spells.json lacks `attack_type` (findMonsterSpell 5e-first) → fix `attack_type:"ranged"` + numeric row DC (MA-0245). Save-path secondary damage: WAS inert; NOW threaded (MA-0427) via `buildSecondaryDamageTransport` → save options → `applySecondarySaveDamageLeg` (separate roll, per-leg half/full via `computeDamageAfterSave`/`Evasion`, own save-damage log + DualDamageNotice copy). Legacy rows without `damage_dice_secondary` byte-identical. `autoDamageSecondaryFormula` remains the separate ATTACK-path seam — do not conflate. Spellcasting chips need `<strong>`/`<em>` markup — plain-text lists = zero affordances (MA-0421); fake chips from prose emphasis ("**Concentration**") cosmetic; tier header must parse "N/Day Each:" — qualifier parenthetical inside header kills gating (MA-0276); 1/Day uses = `monsterSpellUses` key + `automation blocked` refusal; At-Will ungated by design; 1/Day re-arm GM-side (no rest consumer).
- Legendary header fingerprint (MA-0092/0217/0375/0405): "3 (4 in Lair)" is NAME TEXT; no numeric `uses` → no counter, ungated multi-fire, economy consumers exist but unreached = DATA FAIL. Numeric legendary save chips bypass spend when header lacks uses (MA-0184). Multi-defect prose-threshold rows (MA-0352): threshold kill ungated + half-default + name-text "(1/Day)" = three defects one row. Grapple: attack-HIT grapple prose only lands via authored `hit_conditions:[...]`+`escape_dc` (MA-0010 seam; plain `conditions:[]` NOT consumed); save-leg constrict grapple WORKS live (MA-0368); sustained grapple state-machine zero producers (MA-0287/0288/0354); monster auto-adv-vs-grappled prose inert (PC-feat-gated).
- Monster reactions: no-affordance rows (no attack_bonus/save_dc/dice) = inert unless automation `{type,trigger,effect}` authored — RAW-unlimited honest sentinel `usage:"At Will"`+`uses:999`+1/round latch (MA-0006/0300/0305); gated reactions key ONLY off `automation.effect`; monster Counterspell fixed MA-0013 (gate RAW lastAttack spell-origin; read RAW not normalized wrapper). Recharge IS enforced when authored (`monsterRecharge.js`+`monsterRechargeGate`, spend at picker-open, d6 at owner turn-start, `<slug>_refused`) — do NOT pre-assume inert (MA-0100/0225/0236); recharge on ATTACK rows gated MA-0294; re-confirm of already-open picker bypasses gate (known edge). Missing field per-row still = FAIL. Ungated repeat-fire (MA-0406 decay) = missing spend/spend-gate.
- AoE shapes: Cone/Line/Radius parse (`breathAoeShape`) → SaveAttackAoeModal picker; "N-foot Emanation" does NOT parse → single-target degradation (MA-0317); Line picker gate = MAX feet token (MA-0064); picker PRE-CHECKS can drop targets to 0 → `.dsp-overlay`. `dc_success:"half"` default leaks half on condition-only-success rows (MV-20/MA-0218/0229) — fix DATA `dc_success:"none"` (honest copy both surfaces). Trap rows where save gates a non-damage clause need explicit discriminator, `dc_success:none` insufficient (MA-0298 banishing-claw).
- Caster-dependent EB joins (animate-objects Slam MA-0284/0285): no affordance route (chip needs numeric attack_bonus; no action-level advisory; spell_attack_bonus resolves Spellcasting links only) — adjudicated SKIPPED, honest inert preferred. Attack-wording row + null attack_bonus → chip suppressed by `attackRowMissingToHit` (MA-0286). Conditional charge damage = `conditional_damage:{dice,modifier,damage_type,condition}` + HIT-popup offer `ChargeBonusOffer` (MA-0007). Variant clauses (choose-one effects) = chooser modal + parser + grants; MA-0275 template below.
- Multiattack = header TEXT row, components are individual chips; count enforcement GM-adjudicated (MA-0009/0223); replace-with-spell cast level parses the Spellcasting ROW description (MA-0223). Staged ladders: sleep `staged_sleep:{unconscious_minutes}` (MA-0068), paralysis analogue `staged_paralysis` (MA-0248 paralyzingBreathService), roar `staged_roar`+maxUses stage-swap (MA-0268 — swap description too or re-extraction wrong dice). Frightful Presence = full service `frightfulPresenceService.js` w/ OBJECT `success_immunity`+`repeat_save` (booleans kill it, MA-0147), 24h immunity te, EOT repeat seam.
- Speed/AC/stat clauses live via registered te: `speed_half` (MA-0073), `speed_zero` (MA-0146), `ac_penalty` value:N (MA-0115), `no_reactions`+`no_action_and_bonus_action`+`speed_half` = "Slowed" trio (MA-0087 — slowed is NOT a registered condition, don't add it), `concentration_disadvantage` (MA-0038), `giggling_magic_debuff` subtract-die (MA-0093/0207/0212 per-monster die — fix producer not consumer), `weakening_breath` (MA-0102), `push` marker te (MA-0079/0138 push-only hoist), `no_healing` (MA-0016), `banished_demiplane`/`lair_dream_plane` (MA-0104/0107), `gaze_immunity` (MA-0030), `target_prerequisite:{conditions,by_attacker}` gate (MA-0019), `marked_as_prey`/`pesky_swarm` (MA-0275). te `next_attack_advantage`+vexTarget granted BY a hit self-erases in same attack's post-processing (WM-008); PRE-attack te consumed regardless of hit. Attacker-side adv channel = `vexTarget`.
- FT-007 **Boon of Combat Prowess conversion FIXED 2026-11-06** (`bug-boon-combat-prowess-hit-flag-not-persisted`): conversion now writes back `campaign.lastAttack` + `<attacker>.lastAttackRoll` at conversion time (before Done) with `hit:true, missConverted:true, convertedBy` via `persistConvertedAttackHit` (CharSheet.handlers.js, merged re-read/write lane); the damage merge preserves the flags and `lastAttack.hit` riders (Stunning Strike et al.) admit converted hits. Stroke-of-Luck attack-face conversion rides the same helper. Re-arm gotcha for conversion tests: the boon button vanishes once converted for the rest of the round — bump `combatSummary.round` via scoped PATCH to re-arm for a second conversion. The MISS-popup boon button label renders with a LEADING SPACE (` Boon of Combat Prowess`) — exact-text probes must trim (live 2026-11-06). Feature rows that queue a save can ALSO emit the `automation_info` popup AND the `.sp-overlay` prompt together (Stunning Strike, live 2026-11-06) — close the popup's Done to reach Roll Save/Dismiss. **Sheet-modal two-channel merge law (FIXED 2026-11-06, `bug-sheet-nick-mastery-hit-drops-damage-stage`):** CharSheet renders modals from `{...modalState, ...spellModalState}` where the parent mirror (`charActionsModalState`, update-REPLACED by the host) merges LAST and wins. A modal opened by the pipeline/hook-internal setter writes `modalState` ONLY, so any lingering same-key close-`null` that a modal forwarded into the mirror permanently vetoes the mount — the offer/log fires (`modal:shown`, correct rider payload) while ZERO overlays mount and the paused damage stage never dispatches (✓ HIT, no damage row, no `hp_change`, no console error; reload clears it, one healthy cycle per load). Diagnose by tracing the offer broadcast vs overlay count, not the rider handler. Rule: opens must round-trip BOTH channels, or a mirror `null` must never veto a live local open — enforced by `mergeModalStates` (`char-sheet/modalStateMerge.js`) at both merge sites. Also watch `_cunningStrikeCostUsed`: a jammed cycle can let a full-store snapshot resurrect its pre-reset value.
- No consumers app-wide (advisory/GM-enforced, do NOT rebuild without ticket): grapple state-machine, light/vision levels, fall damage, grid token move, range bands on spells, lair initiative-20, monster 8h recast-block, wall objects, poison "can't regain HP", monster rest-rearm of spell/recharge keys, prone registers no expiry on damageless cones (persistent-prone standard), "shake awake" 5-ft. Healing-pool AMOUNT lane full-HP pool spend + fabricated/self-zero audit rows — LIVE-CONFIRMED 2026-11-13 both faces, FIXED 2026-11-13 (commit 4f8c5d9fa, `bug-amount-heal-lane-charges-pool-on-full-hp-target`): `applyHeal` now re-reads capacity at commit, charges/logs only actual HP restored, and refuses zero-capacity presses (`.healing-restriction-note` `<Target> is already at full HP. No pool spent.`, button disabled on `noHealCapacity` dice-parity, Channel Divinity burn moved after success).

## 7. Victims & rigs
- **Audio play-spy rig (live RG 2026-11-19, Item 10 deep; src-field law sharpened live AAA 2026-11-20):** wrap `HTMLMediaElement.prototype.play` (and `.pause`) pushing `{src (origin-stripped — read `this.src` AT PLAY TIME: the pooled cue elements can answer empty `currentSrc` in the wrapper, so a currentSrc-only spy counts plays but cannot name the wav), el:<WeakMap counter>, loop}` — the element counter proves the ONE-pooled-element-per-key dedupe (re-fire = pause→play, same id; total ids never grow for repeats). A `page.reload()` KILLS the wrapper and module pool — reinstall after reload and treat pre-reload events as unobservable (an empty buffer read is a DEAD SPY until you prove it live, e.g. one probe `new Audio(...).play()`). Cheapest cue producers, zero combat: combat cues = `POST /api/campaigns/:c/log {type:'roll',rollType:'attack',hit:false[,isCrit|isNatural1],message:'…'}` lands the cue on every mounted tab (the `message` became MANDATORY with the 2026-11-20 gate, see below) — MOUNT LAW CORRECTED IN PLACE (improve 2026-11-20 audio-cue-app-level, owner ruling): the lane mounts ONCE at APP level — `src/components/common/CombatCues.jsx` (moved out of `components/initiative/`), beside the root Subscriber/MapContextSync campaign consumers — so ANY mounted campaign tab hears cues in ANY view (Characters/Map/Log/Initiative), exactly ONE cue per echoed row per client (no Initiative doubling), and the only cue-silent faces are the campaign-select screen and the per-tab SFX-off toggle; SHARPENED (live YY 2026-11-20, game-sounds deepening bf964645e) the decoder is `cueForLogEntry`; CORRECTED IN PLACE (improve 2026-11-20 log-post-schema-gate) the log POST is NO LONGER schema-free — it is schema-GATED (`ALLOWED_LOG_TYPES` ~51-type allowlist + non-empty human-readable text required in one of `message/description/noteText/summary/text`; forged shapes 400 origin-neutral, never 403; legit allowlisted rows with extra fields pass byte-identically). SHARPENED (live ZZ 2026-11-20): the synthetic rig shapes above are NO LONGER legal bare — `{type:'roll',rollType:'save',saveResult:'success'}`, `{type:'hp_change',threshold:'dead'}`, `{type:'death_save',result:'dead'}` all 400 without text; EVERY synthetic cue row must carry a `message` (e.g. `message:'ZZ probe'`) — extra fields pass untouched and the decoder/rendering ignore it, so all cue faces ride unchanged with the added message. FIXED 2026-11-20 (`fix log-schema-gate-rejects-app-own-combat-rows`): real combat commits LAND and play cues again — client producers gain a stamped plain-English `message` in `logService.addEntry` (see the Log POST schema-gate law), live play-spy heard miss.wav/hit.wav/fumble.wav per scimitar commit and player-death.wav on the death_save finality row; the gate is UNCHANGED, so textless rows POSTed DIRECT to `/api/…/log` (synthetic rigs, non-logService lanes) still honestly 400 — synthetic cue rows must keep carrying their own `message` (the GM-HP-override lane carries `message:'GM HP override'` and always lands). `{rollType:'save',saveResult:'success'|'failure'}` → save-success/save-failure wav, `{type:'hp_change',threshold:'dead'}` → monster-death, +`isPlayer:true` → player-down, `{type:'death_save',result:'dead'}` → player-death; silent faces: `name:'Concentration Save'` saves, special saveResults (`soulstitch_auto_success`), initiative rolls, other thresholds, un-decodable rows. REAL-PRODUCER isPlayer leg: GM card HP input to 0 on a player (`input[aria-label="<Name> current HP"]`, trusted type+Enter) writes `sourceName:'GM HP override'` hp_change with `threshold:'dead'`+`isPlayer:true` and plays player-down; restore plays `heal.wav`; ~5-10 s LATER a `.dsp-overlay` Death Saving Throw prompt arms and INTERCEPTS all card clicks (cancel `.dsp-cancel-btn` before touching any card again; the delay false-negatives a 2.5 s post-commit probe). Cross-client: every mounted campaign-tab shell plays its OWN cue from the SSE log echo, exactly once, in ANY view (fanout re-proved 2-tab post-app-level, live 2026-11-20 improve audio-cue-app-level); tabs in one context SHARE localStorage, so an sfx-off isolation probe needs an isolatedContext, not a second same-origin tab — CLEANEST RIG (same run): `browser.newContext()` + `ctx.addInitScript(() => localStorage.setItem('sfx-enabled','0'))` BEFORE the first goto (a fresh context still has `hasBeenActive===true`, so the toggle is the only thing gating — exactly the law under test), then witness A-off/B-on fanout; destroy the throwaway context at cleanup so no stray `sfx-enabled` rides the shared profile. Heal cue = bucket `currentHitPoints` bump (cs-row PATCH does nothing); levelup cue = XP modal Apply past the roster-level floor (milestone char: untick → Apply deltas; parity/below-roster is SILENT post-a9779b3ec); weather bed = outdoor map doc-PUT `weather:{condition:'rain'}` + mounted HexMap (`clear` plays nothing; stop is instant on SSE clear OR unmount; GM editor tab DOES consume external weather echoes). Always restore `Math.random` and drain the overlay before touching cards again (a dwelling `.dsp-overlay` intercepts clicks).

## 8. Variant-clause chooser recipe (canonical choose-one template)

## 9. Known-accepted residuals (do not chase; note in reports)
- **Placeholder re-bake RESIDUAL RETIRED (2026-11-18 improve placeholder-foes-victory-bank):** the old by-design law (`clearCombat(characters, numOfNpc)` re-seeding bare `+NPC` placeholders so "NPC 1" survived every Clear, and the bank counting a carried placeholder as a foe) is DEAD — `numOfNpc` was deleted from `clearCombat`/`setupCreatures`/the handler factory (Clear rebuilds players-only; a post-Clear board has ZERO `NPC <i>` rows). Bare placeholders (`src/services/combat/npcPlaceholder.js::isBareNpcPlaceholder` — name shape `NPC \d+` + no `monsterIndex`; a RENAMED placeholder or index-less species NPC is a real foe) are class-excluded from the victory bank foes/defeated/fled math and from `trackerHasFoes` in `useTvPresentation.js`, so they can neither kill the banner nor fake a "drove off" line; attacking a placeholder does not un-bare it. Clear-confirm copy re-honestened to "removes every monster … bare +NPC placeholder slots included" (pinned `ClearCombatConfirmModal.test.jsx`).
- **Victory banner auto-hides the instant REAL foes rejoin the board** (`victoryVisible = lastCombatResult && celebrated && !trackerHasFoes && undismissed`; since 2026-11-18 `trackerHasFoes` counts real foes only — bare placeholders never hide it): RECIPE (refined 2026-11-08 improve banner-controls-overlap, placeholder leg retired 2026-11-18): on a foes-free board ONE Clear stamps the result and mounts the banner — adding a `+ NPC` before Clear no longer spoils the ride (it neither gates the banner nor enters the counts). Layout law now pinned (`VictoryBanner.no-capture.test.jsx`): card rides an upper band (`align-items:flex-start` + vh top offset), card BODY passes pointer events through — only `.victory-close` (×) and Continue capture.
- Free-cast/stamp leak family + AoE-save Cancel keeps paid charge (slot-rollback ticket owed). Concentration spells that skip storage.set stale. `remove_*` clock-effect types: SHARPENED live-verified OC 2026-11-19 — the only no-consumer producers are `remove_grease_area`/`remove_sleet_storm_area` (ABSENT from `EXPIRATION_HANDLERS`, and `greaseAreaSaveHandler.handle.test.js` ASSERTS the absence); their recurring-save consumers (`processGreaseAreaSave` + sleet twin) have ZERO call sites app-wide, and the 2024 ruleset routes Grease as plain `save_only` (live: 2024 wizard press = generic description popup, zero `_grease_*` key/clock/ability_use row — only 5e `public/data/spells.json` authors `grease_area_save`), so the residual is a ghost tracking key with zero gameplay harm (stands until rest/admin clear). Un-authored 1/Day monster keys survive LR. Monster 8h-block advisory. Spell-attack context seam (cover/melee-disadv/range-band) absent — reverse Spell Sniper leak into weapons live. `spiritual_weapon` PC seams. Androsphinx MA-0269-71 + MA-0259 manifest stableKey re-stamps owned by orchestrator.
- Full residual census + superseded-era detail lives in the archive (grep before re-discovering).
- Bug files belong in `.opencode/plans/`, never `docs/`.

Same text authored as `damage_dice_secondary` = engine ADDS it every hit (additive transport buildSecondaryDamageTransport MonsterCardModal.jsx:831-845, unconditional) = over-dealt FAIL(a). WITHOUT save_margin, extractConditionsFromSaveEffect whole-string word-scan grants ALL named conditions on EVERY fail = live OVER-grant (not advisory).
