Refusal slug derives from ACTION NAME (`ethereality_refused` vs `etherealness_refused`); automationDetail from effect key (`already_ethereal`) — pin both. Arm predicate: `row.automation.type==='monster_self_buff' && effect` (monsterSelfBuff.js:25); resolver never reads spellcasting_ability on self-buff rows; no whole-DB self-buff armed census exists. **monster_shape_shift**: `automation:{type:"monster_shape_shift",effect:"shape_shift",forms:[{name,speed},...,{name:"True Form"}]}` (imp MA-1020; lizardfolk-shaman; oni byte-twins — junk `attack_bonus` key REMOVED, Imp twin = key absent not null). Chip `mc-dice-link-shapeshift`; chooser `.mc-overlay--shape-shift`/`.sp-modal .shape-shift-row`; pick applies IMMEDIATELY (no Done stage); ONE merged cs POST stamps `{speed:{walk:"20 ft."},shapeShiftForm:"Crocodile"}`; True Form reverts + restores. speed stamps ONLY walk/fly/climb — swim-speed forms are inert advisory legs; a form with NO speed/fly/climb reads as True Form revert. already-in-form / re-pick refuses. Size-change stat-swap = §17 residual. Whole-DB armed-census pin in monsterShapeShift.ma1020-imp.test.js must invert same-pass.
- **monster_grant_reaction** (MA-0882, 3rd monster automation type): service src/services/encounters/monsterGrantReaction.js mirrors monsterSelfBuff byte-shape; recharge gate+spend FIRST; refusal `<slug>_refused` popup+log zero-grant; registerTargetEffect on GM-armed cs.targetName + ONE rounds:1 clock; chip reuses mc-dice-link + mc-dice-link-spell-spent base classes (no new CSS). **monster_redirect_attack**: chip arms off automation.effect (§6); press-over-pending via link.click(); stamps pendingRedirect (GM-armed ally via cs.targetName; refuse self/absent/no_pending_attack zero-spend); consumer swaps victim to ally + lastAttack redirected/retarget fields + `redirect_attack_applied`; te `redirect_attack` rounds:1; round-latch refuse. CRITICAL: resolve consumer MUST identity-gate `context.targetName===pending.originalTarget` (attacker-only match hijacks re-armed attacks).
- **guardian_protection (defender AC bond)**: `automation:{type:"reaction",trigger:"attacked_by_hit",effect:"guardian_protection",acBonus:5}` + At Will/uses:999 → registry entry + dispatcher branch → monsterGuardianProtection.js arms ONE-SHOT buff on GM-pressed defender → `_guardianProtectionAcBonus` fold channel (loggedDiceRollUtils/hitResolution) → consume at attackPostProcessing (oneShot:true). Refusals `no_pending_attack`/`round` log-only. Spell-origin twin: `trigger:"spell_miss_or_save_success",effect:"spell_reflection"` + RAW_EVENT_GATE_RESOLVERS flat-dispatcher; gate = round latch → spell-origin lastAttack aimed at monster (hit:false OR saveResult:'success') → uses → caster-active; gated row suppresses generic chips (gate is sole press surface). **buff-the-roller reaction**: `automation:{type:"reaction",trigger:"ability_check_or_save",effect:"burst_of_ingenuity",bonus:2,range_ft:30}` + numeric uses:2/maxUses:2 → gated chip "…(2 left)" → arms ONE-SHOT activeBuffs{saveBonus:2,oneShot} on press-selected roller → folds at d20RollComputation (bonusDetail "+2 [Burst of Ingenuity]") + saveProcessing/SavePromptModal (burstOfIngenuitySaveBonus); consume on resolve not press; refusals no_target/round/uses log-only.
- **Shield reaction (dedicated resolver)**: `{type:"reaction",trigger:"targeted_by_spell",effect:"shield",acBonus:5}` + At Will/999 (Mind Flayer Arcanist block). Registry Helpers:1026 → resolveMonsterShieldReaction :1797 → buildShieldBuff acBonus||5 → +5 fold getShieldAcBonus (loggedDiceRollUtils:49 @ useLoggedDiceRollAttack:481) → one-shot consume attackPostProcessing:222. Gate = isSpellOriginLastAttack REQUIRED (pending window damageApplied:true = too late); shieldResolved one-per-spell. PC Shield-spell buff is PERSISTENT — monster consume MUST filter `effect==='shield' && oneShot:true` (stamp the flag) or you strip PC shields. `"shield"` effect was grep-ZERO in GATED census before MA-1170. **counterspell**: registered (GATED_MONSTER_REACTIONS, trigger `enemy_spell_cast`, live resolver :1977). Ledger: ability_use "auto-countered (lv<3)" or "ability check…" + lastAttack{counterspellResolved:true,counterspellCheckFailed} + monsterReactionUses.counterspell spend + `_counterspell_usedRound` latch; 2nd click same cast refuses reason 'countered' (identity, before round-latch); refire needs fresh spell-origin lastAttack seed + round bump + reload (seedCampaignRuntimeData seeds once/load). Auto-counter (<3) rolls NO d20 — absence of roll entry is correct. Reaction-less monsters stamp generic `pendingExpirations` scaffold at own turn-start — exclude from latch audits.
- **jinx_negate (pending-miss conversion)**: GATED_MONSTER_REACTIONS gains jinx_negate (no te, parry/hellish pattern); monsterJinx.js gates round-latch→uses→pending-window identity lastAttack{target:hexer,hit:true,damageApplied:false} (refusals LOG-ONLY so Done popup survives); inline machine-stamped ATTACKER WIS save; FAIL→campaign.pendingJinx; handlePlainDamage.consumePendingJinxOnResolve flips pending to hit:false/jinx_negated/zero-damage BEFORE Done. Generic DC chip still renders alongside gated chip — gated is canonical. **hellish_rebuke**: `automation{type:reaction,trigger:takes_damage,effect:hellish_rebuke,saveType:DEX,saveDc,dcSuccess:half,damageExpression,damageType}` + At Will/999 (Azer byte-shape; existing row keys kept, block inserted before them). TIMING ≠ parry: gate requires damage ALREADY COMMITTED (actualDamage>0) — press defender chip AFTER attacker's Done, same round. Save rides GM `.sp-modal` prompt seam even for EB-NPC victims. Refusal log-only, NO popup; chip counter updates in place at first spend; `_hellish_rebuke_usedRound` latch cleared by sanctioned cs POST round:N.
- **split (advisory-by-design)**: `automation{type:"reaction",trigger:"bloodied_or_lightning_slashing",effect:"split",minHp:10,damageTypes:["Lightning","Slashing"]}` (Black Pudding verbatim); consumer live (resolveMonsterSplit :1551, splitGate :1515 size+bloodied+damageTypes, eachHp floor(hp/2), `split_refused`) but NO spawn subsystem — GM adds halves via EB (§17-sanctioned); prose hardcodes "Black Puddings" cosmetic. **utility spell cast lane**: isUtilitySpellCastRow predicate (name≠Spellcasting, spell_save_dc≠null, no save_dc/attack/dice/automation/zone/rays) + resolvable-name guard (5e-then-2024) + monsterUtilitySpellCast.js resolve (recharge refuse/spend + advisory cast record); widened to (spell_save_dc || spellcasting_ability) + uses-leg spend. Whole-DB census ['ice-devil/Ice Wall','ice-mephit/Fog Cloud','imp/Invisibility'] — keep as regression guard. If a real applicator can serve a row the widened predicate arms, migrate + invert census pins same pass.
- **Spellcasting markup**: spell names must be `<strong>`-wrapped — extractSpellNamesFromSpellcasting (MonsterCardHelpers.js:356/:392) harvests ONLY from `<strong>`/`<em>` markup; headers-only or plain-text rows = zero `.mc-dice-link-spell` chips, casts inert, 1/Day tracking dead (extractSpellcastingSpellUses binds marked names only) = FAIL(b)/DATA. Fix = djinni MA-0611 wrap; house-style `<strong>Tier:</strong> <em>Spell</em>` INLINE without `<br>` for originally-inline prose (stripTags byte-equality pinned — yochlol/yuan-ti); `<br>` only for originally-multiline prose. "(level N version)" OUTSIDE the tag; spellCastLevelFromSpellcasting parses it and damage_at_slot_level serves the cast LIVE. "1/Day:" header binds uses-gate (binds WITHOUT "Each", optional group Helpers:440). Spellcasting rows NEVER render DC chips (MonsterAction.jsx:390 isSpellcastingRow early-returns SpellCastLinks before ActionSaveRoll — disk save_dc structurally unrenderable, MA-1294). Spell name canonicalization trap: propose fixes against real spells.json index ("Befuddle ment"→"Befuddlement" 2024-only; findMonsterSpell MonsterCardModal.jsx:1218 5e-exact-first then 2024-exact fallback for possessive renames like "Tasha's Hideous Laughter").
- **uses/usage gates**: numeric `uses`/`maxUses` REQUIRED. `uses:"N/Day"` STRING = Number NaN → no gate/counter EVER (no N/Day-string parser outside spell lanes); `usage:{type:"per day",times:1}` dict renders cosmetic "(1/Day)" only; `usage`-string on usage-only rows renders NOTHING (formatActionUsage reads action.usage only, MonsterCardHelpers.js:1973). Recharge structured dict `usage:{type:"recharge on roll",dice:"1d6",min_value:5}` SILENTLY ARMS the gate (rechargeUsageOf monsterRecharge.js:30-45/:39) — disk-check "At Will" claims before ticketing. Flat `recharge:"5-6"`/`recharge:"1"` fields ARE enforced live (spend, same-round refuse `.mc-recharge-refusal` + `<slug>_refused`, d6 recovery at own turn-start; threshold 1 = certain recovery — judge economy by faces not probability). §6 sentinel `usage:"At Will"+uses/maxUses:999` on advisory rows is inert-honest (no consumer without automation.effect).
- **advisory seam (generic, record-only)**: top-level flat `advisory:"<snake_key>"` (+optional flat `advisory_message`) — AdvisoryLink arms iff `!!row.advisory && !legendaryGate` (MonsterAction.jsx:335-343 → monsterActionAdvisory.js); value UNINSPECTED, no key registry, category-agnostic; popup + ability_use advisory log, ZERO rolls/lastAttack/te writes; reactions[] rows route through shared MonsterAction renderer (zero-code twins). Nested `advisory:{...}` dict = grep-zero consumer; `advisory:true` BOOLEAN renders "casts true" (dishonest). On legendary rows advisory PREEMPTS numeric/delegate legs. Movement-row fix = strip junk `attack_bonus` + advisory. Default copy "…GM-enforced (no engine consumer for this mechanic)." is honest for option-A rows.
- **Lair rows**: clickable iff named+structured (`typeof la==='string'||!isLairRowClickable` inert, MonsterCardBody.jsx:357; monsterLairActions.js:25). Advisory lair dict `{name,advisory,advisory_message,description}` for no-consumer boons; named save dict `{name,save_dc,save_type,dc_success:"none",damage_dice_primary:<static rollable>,save_effect}` for complete-spec rows; zone dict `{radius_ft,no_save:true,noun,effect_key:'lair_fog_cloud',advisory}` (+duration) → zoneOnly picker + tracking `_<effect>_<Monster>`; `advisory` HIJACKS routing before save_dc (coexist NOT). Cloud/lair te (`lair_insect_cloud` :1200, `lair_sand_cloud` :1209 registered-unspawned) needs FULL zone template (radius_ft+repeat_turn_end), not name-only. Young-dragon lair duplicated-adult-prose bare-strings/nameless dicts = inert family; adults' structured rows are fix templates; save-with-damage lair convention = damage_dice_primary + damage_type_primary + explicit dc_success:"half" (plain `damage_type` grep-zero on lair twins).
- **composite attack+save misroute fix**: bite-style "Hit: 1 Piercing plus 2d4 Poison (save DC)" needs `damage_dice_primary:"<attack flat/dice>"` + attack_type + `damage_dice_secondary:"<save dice>"` (Giant Poisonous Snake authors primary Piercing + secondary 3d6). Flat-primary + dice-secondary is now primary-clause-scoped (extractFlatHitDamage guard /(?:Hit|Failure|Success):\s*\d+\s*\(\s*\d+d\d+/) — flat "1" + threaded damage_dice_secondary resolve combined_damage_roll (flat never crit-doubled, secondary doubled); extractDamageDiceFromDescription lives in MonsterCardModal.jsx (not Helpers). Row with primary holding SAVE dice = attack chip deals it full ungated. `buildAttackChipSaveOptions` nulls saveDc on the attack chip (MA-0551, MonsterCardModal.jsx:981) — rider/save chip is standalone. **multi-dice primaries**: ` plus ` word-split REQUIRED ("1d6 + 3 plus 1d8" = prose byte-shape); `+`-joined multi-dice ("1d6 + 3 + 1d8") is UNPARSEABLE (parseExpression diceRoller.js:42 accepts ONE dice term → canRollExpression false → blocked zero-damage, WORSE than status quo). ALWAYS verify canRollExpression on proposed bytes first.
- **damageless hit-clause arm**: `hit_conditions` on a FORMULA-LESS attack row was structurally inert (buildAutoDamage early-returns) — arm with constant `'0'` formula when hitClause stands w/o formula (MA-0322 parseConstant lineage; crit stays 0); clause hits then log total:0 + hp_change delta:0 + stage-2 "0 damage" popup. All 76 prior hit_conditions rows had dice (zero precedent before MA-1400). Flat-prose rows may OMIT damage_dice_primary — extractFlatHitDamage supplies constant (casing of lowercase type token moot; damage entry damageType:null by design on flat rows).
- **variants[] (generic choose-one save chooser)**: structured row `variants:[...]` payload + parseSaveVariantChooser (structured-key-only, byte-inert elsewhere) → SaveVariantChooserModal (stage before picker) → saveVariant threaded handleSaveRoll→SaveAttackAoeModal resolveSaveFailGrant → applySaveVariantClockGrant fail-only ONE clock; per-variant dc_success lives IN the payload (row-level dc_success must stay variant-neutral); recharge refusal precedes chooser; Skip = `save_variant_declined` zero-spend. **saveLegIsConditionRider**: keys on damage-POOL evidence (rollable dice AND word "damage", saveLegCarriesDamagePool) — termination-clause prose ("ends early if takes any damage") no longer disarms rider chip. **DC0 gate (canonical)**: `Number(save_dc) > 0` applied at 8 sites (ActionSaveRoll, ActionDamageLinks, SpellOrSaveLinks, LegendarySpendLink, breathAoeShape via hoisted hasRealSaveDc, resolveBlockSaveDcSuccess, legendary mechanic resolve, buildSaveOptions.dcSuccess, handleDamage arm) — MA-0551/MA-1071. save_dc:0/save_type:"" decoy fields (~200-row household authoring) stay in DATA (strip = mass rewrite; code-gate is the lane). Save chips are `span.mc-dice-link-save-clickable`; gated reaction chips arm ONLY via automation.effect.

## 11. PC feature/spell/feat lanes — durable seams
- **Ruleset-switch ledger WITH real spells rig (live RG 2026-11-19, PT#4 closed):** the switch exists ONLY in a NEW-build wizard (edit-mode `WizardSidebar` hides the Ruleset step — a SAVED character has no ruleset-change lane at all; don't hunt for one). Fast producer of a spell-carrying draft: `Add Character` → `.rules-option` Quick build + 2024 → footer `Next` per step, and at `Step 6: Class` set the class `<select>` to `Wizard` (native setter + change) — the quick draft then auto-seeds 7 spells (cantrips 3/3 + prepared 4/4, including 2024-only `Blade Ward`/`Chromatic Orb` — `public/data/spells.json` genuinely lacks Hex/Chromatic Orb/Blade Ward/Mind Sliver/Tasha's, so a 2024→5e flip honestly drops them); BACK-jumps via `.wizard-sidebar button` (textContent fuses the number: `1Ruleset`) are ungated (forward jumps are gated in fresh builds); clicking the 5e `.rules-option` opens the `.modal-shell-overlay` ledger — verify group arithmetic sums to the header (`6 kept, 3 removed` = 5+1 / 2+0+1), drop reason copy `not in the 5th Edition (5e) spell data`, Alert KEPT, `NAME_LIST_VISIBLE_CAP=6` merges kept+dropped per group and folds the overflow into a NON-interactive `+N more <singular>` span (names beyond 6 per group are never disclosed — backlog); `Cancel` is INERT (ruleset card + draft unchanged); abandon = header × → native `Discard changes to "<draft>"?` (dialog → `browser_handle_dialog` follow-up). Sheet edit entry aria: `button[aria-label='Edit "<Name>"']` — `getByRole('button',{name:'Edit',exact:true})` MISSes (accname is the full `Edit "X"`).
- **BI grant-chooser radios carry HP in the aria-label (live 2026-11-17 fix bi-defense-prompt-producer-orphaned):** the Bardic Inspiration chooser's target radios are labelled `Select <Name>, <N>% HP` — an exact `input[aria-label="Select <Name>"]` probe TIMES OUT; match by prefix (`[aria-label^="Select <Name>,"]` or `startsWith`) — same aria-carries-dynamic-state family as the HoH picker law in §2.
- **Wild Shape cast/release rig (live CE 2026-11-17, first board ride):** sheet `b.clickable` row `Wild Shape:` (exact text) → chooser `.sp-overlay` (head `Wild Shape`, copy `Choose a beast form (CR 1 or lower)`); beast cards are `div.wild-shape-beast-item` (`.wild-shape-beast-name` carries `Bat` + a `CR 0` child — a leaf exact-`>Bat<` click probe finds nothing clickable); clicking a card ENABLES the `.sp-roll-btn` (`Wild Shape`, disabled until selection; `Cancel` = `.sp-dismiss-btn`). Commit lands RUNTIME-ONLY (char file untouched): the druid's OWN combatSummary row transforms in place (`wildShapeSource`, `beastIndex`, `beastName` — no extra board row) + `activeBuffs[{effect:'shape_shift', blocksSpellcasting:true, duration:'half_druid_level_hours'}]` + `wildShapeUses` spent (NO refund on release, 2024 law); `pendingExpirations` stays []. Mounted board face: the card renders the BEAST (`CreatureCard.jsx` formSource precedence — caster `imagePath` skipped, name = `beastName`, remote bestiary image loads). Release = card Wild Shape badge's `.creature-badge-remove` (title `Remove effect`) → beast keys + buffs stripped, card re-anchors to caster (HP input back to sheet value). Honest log pair: `ability_use` `"<Druid> activated Wild Shape as Bat (CR 0)."` + `condition` `action:'target-effect-removed'`. NOTE `GET /change-data/<Char>` single-key lane does NOT exist (404, console error is the PROBE not the app) — read the whole `/change-data` bucket.
- **Castable spell-row testids + routing rule (shipped 2026-11-15 improve caster-row-testid):** every castable spell row across ALL four sheet lanes (Actions grid `CharActions`, Bonus Actions, Reactions, Spells table `char-spells/CharSpells`) stamps `data-testid="castable-row-<slug>"` from ONE helper `src/services/shared/castableRowTestId.js` (name→slug: lowercase, non-alphanumerics→`-`; e.g. `[data-testid=castable-row-revivify]` or prefix `querySelectorAll('[data-testid^=castable-row-]')` for a whole-sheet census) — the old both-lanes-per-caster census + `title="Open spell details to cast"` discovery is obsolete. Routing rule the testid replaces: lane placement is decided by `src/services/ui/spellSectionUtils.js` (getActionSpellNames/getBonusActionSpellNames/getReactionSpellNames — casting_time + damage presence), which is why same-casting-time spells split across fixtures. Grayed non-prepared wizard rows carry the SAME testid (class `not-castable` carries state); a testid-keyed click that opens nothing may have hit a grayed row, not a false negative. 5e fixture censuses live: War_Cleric 21 stamped rows (incl. Revivify in the Actions grid), Wild_Sage_Druid 29 (Spells table).
- **Uses-latch key convention (shipped 2026-11-15 improve uses-latch-key-camel-convention, owner ruling "full conversion, no backward-compat reads"):** every derived feature-uses runtime key comes from the ONE exported helper `getRuntimeUsesKey(featureName)` in `src/services/shared/usesKey.js` — camelCase stem + `Uses` ("Relentless Rage"→`relentlessRageUses`, "Wholeness of Body"→`wholenessOfBodyUses`; apostrophes stripped → `naturesVeilUses`/`thiefsReflexesUses`; `filter(Boolean)` guards whitespace — `'  Flurry  '`→`flurryUses` is the pinned face). Matches the data-declared `resourceKey`s in `public/data/2024/classes.json`; `auto.resourceKey ||` precedence intact (declared wins). Single-word stems stay all-lowercase BY DESIGN (`tirelessUses`, `stonecunningUses`, `indomitableUses`) — NOT legacy. Zero inline `+'Uses'` derivations remain (grep-guard); `+'Pool'` lane deliberately out of scope. Project campaign data migrated once on disk (change-data + char-JSON `_trackedResources` ghosts; canonical twin wins, legacy deleted / renamed when twin absent) — probes can trust camelCase everywhere. Cheapest live uses-latch rig: EvasiveFighter `Action Surge:` (derived-only lane, extra_action, one trusted `b.clickable` click + `.popup-close-btn` Done → `actionSurgeUses` lands in change-data ~2 s), then GM HP-editor → `Second Wind:` for a declared-key spend; `Rage of the Gods` is Zealot-lv18-only (unreachable on the Berserker fixture).
- **Tracked-resource `(cur/max)` clamp laws (read layer shipped `18cf0f422` + display layer shipped 2026-11-14 improve tracked-resource-display-clamp, both live-verified KB 2026-11-14):** `useTrackedResource.resolveCurrent` now runs finite stored bucket values through `clampToMax` = `Math.min(stored, maxGetter())` (non-numeric latches/null pass through; `CLAMP_EXEMPT_KEYS = ['currentHitPoints','hitPoints']` — HP exempt by owner ruling), NO read-side write-back; the level-down overshoot face is DEAD (stored 95 at L13 → badge `65/65`, modal `Pool: 65 / 65 HP`, amount `max="65"`, commit ≤65), and the FIRST SPEND SELF-HEALS the bucket (`poolAfter = clamped safePool − amount` rewrites 95→3 — never expect a stale oversize value to survive a commit). `TrackedResourceInput` additionally renders `Math.min(current,max)` only when BOTH are finite (belt-and-braces over the `playerStats._trackedResources[key].current` producer branch which still passes through unclamped — live face producer-unfindable, unit-pinned); the clamp is render-only, ZERO write-back, and the HP display (`CharHitPoints.jsx`) stays UNCLAMPED (an L13 doc with a 224 bucket honestly shows `Hit Points: 224/147 (cur/max)` — the PT#2 owner-ruling lane). Boolean-latch face: `HiddenInput` renders NOTHING for a boolean current (React skips boolean children) — a latch badge is `Label: /1`, NOT `Label: true/1`; an unguarded `Math.min(true,1)` would poison that face to visible `1/1`, so latch-render pins anchor on the `/1` bytes.
- **Healing-pool / touch-heal lane laws (live EH 2026-11-13):** opener = the sheet's `.char-actions .clickable` row (`Healing Light:` Special Actions for CW, `Lay On Hands:` Bonus Actions for EP, `Hand of Healing:` for Mercy monks) — the summary-grid resource row is DISPLAY-ONLY (trusted click there opens nothing); evaluate `.click()` NO-OPs on the action row (opens only the description popup). OPENER LAW (CORRECTED IN PLACE, improve secondary-target-radio-aria 2026-11-13): a raw `page.mouse.click(rect)` from `getBoundingClientRect()` SILENTLY HITS NOTHING when the row is below the fold (`elementFromPoint` returns null for y > viewport) — use the Playwright locator `.click()` (auto-scrolls, trusted); it is the reliable opener. The aria work's blast radius law: accessible-name changes to `SecondaryTargetModal` only affect lanes rendering the REAL modal with name queries (HealingPool family + `CharBonusActions.lesserRestoration`) — every CharSpells/CharActionModals lane stubs it. Chooser = borrowed `.short-rest-modal` shell (`role=dialog`, labelledby `#healing-pool-target-modal-title`, or `#hand-of-healing-target-modal-title` for the monk picker), label-wrapped radios — SHIPPED 2026-11-13 (improve secondary-target-radio-aria): every candidate radio now carries an explicit `aria-label` mirroring the `Select <Name>` allies-editor precedent (`Select <Name>[, N of M HP | , N% HP]` via `rowAriaLabel` in `SecondaryTargetModal.jsx`, pinned by `SecondaryTargetModal.test.jsx` 4-face set), buttons `Heal`/`Skip` (Skip = self); the monk picker is the LENIENT no-token 5-ft face (every board row passes `collectTouchTargets` when no tokens exist). Dice path: `Roll a d6` presses repeat (Max N dice), `Done` commits + closes; an UN-dismissed result/apply modal PERSISTS across sheet navigation and intercepts sidebar clicks — close it before switching characters. WRITE truth = the target bucket `currentHitPoints` (scoped-POST-able); heals NEVER move `combatSummary` creature rows (GET-proved drift: row 100 vs bucket 134 after real heals) and the bucket hydrates lazily — untouched chars often have `hitPoints` only or no bucket at all, which is what arms the two filed dishonest faces (`bug-healing-light-dice-pool-silent-zero-heal`, `bug-hand-of-healing-modal-overstates-restored-hp`). Pool keys: `healinglightPool`, `layOnHandsPool`; rows `healing_pool {sourceName,featureName,targetName,amount,poolAfter[,diceUsed,rolls]}`; monk lane writes `ability_use` (+`hp_change`) and its log honestly annotates `(Already at full HP)` / `(Regained N HP)`. OVERSTATE LAW DEAD (fixed `d595cff2c`+`e7fb36ef0`, live-verified FX 2026-11-13): the modals now render the clamped `actualHeal` and mirror their audit rows — modal copy == log row on every FX face. Heal a control target by first seeding `{value:{currentHitPoints:N}}` + reload/re-select (§49 mirror law). SHIPPED+LIVE-PINNED 2026-11-13 (improve heal-chooser-bucket-hp): the three heal target-choosers (LoH/Healing-Light `#healing-pool-target-modal-title` + monk `#hand-of-healing-target-modal-title`) now render bucket-first HP via `services/automation/handlers/healing/liveTargetHp.js` (`resolveLiveTargetHp`: bucket `currentHitPoints`/`hitPoints` win; cs row ONLY when bucket missing/`''`/non-numeric — monsters keep the row law via §80 "monster HP runtime keys never exist"), so chooser == the modal one click later (proven 3/3: chooser 110/143==main modal, re-open after a real +10 heal 120/143 while the cs row stayed 100/100; monk result 131/143 = bucket 120+11, never the row-111 face; pinned by `liveTargetHp.test.js`, `healingHandler.touchTargetLiveHp.test.js`, `healingPoolHandler.test.js` creatureTargets-scan legs). CHOOSER DRIFT RIG (cheaper than damage): full-store POST `/combatSummary` {creatures:[{name,type:'player',currentHp,maxHp stale}…]} + full-store POST `/<Char> {value:{currentHitPoints:HIGHER,hitPoints:…}}` BEFORE campaign-select — sheets mount WITHOUT a board (no Initiative visit = no mount-merge eviction), the chooser's `getCombatContext` GET serves the seeded rows verbatim. Mercy rows derive from a disk `class.subclass.name` str.replace on the NEXT sheet mount (no reload needed); restore byte-exact after (md5 `ba4d456c…`). PITFALLS: `button:has-text("Done")` clicked in the same settle tick as a result-modal mount can miss — re-probe overlay count, retry once. HOH SPEND-SEAM LAW (shipped 2026-11-13 improve hoh-prespend-full-hp-gate, supersedes the old "Skip at full HP still spends 1 Focus" pitfall): the HoH row-click NO LONGER pre-spends Focus in `useCharActionsAutomation.spendMonkFocusPoint` (it keeps only the exhausted-pool click refusal + `…_refused` row) — `healingHandler.performMonkHeal`'s `gateMonkHealCommit` is the SOLE Focus writer for the lane (HoUM/patient_defense sole-writer precedent). At CONFIRM time it re-resolves the target bucket-first via `resolveLiveTargetHp` and REFUSES a full-HP target (or drained pool) with an `automation_info` popup the picker renders inline as `.healing-restriction-note` ("X is already at full HP. No focus spent."), picker staying open, ZERO spend / ZERO roll / ZERO row; picker Escape-without-commit now loses 0 Focus (was 1). Skip = self-heal face: refused too when self is at full HP; heals normally (1 spend) when damaged. AUDIT-CASING LAW (shipped 2026-11-13 improve cure-audit-condition-casing): every `condition broken` audit row's `condition` field is the CONDITIONS-catalog DISPLAY LABEL (`conditionLabel()`, `HealingPoolModal.jsx`) — LoH single/batch and Physician's Touch all unified; a raw-key pre-select seed is now INDISTINGUISHABLE from label-cased at the audit layer, so casing regressions (esp. the un-ridable `applyCure` single tier — L14 Restoring Touch replaces it on every 2024 paladin fixture) must be pinned at unit level.
- **HoH picker-stage live echo law (shipped 2026-11-14 improve hoh-picker-live-echo):** `HandOfHealingPickerStage` subscribes `useRuntimeValue('combatSummary','creatures')` and re-anchors each mount-snapshot row's HP pair on the live board via `resolveLiveTargetHp` (bucket `currentHitPoints` canonical for `N`, live cs row supplies `M`/fallbacks); the commit-time full-HP gate in `healingHandler.js` (`resolveMonkHealGateRow`) resolves its row from the SSE-adopted combatData cache FIRST, captured `cs` only as fallback — stale max can no longer refuse a legitimate heal. ROSTER STAYS THE OPEN-MOMENT SNAPSHOT: board creatures added mid-dwell never appear as new radios (range/liveness law), only existing rows' HP re-anchor — reopen to mount new rows. RIG PITFALL: `App.jsx seedCharacterRuntimeData` seeds EVERY char's `_trackedResources` (incl. `currentHitPoints`) into the client store at campaign-select with NO server key, so a cs-row `currentHp` PATCH honestly does NOT move a real PC's aria `N` (bucket canonical) — for a pure cs-driven `N of M HP` live face use a board-only player-type row (e.g. `Recruit_Uno` via `POST /combatSummary`; its heal `oldHp` resolves bucket `?? 0` — PT#3 write-side family). Aria shape law: MONSTER-type cs rows echo `Select <Name>, <pct>% HP` + `.secondary-target-hp` = `36%`; the `N of M HP` aria form is player-type rows only.
- **Post-fix healing-lane laws (live FX 2026-11-13):** (1) GATE probes: the full-HP dice gate is a REAL `disabled` attribute — evaluate `.click()` brute-force on it lands nothing (zero roll-details mount, zero POST, pool GET-stable), so a "button inert" reading is now the PASS face, and the copy is `X is already at full HP. No die spent.` / `These rolls already fill X to maximum HP — further dice would restore nothing.`; capacity-clamp commits write `amount` = clamped heal (`Rolled 3d6: 1 + 2 + 3 = 4 HP to restore` prints 4, not 6). (2) DWELL law, CORRECTED IN PLACE (shipped improve heal-modal-live-hp-echo; EXTENDED 2026-11-14 improve cure-echo-live-conditions): mounted heal UI is NO LONGER display-stale by design — HealingPoolModal main + both heal choosers (via `SecondaryTargetModal liveHp` / `LiveHpTargetRow`) subscribe to the target's `currentHitPoints`/`hitPoints` buckets and re-render header, visible HP text, radio `aria-label` and the full-HP gate on an external mid-dwell PATCH within one SSE tick; the echo subscription now ALSO covers `activeConditions` — Cure Conditions sections on BOTH tiers mount/retire `button.char-btn` entries live on external writes (full-store POST), a selected-but-externally-cured entry prunes `Cure Selected (…)` to disabled 0, the whole section retires with its last entry, and re-selecting a target re-anchors the sections (pins: `HealingPoolModal.live-conditions-echo.test.jsx`; snapshot consumers of SecondaryTargetModal, i.e. non-heal lanes, still render open-moment rows byte-identically). Commit-time truth was already fixed by `88daae2be` (`Done` re-resolves via `resolveTargetCurrentHp`, never the render closure; the monk lane commits on the picker click so it has no dwell window). (3) Cure tiers: `Restoring Touch` is the 2024 PALADIN LEVEL-14 CLASS feature (`Paladin/class_levels/13`) — any L14+ 2024 paladin rides the BATCH tier (`Cure Conditions (N HP each)` filtered to the target's ACTUAL conditions, label-cased; commit = one `condition broken` row each, featureName = the pool feature); the `!hasRestoringTouch && alsoCures` SINGLE tier (every genuine sub-L14 2024 Paladin rides it) DID render unfiltered and charge+log on unafflicted targets — FIXED 2026-11-13 (commit 615a7580a, `bug-single-cure-tier-unfiltered-costs-pool-and-fakes-audit-row`): it now renders the batch tier's filtered `curableEntries` (catalog-cased labels, key-deduped) and `applyCure` re-reads the live bucket at commit, refusing a no-match press with ZERO pool spend / zero target write / zero audit row plus inline `.healing-restriction-note` `<Target> is not affected by <Label>. No pool spent.` — the note renders as a SIBLING of the section (the re-filtered section unmounts and would swallow an inside-note). (4) `bloodiedOnly` face = Preserve Life via a sanctioned `War_Cleric` disk swap `Light Domain`↔`Life Domain` (single occurrence, md5 `1a4c9643…`); its commit consumes 1 Channel Divinity (`channelDivinityCharges` bucket key + `(N/M remaining)` ability_use row) and the gate RE-CLOSES when the target leaves bloodied mid-modal (honest). (5) Physician's Touch (Mercy result modal, POST-`544b9cbe2` laws, live-verified KB 2026-11-14): options track the target's `activeConditions` BUCKET live (echo subscription) — an external mid-dwell cure RETIRES the `Remove <Condition>` button in one SSE tick; an external ADD surfaces a new button; AUTO-CURE IS MOUNT-ONLY (owner decision 2026-11-14, shipped improve hoh-autocure-mount-only): the effect fires solely on the first render after mount from the MOUNT-time curable set; an echo shrinking the set to exactly ONE never spends the touch — the section re-renders with the single `Remove <Label>` button and copy `Select the condition to remove with the free Physician’s Touch:` (zero write/row/spend), and pressing it cures honestly. Mount-with-one still auto-cures. The old `curedOnMount` spent-latch is gone (echo cannot cure at all). CS ROW IS SUBSCRIBED TOO (shipped 2026-11-14 improve hoh-cs-condition-echo, live-pinned): `PhysiciansTouchSection` additionally reads `useRuntimeValue('combatSummary','creatures')`, and SSE adoption refreshes the combatData cache `getTargetConditions` merges from (bucket ∪ cs-row) BEFORE the store notify, so a cs-row condition cure/add RETIRES/surfaces `Remove <Label>` buttons within one tick (write→render resolved <5 ms — measure sub-frame latency by POSTing from `page.evaluate` and rAF-polling the DOM in the same context). The commit guard STANDS: a stale button now arises only in the pre-echo click race, and the press still REFUSES at commit via the guard re-read with sibling `.healing-restriction-note` `<Target> is not affected by <Label>.`, ZERO write, ZERO row (copy family with 615a7580a); holding a stale button deterministically in tests requires mutating the combatData board WITHOUT issuing the `change-<c>-combatSummary` echo. After a CLICKED cure the cured-panel early-return holds: later mid-dwell arrivals (bucket or cs) produce NO second cure, NO new buttons (`Done` only), panel stays (echo-cannot-cure makes the retired spent-latch redundant). Escape on the multi-option picker still dismisses the modal ZERO-CURE; audit `condition` carries the catalog display label from every cure writer (PoT/LoH single/batch — the per-lane casing split was fixed in 4aee89a64). Picker `Heal` locator: getByRole exact — `button`+hasText `/^Heal$/` TIMES OUT on this modal. (6) SEED-STABILITY pitfall: campaign RE-ENTRY re-bakes the auto-selected char's bucket to sheet truth over a previously seeded `currentHitPoints` (witnessed 110→136) — seed scalars AFTER load+select, arrays still need full-store POST + reload/re-select. (7) BATCH-CURE WRITE SHAPE (shipped improve batch-cure-single-write): a confirmed N-condition batch = exactly ONE merged `setRuntimeValue('activeConditions')` (commit-time re-read, all selections removed at once) + ONE pool write — the old N-sequential-full-bucket-POSTs face is dead, pinned by the re-titled `HealingPoolModal.curing.test.jsx` merged-write tests; audit stays N `condition broken` rows. PROBE LAWS (same run): the sheet row label is `Lay On Hands:` (colon) — a document-wide `startsWith('Lay On Hands')` clickable probe false-hits the summary's `Lay On Hands Pool` div; and inside ONE synchronous `page.evaluate`, toggling cure entries then clicking `Cure Selected (…)` NO-OPs (cure entries are `button.char-btn`s carrying the catalog-cased label — NOT input checkboxes; an `input[type=checkbox]` census inside the modal false-negatives, re-lived HZ 2026-11-14) (React hasn't flushed the label/state yet) — split toggle and confirm into separate evaluates with an enabled-wait between. A/B live bisection WITHOUT git writes: `cp <file> /tmp/keep && git show HEAD:<path> > <path>`, ride, `cp` back, `git diff --stat` proves restoration. (8) HEALINGPOOLMODAL DOM-FAMILY law (live improve tracked-resource-ghost-keys): the pool lane mounts the TARGET PICKER in `.sp-overlay` but the dice/amount STAGE in `.short-rest-overlay` (the class name lies — not the ShortRestModal); a `.sp-overlay`-keyed "modal closed" reading after `Heal` is a FALSE NEGATIVE, and `Heal` on the picker only ADVANCES to the stage (not commit) — the EP LoH stage is Amount-input + `Apply Heal` with the amount PRE-FILLED at remaining capacity (typing APPENDS unless you clear first). (9) GHOST-POOL-KEYS law (shipped improve tracked-resource-ghost-keys): `healinglightPool`/`preserveLifePool`/`layOnHandsPool` exist in a change-data bucket ONLY for their owners (CLA-364 owned-only seeding; the long-rest `GATED_POOL_KEYS` guard keeps unowned buckets ghost-free) — absence is a first-class registry state in every read path; every OTHER `LONG_REST_RESOURCES` latch (incl. handler-owned `tranceOfOrderUses`, `warriorofthegodsPool`) keeps the unconditional CLA-130 null fan-out BY DESIGN, and the class-agnostic 0-seeds (`sorceryPoints:0`, `ragePoints:0`, `*Uses` ghosts) remain a known separate fan-out — RETIRED IN PLACE (shipped improve tracked-resource-ghost-keys ownership pass, 2026-11-14): the classification completed — EVERY statically-ownable catalog key now seeds owned-only (`GATED_TRACKED_KEYS` in `trackedResources.js`, imported by the long-rest `GATED_POOL_KEYS` guard, which also gates the resetFlags dupes so a Long Rest fans zero gated ghosts into non-owner buckets while owners keep byte-identical null re-arms); the case-variant twin seeds (`secondwindUses`/`actionsurgeUses`) are retired camelCase-canonical (the lowercase `actionsurgeUses` row still APPEARS at runtime when extraActionHandler spends the automationInfoBuilder-derived key — null-tolerant, SR-re-armed — its ABSENCE from a fresh bucket is the PASS face, not a bug); handler-owned latches (`tranceOfOrderUses`, `warriorofthegodsPool`, `warpingimplosionUses` family — `HANDLER_OWNED_LATCH_KEYS`) stay UNSEEDED with the unconditional CLA-130 null fan-out BY DESIGN (they can't be `in tracked`-gated: owners' buckets lack them too). LIVE CENSUS LAW (re-derived 2026-11-14): fresh-select + ANY single `setRuntimeValue`-lane write POSTs the WHOLE in-memory store (full-bucket `POST /api/campaigns/<c>/<Char>` `{value:{…}}`) — that's the ghost-persistence lane and the census trigger; GET after ~10 s debounce. Post-fix baseline: EvasiveFighter fresh bucket = 18 keys (was 51), ElderPaladin = 18 keys riding `layOnHandsPool` — both with ZERO case-dup pairs and ZERO foreign-class keys; after a Long Rest each bucket grows to ~165 keys, which is the RETAINED unconditional handler-latch fan-out (nulls) — judge ghost-freeness by membership (no gated non-owned key may appear), never by raw key count. Residual (documented, out of scope): the SHORT_REST_RESOURCES fan stays unconditional, and legacy ghost rows already persisted on disk in PRODUCTION campaigns rehydrate via `seedCharServerKeys` until those buckets are cleared (test-campaign admin-clear gives the clean census).
- **LoH SINGLE-tier synthesis rig + commit-guard stale-press recipe (live 2026-11-13 fix single-cure-tier):** temporarily rename `Restoring Touch` → a unique variant in `public/data/2024/classes.json` (single occurrence, `/6/class_levels/13/features/0`) to drop an L20 paladin fixture onto the `!hasRestoringTouch` SINGLE tier — the handler (`healingPoolHandler.js buildHealingPoolPayload`) finds RT by NAME in `playerStats.specialActions`, so the tier flip survives reload; restore with the one-line reverse str.replace and prove byte-exactness with an empty `git diff` (md5 `8d06a8c2`). Commit-guard face (SHARPENED 2026-11-14 improve cure-echo-live-conditions): an external `activeConditions: []` full-store POST now live-RETIREs the stale button on the dwelling modal (the cure-section echo), so the live-reachable stale-press face requires the combat-summary row to still carry `conditions` — `getTargetConditions` merges bucket ∪ cs-row, so a hand-seeded stale cs row keeps the button up while the bucket is `[]`; press it and the commit-time re-read refuses honestly with `<Target> is not affected by <Label>. No pool spent.` (zero writes, GET-provable). CHOOSER-SKIP law (live 2026-11-13 fix amount-heal): `showTargetSelection = creatureTargets.length > 1` — a board with ≤1 eligible target SKIPS the LoH chooser and opens the main modal directly self-targeted (an off-board/self else-branch ride needs no `Skip` press and is UNREACHABLE via a one-target chooser); a chooser-dependent ride needs ≥2 in-range targets. Probe-order law (same run): the modal's `loading` state resolves async AFTER `healing-pool-modal-title` mounts — waiting on the title then immediately probing `Apply Heal` false-negatives; wait on the BUTTON itself. **NATIVE sub-L14 paladin rig supersedes the synthesis for tier flips (live IZ 2026-11-14 explore, PT#1):** the SINGLE tier needs NO classes.json edit — one-field disk surgery `"level": 20`→`"level": 13` in `public/campaigns/test-campaign/ElderPaladin.json` (string unique; the fixture has no `class_levels` key) + full-store `POST /ElderPaladin {value:{}}` to reset `layOnHandsPool` (stored current does NOT auto-rederive — wipe or the pre-surgery number rides; the overshoot face itself is unprobed, see map PT#2) + reload/re-select: badge reads `Lay On Hands Pool: 65/65 (cur/max)` (= 5×level, `addPaladinDivineResources`) and the `Restoring Touch:` row vanishes natively (classes.json lv14 row); reverse + md5 `6e4341e4242234fd9178a9cc3a3171c7` proves restore, badge 100/100 live after. SHEET-FEATURE opener law: feature rows are `<b class="clickable">` labeled `<Feature>:` (`Lay On Hands:`) — `getByRole('button',{name:'Lay On Hands'})` NEVER resolves them (30 s timeout); evaluate-click on the exact text. LOH CHOOSER COMMIT = force-click the radio `input[aria-label="Select <Name>, N of M HP"]` THEN the picker's own `Heal` button — the radio alone does not resolve (HZ's `Select EvasiveFighter…` note predates the commit leg). Cure-echo (post-`82f517b26`): full-store `POST /<Victim>` with changed `activeConditions` ADOPTS mid-dwell in one SSE tick (rows appear/retire/selection prunes/section abolishes); non-reactive-readers law unchanged. Pool-exhaustion face: type 90 vs pool 60 → input displays 60 (controlled clamp `Math.min(healAmount,safePool)`), commit charges exactly the pool (ledger `poolAfter:0`), cure rows render `disabled` when safePool<cost, brute-click inert. Sheet-open latch census: `fanaticalFocusUsed:false` lands on EVERY opened bucket (`CharSheet.jsx:603`) — expect it in non-owner censuses, it is not a new ghost.
- **Tracked-resource read-clamp law + pool-seeding rig correction (shipped 2026-11-14 fix stale-level-down-pool-overshoot):** `useTrackedResource.resolveCurrent` now clamps a finite stored bucket to the level-derived max AT READ (`Math.min(stored, maxGetter())`) — non-numeric latches pass through (`hasInspiration` booleans, null-as-armed), HP family (`currentHitPoints`/`hitPoints`) is exempt by owner ruling, and there is NO write-back: the modal's `safePool - actualHeal` commit rewrites the bucket to a valid value on first spend (self-heal, live-proved 95→0 after a 65-spend). RIG CORRECTION IN PLACE: "open the sheet once to seed `layOnHandsPool`" (the bug-file/JA reading) holds ONLY in the client mirror — server change-data has NO pool bucket for an owner who has never SPENT, so a scoped `PATCH …/pool` 404s ("no object stored") until a real heal (or full-store POST) materializes it; a level-down rig must do the real spend first, then PATCH. Clamp-hint composition: a typed amount over a clamped `safePool` rides the shipped `Amount clamped to the remaining pool of N HP.` note verbatim (633d5c552 lane) — no new UI. Latent pins in `useTrackedResource.test.js` (a `maxGetter` call-count, a stored-above-max plumbing value) are tripped by any display-side clamp too — updated in place, read before re-pinning.
- **Feature-choice riders**: 2024 domain/maneuver-style riders stay SILENT until the option is chosen; chosen-option stamp key = `_<OptionOrUpgradeName>_option` (e.g. `_Blessed_Strikes_option`; at lv15+ the key is the upgraded rider `_Improved_Elemental_Fury_option`, dice 2d8 not 1d8 — option key = bonus.upgrades||name). Legacy-named feature stamps (`_Divine_Strike_usedRound`) ride cosmetic with zero dice. WEAPON-ONLY rider gate = `isSpellAttackContext` (attackRollBonuses.js:315) — spell attacks exempt.
- **Blessed Strikes shared cantrip lane**: cantrip +WIS fold rides directSpellDamageSteps.js:24-40, formula label `[Blessed Strikes]`; once-per-turn rider latch consumed at modal confirm (useModalHandlers.js:70). Data: classes.json :2354/:2546 (lv7 1d8 / lv14 2d8 "Necrotic or Radiant").
- **Damage-cell vs hit-cell split**: sheet attack **Damage cell** = useSimpleDamageRoll direct roll (NO target/pipeline, no riders); only name/to-hit cells run adjudicated attacks. Sheet has TWO "Attack (to hit):" cells — the Spells-section one rolls a ONE-stage spell attack (no damage Done stage, lastAttack stays damage-less); the Actions-grid weapon row is the 2-stage lane (+N chip by rect.y match → HIT Done → damage Done merges primaryDamage/actualDamage into lastAttack). Damage-cell click lane produces `characterName:null` rolls bypassing damage-side enders.
- **Giant-ancestry chip lane (Fire's Burn / Frost's Chill / Hill's Tumble)**: affordance is the always-clickable Actions-panel `b.clickable "<Feature>:"` chip, NOT a hit-popup rider offer; shared `attackerRollGate` (giantAncestryUtils.js:179/:181, routeFireBurn shared by fire_burn/frosts_chill/hills_tumble) requires `attackEvent.hit===true` AND `findLastAttack` totalDamage>0; miss/no-damage press = popup refusal + `<slug>_refused` (`attack_missed`/`no_damage_dealt`), zero spend; 5th gate param is campaignName. Host drift: giant-ancestry renders only while `race.subrace` disk-true.
- **Reaction press-models**: (a) post-damage rollback-heal (Glorious Defense / Protective Field / CLA-345 family) — affordance in Reactions section AFTER a hit resolves, handler pulls findLastAttack (rollback rider requires totalDamage>0), retroactive AC raise re-adjudicates + rolls back damage; (b) raw pre-reduction press (CLA-158 family) arms on attack ROLL, totalDamage not required. Holder-targeted reaction_damage MUST pull attacker from findLastAttack (`target===holder && hit`), never resolveTarget(holder) (defender's armed slot is empty post-hit → permanent refusal). FP-free reaction exemptions live ONLY in the lane providing the free use (FOCUS_COST_SKIP_FEATURES = flurry rows only), NEVER in shared reactionDamageHandler/useCharActionsAutomation pre-spend (standalone charges 1 FP raw, paid-at-row-click, abandon unrefunded).
- **Frenzy fold ownership**: single owner = attackRollBonuses.applyFrenzyBonuses (roll+apply+latch); contextBuilder-sync contributes NO frenzy — diceRoller rolls `' plus '`-joined segments, so baking "+Nd6" into a "display" formula is a REAL second roll (double-apply fingerprint = count d6 GROUPS in log formula).
- **Flurry lanes**: row click PRE-SPENDS Focus before distribute modal opens; `input.flurry-target-input` one per target row (fill strict-violates — fill nth(0)+"Strike All"); base "Flurry of Blows" row FILTERED by classRules2024.js:152 on lv10+ Mercy monks → trigger via "Heightened Flurry of Blows" row (same bonus_attacks consumer); unarmed die: resolveFlurryWeaponStats(playerStats, auto) selects weaponType:'unarmed' then name-match for attackType:'unarmed_strike', fallback attacks[0]; attackType-less rows byte-identical. `after_attack_action` gate (`_attackActionTakenRound`, armed by handleAttackClick non-BA rows) is NON-consuming — one Attack action arms Flurry+Patient Defense+Step of the Wind all round; gate runs BEFORE FP pre-spend, refusals burn 0 FP + `<slug>_refused` (`no_attack_action_taken`).
- **Summon lane**: AC slot-scaling is opt-in `armor_class_scales_with_slot:true` on the MONSTER block (monsters.json, read via variant.monsterIndex at summonSpiritHandler.js:140-141) — flag on spells.json is a NO-OP; HP ladder separate (`hpPerLevelAbove`); single-variant summon_spirit skips chooser (instant spawn). Upcast radios: WITHOUT `upcast_at_slot_level` map, `summonHpLadderLevels` arms canonical ladder (base level payable); WITH map, radios = map-only (base shadowed). Twins: Beast lv2 +5HP/lvl Air/Land/Water; Construct lv4 map 5-9 AC13; Elemental lv4 no-map AC11 HP50+10; Celestial lv5 Avenger/Defender 40+10 HP. Find Steed rides summon_spirit lane (spells.json automation both rulesets + otherworldly-steed monsters.json block; HP scales by SLOT not caster level); `clearAllExpirationEffects.js:233` purges ALL spell summons at Long Rest. Summon concentration lives on caster cs entry (`creatures[caster].concentration`), NOT a top-level change-data key. Caster with blank cs.initiative → spawn uses random-roll−0.1 fallback (summonSpiritHandler:215). Free-grant halves-HP lane numeric halve lands ONLY at EB→initiative join (encounterToInitiative resolvePhantasmalHp floor(HP/2)); sheet-cast lane is log/stamp only; exact-name match gap: "Bestial Spirit (Land)" joins FULL HP despite live stamp (initiativeService.js:114).
- **Dominates**: family (beast/monster/person) shares findDomination repeat-save machinery (applyDamage.js:686), inline NPC auto-rolls; success purges activeConditions + cs.concentration + pendingExpirations even on DEAD target; type-mismatch refund is consume→refund — judge slot LEDGER NET post-debounce; humanoid/Beast gate is SERVICE-seam only (refusal+refund), picker UNFILTERED (absent from spellGateMap) — gate/picker asymmetry family.
- **Channel Divinity economics**: pay-at-CONFIRM/APPLY in `SetConditionModal.handleApplyOverride` (charge −1 + ability_use spend at apply, NOT pay-at-open — mid-modal GET honest); exhaustion refusal popup-only; verbatim "No Channel Divinity charges remaining."; Greater DI cooldown truth = `_divineInterventionWishCooldown` (2d4) + `divineInterventionUses:-1`, LR ticks −1, re-arms at 0 (restRules-longRest.js:316); lv20 chooser Wish-only (non-Wish control unrunnable on lv20 host). Divine Spark was the row-click EXCEPTION — FIXED 2026-11-05 (improve): `divineSparkHandler` no longer writes/spends at all; it resolves the target, refuses zero-spend, and passes the open-moment charge count (`stored ?? pool max`) in the modal payload; `DivineSparkModal.spendCharge` writes the single decrement + matching ability_use row at branch-confirm AFTER the 30 ft gate (harm: prompt armed; heal: heal landed — choke-point-null refusal stays zero-spend); Cancel/out-of-range are now zero-spend; post-confirm Dismiss keeps the charge spent (legit confirm spend). Modal `sp-header` renders "N charge(s) remaining" (`N charge remaining` singular; `.sp-resource-info` chip beside the feature name). CD max fallback: handler-side fallback table when the char JSON has no class_levels entry (Cleric lv2-5:2, lv6-17:3, lv18-20:4); runtime `channelDivinityCharges` key overrides; absent key = full pool. NOTE: opening a character sheet SEEDS `channelDivinityCharges` = pool max into the runtime bucket (App.jsx seedTrackedResources) — an "absent key" is only observable before first sheet load.
- **Divine Spark lane (CZ)**: feature row AUTO-DERIVES from classes.json (5e/2024 Cleric lv2 "Divine Spark") straight onto the sheet Actions list as `b.clickable "Divine Spark:"` — NO fixture features/specialActions authoring needed (prior runs' "fixture grep 'divine spark'=0 ⇒ rig owed" was a false blocker; disk classes.json row suffices). Unarmed cs targetName previously self-targeted via `|| playerStats.name` (FIXED 2026-11-05: `divineSparkHandler` + `saveOnlyHandler` resolve target BEFORE the spend and refuse `no_target`/`cannot_target_self` with popup + zero-spend `<slug>_refused` row; Stunning Strike FP/latch burn FIXED 2026-11-05 too: `gateStunningStrike` resolves the armed target (getTargetFromAttacker) BEFORE `spendMonkFocusPoint` and refuses `no_target`/`cannot_target_self` via `refuseTriggerGate` — zero FP write, zero latch stamp, one refusal row; handler refusal stays as defense-in-depth for zero-cost non-Monk save_only consumers). Harm queues a SINGLE-TARGET prompt through the `campaign.pendingSavePrompts` bucket (NOT per-target `savePrompt-<T>-<ts>` keys — two prompt channels coexist; check both). CON saves skip the Leading-Evasion chooser (that stacker is the Dex/AoE lane). Dismissing the UN-ROLLED prompt now dispatches `save-dismissed` and DivineSparkModal shows its refusal view with a working Done + `divine_spark_refused` log row (log fixed 2026-11-05 — see §5 save-dismiss doctrine; the charge was legitimately spent at branch-confirm, Dismiss adds no second decrement — the §9 pay-early leak is CLOSED for this feature). Harm rig reproduces WITHOUT any map — gridless/unpositioned = lenient range pass (no depth-seed map needed). Corona async lane self-loads the ACTIVE map (`__map__.activeMapName`) — no mapData threading required by the caller; damage-heal lane needs victim RUNTIME `{hitPoints,currentHitPoints}` FULL-STORE POST first (cs placeholder maxHp:1 poisons any `creature.maxHp||…` precedence).

Silence: caster-side keys persist, TARGET grants client-only (server activeBuffs null — reload loses gates). **Globe/emanations**: self-range globe opens `.sp-modal` with `.secondary-target-row` checkboxes + footer "Activate Globe (N)"; clicking the NAME text closes without casting; footer button toggles OFF on re-toggle. `blockChecks.js checkGlobeOfInvulnerability` gates <=lv5 out→in (gridless geometry advisory). - **Polymorph/Shapechange**: polymorph fail → beast chooser honoring TARGET CR; cs stamps maxHp←beast, ac/speed←beast, `polymorphOriginal{hp,ac}`, runtime tempHp=beast HP, te polymorph{beastName}; early-end-at-0-THP live (temp absorbs first, auto-revert). Shapechange: first-transform THP gate MUST be `!!shapechangeOriginal` (te-check always-true re-grants on recast); concentration DC read canonical `spellAbilities.saveDc`; pay-at-chooser-open (abandon burns slot); mid-combat change-form affordance does not exist (badge × = full revert). BlocksSpellcasting is shape_shift-only. **Fall lane**: GM te chip 'Falling' (Movement group, fields ['value']=feet) is the only UI producer; produceFallDamage (src/services/combat/fallDamage.js) must stamp a FRESH lastAttack chain header (GM chip has no attack-roll leg — without it the stamp MERGES as secondaryDamage onto the stale lastAttack) + 2d10/10ft cap 20d10 + applyDamageToTarget(trigger:'falling') + fall_damage log. applyDamage buildLastAttackUpdate = single hit descriptor, trigger stamped explicit-null (spread-leak fail-open closed for all matchesTrigger consumers). Feather Fall reactive undo heals back the stamped fall damage; latch `_Feather_Fall_negatedStamp` on caster; findLastAttack surfaces timestamp ONLY on attackEvent (top-level read yields 0===0 self-refuse). Refused reaction-spell cast still pays slot (prepareSpellCast pays before handler lane). 2024 lv3 / 5e lv4.
- **Stand/stabilize**: Spare the Dying stable model = `deathSaves:[true,true,true]` in the RUNTIME bucket (cs mirror never updates); dying-rig = full-store cs POST `{currentHp:0,deathSaves:[F,F,F]}` + reload + re-select; 0-HP GM-filled join = dead-model gate refuses. Power Word Heal chain: heal-to-full + strips charmed/frightened/paralyzed/poisoned/stunned + prone→`powerWordHealStandPermission:true` → clickable "Stand (Power Word Heal)" reaction (CharReactions.jsx). 0-HP interceptors: ZERO_HP_INTERCEPTORS (applyDamage.js ~:588) — Relentless Endurance (HP=1, key relentlessEnduranceUsed, LR re-arm) 4th-in-order Relentless Rage (rage-gated DC ladder 10→15→20, key relentlessRageUses — CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-15 improve uses-latch-key-camel-convention, see next bullet), HP=2×lv, fail→death saves). cannotAct enforcement: paralyzed/stunned holder's reaction row click now POPS the shared reason via assertCanAct (CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-15 improve canact-silent-gate-feedback — the old silent swallow (zero log/popup) is dead; write-side stays zero; `pendingOaAttackIndex` budget bypass for spent-prompt cases kept by design). **Regen/tick lanes**: turn-start heal ticks (Regenerate, Heroism temp-HP) are LIVE but LOG-LESS — judge by GET change-data poll at the target's turn (walk then read). `combatSummary.currentHp/maxHp` mirror can lag/clamp oddly (80/11) — in-handler applyDamageToTarget may return finalDamage 0 while damage lands via deferred logs, silently gating all finalDamage>0 consumers.
- **Reaction economy gaps**: ZERO reaction-economy key app-wide for generic reaction_damage rows (no reactionUsed key) — stale re-press re-fires self-targeted OA; abandon via Escape (Done self-damages); once-per-cast/post-cast distributors need service-stamped monotonic tokens (`inspiringSmiteCastToken`=Date.now at cast choke + confirm-stamped token + equality-refusal), survives lastAttack clobbers; no round latch = repeat clicks re-roll/re-spend. Feature-step latches need `getCurrentCombatRound(ctx.campaignName)` AND key registration in PLAYER_ROUND_LATCH_KEYS, else once-per-turn becomes once-per-combat pinned at stamp-round.
- **Spell Thief / steal lanes**: stolen spell must NOT already be on thief's list ("already in list → continue" skip-by-design); reliable steal victim = lv5 upcast Charm Person (picker accepts exactly 1 despite "5 targets" label); RAW cannotAct on the holder is real enforcement.
- **DS chooser post-apply Cancel lock (2026-11-06 improve)**: AttackRiderModal latches `dispatched` (state) + `dispatchedRef` (same-tick re-entry) at Apply-Effects press — while the dispatch is in flight BOTH Cancel and Apply render `disabled` and a `.sp-note` committed copy shows (CS-trio wording via `isCunningStrikeFeatureName`, now the single source in cunningStrikeUtils.js replacing the twice-duplicated literal list), because applyRiderOption PAYS the Cunning Strike cost before the save prompt stacks behind the still-open chooser (the §9 "Cancel keeps paid charge" family — the Cancel-after-Apply leak is CLOSED by disable-on-commit, orchestrator ruling: no cost rollback). Pre-apply Cancel remains the free "roll with no effect, nothing spent" exit. Live law re-proven same run: hand-rolled board + scoped-PATCH round bump re-arms the once-per-turn latch and TWO healthy sneak cycles flow in one load (e81bdd985 mergeModalStates fix holds — the DG queue-degradation no longer reproduces); the death_strike CON DC15 prompt + extra doubled hp_change fires once per cycle even on a clean single-Trip apply — cs player stub (currentHp:1/maxHp:1) adjudicates the victim downed at 0, rig artifact not a chooser defect. **DS VT offer on save-SUCCESS**: Trip-on-save-SUCCESS still offers the Versatile Trickster chooser ("Trip applied to <T>. Versatile Trickster allows you to also Trip another creature…") with the primary damage stage parked behind it — Skip → delayed damage still lands cost-deducted. The §5 evict law covers the te row but not the VT offer itself.

Grant fires ONLY from a card-input initiative commit at round 1 (`handleInitiativeChange` → `maybeGrantThiefsReflexesSecondTurn`): TRUSTED fill+Tab `'20'` → board gains `"<Name> (Second Turn)"` init 10, `secondTurn:true`, `holderName` set + one `ability_use` row + `thief'sreflexesUses→0`. Re-stamp RE-SYNCS the row (init follows, no double-spend — `resyncExistingSecondTurn`). WATCH: the row is evicted by any Initiative remount while spent (filed `bug-initiative-remount-drops-second-turn-row`) — verify faces in ONE mounted session.
- **Power-of-the-Wilds (Ram) lane without disk surgery (live 2026-11-10 ram-prone fix run)**: `contextBuilder-sync.scanBuffsForAdvantage` arms `ramActive` from the ATTACKER's runtime `activeBuffs` on `buff.optionName === 'Ram'` alone (no class/rage gate in the consumer) — curl full-store `POST /<Barbarian> {value:{activeBuffs:[{name:'Power of the Wilds',effect:'power_of_wilds_option',optionName:'Ram'}]}}` BEFORE campaign-select arms the sheet lane on the registry fixture (DraconicDragon lv20 Barbarian); Prone victim face = pre-seed `POST /Goblin {value:{activeConditions:['Prone']}}` the same way; the write side is the sheet lane (DD card `[data-testid=target-select]` arm + Warhammer row → Reckless chooser `.sp-overlay` opens FIRST — click `Normal Attack` for the plain path — then two-stage Done drains). EB-joined Goblin cs row carries `size:'Small'` for the Large-or-smaller gate; a 0-HP joined monster revives via scoped `PATCH combatSummary {value:{creatures:[{name:'Goblin',currentHp:40,maxHp:40}]}}` (applied:1, SSE-adopted live). Consumer is SINGLE for both rulesets — no 2024 twin of `applyRamProneCondition` exists (grep before looking).
- **Sheet-open write census law (live 2026-11-14 improve fanatical-focus-latch-gate):** post-gate, switching to a character whose server bucket is EMPTY POSTs NOTHING on open (the old unconditional `fanaticalFocusUsed:false` write was the per-open carrier; the reset is now write-only-if-changed via a `useRuntimeValue` subscription in `CharSheet.jsx`). To census-verify tracked-resource seeding server-side, one legitimate write materializes the whole mirror bucket — the sheet inline HP editor (click the `.hidden-input` span, prototype value setter + Enter) is the cheapest.
- **HealingApplySection note-seam audit law (live 2026-11-14 improve heal-amount-clamp-hint):** the `.healing-restriction-note` seam in `char-sheet/modals/divine/HealingPoolModal.jsx` `HealingApplySection` hosts FOUR faces (bloodied, full-HP/refusal, clamped-to-pool `Amount clamped to the remaining pool of N HP.`, exhausted `Healing pool exhausted — …`) — pool-0 tests asserting note COUNTS must count classes, not assume one; the clamp hint re-reads `safePool` live so an external pool write mid-dwell updates its number without reopen. - **Heal-modal echo & commit-guard laws (shipped 2026-11-14 fix hoh-physicians-touch-stale-conditions-phantom-row; CORRECTED IN PLACE by same-day improves hoh-cs-condition-echo + hoh-autocure-mount-only):** the touch-heal lane's display-stale faces are dead — `HandOfHealingModal`'s Physician's Touch section subscribes BOTH `useRuntimeValue(targetName,'activeConditions')` and `useRuntimeValue('combatSummary','creatures')` and recomputes per render, so externally-cured options retire AND cs-row condition edits retire/surface `Remove <X>` badges within one SSE tick; a press on a no-longer-present condition is refused at COMMIT time — re-read live conditions first, absent → inline `.healing-restriction-note` `<Target> is not affected by <Label>.`, zero write, zero log row (`condition broken` only ever for a condition that actually broke). AUTO-CURE IS MOUNT-ONLY (see the mount-only law in item (5) above — the old "multi→single echo auto-cures once via the spent-latch" face is dead). STALE-BUTTON RIG for commit-guard rides: a live write can no longer hold a stale badge; deterministic staleness requires mutating the `combatData` board the commit re-read sees WITHOUT issuing the `change-<c>-combatSummary` echo (pre-echo click race, unit-pinned).
- **Death-save evict lane (fixed 2026-11-14 improve death-save-probe-404-noise):** `clearDeathSavePrompt` is MIRROR-GATED — skips the wildcard `DELETE` when the `deathSavePrompt-<Name>` key is absent from BOTH mirror lanes (campaign-select seed into the `'campaign'` store; live `change-<c>-deathSavePrompt-<N>` echo into a key-named ghost bucket, since it's NOT in `CAMPAIGN_KEYS`). A never-armed key ⇒ zero DELETE, zero console 404 (the KB backlog's "GET 404" was really the wildcard **DELETE** 404 — GET on an absent key answers `200 {value:null}`). The `DeathSavePromptModal` mounts app-wide off the SSE echo and is armable WITHOUT initiative: `POST /api/campaigns/<c>/deathSavePrompt-<Name> {value:{promptId,targetName}}`; recovery then rides the full mount→DELETE 200→cross-tab unmount chain. MOUNT-SIDE STALE RE-VERDICT (shipped `77cf70cd0`, live-re-verified explore RB 2026-11-15): a head prompt whose PLAYER target's bucket `currentHitPoints` > 0 now SELF-EVICTS at mount — exactly one `clearDeathSavePrompt` DELETE, zero roll/HP/counter/log writes, queue advances (a brief overlay flash is the accepted face), and since improve 2026-11-20 dsp-stale-evict-debug the branch emits one `console.debug` naming the re-verdict (`[DeathSavePromptModal] auto-evicting stale prompt for <Name> — target HP n > 0`, pinned in `DeathSavePromptModal.stale-mount-recheck.test.jsx` — the trace reads the CLIENT mirror, so it fires even while the server char-bucket GET still answers `{value:null}`); the gates preserve the face byte-identically for HP≤0 (genuine arm persists), absent bucket / absent key, result-stage heads, and targets OUTSIDE the player roster (monster/NPC prompts keep the replay face). PITFALL (RB): sweeping an armed prompt with an OUT-OF-BAND direct `DELETE /api/campaigns/<c>/deathSavePrompt-<Name>` removes the server key but NOT the client mirrors — a later clear-leg honestly re-DELETEs and logs a benign 404 console error; sweep prompts with the TRUSTED GM Cancel instead, or judge a session by the eventual `{}` clear. **Death-save lane full-rig laws (live LC 2026-11-14, first exhaustive ride — every face PASS):** ARM = Initiative PC-card HP input `input[aria-label="<Name> current HP"]` with a REAL transition >0→0 (`writePlayerHp` resets counters + one `POST deathSavePrompt-<N>`); a 0→0 fill arms NOTHING, and the sheet CharHitPoints editor NEVER arms (drop-to-0 there = HP write + `hp_change` `isUnconscious:true` + `.death-saves-container` in-summary). Modal `.dsp-overlay`: roll/Done/Next ALL share class `.dsp-roll-btn` (text-anchor locators); SHARPENED 2026-11-14 (improve death-save-dismiss-for-accidental-arms): a GM-only `Cancel` (`.dsp-cancel-btn`, `isGmHost()` gate) now rides beside Roll in the PRE-ROLL census only — visitor census stays `[Roll Death Save]` byte-identical; Cancel = the void-guard's zero-write lane (`clearDeathSavePrompt` DELETE + advance, verified live: exactly one request, prompt key ABSENT on GET, bucket stays 0, counters/isDead untouched, zero death_save rows, second tab retires live on the null echo) and is absent from the result stage; queue copy `(i of n)` appears only when 2+ targets are armed, absent on the last; late-joining campaign-select REPLAYS an armed prompt (seed lane feeds the modal), cross-tab retirement rides the cleared-key null echo (one save per down event). Roll network trio: `POST deathSaveResult-<N>` → `DELETE deathSavePrompt-<N>` → `DELETE deathSaveResult-<N>` (result key always self-sweeps, GET shows ABSENT). FINALITY seeding (CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-19 improve run — the LC-era "scoped PATCH never merges arrays" claim is DEAD): a scoped `PATCH /<Name> {value:{...}}` MERGE-REPLACES array fields wholesale and preserves sibling keys (orchestrator curl-proved on disk: seed `{currentHitPoints:5,probeScalarKeep:1,deathSaves:[f,f,f]}` + PATCH `{deathSaves:[t,t,t],isDead:1}` ⇒ bucket holds ALL FOUR keys, track replaced, scalar untouched), and the loaded tab live-adopts the echo (~1 tick, no reload — the DEAD badge mounts from a forged full-merged PATCH alone; PD farm-reset law re-lived); `applied:0` on the PATCH response stays a false negative — always GET/DOM-verify. A full-store `POST /<Name> {value:{...existing+new}}` remains the alternative when you must control every byte; then Roll on the armed prompt → DEAD panel, `isDead:1`, and the terminal row `death_save|result:'dead'|totalFailures:3` beside the roll row (modal seam writes finality too, `logFinalityRow` live-proved; sheet seam pinned the `stable|3/…` face — RAW: stable keeps HP at 0, no restore write). VOID guard (#1400/#1403): external scoped PATCH `currentHitPoints:>0` while the prompt stays armed, then press Roll → prompt DELETE + advance, counters untouched, ZERO death_save rows. Dead-state sheet face: DEAD badge + GM-only `.death-saves-dead-remove` × (title `Clear death state` — renamed in place 2026-11-19 improve death-save-remove-copy; it only clears isDead + tracks at 0 HP, never restores HP) → `removed|0/0` row + counters reset. Mirror-gate residual RESOLVED (shipped 2026-11-14 improve death-save-ghost-key-404-sweep): `clearDeathSavePrompt` now evicts BOTH mirror lanes (`evictDeathSavePromptMirror`) on any server-confirmed DELETE answer (2xx ⇒ removed, 404 ⇒ never there), so a resolved prompt never replays the double-sweep — later clear legs (sheet roll press, card revive) send ZERO requests; refused/network-failed answers (403/423/5xx/rejection) keep the mirror so a genuinely pending evict is retried, never silently skipped (pins: `savePromptService.death-save-evict-mirror.test.js`). ORGANIC-FINALITY ADDENDUM (live RB 2026-11-15, both terminal states reached via real rolls, NOT harness seeds): an organic death stamps `isDead:1` + `deathFailures:[t,t,t]` and attaches ZERO conditions (bucket `activeConditions:[]` — the "does finality stamp unconscious?" question is answered NO; the unconscious condition belongs to the damage/`hp_change` lanes only), sheet collapses to the `DEAD` panel + `.death-saves-dead-remove` ×, terminal row `death_save|dead|0/3`; 3 organic successes → `Stable(13)` panel, HP stays 0, `stable|3/0` row; a NAT20 at the modal seam renders `NATURAL 20 — STABILIZED!` and writes `restoredToHp:1` (live-witnessed, was unit-only). One save per arm: after each modal roll the trio sweeps the prompt key and the panel awaits `Done` — to chain saves press `Done`, then either top-up+card-drop to re-arm (modal lane, resets counters) or roll at the sheet's `div.death-saves-roll[role=button]` (accumulates across presses without re-arming). SHEET-EDITOR RE-DROP LAW (live improve 2026-11-19): once Stable (3 successes) hides the Roll area, an HP-editor `0→0` commit does NOT reset the matured track — a re-drop-only farm loop burns iterations (witnessed: 4 rolls + 12 no-op re-drops); to re-arm fresh counters revive `>0` first (the merged sheet-revive batch resets both tracks + isDead), then re-drop.**MODAL-ROLL-LANE SINGLE-WRITER LAW (fixed 2026-11-19 `bug-death-save-prompt-modal-roll-lane-sequential-bucket-posts` → resolved; sibling of the sheet-lane roll-single-write fix af8dc4a83):** `DeathSavePromptModal.handleRoll` is now EXACTLY ONE char-bucket POST per roll — a merged `setRuntimeBatch` carrying `deathSaves`+`deathFailures` (+`isDead:1` fatal, +`currentHitPoints` on a nat20), roll trio/log-row order and shape unchanged. The modal is the SOLE dispatcher of the `death-save-result` window event; when it merges the restore it declares `hpWritten:true` on the event detail (absent — detail byte-identical — on non-restoring rolls), and the CharHitPoints sheet listener + Initiative combat-board listener key off that flag to SKIP their own whole-bucket `currentHitPoints` POST (chip faces and the Initiative `combatSummary`-row mirror stamp keep running — the cs lane is a separate write; flagless bare-restore events must still write). Pre-fix live-wire (witnessed 2026-11-19): fatal roll shipped 2 sequential bucket POSTs (intermediate `isDead:0` beside third-failure tracks) and the nat20 shipped 3, one of them the Initiative listener's `currentHitPoints:1` beside PRE-roll tracks. Wire pins: `DeathSavePromptModal.roll-single-write.test.jsx` (real store + real savePromptService + fetch spy; the CharHitPoints CO-MOUNT is the "HP written exactly once" oracle — one bucket POST total), `initiative-callbacks.test.jsx` hpWritten pins (skip POST, keep NPC cs stamp), `CharHitPoints.death-save-chip.test.jsx` skip pin. PROBE TRAP (same run): the campaign log GET serves a PLAIN ARRAY — `log.entries || log` resolves to Array.prototype.entries (a function, no `.filter`) and TypeErrors; index/filter the array directly.
- **Vite HMR hot-applies a fix INTO a dwelled modal (live 2026-11-14 hoh fix run):** editing source while a picker/result modal is open remounts its internals mid-dwell and the new echo logic can fire the honest auto-behavior immediately (a stale single condition auto-cured the instant the fix landed) — great as a free proof of the echo leg, but keep repro and fix-verification on FRESH navigations and re-judge after `page.goto`+re-select, never from a mid-edit dwell.
- **Sheet-revive merged-POST + death-state reset laws (live NP 2026-11-14, post-`db0ad4cf8`):** a `Hit Points:` commit >0 from the sheet is now exactly ONE full-store `POST /<Char>` carrying `currentHitPoints` + `deathSaves:[f,f,f]` + `deathFailures:[f,f,f]` + `isDead:0` (was 4 POSTs/4 echoes); it rides EVERY >0 commit face (not only 0→>0); `setRuntimeBatch` valuesEqual self-skip ⇒ a same-value commit POSTs nothing. The clear-leg's prompt DELETE rides the mirror gate independently (NP rode a genuinely-armed prompt: the roll seam had already run the trio, so the sheet revive honestly sent ZERO prompt requests). Roll network trio re-lived on a NON-final single-failure roll — it fires on EVERY answered roll, finality only adds the rows. GM-card re-arm after a resurrection resets the counter ARRAYS (failures [t,t,t] → fresh roll lands [t,f,f]) but does NOT clear `isDead` — see the HoUoM bug bullet. **Hand of Ultimate Mercy lane laws (first full ride live NP 2026-11-14):** sheet row `Hand of Ultimate Mercy:` (Warrior-of-Mercy disk swap) dispatches through `useCharActionsAutomation` → `handOfUltimateMercyHandler.js`. Refusal faces are zero-spend popups (`.popup-modal`, no role=dialog): no cs `targetName` armed → `Select a target in combat first.`; armed target whose BUCKET `currentHitPoints` ≠ 0 → `<Target> is not at 0 Hit Points.` (bucket-read law, L25 — the "corpse" byte-state is isDead:1 + HP 0, exactly what death-save finality leaves, so a natural corpse always passes the gate). Corpse commit: popup `Returns to life with N HP. Expended 5 Focus Points.`, ONE `POST /<Target>` (full mirror), focus ladder 18→13, audit is a `type:'hp_change'` row with `resurrection:true` — CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-14 (improve resurrection-log-family-split): the row used to be a `type:'healing'` outlier and now rides the hp_change heal family (positive `delta` = actual bucket lift, `currentHp`/`maxHp` frame, `isHealing: delta > 0` sign-stamp, `sourceName` = caster, `note` = ability, `characterName` kept for the log Character filter); `HpChangeEntry` gained a resurrection arm (fa-dove + 'Brought Back to Life' + Resurrection badge, reusing `.log-resurrection*` CSS) while `HealingEntry`'s legacy resurrection face still renders pre-split stored rows; whole revival family aligned — revivifyService's literal `isHealing:true` → `actualHeal > 0` (delta-0 heal-block rescue now honest), revivificationHandler's UNREGISTERED `type:'heal'` (rendered via GenericEntry, escaped every hp_change audit) → hp_change, and the undyingSentinel manual+auto + boonOfRecovery Last Stand `type:'healing'` 0-HP rescues → hp_change (redundant `amount` byte retired; auto-lanes keep their forged-source `sourceName` per the 'sheet editor' convention). **BUG FIXED 2026-11-14 (`fix(houm-resurrection-leaves-isdead-stuck)`):** the handler used to write only `currentHitPoints` (bypassing the `applyHealingToTarget` oldHp≤0 reset block) — `isDead:1` and stale `deathFailures` SURVIVED the resurrection, and the resurrected creature's next real death rendered the DEAD-skull panel with no counter/roll lane (`DeathSavingThrows.jsx:58/102/167` short-circuit on `isDead`) until GM ×-remove or a long rest (`restRules-longRest.js:168`). Post-fix player-lane commit face: ONE full-store `setRuntimeBatch` POST carrying `currentHitPoints` + `deathSaves:[f,f,f]` + `deathFailures:[f,f,f]` + `isDead:0` (canonical CLA-092/sheet-revive reset values — tracks are all-false arrays, NOT empty); popup/focus/log contract byte-unchanged; NPC lane writes without the reset (monster death derives from cs-row `currentHp`, no dead flag to clear). Re-verified live: clean post-resurrect bucket + second death renders counter + working Roll (`bug-houm-resurrection-leaves-isdead-stuck.md` in `resolved/`). **CHOOSER FACE (live OV 2026-11-14, post-`adb85b54b`):** un-armed press with a bucket-0 board corpse opens the shared picker — dialog root `[aria-labelledby="hand-of-ultimate-mercy-target-modal-title"]` (same `.short-rest-modal` shell as the HoH picker), visible title `Choose corpse for Hand of Ultimate Mercy`, radio aria `Select <T>, 0 of <M> HP`, footer `Revive`/`Cancel` (Skip relabeled; Cancel + Escape zero-write); `Revive` commits the SAME merged contract verbatim, and an ARMED targetName keeps the direct lane (no chooser, press→commit). Audit frame law (CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-14 improve resurrect-audit-maxhp-fallback): `resolveTargetMaxHp` in `handOfUltimateMercyHandler.js` now resolves the hp_change row's `maxHp` bucket `hitPoints` → loaded character data `characters[].computedStats.hitPoints` (the EXACT value `seedCharacterRuntimeData` seeds the bucket from — `handle` receives the dispatcher's 5th `characters` arg, `index.js:743`, previously dropped) → combat-summary row ONLY as a last resort and LOUD (console.error). The old silent `maxHp:1` face on a thin-echo-evicted mirror + stale select-baked `1/1` row is DEAD — live-proved `maxHp:112` with the bucket carrying no `hitPoints` at write time; 4 unit pins cover both lanes (armed-direct + picker commit) plus bucket-beats-char-data and the loud last-resort leg; monsters unchanged (summary-canonical). Reuse envelope (PT#2 consumed): FOUR consecutive resurrections of re-seeded corpses, the only gate is the Focus ladder (20→15→10→5→0 census'd live), exhaustion popup `Not enough Focus Points. Need 5, have 0.` with ZERO requests; no per-long-rest/once/24h flag exists in the lane — RAW-envelope intent stays an owner question (do not file blind). **CHOOSER MID-DWELL RIG (deterministic single-tab, live 2026-11-15 explore run PW — closes the OV "recipe not yet found" gap):** the picker dwells across `browser_run_code_unsafe` calls; with it open on a thin 0-HP corpse, an external scoped `PATCH /<Victim> {value:{currentHitPoints:5}}` + ≥500 ms echo, then the `Revive` press, lands the inline dialog note `<T> is not at 0 Hit Points.` with the picker STAYING OPEN and a fetch-capture window showing ZERO requests, focus untouched; the same dwell answers a focus-DRAIN via `PATCH /<Caster> {value:{focusPoints:2}}` → inline `Not enough Focus Points. Need 5, have 2.` zero-spend (never negative); restoring both bytes then pressing commits normally (picker commit = ONE 4-key merged POST, byte-verified). No-corpse open refusal: a board of ONLY bucket-less rows + bucket-positive corpses presses `No target at 0 Hit Points.` with the chooser never opening — gate order is focus FIRST, then corpse census; select-baked `1/1` bucket-less rows are NOT counted as corpses (`resolveLiveTargetHp` row-fallback reads currentHp 1). (d) Success-leg re-lived under the camel law: armed Roll on DC 10 (+11 dial) → heal sets `currentHitPoints` to 36, bumps `relentlessRageUses:1`, `save_result` + success `ability_use` pair, all channels drained.
- **Unpaid-skip pay-state gate law (fixed 2026-11-15 fix revivify-chooser-skip-rollback-inflates-unspent-slot):** `createSkipHandler` and the five metamagic skip/separate-modal lanes (StoneSkin/PfP skips, Resistance/EnhanceAbility/PfE two-stage, GR no-effects picker dismiss) now short-circuit unless `pending.slotConsumed` — the canonical PAID stamp `createConfirmHandler` sets at its `prepareSpellCast` pay seam (castRefused precedent). Verdict of the full audit: EVERY gated chooser arms UNPAID (`spellGates`→`makePending` spends nothing; payment is confirm-only; the `gateUpcast` deduct-at-arm hook has no live chooser wiring; Power-Word `spendPowerWordSlot` cast-and-spend on its own modal skips is NOT a `createSkipHandler` consumer and stays untouched). Consequence: an unpaid Skip is a zero-write/zero-row dismiss; `useSpellMetamagicFlow.skip.test.js`'s old 'refund'/'free' authority table is INVERTED to whole-lane 'clear' semantics (the CLA-086 upcast refund pair survives byte-identically only on paid rigs — `useConfirmableFlow.skip-paid-gate.test.js` + narrowed cla086/sp127 pins). RIG FACE for unpaid-open proof: the caster's `spell_slots_level_N` bucket key stays ABSENT (mirror-only until first write) — "unchanged" = still absent after Skips; the refusal control's pay POST then materializes the full mirror and re-proves baseline equality. SPELL-DETAIL MODAL is `.popup-overlay` chrome (the `.sp-overlay` face is the CHOOSER) — a `.sp-overlay`-keyed "detail didn't open" probe false-negatives.
