Sequential writes stay honest (350 ms same-tab assign→arm lands clean). `handleControllerChange` is the only lane that deletes the `controller` stamp while keeping the row (remove/clear/victory retire the whole row, arm dies with it — MB re-lived: Clear evicted the delegated row and the visitor overlay went with it, so a victory banner can never actually stack over a live delegated card; the banner itself broadcasts to LAN tabs and `Continue` is per-client). Pitfall: because the gate re-evaluates on every echo, RE-delegating control RE-MOUNTS the visitor's previously-open card (open state preserved by design) — an avatar click landing during that window can be intercepted by the re-mounted overlay; judge by overlay presence, not click target. Arm-selector recipe (live 2026-11-11 improve run): the monster card's target `<select aria-label="<Name> target">` lives on the CAROUSEL card, NOT inside `.mc-overlay` — `browser_select_option` with `.creature-card:has(img.avatar-image[alt="Goblin"]) [aria-label="Goblin target"]` arms cleanly from a visitor tab; SHARPENED (MB-2): if the stat-block overlay is shut/absorbed the wrapper `.creature-target` can measure 0×0 and `selectOption` times out `element is not visible` — the select still FUNCTIONS via the prototype-value-setter + bubbling `change` (cs GET-verified); an unarmed attack-chip press logs exactly one `roll` row with `targetName:null` and zero `hp_change` (honest unarmed face). HARNESS TRAP (live 2026-11-12 NN): `selectOption({timeout:N})` with NO value argument selects NOTHING but still fires `change` — the arm lane then honestly writes UNARM (`targetName:null` with the key PRESENT, GET-visible); a null-read after such a call is a bad probe, not an app swallow (the feared "assign-echo swallows a fast arm" was this, not remount interference — a same-tab assign→arm 350 ms apart lands clean). Code-Mode `execute` `fetch` needs ABSOLUTE URLs (`http://localhost/api/...` — relative paths throw `TypeError: fetch() URL is invalid`).
- **Sheet badge editors (`HiddenInput`, HP/Gold/tracked-resource badges) (sharpened live KB 2026-11-14):** click-to-reveal spinbutton (`span.hidden-input.click()able` → spawned number input + submit); fill MUST Meta+A select-all first or digits CONCATENATE ("11280"); macOS = Meta not Control. CLEAR-THEN-BLUR/ENTER CANCELS and ESCAPE CANCELS (fixed 2026-11-14 `08ebc98fd`, live-verified LC on both the HP and `Lay On Hands Pool` badges: blank-trim commit and Escape write NOTHING, close the editor; an EXPLICITLY typed `0` still commits — HP 224→0 with its `hp_change` row is the PASS face, not damage). The old KB law (silent 0-commit, Escape INERT, filed `bug-hidden-input-empty-commit-silently-zeros-hp-and-resources.md`) is DEAD — the unit pin flipped with the fix. textContent while editing reads `Label: /max (cur/max)` — input VALUES are not text nodes (probe trap; distinct from the boolean-latch `/1` render face). NPC card first input is NOT HP (native-setter wrote into NAME — repair via full-store cs POST). Arrow-clicks decrement by 1; `fill` may not persist maxHp (statblock re-derives on disk) but currentHp fill persists. `+NPC` card HP commits via focus setter+blur. Manual-edit log stamps (live 2026-11-14 improve sheet-hp-change-source-name): sheet-editor `hp_change` rows carry `sourceName:"sheet editor"` (CharHitPoints.jsx addEntry call-site), GM card rows keep `"GM HP override"` — the two families are distinguishable in the Log. CHEAPEST GM-card control face: a NON-zero card edit (e.g. 100→95) logs the override row WITHOUT arming the death-save `.dsp-overlay` (no Roll→Done ritual needed). Log writes are IMMEDIATE (~1 s fetchable via GET /log — only change-data has the 10 s debounce). PROBE SHAPES (live 2026-11-14 improve death-save-ghost-key-404-sweep): `span.hidden-input` renders the CURRENT value only (`50`, not `50/224`) — `.first()` on a sheet is the HP badge; the sheet death-save Roll is `div.death-saves-roll[role=button]`, NOT a `<button>` (hasText-button locators miss it); Initiative/Admin/Log sidebar buttons lack `.sidebar-link` — use `getByRole(name, exact)`. Lane request-capture without touching EventSource: wrap `window.fetch` post-load via `browser_evaluate` (guard on a `window.__*Log` flag) to record method+status per request family. Side face CLOSED: a sheet-editor HP-0 drop and recovery now issues ZERO `deathSavePrompt-<Name>` requests (mirror-gate `4015d88d4`, LC 2026-11-14; the pre-fix evict was a wildcard **DELETE** 404 — GET on an absent key answers `200 {value:null}`).
- **Save/queue ops**: save prompts queue one-at-a-time ("Next Save"/Done); Leading Evasion modal queues post-Done (Skip); Charm/Empowered-Strikes/Combat-Superiority chooser modals interpose before save prompts on nearly every PC hit — flush chain: Done→Roll Save→Skip. `sp-roll-btn` RE-CASTS on every click (count ability_use). Modal-lane save results: `saveResult-<T>` lags one prompt behind — popup + save-damage log are decisive.
- **`browser_take_screenshot` save-path law (corrected in place 2026-11-12 improve rules-popup-blocked-feedback)**: the tool REJECTS paths outside its allowed roots (repo cwd + `.playwright-mcp/`) — an absolute path under the approved temp dir fails with "outside allowed roots", and it now requires the `scale` key. Save witnesses to `.playwright-mcp/<name>.png` (gitignored) and `rm` at exit, or use `page.screenshot({path})` inside `browser_run_code_unsafe` (that CAN write the temp dir) when you don't need the inline view. Related Code Mode note: playwright tools are reached as `tools.playwright.*` via `execute` — a bare `playwright` tool name errors "No tool named playwright".
- **Proving a data-integrity guard past a UI gate (live 2026-11-08 fix run)**: to show the handler/service refusal (not just the disabled input) works, programmatically lift `disabled` in `page.evaluate`, then use *trusted* `page.keyboard` presses + Tab to commit — a synthetic `dispatchEvent(new FocusEvent('blur'))` may not hit React's delegated handler — and verify the refusal via the trusted-event route plus a `page.on('console')` listener for the guard's error line.
- React-19 controlled `input[type=number]` live-driving (live 2026-11-12 improve dungeon-grid-clamp-notice): set the value with the prototype `value` setter + a bubbling `input` event (React 19 onChange needs it); a CLEARED field reads `Number('') === 0` (not NaN) through the component's `onChange={e => setX(Number(e.target.value))}` — range gates keyed on `< min` honestly catch blanks, so a blank-grid probe expecting a NaN/required face actually sees the below-min gate.
- **Page-vs-node "same code different output" forensics (live 2026-11-12 UF descent):** before believing any browser≠node behavioral split, rule out module-cache ghosts: `page.context().clearCache()` DOES NOT EXIST in this Playwright build — use `const cdp = await page.context().newCDPSession(page); await cdp.send('Network.setCacheDisabled', { cacheDisabled: true })` then `page.goto` (persists for the page). A dynamic `import('/src/…/file.js?v=N')` with a FRESH query each probe bypasses the document's loaded-module cache; Vite itself busts invalidated imports with `?t=<epoch>` rewrites (visible in served text). Verify identity in stages: (1) curl the served URL and normalized-diff vs disk (Vite rewrites import paths, hex→decimal literals, object shorthand, quotes — all cosmetic), (2) run the served copy in node (import-path sed `"/src/services/maps/x.js(\?t=\d+)?"` → `"./x.js"`, strip sourceMappingURL; a copy with query-suffixed imports resolves as SEPARATE module instances — safe as probes), (3) instrument the smallest shared dep (rng.js draw counter grouped by `new Error().stack` caller frame) on BOTH sides, restore byte-exact (md5 == `git show HEAD:…`). If all three agree but output still splits, suspect engine internals (see the random-sort law in §2) — V8 tier-up can also flip behavior MID-SESSION in one page. Also: `browser_evaluate` accepts NO `args` param in this MCP build (params silently dropped → undefined-inside-function error) — embed values in the function string. **Sheet fetch census (live 2026-11-15 improve revive-strip-unconscious):** wrapping `window.fetch` via `browser_evaluate` (push `{method, url, body}` into an array on the page, read it back in a later call) gives per-commit byte-accurate POST/method/body logs without disturbing SSE traffic — the cheap write-side proof for revive/batch/one-merged-POST laws; arm it around the commit and report the whole body, not just counts.
- **Two-tab decision-race rig must be click-synchronous (live 2026-11-14 improve drafts-race-loser-copy):** Playwright `locator.click()`'s actionability window (~50–100 ms) lets the winner tab's SSE broadcast disable the loser's Approve/Reject buttons BEFORE its click lands — no server race fires and you get a false-clean pass. Fire BOTH tabs via `Promise.all(pages.map(p => p.evaluate(() => enabledButton.click())))` — reproduced the 404 first try. Related probe law: when asserting "row gone" on the loser tab, `getByText(name)` also matches a banner that NAMES the draft — scope the probe to `.campaign-builder-row` or trust the empty-state copy.
- **`setRuntimeValue` dedups equal-value writes (live 2026-11-13 healing-light fix run):** a commit that writes the SAME value the bucket already holds fires NO POST at all — a "silent zero-effect write" (e.g. healing a full-HP target) leaves no network trace and no change-data delta; probe the netlog / GET-diff before assuming a failed or missing request. **Feature-row mount lag after sidebar character switch (live 2026-11-13 healing-light fix run):** wait on the *feature row itself* (e.g. `waitForFunction` for the `Healing Light:` `.clickable` inside `.char-actions`), not just the `.char-actions` container — the row mounts ~1 s later and a rect lookup taken early returns "row gone". **Fresh-select board rows lie about HP; buckets are truth (live 2026-11-13 hand-of-healing fix run):** on a freshly-selected campaign the mounted `combatSummary` hydrates with all PC rows at stale `1/1` — any heal/damage adjudication must read the bucket mirror (`currentHitPoints`), never the row HP; a picker built from rows will mislead. The row-vs-bucket deferral is OVER: the HoH pre-spend gate shipped (`f3950317c`) and resolves the target's LIVE bucket HP at confirm time, so a stale-picker face can never spend focus on a full target (GZ re-lived 2026-11-13: radio aria stayed `60 of 112` after an external refill, confirm still refused zero-write). Mercy subclass swap recipe: one-line disk edit of the fixture's `class.subclass.name` + sheet reload arms `Hand of Healing:` with no EB/initiative rig (restore byte-exact, md5 `ba4d456c…`).
- **Divergent-dwell HoH rig is mirror-dependent (live 2026-11-14 hoh stale-snapshot fix run):** whether the divergent (gate-live vs application-stale) face appears at all depends on the victim's client-store `hitPoints` mirror — a fresh select/mount can seed it and the picker then honestly reads `60 of 112` (control face, bug door closed). To re-derive the bug face deterministically, after re-select delete `hitPoints` (keep `currentHitPoints`) from the victim's in-memory store via `getStore(name).delete('hitPoints'); notify(name)`.
- **Heal-lane dwell-refund rig laws (live GZ 2026-11-13, post-`88daae2be`):** mid-dwell external drift rides an in-page `fetch PATCH {value:{currentHitPoints:N}}` (same-origin, no CORS) inside the SAME `run_code_unsafe` call as the roll presses + `Done` click, with `page.waitForTimeout(1200–1600)` between (SSE echo settle); a `page.on('request')` write-monitor armed AFTER the rolls proves the refund face (zero target POST + source-bucket pool POST + `amount:0` log row). Mounted heal UI is NO LONGER display-stale (improve heal-modal-live-hp-echo, re-lived HZ 2026-11-14: the modal header + `Heal — X (a / b HP)` section line + radio aria adopt an external mid-dwell PATCH within one SSE tick and the full-HP gate flips live) — commit truth still rides the write monitor + GET. HoH/heal result modals DWELL across sheet navigation until their own `Done` — an un-closed overlay makes every sidebar `button` click time out with `short-rest-overlay … intercepts pointer events` (that timeout message is itself the open-modal census probe). Single-cure-tier synthesis (GZ, for `bug-single-cure-tier-unfiltered…` fix-verify): one-line rename `Restoring Touch`→anything in `public/data/2024/classes.json` (single occurrence `/6/class_levels/13/features/0`; the handler looks RT up by NAME in specialActions) flips an L20 paladin's LoH modal to the `!hasRestoringTouch && alsoCures` tier; reverse str.replace restores byte-exact (md5 `8d06a8c2…`, `git diff` proves it) — the same trick rides any fix-verify of the tier. HP-ONLY-ECHO law (re-lived HZ 2026-11-14): the live-echo adoption covers the target's HP buckets ONLY — a cure section snapshots `activeConditions` at open, so an external re-apply mid-dwell stays INVISIBLE until reopen, and an external clear mid-dwell leaves the stale button clickable (exactly why the commit-guard face is live-reachable); the section re-filters live after the modal's OWN commits (both tiers witnessed).
- **Initiative-board Tab-reach probe (live 2026-11-17 improve visitor-avatar-keyboard-zoom):** a settled initiative board exposes ~55 visible focus stops — a Tab walk from document start to reach a card avatar burns the call budget; enumerate the focus-stop list once, then focus the preceding stop and press Tab once, asserting `document.activeElement` landed. **`keyboard.press` on a stray focus switches views (same run):** while a sidebar link holds focus, a press sequence silently navigates the app mid-probe (the card you meant to drive is gone on the next read) — re-probe `document.activeElement` immediately before every `keyboard.press` batch. **Sheet two-stage popup drift (live 2026-11-16 attack-popup fix run):** the damage stage dismisses on click, so a Done-click that fires BEFORE the to-hit verdict mounts consumes a *stale* Done and desyncs every later probe by one stage. Recipe: assert `.popup-overlay` text contains HIT/MISS before clicking Done, then read `.dice-roll-damage-applied` before any further click. Lethal-blow rigs need OVERKILL damage to exercise the clamp path — if a roll lands exactly at remaining HP, re-attack; revive via the HP badge (Meta+A fill) between kills.
- **Portaled-to-body yet offset (live 2026-11-17 improve avatar-zoom-overlay-fullscreen):** an `x=180` measurement on a body-portaled `position:fixed` overlay is the rule's OWN authored inset, never a positioning-host failure — ~14 content-area modal families hardcode `left:180px` (`.popup-overlay`, `.sp-overlay`, `.avatar-modal-overlay` pre-fix), so audit the rule's own fixed insets before blaming ancestors. Reference family for a true full-viewport scrim is `.modal-shell-overlay`/`.loading-overlay` (inset-0 + `var(--background-color-overlay)`); `.sp-overlay` is content-area, NOT a full-viewport reference.

- **Party-Inventory coin-row scoping (live 2026-11-17 fix retired-inventory-wildcard-shadow):** the coin label span `.pi-currency-label` is NOT a leaf (it carries an icon node) — a `children.length===0` text matcher false-negatives on it; scope the coin chips via the ancestor `.pi-currency-row` whose `textContent` includes the coin name.

- **Row-anchored button lookup MUST prove the row, and every destructive confirm MUST be READ before accepting (live explore FI 2026-11-17, near-miss):** walking UP ≤6 parent hops looking for an element whose `textContent.includes(<slug>) && length < N` silently matched a MULTI-ROW ancestor (the whole list container carries every slug) and grabbed a NEIGHBOUR row's Delete — the shared `DeleteConfirmModal` then named the real victim (`This will permanently delete the map 'Battle Arena'`) and a scripted follow-up clicked `Yes, Delete Permanently` on it (owner fixture battle-arena.json lost; recovered byte-exact — see next bullet). Two locks from now on: (1) anchor row buttons on the TIGHTEST repeatable row container (collect ALL candidates, assert exactly one row's own text matches, and assert the candidate BUTTON lives inside it), and (2) read the confirm dialog's quoted NAME inside the same call and ABORT if it differs from the intended target — never click a destructive confirm without echoing its victim name. Escape hatch: the dialog copy naming the target is itself the witness — capture it before confirming.

- **Maps-manager row + hex POI right-click rig laws (live improve context-menu-viewport-clamp 2026-11-17):** (a) Maps-manager rows are `li.maps-manager-item` with per-row `Open/Activate/Rename` buttons carrying exact `aria-label`s — anchor inside evaluate with `getAttribute('aria-label') === 'Open "<name>"'` (CSS attr selectors with embedded quotes SyntaxError through the MCP string layer, per the escape-ban law); climbing ancestors for "the row" can overshoot to the whole-list container and click the FIRST row's Open (witnessed: opened owner map Battle Arena instead of the probe — recovered by md5 verification, zero writes; pairs with the row-anchoring lock above). (b) Hex POI right-clicks land on the `.poi-item` group's `getBoundingClientRect()` centre — world-center math can miss the ~41px hit rect at fit-zoom (dispatch target is still `rect.placed-hit-area` per the POI context-menu laws).
- **Owner-map files ARE recoverable byte-exact from the campaign snapshot (live FI 2026-11-17):** `public/campaigns/.snapshots/<campaign>-<ts>.zip` holds `maps/*.json` (unzip -j + md5 against the recorded pin BEFORE copying into place — the 11-01 snapshot's battle-arena.json verified `94fcd0bfe9…` exact). A wiped fixture's restore proof is md5-vs-pin + API 200 re-GET, not vibes; `git status` proves nothing (campaigns are git-IGNORED, §61 law).
- **Synthetic adjudication-prompt rig + initiative picker probe + stale-echo law (improve 2026-11-17; SHARPENED by GK explore same day):** POST change-data `savePrompt-<Target>-<promptId>` `{value:{promptId,targetName,attackerName,saveType,saveDc,damageFormula,damageType,dcSuccess:'half'}}` mounts the SavePromptModal DC face in a mounted localhost tab with ZERO combat; `<attacker>-biPrompt` synthetic rig is DEAD (2026-11-18): the whole BI-prompt modal/key lane was deleted by owner decision — see PROVENANCE LAW below. DELETE on a seed key echoes a null payload which the listeners treat as NOT-a-dismissal — dismiss via the in-modal Skip/Dismiss instead, then verify eviction: the SAVE lane's Dismiss really DELETEs the key server-side (GET shows no `savePrompt-*` residue; only a `leading_evasion_declined` automation row when the DEX+damage seed also stacked the `.sp-overlay--evasion` chooser — drain that FIRST, its Skip button intercepts Dismiss clicks on the prompt below). PROVENANCE LAW (HL same run; defense half CLOSED 2026-11-17 fix bi-defense-prompt-producer-orphaned; LANE TERMINATED 2026-11-18 fix bi-offense-modal-rider-gate-arg-shape, owner decision): the whole BI-prompt modal/key lane was DELETED — its sole producer, the pipeline rider, called `hasBardicInspirationOffense(ctx.playerStats.name, …)` (a STRING) against the gate's `(playerStats, campaignName)` signature → a string's `.name` is `undefined` → the gate was FALSE for every holder → the modal NEVER mounted on real hits (the fully-mocked rider tier pinned the broken string call and masked the defect; the earlier biPrompt eviction/stranded-twin/deferred-queue laws are moot with the lane). The popup-inline `Bardic Inspiration - Offense/Defense (dN)` button (its two call sites pass the REAL playerStats object: `handlePlainDamage.js`/`useLoggedDiceRollAttack.js`) is the SINGLE BI adjudication lane (§17). Entrance-focus census (GK; SyncPrompt/AllySelection remain after the 2026-11-18 BI-modal deletion): SyncPrompt/AllySelection pull entrance focus and restore the pre-open anchor on close; SavePromptModal's pull lands on the first focusable, which can be the SECONDARY-TARGETS checkbox inside the stacked evasion overlay (an `active-save-prompt`-scoped `contains(active)` probe false-negatives "no pull" — probe containment against ALL `.sp-overlay` nodes). Initiative card target picker is `select[aria-label="<Name> target"]` — an `input[aria-label$=" target"]` probe false-negatives; keep the element-neutral `[aria-label="… target"]` shape. AGENT-HARNESS SIBLING: `read`/`edit` tool echoes can go STALE mid-session (file appears un-rewritten, an edit "succeeds" against phantom text) — ground-truth with `grep`/`md5` on disk before believing either direction.

- **BI-offense popup-inline natural ride + rider-death census (live IJ 2026-11-18, filed AND fixed same day — `bug-bi-offense-modal-rider-gate-arg-shape`: rider/modal/key lane DELETED, popup-inline is the single offense lane):** holder arming for a NON-bard = three bucket keys — `bardicInspirationDie` (number), `bardicInspirationCombatOptions` (a JSON STRING, e.g. `'["offense_add_to_damage"]'` — the gate JSON.parses the RAW value, a real array stringifies via the parse to nothing), `bardicInspirationUses` (`{current,max}` or bare number); seed the live-mirror way via in-page `await import('/src/hooks/runtime/useRuntimeState.js')` + 4-arg `setRuntimeValue` (same-Vite-URL import gets the app's OWN module instance — the rider in-place probe `buildBardicInspirationOffenseStep` was retired with the lane 2026-11-18; the structural pin is now the golden chain census in `attackRollDamageSteps.test.js`: 23 steps, `charger:checked`→`rollBaseDamage` seam). Sheet attack press = `.attacks:has-text("<Weapon>") .clickable` first cell (the NAME cell IS the trigger); on HIT the chain stages a `.popup-modal` (guarded `Done`/`Cancel attack`) — CENSUS PROBE LAW: a rider-chain probe keyed only on `.sp-header` false-negatives "rider never ran" while the staged popup holds the chain; read popup innerText too, press `Done` to commit, and note the damage face re-renders with `.dice-roll-reroll-btn` offers (BI-offense/defense, superiority rerolls…) AFTER the base damage is applied — pressing the BI button rolls, rewrites the total ("1d8 → +2 → 9"), `Done` dispatches ONE `ability_use` row + spends uses + nulls the trio (die/options/grantedBy). Overkill is honest: the ledger total updates, the target HP floors at 0. The sheet HIT also runs the rider chain (cunning chooser pauses/resumes via `handleAttackRiderClose`) — a chooser Cancel resumes WITHOUT latching a skip flag (charger DOES latch) — repeat-hit same-round re-offer is the open behavior face (backlog).
- **Playwright action-wait budget (live 2026-11-18 clear-latch fix run):** `selectOption`/`click` inside `run_code_unsafe` default to 30 s actionability waits — one hidden/intercepted element can burn the whole 20 s run budget before the step's real work runs; pass an explicit `{ timeout: ≤5000 }` on EVERY action and judge end state by API probe (in-page fetch/GET), never by the action resolving.
- **run_code_unsafe scope + fetch-spy census isolation (live 2026-11-19 death-save-batch fix run):** (a) `window.__x = await page.evaluate(...)` written at Playwright (Node) scope silently throws `ReferenceError: window is not defined` at Node scope AFTER earlier in-call browser evaluates have already run — the page state mutated even though the call "failed"; resume from the mutated state, never re-run the whole call. (b) Wire-shape POST censuses must slice by index (`window.__posts.slice(n)` captured right before the roll leg) around harness PATCH/seed traffic, otherwise forge/setup POSTs pollute the roll's POST count.
- **Code-Mode-generated Playwright code interpolation trap (live 2026-11-19 zone-double-arm fix run):** when you build `browser_run_code_unsafe` code from inside a Code Mode `execute` template literal, inner `${...}` expressions (including Playwright string templates) are interpolated by the OUTER runtime → `SyntaxError` or silently-substituted values; build the inner code via string concatenation or escape `\${`.
- **Silent-success select face: `selectOption` can miss React state (live 2026-11-19 improve ruleset-ledger-more-fold):** on the wizard's class `<select>`, `locator.selectOption({value:'Wizard'})` set the DOM value (a follow-up probe even read `'Wizard'`) but the wizard's React state never received the class — the armed ruleset ledger honestly showed NO Spells group, a nasty false "premise broken" reading. The native `HTMLSelectElement` value setter + bubbling `change` (the RG ledger-rig recipe) fires the handler for real (witness: Next lands on `Step 7: Subclass` with `Abjuration` pre-seeded). Law: after ANY select-set, verify through the app's OWN downstream state (next-step content, pre-seeded data), never by reading the select's value back; a stale inert dialog can be Cancelled and the select re-set natively with no state damage.
- **`page.waitForFunction` options are the THIRD arg (live BBB 2026-11-20):** the signature is `waitForFunction(fn, arg, options)` — passing `{ timeout: 5000 }` as the SECOND arg binds it as the pageFunction argument and the call silently runs with the 30 000 ms tool default, blowing the bounded-execution ceiling when the predicate never lands (witnessed: 30 s TimeoutError on a spawn card whose selector was genuinely absent). Correct form: `waitForFunction(fn, null, { timeout: 5000 })`.
- **`browser_evaluate` sandbox blocks `Function(...)` construction (live 2026-11-19 improve fog-ghost-memory-across-flips):** the evaluate runner rejects `Function('...')`/`probe.toString()` re-hydration tricks (`Unexpected identifier 'Function'`) — pass literal function bodies per call instead of shipping a serialized probe for reconstruction.

- In `browser_run_code_unsafe`, a hoisted `function` declaration placed AFTER a top-level `return` bounces `SyntaxError: Unexpected token 'function'` — the harness wraps the code so the trailing declarations die (live re-pit 2026-11-20 improve audio-cue-app-level); inline any helper loops inside the single `async (page) =>` arrow instead.

- **Sheet attack-grid DOM + log-timing (live 2026-11-21 improve furniture-cover-reason):** `.attacks.mastery-enabled` is a FLAT grid — the weapon-name cell and all chips are siblings, so a row-scoped `hasText:'Longbow'`-style filter matches the whole grid and the locator stalls 30 s; press the exact-text to-hit chip (e.g. `+7`) instead. And the attack `roll` row lands only when the result popup's `Done` commits — a `GET /log` while the popup dwells reads `[]`; never conclude "no audit" pre-dismiss.

## 13. Rules-engine mechanics facts
- **Ruleset-switch stale-subclass law (shipped 2026-11-20 improve existing-char-ruleset-switch):** the same class prints subclasses under DIFFERENT names across rulesets (Wizard/Abjuration 5e vs 'Abjurer' 2024) — a switch that keeps a target-unresolvable `class.subclass.name` makes `rules-proficiencies` hard-throw (`expected class.subclass to be an object`), the sheet's `getPlayerStats` dies and NO sheet/affordances render (witnessed live pre-fix). Guard: `useWizardNavigation.step7Valid` requires the chosen subclass to be a MEMBER of the CURRENT ruleset's `class.subtypes` list (string-or-object rows), so the save stays gated until the subclass step is re-confirmed post-switch; fresh builds unaffected (names always come from the current list). Edit-mode wizard now shares the ONE canonical step index with create (Ruleset step visible in both; editing only changes the ARRIVAL step to Basic).
- **Subclass-spell reference grammar law (live 2026-11-19 improve classes-json-dangling-spell-refs):** subclass spell refs resolve by EXACT `name ===` against the ref's OWN ruleset pool only (`loadSpells(version)` routing 5e→`/data/spells.json`, 2024→`/data/2024/spells.json`) — lanes never cross pools (5e: `spellCalc.addSubclassAlwaysPreparedSpells` + `getPreSelectedSpells`→`useWizardSpells`→`WizardStepSpells`, `prerequisites[0].index` supplies the level gate; 2024: `majors[].spells[]` `{name,level[,landType]}` + bare-string automation refs). A casing/typo mismatch is therefore a SILENT dead row (offers nothing). Monster-card spellcasting resolves 5e-FIRST with a 2024 fallback (`findMonsterSpell`): ADDING a canonical 5e row for a formerly-2024-only spell flips affected 5e monsters onto the 2014 face (e.g. Death Knight Destructive Wave CON→WIS `half`, DC still the row's authored value) — audit `MonsterCardModal.*` data-locks asserting pool absence when touching `public/data/spells.json`. Durable pin: `src/services/rules/spells/subclassSpellIntegrity.test.js` walks every ref shape on disk (coverage floor + retired-spelling pins).
- **Combat Superiority prompt holder-gate law (shipped 2026-11-17 improve superiority-prompt-monster-gate):** `pendingCombatSuperiorityPrompt` stamps ONLY for maneuver holders — the gate is `getKnownManeuvers(characterName, campaignName).length === 0 → return` (battleMaster.js reads the `BattleMasterManeuvers_selection` runtime key; the same predicate targetResolution.js uses at the roll seam and the consumers `combatSuperiorityQueries.js` handleAttackRiderPrompt/handleSkillCheckPrompt enforce, so pre-gate non-holder writes were always dead residue nulled downstream). One write site shared by 5e+2024 (sole caller `processAttackAfterResult`); `_lastRollContext` + `lastAttackRoll` still land in monster buckets by design (generic records). Consumer census: only the sheet-mounted `useCombatSuperiorityModal` poller reads the key (own-bucket, monster cards never mount it) — the monster-bucket face was inert residue, no bug file needed. RIG LAW: the poller DEFERS while the sheet's staged-hit popup is visible (`popupHtmlRef` gate) — the Attack Rider chooser mounts only after the final Done drains; capture the stamp object via a store-`set` patch, not a GET race.
- **HAM clamp-witness rig + applied-delta law (live 2026-11-17 improve ham-broken-by-clamp):** Goblin Scimitar 1d6+2 (3–8) vs HAM capacity 6 — repeated chip presses on the SAME monster reliably land incoming-≤5 clamped HITs, and HAM applied on every commit (no once-per-turn refusal observed from repeated presses of one monster's chip). Canonical metadata shape since this improve: `applyDamage.js` derives `appliedReduction = reducedFrom − amount` ONCE at the single producer and stamps it into `featureReduction.reducedBy`, the `heavy_armor_master_applied` automation row, and `damageReducedByFeature` (zero consumers outside applyDamage + its tests) — so `reducedBy > reducedFrom` is now structurally impossible; the Log renderer only ever prints `reducedFrom→amount`.
- **Save-popup leg-order asymmetry (live 2026-11-16 attack-popup fix run):** `handleNpcSaveDamage` applies the **secondary** damage leg BEFORE the primary (opposite of `saveProcessing`) — for its combined popup the pre-hit HP must come from the SECONDARY apply's `oldHp`; taking the primary's reads post-secondary and misprints the transition. Pinned by comment at the site; since the fix, damage feeds carry the applier's true `applyResult.oldHp` as `targetOldHp` and `DiceRollResult` prefers it over the additive `targetCurrentHp + finalDamage` reconstruction (additive is fallback for never-applied feeds like immunity popups). Adjacent suspect NOT yet filed: `HealOutcomeNotice` (same file) uses the same reconstructive math against a max-clamped heal — looks wrong on overheal, needs its own repro.
- **Victory-banner law + `lastAttack` shape (shipped 2026-11-13 improve victory-banner-zero-attack-clear):** `campaign.lastAttack` payloads from the monster-card lane can LACK `timestamp` (Goblin Scimitar ride proved it) — attack freshness can never be gated on a timestamp boundary; a whole-object JSON fingerprint is the reliable per-board-generation attack evidence. Clear lane accordingly stamps `campaign.lastCombatResult` with `celebrated` + `attackEvidence`: celebrated = a `lastAttack` exists AND its fingerprint differs from the copy the PREVIOUS clear banked (the Clear is the board-generation seam; the stamp ALWAYS writes, so a zero-attack prep clear advances the boundary and outranks stale banners with `celebrated:false`). Display gate: `useTvPresentation.victoryVisible` requires `celebrated` — and `VictoryBanner` mounts ONLY in `Initiative.jsx` via that one gate (TV and non-TV share it). SHARPENED (live 2026-11-16 improve victory-banner-singular-count1): a bare cs-PATCH kill NEVER mounts the banner (the stamp is authored ONLY by `confirmClear`, and a fresh session with zero attack rolls writes `celebrated:false`); cheapest honest evidence = arm `select[aria-label="<Monster> target"]` → avatar → `.mc-overlay [data-kind="attack"]` → Done → card Close (~6 s, one monster). SECOND boards need FRESH distinct evidence — re-rolling the same monster/weapon/target can reproduce the prior fingerprint, so switch monster (Goblin Scimitar → Bugbear Morningstar) to guarantee distinct `attackEvidence`. Dismiss-pref comparison is `!==` against the stamp, so a stale epoch-ms pref never suppresses a new banner. Count-1 grammar since the improve: `1 foe defeated` (no `All` prefix), plural face `All N foes defeated` unchanged (pinned `victoryLines.test.js`).
- **Split-party presence law (live EP 2026-11-08, ee56b9f65)**: `away:true` on a PLAYER combatSummary row = present=false — turn walk skips it, TargetSelect/spell targets (`getCsAndTargets`)/metamagic picker/EffectAdder/TV up-next all hide it, card greys (`.creature-away`) + parks LAST via `sortCreaturesForBoard`. GM-only authoring: toolbar `Split Party` (role=dialog "Split party", `.secondary-target-row` checkboxes aria-label `Keep <n> present`, `Apply`) and per-card `.away-toggle-btn` (aria-label `Mark|Rejoin <n> …`); visitor tabs see NO toggle, NO toolbar, disabled inputs (live-verified), and adopted live via SSE without reload. Split WIPES initiative; rejoin auto-lands at lowest PRESENT roll ("end of round") or honest `initiative not yet rolled` audit; walk from an AWAY/unknown active creature moves to nearest present neighbour WITHOUT round increment; all-away board falls back to legacy walk (never stalls); audit types `party_split|party_rejoined|initiative_while_away`; enforcement-ON awayDenial text "…is away from the party — the GM must rejoin them…" + `enforcement_denied` log (that lane's vocabulary is `creature:`, not characterName — not an attribution bug). Filed defects: initiative input on away cards is unguarded (type+Tab resurrects slot AND un-parks the card — `setInitiative` raw sort) and away-roll `awayNote` has zero popup consumers. FIXED 2026-11-08 (40a91cb87): presence gate in `handleInitiativeChange`/`setInitiative` (refuses away rows ahead of all writes, Thief's-Reflexes grant face closed too), `sortCreaturesForBoard` replaces the raw sort in the service, `InitiativeControl` input is presence-keyed + disabled on away rows (split/rejoin re-syncs the display), `(Init n)` modal annotation suppressed for away rows. The away-roll `awayNote` zero-consumer defect FIXED same day (99c3843a9): `DiceRollResult` renders the note under the roll summary via a file-local `AwayNotice` in the amber notice family.
- **Spell overlay is visitor-WRITABLE — INTENTIONAL, owner-ratified shared-table authoring (EP 2026-11-08; authority question CLOSED by owner decision 2026-11-08, matrix in AGENTS.md §"Map Lane Write Authority")**: LAN visitor's Spell button → shape panel → real-click on a `.grid-cell` LANDS `POST /spell-overlay {action:'add'}` (2xx — route gates only CAMPAIGN_LOCK, no refuseUnlessGm, unlike the save-listener 403 lane — BY DESIGN, do not file or gate); visitor Clear-All works via role=dialog "Yes, Clear All" ("overlays placed by everyone" copy confirms multi-client authoring is intended). Store lives OUTSIDE change-data (GET /spell-overlay returns `{value:null}` shape; panel "Active (n)" is the truth). Visitor Ruler = 2 real cell-clicks → "75 ft (n cells)" readout (per-client `useState`, never server-shared); visitor 3D mounts canvas zero-errors (toggle likewise local).
- **dc_success**: engine default `action.dc_success ?? 'half'` (MonsterCardModal.jsx:255/:1011/:1032/:1145/:2158, applyDamage.js:88-97, saveProcessing:127) — half-on-silence is app convention; RAW success-zero rows MUST author `dc_success:"none"` (MV-20 half-leak family MA-0481/0622/0781). Picker auto-fabricates "takes half damage" copy whenever dc_success absent — copy + mechanic are ONE fingerprint. `dc_success:"none"` inline zero-success legs STILL emit save-damage entries at finalDamage:0/total:0 with NO hp_change (picker route emits none at all) — adjudicate via saveSuccess:true + finalDamage:0 + ABSENT hp_change, never a dcSuccess stamp (inline seam never stamps it). Half = floor: fd=floor(rolled/2).
- **Save lanes**: EB-NPC saves auto-resolve INLINE (no .sp-modal prompt; DC+type stamped honestly by formatter); damage-bearing AoE monster saves go inline NOT .sp-modal; damageless AoE monster spells early-return without prompt (spellDamagelessSaveCondition Helpers:371). PC prompt = `.sp-modal` "Saving Throw Required" + Roll Save/Dismiss (stage discriminator: picker = "Select creatures…"+Cancel; prompt = "Roll Save"); NPC prompt auto-rolls but stays OPEN — Dismiss only (Roll Save double-adjudicates). "DC Unknown — no success or failure" popup copy is cosmetic while lastAttack/log carry real sdc/saveType. Spellcasting-row save_type = CASTING ability, not the target save axis — adjudicate vs spell-DB dc_type (Entangle RAW = STRENGTH; hunger-of-hadar 2024 = DEX). Monster cast-save rows are ROW-LOCAL: ActionSaveRoll (3-arg) never calls findMonsterSpell — spells.json axes never auto-pulled (unthreaded gap). Save-prompt seam logs save-damage via `logEntry` PROP — harness addEntry captures only direct calls; pin both channels.
- **Save-rig seams are lane-specific**: block-save lane consumes `saving_throws.<abbr>.{modifier}` OBJECT (bare int → NaN); picker lane reads FULL-WORD `saveBonuses.<word>` (abbrev coexists but picker ignores); monster-chip lane uses `ability_score_modifiers.<abbr>` (numeric saving_throws there yields NaN). Deterministic inline NPC save: full-word `saveBonuses.<word>:±19` + nested `saving_throws:{<abbr>:{modifier:±N}}` in ONE full-store cs POST — folds into victim `total` while popup prints "+0" cosmetically — TOTALS are truth, popup modifier text cosmetic. NPC AoE auto-save NEVER consults rigged bonuses (saveBonus:0 even after stamps) — use a PC victim or low DC for success faces. cs `saving_throws:{con:{modifier:N}}` full-store POST = deterministic save-success rig; full-word ±19 flips picker determinism mid-fight, survives initiative walks, shares POST body with HP rig without clobbering (if re-stamped). At DC10, ±5 stamps only ~70% control.
- **Recharge economy**: spend stamped at fire/picker-open (`monsterRecharge {recharged:false,threshold:N}` per-char change-data key `<Monster>.monsterRecharge` — cs stamp inert); re-press before recovery REFUSES (`.mc-recharge-refusal` + `<slug>_refused` log + chip class `mc-dice-link-spell-spent`, zero victim rolls even on inline rows); recovery d6 rolls at owner's own turn-start only — cs round-POST does NOT roll recovery (turn-start engine: walk rounds; recovery lands first Next after array end; d6 can starve ~5 rounds — budget). Range-empty emanation rows still spend recharge at fire-time (inline fire). Refire after re-charge RE-SPENDS with fresh stamp. Judge by recharge/recharge_failed log dice, not gate mirror; reload mid-round may already have wrapped.

Fresh EB join leaves cs.activeCreatureName ABSENT → first press allowed, latch stamps ''. Child `uses` arms a phantom dawn-counter double-economy — economy rides rows[0] header only. Save-chip self-suppresses legendary Expend chip (absence correct).
- **Damage logs / lastAttack**: log entries `type:"roll"` + `rollType:"attack"|"damage"` (e.type=='attack'/'damage' filters FALSE-negative; `type=="damage"` zero); attack entries key `name` not abilityName; `log.attack.total` = RAW NAT (bonus in bonusDetail/targetAc separate) — never rebuild nat+bonus from log rows; the +bonus total lives only in popup chrome + lastAttack. Secondary damage rides the SAME primary damage entry via secondaryFormula/secondaryRolls/secondaryTotal/secondaryDamageType/secondaryFinalDamage — don't await a second rollType:damage entry. hp_change key is `damageBreakdown:[{damageType,amount,resisted,status}]`; no boolean `resisted` key — unreduced proof = resistanceReduction:0 + resistanceNotice:null + victim resistances:[]. SYSTEMIC: post-damage lastAttack mis-stamps secondaryDamage/secondaryDamageType/actualDamage = PRIMARY values on structured-secondary rows (handlePlainDamage :156/:160 order) and attack-log rolls can dupe — damage log + hp ledger are exact; cite family, don't file per-row. Weapon-type labels unreliable BOTH directions (reach-melee→"ranged", javelin→"melee") — judge lane from reach field/damage entry, rangeReason:null = gridless fingerprint; attack entries can stamp NEITHER weaponType nor range. Reach>8 authored fires 4/4 first-click gridless (rangeReason:null). Band "20/60 ft." inert: dual-mode chooser arms ONLY on authored damage_dice_ranged (useLoggedDiceRollAttack.js:153/274); resolveAttackRange default 30, reach-first.
- **Crits**: dice double, flat modifier NEVER doubles ("1d8*2+2 (N)", "2d8*2+4 (6, 2)"; popup per-die chrome "6*2, 2*2 +4"; literal "2d8*2 + 4" with spaces exists on NEITHER surface; single-die crit keeps "1d8" token doubled-in-place). Secondary doubles silently in resolveSecondaryRoll (handlePlainDamage.js:87) while secondaryFormula text stays undoubled — judge secondaryTotal==2×die; crit proof needs sTotal > single-die max. Engine does NOT auto-miss on nat1 — "+14 vs AC12" logs hit:true under a "CRITICAL MISS!" banner; popup total==20 with nat<20 is not crit; judge crit via nat token + isNatural1/isCrit. Popup leading number = TOTAL, `d20 N` token = nat; parse nats `/d20 (\d+)/`; popup glues can fuse numbers ("8 | d20 1 +7", "20d20 13 +7") — parse nat separately. Nat1 vs low AC still logs hit:true with both banners — adjudicate hit by log hit:true/targetAc.
- **Negative-mod dice**: "1d4 - 1" parses ((?:[+-]\d+)+ fold, diceRoller.js:42) → total 0 possible, ZERO clamp-to-1 — hpΔ==fd on fd-0 legs is RAW-honest. Crit doubles dice only, flat stays negative.
- **Expiration clocks**: live under runtime key `pendingExpirations` (turnStartEffects KEY), NOT expirationQueue; addExpiration omitting rounds → `expiryRounds:null` = Infinity clock = NEVER auto-expires (CLA-033 family — features with "1 minute"/"1 hour" text and no rounds: stamp persist forever); anchored clocks (`expireOnCreatureName:caster`) drain at the ANCHOR's next TURN-START (end-of-victim-next-turn RAW = accepted MA-0542/MA-0995/MA-1147 approximation); same-round expiry NEVER fires (next-turn-start anchor is the writer-side approximation). registerTargetEffect merges extraProps verbatim, REPLACE per (target,effect,source) — refire keeps value, does not accumulate (consumers accumulate across DISTINCT sources only). `duration:"1 hour"` + zone → clock recorded appliedRound/expiryRounds with live consumers (weakenedNamesFor/excludeNames/clearExpirationEffects).

- **Cover no-stack law (shipped 2026-11-21 fix `7ff891825`, annotation folded 2026-11-21 improve, pinned `targetAcComputation.cover-no-stack.test.js`)**: cover lanes never sum — `computeTargetAc` folds the enforcement-zones `_cover_shot` stamp into the CONTEXT COVER CHANNEL (`coverAcBonus`/`coverLevel:'half'`/`coverReason`=blocker name, only when the stamp BEATS the context bonus and the line is not already AUTO-MISS) instead of bumping `targetAc` (CORRECTED IN PLACE: the original fix folded into `targetAc` and left the row bare-unannotated — the improve made the enforcement face ride the furniture lane's shape). Annotated attack rows therefore keep `targetAc` RAW and `targetAc + coverAcBonus` is by construction the AC the resolver used; both lanes print the same chip vocabulary (`vs AC 17 (1/2 Cover +2 AC)`, blocker named in the row's `coverReason`). Monster chip-lane rolls can NEVER observe an enforcement stamp: `readCoverShot` matches `attackerName` and stamps are written only by the PC-sheet/spell gate. RELATED: a sheet MISS by a Studied Attacks holder grants advantage on the next press — the forced-d20 seam pins the FIRST draw only, so a kept die of 11 under advantage is honest only when `rolls` r2 ≤ 11 (always read the row's `rolls` array).
- **Dice engine census (live 2026-11-06 DG)**: sheet attack lane rolls TWO d20s every attack even in normal mode (`log.rolls:[r1,r2]`, `total=r1`) — r2 is the spare for the popup's post-hoc Advantage/Disadvantage toggle, NOT a silent best-of (probes showed total matching r1 both ways). Suspect skew? `await import('/src/services/dice/diceRoller.js')` inside page.evaluate and loop `rollD20()` ×20k — DG measured uniform (971×nat1, 1049×nat20 / 20k). `rerollOnes` exists but ONLY the monk healing lane opts in (`hasRerollHealingOnes`, healingHandler.js) — attacks never reroll 1s. **Rest semantics**: sheet "Long Rest"/"Short Rest" buttons fire INSTANTLY, no confirm (only Admin Full Reset/Clear carry native confirm) — verify by change-data GET deltas. ShortRestModal opens a hit-dice modal needing explicit "Complete Short Rest" — rest unapplied until clicked. Short Rest WIPES `activeConditions`+meta (restRules-shortRest.js:315) — seed conditions AFTER the rest for end-condition-feature probes. Rest-latch lanes are separate: keys in LONG_REST_RESOURCES are NOT reset by Short Rest; "Short or Long Rest" specs need a SHORT_REST(_NULL_FLAG_KEYS) entry too. Pattern: latch stamp + SHORT_REST_RESOURCES null re-arm in ONE merged setRuntimeBatch per target; readers `stored ?? max` — null = re-armed (0→null is the visible proof). Do NOT add consumed keys to SHORT_REST_NULL_FLAG_KEYS when the same handleComplete restamps them (null-then-restamp re-arms instantly). Long Rest nulls `_RendMind_Used` but NOT `_SneakAttack_usedRound` — round-scoped latches re-arm only via initiative round-advance; LR re-arm proof needs a round-walk. Long Rest button grants only the ACTIVE character. LR auto-refreshes portentDice (lv>=14 → 3), sorcery points + free-cast counters (per-spell counters→null, list→[]). **Rest-modal labels are cosmetic**: NEVER trust "✓ applied / Resources restored" spans — always verify the change-data counter actually incremented (fixed lane: applyShortRest returns `fontOfInspirationRestored` true only when a numeric<max was written; labels must read the return).

rage lv20 = 6 rages, +4 rage damage; Primal Champion = abilityCalc2024.js:14-16 `min(total+4, 25)` Barbarian lv>19 STR/CON, always-on (NOT rage-gated), app cap=25 literal, 5e twin +4 uncapped; Power Word Fortify lv7 pool "120+((slot−7)*5)" no concentration; Regenerate lv7, 4d8+15 initial +1/turn, 1 hour NO concentration; See Invisibility lv2 concentration:false 1h; Sanctuary 2024 concentration:false; Phantasmal Creatures = Illusionist lv6 (lv14 is Illusory Reality); step-7 subclass survives nav; runtime spell slots stay STALE 0 after level-up — Long Rest before expecting new-tier slots; level-up does NOT bump current slots to new max (x/max + LR surfaces only); keepSpellRow silently hides lv5+ spells on sub-lv9 hosts (honest no-row). **Feats/ASI mechanics**: featAbilityChoices keyed "<Feat>-N" {assignment:Ability}; benefit-action rows carry the BENEFIT name not the feat name — handlers must thread `featName || action.name` through prefix-match, else silent fallback to max(CHA,WIS) (discriminator: set chosen ability BELOW CHA). CoP stacked + Leading Evasion + Incapacitated exemption) — "Reroll Save (1 FP)" style prompt-note reads full characters while stacked modals compute from cs stubs; note proves nothing, ledger finalDamage/hp_change decisive. - **combatSummary rows carry NEITHER `speed` NOR `automation` (live FZ 2026-11-09):** witnessed key sets — player rows `[name,type,initiative,armorClass,maxHitPoints,currentHp,targetName,concentration]`, EB monster rows `[ac,concentration,currentHp,immunities,initiative,maxHp,monsterIndex,monsterType,name,resistances,saveBonuses,size,targetName,type,vulnerabilities]`. Consequences pinned live: every per-card `TurnEconomyStrip` renders the 30 ft `DEFAULT_SPEED_FEET` denominator for ALL row types (Wolf 40 reads 0/30; a Speedy-buffed EF card reads 30/30 while the header strip beside it reads 30/40); only the Initiative HEADER strip gets real sheet speed via the `characters.computedStats` lookup — so any name-keyed lookup in the header lane must resolve through the ST holder-remap (`economyNameFor`) or a twin anchor silently falls to defaults; and any refusal/consumer gate that reads `row.automation?.passives` (e.g. `CharReactions.buildOpportunityAttackRefusal` via `hasSpeedyOpportunityDisadvantage`) is DEAD on live boards — resolve through the characters lane (Initiative.jsx:807 idiom `character?.computedStats || character`) instead. The map drag budget reads `player.speedFeet` on map tokens with ZERO app-wide producers (tokens are `{id,name,gridX,gridY}`) → movementBudget caps every mover at 30 ft (`usePlayerDragging.js:52/:134`, `useSelectMove.js:57`) — filed `bug-movement-budget-caps-all-movers-at-30`.

- **Race trait-level `proficiency_choices` shape canon (fixed 2026-11-19 `fix(racebuff-single-object-proficiency-choices)`):** at RACE-TRAIT level the shape is ONE object `{choose, from:[…]}` in BOTH `public/data/races.json` (Dwarf Tool Proficiency, Half-Elf Skill Versatility) and `public/data/2024/races.json` (Elf Keen Senses, Human Skillful/Versatile) — five consumer lanes read the object (`addChoiceOptions2024`, `proficiencyUtils` skill-cap, `skillValidation/parse-skills`, `race-sources`, `rules-proficiencies`); class/feature-level `proficiency_choices` (classes.json majors, e.g. Student of War) is a separate ARRAY source with its own lanes. NEVER assume the trait lane takes an array — the lone array-`.forEach` outlier in `add5eTraitProficiencies` threw unhandled TypeErrors on every Dwarf/Half-Elf `computeRaceBuffs` (via `validateAbilityTotals`) until this fix. Disk-row pins live in `raceBuffService.test.js` (JSON-import of the real rows).

## 14. Victims & rigs (addenda + current roster)
- **Delegation multi-press ledger law (live 2026-11-08 EQ, PT4):** N card-presses spread across M delegated-visitor contexts = exactly N `roll` rows (distinct `rolls` arrays — identical display TOTALS can be real duplicate dice, diff the arrays before crying cached-replay) + one `hp_change` per applied HIT; each tab drains its OWN popup (cross-context walk `page.context().browser().contexts()` shows zero lingering overlays elsewhere after drains); visitor tab shows NO popup for another visitor's roll. Force an active-onto-away state by POSTing top-level `activeCreatureName {value}`; a subsequent EB join re-anchors it to board head.
- **Bandit (AC12 HP11 unsuffixed) = canonical victim**; exact anchor `startsWith('Bandit0.125')` (name+CR concat defeats ^Bandit\b). Unclamped HP999 rig gives clean unclamped hpΔ ledgers. Zero-cond/rider adjudication keys on per-HIT audits, never miss legs.
- **AC-rig**: full-store cs POST victim `ac:N` applies BETWEEN rolls (fresh press re-reads AC; mid-popup doesn't) — manufactures misses far cheaper than RNG hunting; judge targetAc per-press from lastAttack. Only an AC-stamped press is a boundary proof (rig prints the rigged AC). SHARPENED (live ZZ 2026-11-20): the stamp works on MONSTER victims; a PLAYER victim's chip-attack AC is still read from its character data — stamping `ac:1` on the ElderPaladin cs row left the Goblin Scimitar popup resolving `vs AC 19` (row had no `ac` key pre-stamp; the stamp neither appeared nor influenced the roll), so manufacture PC-victim misses by roll repetition, not ac stamps. ≥+10 bonus rows: miss windows run blank (~15%/press at nat≤3) — escalate rig AC to bonus+15 for an honest miss in ~1 press; AC-flip tie-geometry: a tie face (nat+bonus==AC) is fixed 5%/press, no rig manufactures it — straddle honestly. +3-vs-AC12 and +4 rows self-serve real misses; +13/+14-vs-AC12 structurally unmissable (miss ONLY via AC-rig). Miss popups self-validate the rig (print the rigged AC). **HP rig**: stage currentHp AND maxHp (cs POST maxHp does not lift currentHp; swarms some builds have only 2 HP keys — don't fabricate *HitPoints); stamp ALL FOUR HP keys where present; cross-session rigs persist invisibly — re-read live AC/HP before math; fresh sessions inherit prior rigs. EB-rejoin cs-HP full-store POST can be clobbered by the tab's stale snapshot — settle ~10s, re-stamp, GET-confirm. Ororo/client desync: cs 999 may not reach client combat state — re-read client before trusting |hpΔ|==fd on previously-fought victims. Downed victims clamp hp_change to 0 — judge ledger by roll entries. Initiative-card target-select resets to No-Target after each attack on some hosts — re-arm before EVERY roll.
- **Deterministic save faces**: force-fail = full-word saveBonuses.<word>:-15/-19 + nested saving_throws (both, or lane-specific per §13) in one cs POST; force-success = +19 same shape (popup "+0" cosmetic, total truth); -5@DC10 only ~70% control — judge legs by saveResult not plan order. Recipe to force a MONSTER spell-save fail: cs POST victim saveBonuses.dex:-15 (+nested) → PC caster casts cone/save spell → picker → Results modal stamps saveResult:"failure". NPC AoE ignores rigs entirely (see §13). **EB-join side effects**: Join of ANY monster lands the whole saved PC party as 1/1 AC-less placeholder entries — filter `type!=='player'` when picking victims; App.jsx:256 re-seeds cs PCs `maxHp:1` (heal-zero family); "+NPC" joins ship entries with NO monsterIndex (qty1 unsuffixed) — identity proof = disk-exact AC/HP + card content, clobber-check by monsterIndex not suffix; joined NPC combatants SNAPSHOT master stat blocks at join — monsters.json edits need remove+re-join to take effect live; EB join mid-flow auto-navigates to Initiative (stale EB evaluate joins duplicates); loading a saved encounter ≠ join — explicit "Join Encounter" click required; Join with empty selection spawns blank NPC 1; cs names sometimes suffixed ("Mage Apprentice 1"), sometimes bare — dump cs names AND avatar alts before anchoring; cs join order ≠ join-click order, ≠ init order — poll names, never carry idx.
- **Zero-grant proof ladder (strongest→weakest)**: victim change-data STORE KEY ABSENT > zero log lines > condition log lag (lag hides lines, never a key once applyHitClauseConditions ran). Success-leg zero-effect proof: pre-clear victim cd `{activeConditions:[]}` POST then key-stays-empty after sr:success fire; fire the +rig leg FIRST before any fail contaminates victims. Whole-store zero-grant scans must exclude `combat-ui-viewingMonster.actions[i].description` echoes and change-data push greps likewise. **Caster pre-flight**: post-admin-clear FIRST sheet cast by any caster THROWS "activeConditions must be an array for caster" AFTER slot burn — seed caster `activeConditions:[]` via full-store POST or EffectAdder add→remove round-trip BEFORE first cast; always pre-flight a free cast before relying on a spell roll for a verdict. Casting at a combatant lacking activeConditions:[] throws — seed [] first.
- **Control hosts (registry-permanent)**: EvasiveFighter = AC10/HP94 no-resists damage control (but wizard ASI-hydration drift STR>cap hard-disables Save on that feat host — use AasimarTest); HexWarlock lv14 reusable DC16 CHA fear caster (no Fear on disk for cone needs roster edits; no weapon chip); LightfootHalfling maxHP12 death-clamp trap, permanent Brave host; Disciplined_Monk lv18 reroll host; HeroesFeastBard Valor lv20; Yuan-ti Poison Ray / Cambion Fire Ray / Abominable Yeti Claw / Behir Bite = energy carriers for differential rigs; Fire Elemental (CR5, unconditional B/P/S resist, AC13 HP93) = clean resisting target; NEVER Unarmed Strike vs monk hosts (Empowered Strikes Force-converts on resisted Bludgeoning, masks the test) — use Quarterstaff; grid cells=5ft reach-differential: 3 cells (15ft) vs 5 cells (25ft) with `lastAttack.rangeReason` stamp. **ROSTER SUPERSEDES the retired durable-extraction doc's host list — test-campaign deduped to ONE PC per class**: AasimarTest (Rogue), AberrantSorcerer (Sorcerer), Disciplined_Monk (Monk), DivinationWizard (Wizard), DraconicDragon (Barbarian), ElderPaladin (Paladin), EvasiveFighter (Fighter), FeyRanger (Ranger), HeroesFeastBard (Bard), HexWarlock (Warlock), War_Cleric (Cleric), Wild_Sage_Druid (Druid). DELETED: DragonbornTest, DwarfTest, ElfTest, FeyNewTest, LightfootHalfling, Divine_Cleric, TieflingTest — older entries naming them remain valid as evidence; re-host on survivors. Initiative-normal control → EvasiveFighter; ElfTest fear-immunity and min-HP warnings now N/A. **Host quirks (survivors)**: DivinationWizard = Evoker currently (round-trips Evoker↔Abjurer via step-7; sole wizard lane for subclass-gated rows); War_Cleric = sole Cleric/Druid cantrip lane, sanctuary KEPT, Warding Bond learned; HexWarlock = Potent Poison ignore_resistance holder — NEVER use for poison-RESIST differential (fakes resisted:false; use DivinationWizard Ray of Sickness); EvasiveFighter = Skill Expert feat → ALL saves, invalid save-proficiency control (use AberrantSorcerer, saveProficiencies:[]), also holds Mage Slayer + Relentless free-d8 + Superior Technique, Psi-Warrior swap md5-clean; DraconicDragon STR=21 (+5) Berserker lv20 host; ElderPaladin is ALREADY Goliath (Storm Giant) — reuse for size/build lanes, but race.subrace DRIFTS across sessions (was left "Hill Giant") — verify disk before giant-ancestry legs, restore via PUT + reload; FeyRanger longbow +8 (not +9), Sharpshooter host, Swift Quiver left prepared; HeroesFeastBard lv20 College of Dance CHA20 DC19, native Skulker, Otto's permanently learned; Disciplined_Monk AC22 = high-AC miss-forcing rig (raw≤8 miss vs +11) and reroll host; AasimarTest lv20 class-independent host for racial Revelation options, can hold Command/Color Spray/Phantasmal Killer prepared.

Verify writes by GET: in a scoped-PATCH response `applied` counts cs `creatures` row merges only — a char-bucket field write rides `appliedFields` (2026-11-21 counter law).

- **Cover rig**: needs ACTIVE map (`__map__.activeMapName` — cover checks fail silently otherwise); Barrel=half, altar=three-quarters, Paint wall=total; token drag = synthetic DragEvent+DataTransfer text/plain on `.grid-svg`; grid→screen ONLY via live `getScreenCTM` + `DOMPoint(gx*40+20, gy*40+20)` (CELL_SIZE 40, rendered ~43px/cell, viewBox 800×800; rects go stale when side panels open); Paint = mouse.down → per-cell mouse.move (80ms) → up; walls persist `public/campaigns/<c>/maps/<slug>.json` + SSE `map-data-<c>-<slug>`; char→token drop: Items panel chips carry `character:<Name>` payload, browser_drag chip→svg lands `{id,name,gridX,gridY}` in players[]. **Misc rig facts**: EB-joined Bandit lands `saveBonuses.con:+1` (not raw 0) and exact instance names may carry TWO trailing spaces ("Bandit 1  ") — match names byte-exact incl. spaces; EB re-join re-rolls initiative; te survives multi-round Next-walks; dead monsters stay in the Target dropdown — GET hp before targeting; "+NPC" defaults name "NPC N", ac:10, hp 10, empty saveBonuses (initiativeService.addNpc) and have NO attacks and NO stat block — EB Join is the only attack-capable lane; "+NPC" row rename via native value setter+input event + Enter is a cheap instant target; `+ NPC` join lost on server self-restart leaving placeholder cards — verify img alt + aria HP inputs; :80 API server can self-restart mid-run wiping runtime change-data — GET change-data BEFORE every attack sequence. **Cover-stacking rig (live 2026-11-21 fix cover-sources-stack)**: doc-PUT staging (barrel + FeyRanger swap on the shot line's interior cell) needs NO reload between faces — the sheet press refetches the map every click, and the zones gate re-adjudicates per press, so a clear-line press null-casts the stale `_cover_shot` stamp before AC math. PITFALL (same run): a stale fetched map-doc snapshot used as the edit base silently reverts concurrent field changes — always GET the doc fresh before mutating for the next face. **ATTACKER-choice law (live 2026-11-21 improve furniture-cover-reason):** FeyRanger carries `ignore_cover_ranged` — its furniture shots land `coverReason:'Sharpshooter'` with cover zeroed; use **EvasiveFighter** (Shortbow `+7`) for furniture-cover faces. Furniture half/¾ rows now carry a source-named `coverReason` (`coverSourceLabel` in `coverService.js`: GM rename wins, else capitalized type `'barrel'`→`'Barrel'`; grants/full-cover reasons never clobbered), rendered as the Log view's `.log-range-reason` badge. **FULL-tier prose (shipped 2026-11-21 improve automiss-full-cover-prose):** a full-cover row prints `vs AC <raw> (Full Cover) — AUTO-MISS` (`isFullCoverChannel` in `logService.js` keys off `coverLevel:'full'` OR the verbatim reason, never off `isAutoMiss` — an out-of-range AUTO-MISS keeps bare bytes); log-search `(Full Cover)` is the one-call discriminator, and the live negative out-of-range face on the SHEET lane is unriggable (320-ft bands > any sane gridSize — use the monster-chip lane's CCC recipe if a live negative is ever wanted).

RR-bearing Barbarian lane still needs the sanctioned EF Berserker sed-swap (restore byte-exact, md5-check).

An attack-less NPC NO LONGER fires a native `confirm` — the service returns a zero-write `'needs-weapon'` and the host arms `NpcUnarmedConfirmModal` (shared `DeleteConfirmModal` role=dialog tier, honest `Add anyway`/`Cancel`, hosted by NPCs.jsx row + save-and-add lanes and the Sessions.jsx quick action); `Add anyway` re-invokes `addNPCToInitiative(…, {addUnarmed:true})` with join bytes + log row identical to the old proceed path. UNARMED-RIG RECIPE: `PUT /api/campaigns/:c/npcs/<name>` with `{"armorClass":12,"hitPoints":7,"actions":[]}` — `npcHasAttacks` needs an action with name + attack_bonus/damage dice, so an empty actions array arms the refusal; the init-btn stays disabled only while AC/HP are null. Throwaway NPC via full-list `POST /api/campaigns/:c/npcs {npcs:[…]}` (`GET /npcs` returns the same `{npcs:[…]}` envelope), clean up with `DELETE /npcs/<urlencoded name>`; CAUTION — the full-list POST re-stamps `updatedAt` on EVERY row including owner fixtures.

- **Deterministic d20 faces via one-shot `Math.random` wrap (live 2026-11-19 fix death-save-terminal-roll-chip):** `rollD20` is `Math.floor(Math.random()*20)+1` and the sheet death-save roll is SYNCHRONOUS inside the click handler — so wrap ONE roll click in a `Math.random` override (`()=>0.999` → NAT 20, `()=>0.3` → roll 7, `()=>0.6` → roll 13) and restore it immediately after `.click()`; no rules-module surgery, no ~30-roll organic farming for terminal faces (NAT 20 revive / fatal 3rd failure reachable on demand after a `deathFailures:[true,true,false]` scoped-PATCH rig). Generalizes to any synchronous sheet roll lane.
- **Admin-clear native `confirm()` pairing (live 2026-11-19 fix run):** a late `page.once('dialog')` registered in the SAME evaluate as the clear click does NOT catch the Admin change-data/log clears' native confirm — pair the click with `browser_handle_dialog` accept in a FOLLOW-UP call, then prove the clear by disk/GET (`change-data` `{}`, log absent).

QMS TOKEN-PLACE PENDING FACE (shipped 2026-11-21 improve qms-type-pending-placeholder): `useActiveMapType` returns `{mapType, pending}`; a 300 ms-DELAYED spinner note `Checking the active map for token placement...` (`role=status`, `data-testid=qms-token-pending`, `.qms-note` idiom) paints ONLY while the list GET is in flight past the window — warm/fast resolves never flicker it; indoor swap-in/outdoor-absent/abort-fail-closed faces unchanged. Rig: `page.route('**/campaigns/test-campaign/maps')` + in-process `setTimeout` then `route.continue()` freezes exactly the QMS list lane (no query string on that GET); sample the slot ms-precision right after `.qms-overlay` mounts to prove the no-flicker bound (t≈5 ms inside the window). PITFALL: a mid-session curl activate/deactivate leaves a mounted tab's `mapName` stale — reload + re-select before judging resolved faces. (b) WIZARD quick-build: step-1 mode+ruleset pair cards are `.rules-option` (selected = class, no aria); sidebar `.wizard-sidebar button` step-jump is SEQUENTIALLY-GATED in a fresh Custom build (Feats renders `disabled` until Basic Info/Class are satisfied) — the "jumps freely, state survives" law only holds once steps are validly filled / in review mode; don't use sidebar jumps to reach an early step's select locators; footer `Cancel` exists ONLY on step 1 (elsewhere abandon = `.wizard-header ×` → native `Discard changes to "<name>"?`); the draft lands ON STEP-7 ARRIVAL (subclass defaults to first option; BACKGROUND PARITY LAW — live 2026-11-19 improve quickbuild-background-single-derivation: the old silent Guard→Criminal REPLACE is DEAD — both the step-5 prefill and the draft resolve through ONE `deriveQuickBackground({ruleset, className})` in `quickBuildService.js` and the draft fills only a STILL-EMPTY background, so a reviewed prefill stands verbatim; a skipped/empty Background step gets the class default e.g. Rogue→Criminal); quick-build with a Rogue leaves the 2024 spell step 0/0 HONESTLY (`classes.json` Arcane Trickster lv1 `cantrips_known:0`).
