(d) XP lane: sheet subtitle `.clickable` `(milestone)` opens `.xp-modal-overlay`; unticking Milestone closes the modal + writes the runtime bucket EXACTLY ONCE — one whole-bucket POST from `setRuntimeValue ← handleXpModeToggle` (`CharSummary.jsx:607`, no char-file write; the RF-era "writes TWICE" claim is DEAD — correct in place by the xp-milestone-toggle-single-write BLOCKED re-verify 2026-11-19: a census counting 2 is spy LAYERING double-counting, cross-check with `browser_network_requests` — and when display-mode and hydrated bucket already agree the toggle correctly writes NOTHING, which also means clicking the checkbox to "restore milestone" on a fresh client can be a zero-POST no-op, verify by bucket GET not the checkbox face; sheet-open bakes the viewed char's full bucket — evict with wildcard `DELETE /api/campaigns/:c/<CharName>` to restore a bucket-free baseline); reopen → XP number input + `Apply` = runtime-only award; `.level-up-banner` mounts app-wide on awarding + echoing tabs (per-client ephemeral; cold re-select never replays); `Open wizard` seeds the edit wizard on the Level field, Cancel clean when nothing edited. DISPLAY-TRUTH LAW (live 2026-11-19 xp-celebration fix run): the subtitle suffix and the modal's `Current: N XP` preview read the DISK-derived `playerStats.xpMode`/`xp` (in-session toggles mutate local `playerStats` only, `CharSummary.jsx:609`), so after reload+re-select the sheet shows `(milestone)` even while the runtime bucket carries `{xp:26500, xpMode:'experience'}` — the very state `LevelUpCelebration`'s watcher consumes; judge XP-lane state by the change-data BUCKET, never the subtitle; modal Apply deltas compute against the disk `displayXp` baseline after a reload (an `Apply -26500` lands `xp:0` via `Math.max(0,…)`). MILESTONE RE-TICK INVERSION TRAP: the checkbox mirrors the DISPLAY mode, so clicking it to "restore milestone" post-reload can silently re-write `experience` — restore reliably via admin runtime-bucket clear (`xp`/`xpMode` evicted, closed-tab two-pass), md5-pin the char file to prove byte-untouched. CELEBRATION FLOOR LAW (fixed 2026-11-19 fix(xp-lane-celebration-ignores-actual-level-fake-award)): the XP lane floors at `max(impliedLevelFromXp(previous.xp), roster level)` — celebrate only when implied EXCEEDS the floor, banner reports the honest roster `from`; parity/catch-up adopts silently. ECHO POSITIVE-CONTROL LAW: when judging "no banner on the echo tab", always follow with a genuine above-level award whose banner MUST appear on BOTH tabs — it proves the `change-<c>-<Char>` echo lane was live during the silent legs. (e) PLAYER LANES: quest board rides ONLY `GET /api/campaigns/:c/quests/board` + `/factions/standings` (field whitelists, visitor-open; `POST /quests/board` 404s — no write door; visitor sidebar KEEPS `Quest Board` + drops the ten GM-only views; GM planner status flip re-groups visitor boards live via `quests-list-*`, zero visitor writes); GM Screen pins exactly 2 GETs (`/data/conditions.json` + `/data/actions.json`) and paints `activeConditions:["blinded"]` (string-array — objects 400 `write_rejected` honestly) into its In-Play column live.

## 15. Test/registry conventions & JSON safety
- **jsdom optgroup census law (live 2026-11-21 improve author-select-optgroups):** in this Vitest/jsdom env `querySelectorAll('optgroup')` yields a NodeList WITHOUT `.map`, and `HTMLOptGroupElement.options` is `undefined` — census helpers must use `[...node.querySelectorAll('optgroup')]` + per-group `querySelectorAll('option')`.
- **Strict-mock export addition law (live 2026-11-21 improve write-recap-disabled-when-ai-off):** when a component gains an import from a module some sibling tests already `vi.mock` with a strict factory, EVERY such factory must gain that export or Vitest throws `No X export is defined on the mock` at import time (adding to one test file is not enough — grep all tests co-located with the component for mock factories of the newly imported module).
- **`it.each` negative-face spread trap (live 2026-11-21 improve automiss-full-cover-prose):** spreading a COVERED base-row fixture over a `{}` negative case silently inherits the cover fields — negative faces must null the channel explicitly (`coverLevel:null, coverReason:null, coverAcBonus:0`).
- **Log allowlist drift pins (shipped 2026-11-20 improve ZZ):** `ALLOWED_LOG_TYPES` (server/routes/log.js) is PINNED by derivation in `log.schema-gate.test.js` against the REAL client registries — `src/components/log/entryComponents.js` `ENTRY_COMPONENTS` (extracted from Log.jsx into a plain .js module for exactly this) and `src/components/campaign-admin/auditTypes.js` `AUDIT_TYPES` (extracted from SessionSection.jsx); a new render/audit type without a Set member fails the pin red (negative-proved live: fake keys named in the `missing` array). Adding a log type = edit the registry AND the Set in one change. First drift the pin surfaced: `enforcement_clock_expired` was listed in AUDIT_TYPES but missing from the Set (added) — note its only real producers write it as `automationType` UNDER `type:'automation'` (zoneDurationClock.js), so `entryMatchesAudit`'s `type`-membership leg never matches those rows; only the `automationType.startsWith('ai_')` leg catches AI rows (audit-visibility gap, not fixed here).
- **Admin rendering-idle contract (live 2026-11-17 improve admin-change-data-key-census):** `CampaignAdmin.rendering.test.jsx` pins ZERO `role="status"` elements on a fresh Admin mount — any new fetched Admin section must render its transient loading note WITHOUT `role=status` (only the settled none/error faces are live regions), or the rendering test goes red.
- **Never overlap a full-suite run with live-fixture sed surgery (live 2026-11-16 improve save-result-unknown-attribution):** `trackedResourcesOwnership.test.js` imports `public/campaigns/test-campaign/*.json` directly — running the suite while an RR Barbarian/Berserker swap is in flight produces two FALSE failures; finish the surgery + cp-restore first, or re-run the file alone to confirm (clean run: 9/9).
- **Partial `vi.mock` of `useRuntimeState.js` on a new-export seam (live 2026-11-18 fix round-reuse-after-clear):** a component seam that newly calls an added `useRuntimeState` export through the async React-dispatch lane surfaces the missing mock export ONLY as suite-level unhandled `Errors` (N errors, EXIT 1) while EVERY test file still reads "passed" — judge suites by `Errors`+`EXIT`, not per-file pass lines, and update every partial mock factory in the graph (the fix run touched three `createCreatureHandlers`/initiative test mocks) when a seam gains a store call.
- **Monster-card modal rigs must ARM the attacker row (live 2026-11-16 improve unarmed-chip-loud-refusal):** any test whose attacker row (or `findCreatureByName` mock return) lacks `targetName` now reads a `buildNoTargetRefusalPopup` refusal instead of a `rollAttack` dispatch — five existing rig files needed arming; new modal tests should carry `targetName` on the attacker row from day one. Also: a popup-stub reset (`_setPopupHtml(null)`-style) must be followed by `mockClear()` or call-count pins read the reset as a dispatch. **CharBonusActions unit-rig mount requirement (live 2026-11-15 improve canact-silent-gate-feedback):** conditional grant rows (Fleet Step, Expeditious Retreat dash, spiritual-weapon move…) do NOT count toward `hasBonusContent` — a section-less jsdom render silently mounts nothing; stub a non-empty `bonusActions` array (or render a real row source) so the section mounts before asserting row-level gates.
- **Display-layer test seam over the tracked-resource producer path (live 2026-11-14 improve tracked-resource-display-clamp):** mock ONLY `useRuntimeState.js` and let the real `useTrackedResource` run — `hasRuntimeValue→false` forces the unclamped `_trackedResources` branch (the exact hole the root fix leaves open), and a `setRuntimeValue` spy renders-through the component to prove zero write-back on the clamp face.
- **Mutable-capture + functional setState updaters is scheduler-dependent (live 2026-11-13 improve batch-cure-pool-running-total):** a batch loop that decrements a mutable `let` counter and pushes via `setLog(prev => …)` PASSES unit tests but shows the counter's FINAL value in every row live — `setPoolRemaining` before the pushes defers ALL functional updaters to the re-render where they close over the shared binding; jsdom's eager-state path (empty queue) masks it for the FIRST updater only. Capture an iteration-local `const` (compute per-index) inside the loop; to mirror live React in a unit rig, make the mocked resource `update()` dispatch a real re-render (useReducer forceRender inside a `useTrackedResource` mock override) — that deferred-updaters test is the pin that catches the mutable-capture variant (negative-proved: fails ONLY that test, 19/20 pass). **jsdom stale-closure test idiom (live 2026-11-13 healing-pool stale-closure fix):** to pin a commit-time re-read against a render-closure snapshot, mutate the mutable mock runtime store *between the last state-triggered re-render and the commit click* — any intermediate re-render (pool update, roll) re-bakes the closure and makes the test vacuously pass. Pin a refund lane by asserting the exact `poolUpdate` call sequence (e.g. `[[20],[19],[21]]`), not just the final pool value.
- **Never run `npm run build` concurrently with the vitest suite (live 2026-11-12 improve hex-board-open-put-coalesce):** `sse.test.js` serves `dist/index.html` — the mid-run rewrite flakes ~5 tests spuriously; sequence them.
- Render-based data-lock tests MUST be `.test.jsx` (vitest oxc: "JSX syntax is disabled" in .test.js); .js twins only if they never render. `npx vitest run $files` from rg -l output in zsh passes ONE giant filter → false "No test files found" — pipe through xargs. `rg -r` replace-flag garbles dumps — plain grep. Tests co-located, matching source file casing. **CSS-contract harness (live 2026-11-12 improve loading-overlay-blocking-css):** `src/App.css.test.jsx`'s `styleFor`/`parseStylesheet` pattern asserts real stylesheet rules (incl. `::before` selectors and `@keyframes` blocks) — the reusable way to pin visual contracts (position/z-index/backdrop) that jsdom rendering cannot; new co-located `<File>.<aspect>.test.jsx` naming fits it.
- **NEVER json.dump whole monsters.json** (608 monsters: reformats 250+ lines, breaks U+00AD soft-hyphen escapes) — restore via `git show HEAD:path > path` then surgical `str.replace` anchored on unique neighbor bytes with assert count==1; unique app-wide anchors like `"damage_dice_primary": "3d6",\n "damage_type_primary": "Lightning"\n }`; repeated action names ("Rend" ×68, "Vile Slime", "Claw") need FULL description+digits anchors; "from one of two claws" ×3 NOT unique; anchor inside the unique monster object block. Byte-check proposed multi-dice bytes with canRollExpression in step 1. Inserting automation after a last-position description needs the comma appended (anchor strip+`,\n`). Manifest: surgical line-number edit anchored on unique "id" line, never full re-dump; manifest rows VERBATIM from row-detail.mjs (paraphrase = fabricated-text flag); manifest single-line JSON — parse records, windowed grep stitches false; manifest claims are NOT disk-true (attackBonus label ≠ disk; conditions[] extracted ≠ authored) — disk-check every claim before adjudicating.
- **Stale-pin inversion**: fixing a row MUST invert/narrow same-pass every pin hard-locked to its broken state — whole-DB armed-census pins (monsterShapeShift.ma1020-imp.test.js toEqual(['imp/Shape-Shift']), multiattack/spellcast chip censuses), sibling byte-pins naming the pending row (ma1595 tiger test pinned winter-bite hit_conditions undefined), legendary-uses-{androsphinx,animal-lord}.test.jsx header pins, monsterLairActions young-inertness FALSE pins (grep young-<color> + invert ALL same pass; unfixed siblings stay FALSE-locked; lair trio edits index-scoped, never reorder/delete — keys index-bound); data-lock twin tests parametrize+reuse (kissContext pattern). Correction pattern (legacy ref): when a fix lands before browser verify boots, pre-fix repro is impossible (server serves fixed bytes) — adjudicate broken-state axes against ledger, verify fixed behavior live.
- **Registry discipline**: subagents write docs/test-monster-registry.json DIRECTLY (never .tmp); orchestrator ALWAYS disk-verifies claimed writes and merges only new keys — never wholesale overwrite (stale snapshots); subagents NEVER write the manifest (forbidden; orchestrator diffs post-session); multi-row monsters use `verifiedRow2..N` keys, not ticket-suffixed; json registry append: parse-check BEFORE dump (retry duplicates rows otherwise); quoted-key json.dumps embeds literal quotes — anchor key text raw + git-diff hunk head after append.
- **Git safety**: NEVER git checkout/restore/stash/clean any doc (a subagent's `git checkout -- registry` wiped uncommitted host notes once; reconstruction with markers); never chain `git checkout -- file` after edits; a running fixer may refuse cleanup when injection-poisoned — orchestrator completes bug-file deletion + manifest edits after its own test/lint verification; runtime campaigns dir git-untracked — byte-clean PC restore = re-PUT parsed JSON, shasum before/after; `git diff` noisy on dirty trees — registry delta = dict-diff; manifest innocence via mtime-window + per-row re-read, not diff hunks.
- **jsdom focus-tier unit rigs (live 2026-11-16 improve avatar-modal-focus-pull; SHARPENED 2026-11-17 improve chooser-focus-grab):** `fireEvent.click` never MOVES focus in jsdom, so a keyboard-open face (asserting an on-open focus pull) needs `el.focus()` on the trigger BEFORE the activating click/keydown — otherwise the opener-capture and pull assertions read vacuously. Never manually `.remove()` a React-managed node to model a vanished anchor (unmount throws `NotFoundError`) — append a STANDALONE opener element and remove that instead. REACT-19 DEFERRED-DETACH FACE: `root.unmount()` detaches the DOM one task AFTER the passive cleanup + microtask, so close-restore unit tests must close via a STATE-FLIP host (rerender), and any restore hook needs an idempotent 0-tick `setTimeout` re-check after its microtask pass to cover view-swap unmounts (benign focus/blur UI category).
- **Overlay entrance-focus tier (shipped 2026-11-17 improve chooser-focus-grab):** `src/hooks/ui/useOverlayEntranceFocus.js` is THE drop-in for sheet `.sp-overlay` choosers: `useOverlayEntranceFocus(overlayRef, ready)` composes the DeleteConfirmModal first-focusable entrance pull + popup.jsx never-fight-inside rule + useMenuRoving anchor-capture/tabindex-stamp/microtask-restore. Census: ~85 `.sp-overlay` files still have NO focus mechanism (MassHeal/Prayer→CreatureSelectionModal/SavePrompt/PartyPresence/choice-modal families → Backlog); the `.popup-modal` family (SpellDetailPopup, OFF-leg popups, mass-heal results) self-pulls already, but its CLOSE path can orphan focus on BODY (open-pull fine, no restore).
- **Generation-sweep tests need per-case jsdom timeouts (live 2026-11-12 fix adjacent-dungeon-small-grid-crash):** vitest cases that run thousands of procedural generations (grid×seed sweeps, e.g. `it.each` over grids 7..100 × 60 seeds) are ~5× slower under jsdom than bare node (same sweep: 2.8 s node vs >5 s/case default) — set an explicit per-test `{ timeout: ~20000 }` on the heavy sweep cases or they flake-red on time, not on behavior. Keep the sweep bounded (fixed grid spot-list, fixed seed range) so it stays a regression pin, not a fuzz run.

- **Shared-module `vi.fn()` mocks need per-test clearing (live 2026-11-17 fix opened-map-delete-leaves-ghost-active-map):** sibling server tests mocking `changeData` with a MODULE-LEVEL `vi.fn()` `publish` accumulate calls across tests — adding a `not.toHaveBeenCalledWith` negative pin requires `publish.mockClear()` in setup, else an earlier test's honest publish false-fails the negative.
- **Retired-lane shadow tests must mount the COMPOSED route stack (live 2026-11-17 fix retired-inventory-wildcard-shadow):** a router-unit test that mounts the router in isolation (the inventory.test.js 404-pin pattern) never sees Express fall-through into a later catch-all wildcard — retired `POST /inventory` answered 200 + wrote a phantom change-data `inventory` key via `campaigns-changedata.js` `POST /:campaign/:key` while the unit pin stayed green. Any path-level contract about WHICH handler owns a route needs the full mount-order stack (see `inventory.retired-snapshot-wildcard-shadow.test.js`).
- **Dormant `vi.mock` relative paths silently run the REAL collaborator (pitfall, 2026-11-18 fix bi-offense-modal-rider-gate-arg-shape):** `handlePlainDamage.popupData.test.js` mocked the BI gate via `vi.mock('../../combat/auras/bardicInspirationState.js')` from `src/hooks/combat/handlers/` — that path resolves to the non-existent `src/hooks/combat/auras/`, so vitest never errored, the mock was DORMANT, and the lane silently ran the REAL gate over the mocked store; a `toHaveBeenCalledWith` pin only fired after correcting to `../../../services/combat/auras/…`. Prove a mock is live before trusting call-shape assertions: give it a distinguishing `mockImplementation` and watch the pin move (or count the `../` hops against the real tree). Same-pass defect family: a tier that MOCKS a gate AND pins the call shape against the mock actively PROTECTS arg-shape mismatches (`attackRollRiders.test.js` pinned `toHaveBeenCalledWith(HOLDER_STRING, …)` for a gate with a `(playerStats, campaignName)` signature) — every arg-shape contract needs a real-module falsifier tier (`bardicInspirationState.test.js`: arming trio + full object → TRUE, bare name string → FALSE).
- **Attack-chain step-index pins live in TWO forms (pitfall, 2026-11-18 fix bi-offense-modal-rider-gate-arg-shape):** removing/inserting a step in `buildAttackRollDamageSteps()` shifts every downstream index pin — a `steps\[N\]` sweep MISSES bare `buildAttackRollDamageSteps()[N]` expressions (found in `attackRollDamageSteps-featureDamage.test.js`); grep BOTH forms on any chain change, then re-derive the golden census in `attackRollDamageSteps.test.js`.

- **Cross-realm `instanceof Map` false-negatives in jsdom tests (pitfall, live 2026-11-19 improve 3d-ghost-parity-ruling):** a `Map` built by the component module and asserted in the test file may fail `instanceof Map` (realm skew between Vite-transformed module globals and the jsdom/test global) — assert STRUCTURALLY (`.has(k)`/`.get(k)` values), never by `instanceof`.

## 16. SSE/change-apply lane (fixed 2026-10-27, 947b9702c)
- **Prove the tab's campaign SSE is UP before judging any echo-driven consumer (live NP 2026-11-14):** a re-selected tab can settle FULLY HYDRATED (change-data GETs, fan-out POST, buckets, picker values all correct from select-rehydration) yet have NO live `GET http://localhost/subscribe?campaign=<c>` connection for the CURRENT page — every PATCH/POST echo then lands server-side (curl `--max-time` frame probe proves the broadcast) while the tab silently never adopts, and every mid-dwell-echo rider reads INERT. Recipe: after `goto`+select, list `browser_network_requests({static:false, filter:'subscribe'})` and require a campaign subscribe 200 newer than the page's teardown `subscribe?` ERR_ABORTED before riding any echo law; a real-Playwright `.click()` on `.campaign-button` re-established it when the evaluate-dispatched select had not (NP: two consecutive re-selects one page apart — evaluate-click page had no subscribe entry, locator-click page did; the false negative also collided with the bucket-first mirror law in §2 — check BOTH before declaring an echo dead).
- **sseClient console severity policy (shipped 2026-11-11 improve sse-benign-error-downgrade):** the shared pool's `onerror` logs `console.debug` while `readyState===CONNECTING` and the streak of consecutive reconnecting errors is <3 (benign reload/tab-close/dev restart — a `page.goto`/close now leaves ZERO `[SSE]` console.error rows); it escalates to `console.error` from the 3rd consecutive reconnecting error (API persistently unreachable under a live tab — loud by ~6s) and immediately on CLOSED (native retry over). `pagehide` pins the face to debug; any open/message resets the streak; no timers. LAW: the SSE URL is `http://<hostname>/subscribe` — it BYPASSES the Vite proxy and hits :80 directly, so an API kill produces CONNECTING network-error retries, never a proxy-500 CLOSED face. Shared-pool probe idiom: in-page `await import("/src/services/ui/sseClient.js")` + `subscribeToSSE(campaign, h)` joins the app's live fan-out (same Vite module URL) — clean POST to SSE round-trip proof without wrapping EventSource. Console collectors miss teardown logs entirely (Chromium drops handler events mid-unload) — clean-buffer-after-reload is the harness truth; prove the mechanism via the kill face. `browser_console_messages` buffer is per CURRENT page (`all:true` = page session, resets on tab switch/navigate). SHARPENED (2026-11-12 improve sse-close-error-tier, premise BLOCKED): the EventSource `error` handler NEVER dispatches during reload/close on this Chromium — a localStorage-surviving teardown probe (listeners write before document destruction, read back from a second same-origin tab) shows pagehide runs while ES error never lands — so a CLOSED-at-unload console.error face is live-UNVERIFIABLE, and any observed error-tier `[SSE] connection error` rows should be attributed to the kill-escalation face instead (once the 3-streak trips under a live tab it console.errors on EVERY ~3s native retry — e.g. inside the supervisor's ~60s dev-restart window, easily mis-timed as caused by adjacent tab closes). Multi-tab instrumentation scope law: `Page.addInitScript` is PAGE-scoped (late `context().newPage()` tabs don't get it) — use `context.addInitScript`; pending `text/event-stream` responses never appear in `performance.getEntriesByType('resource')` — probe EventSource via a constructor wrapper, not resource timing.
- **Raw `/subscribe` frame probe (live 2026-11-11 fix run):** SSE frames carry the event name INSIDE the JSON payload (`data: {"key":"denied-<campaign>-<key>",…}`) — grep `"key":"denied-…`, never `event:` lines (a substring probe on `event:` false-negatived on a real denied-* broadcast). And when counting `write_rejected` rows in the Log DOM, use exact `.log-write-rejected` — the harness `[class*="write-rejected"]` substring selector counts nested elements (~4× per row). App.jsx `handleRuntimeEvent` + `handleChangeKeyEvent` had TWO stacked guards dropping null/scalar `change-<campaign>-*` SSE echoes (outer `event.data == null` return + inner object-only return); relaxed so campaign-key null/scalar echoes route to `applyStoreUpdate` (947b9702c) — string/number/null runtime keys (travel-mode, travel-lastMessage, travel-pendingEvent clears) now mirror cross-tab; character-snapshot keys still DROP null as corrupt; object lanes (`saveResult-`) byte-identical (the `biPrompt*` object lane retired 2026-11-18 — §17). Unit-test rig: capture handler via mocked `Subscriber` in `App.runtime-events.test.jsx`, assert store Map + `notify('campaign')`. LIVE-HARNESS corollary (improve 2026-11-17): `getStore` is keyed by CHARACTER, not campaign — patch `getStore('<Char>')` for a bucket recorder; and harness-side `setRuntimeValue` calls MUST pass campaignName (3-arg) or they 403 against `/api/campaigns/undefined/`.
- **Character-bucket EVICTION self-heals mounted consumers (live XX 2026-11-20, FIXED `fix(character-bucket-delete-echo-never-self-heals)`):** `DELETE /api/campaigns/:c/<charKey>` evicts server-side and fires the unconditional null `change-<c>-<key>` echo; `App.jsx handleChangeKeyEvent` now routes character-key null payloads through the REMOVE path too — the mirror is `replaceRuntimeObject(storeKey, {})`, the pre-existing whole-bucket eviction primitive ("server truth is the empty snapshot"; NEVER store null — the hex-arrival crash precedent). Mounted consumers on every open tab drop the phantom rows within ~2 s, no reload. Non-null scalars on character keys stay dropped as corrupt; the campaign-key null-echo lane is byte-unchanged; a 404 missing-key DELETE echo is an inert remove. Live-verified post-fix: GM Screen `ElderPaladin / Poisoned` line vanishes from both mounted tabs on DELETE, targetEffects add/delete and 404-leg sanity clean. UNIT RIG: `src/App.bucket-null-echo.test.jsx` — the App level-up-celebration harness pattern (`vi.importActual` on `useRuntimeState.js`) is the template for pins that must run through the REAL production store rather than a mock double.
- PITFALL: `browser_run_code_unsafe` does NOT reliably auto-follow `browser_tabs select` from a prior execute block — a silent "tab B" read can run on A; stamp `window.__tab` marker and re-assert tab identity inside every two-tab probe.
- **Producer-side runtime-store clears are cross-client by construction**: a registered CAMPAIGN_KEY written as null by the PRODUCING tab (e.g. `clearAllPendingOas` at the EB-Join/NPC-add seams) emits the null echo that REMOVES the key in every tab — an SSE-observer tab needs no mount-side sweep to evict it (live-verified 2026-11-10); prefer producer-side clears over defensive mount sweeps, which risk retiring legitimately-armed state that must survive remounts.
- Toolbar Travel-tool absorbed-first-press (CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-11 improve travel-tool-reentry-parity): the lying `.active` ghost is dead — the button's `.active`/`aria-pressed` and the TravelPanel mount now read ONE committed boolean (`useTravelToolSync` returns `travelPanelOpen` = synced travel-active, fed to both faces in the same render). An absorbed press reads honestly closed/closed; `aria-pressed` alone is now trustworthy panel proof — re-press once instead of the old press→probe→press dance. Related (same improve): reopening a map whose FILE carries a live trip auto-ADOPTS (faces pressed+panel with no press) — for an idle-mount repro cancel via `.travel-panel-close` first; `PUT /maps/:slug/deactivate` 409s when not active (harmless pre-delete). Campaign-bucket runtime reads MUST use the literal `'campaign'` characterKey: `getRuntimeValue('campaign', 'travel-mode')` — passing campaignName as the charKey console-ERRORs and misreads (14-error flood discovered live, 2026-10-27 hex-trip fix). Per-tab provenance recipe (75f2aa061): shared stamp (`travel-started-by` in campaign change-data) + `sessionStorage` token (survives reload/reopen, unique per tab, UI identity ONLY — zero game state) lets a client distinguish "my trip after refresh" from "another tab's trip"; see `src/services/maps/travelSession.js`. SHARPENED (live 2026-11-11 improve arrival-notice-shared): for a two-tab harness ride, seed `travel-started-by:'<foreign-token>'` (a token NEITHER tab holds) via pre-select curl — BOTH tabs then adopt the pre-seeded trip as observers with zero clobber risk; never press the Travel tool on such a rig (a press with no foreign stamp runs `startPlanning` and replaces the seeds).
- **Echo-adoption UI latency is ms-scale; only GET reads see the debounce lag (live 2026-11-11 improve arrival-notice-shared):** an observer tab's DOM (`.travel-toast`) adopted the GM's arrival press within ~15 ms via the SSE echo — the "travel writes lag 1–2 s, poll before judging" law applies to GET reads of change-data (2 s persist debounce + write round-trip), NOT to echo-driven UI adoption. For cross-tab timing proofs poll the DOM in the harness tab; reserve GET polling for disk truth. **Twin economy-strip readout rig (live 2026-11-09, fix of TurnEconomyStrip holder-remap)**: the whole ST-row economy readout ride works on a SETTLED Initiative tab — seed a 3-row full-store cs POST + top-level `activeCreatureName` POST → trusted Next click → curl full-store `turnState` POST charges land live via SSE echo (~2–3 s) and every economy strip re-renders in place; no reload needed to observe readout changes.
- `page.evaluate` cannot reference outer-scope consts in the serialized function string — recompute inside the evaluate.
- **Ops hygiene**: admin clears confirmed by API empties/own curl exit codes, retried individually (`curl -X POST url1 -X POST url2` runs both, prints only the last); `curl | python3 <<EOF` stdin collision kills POST silently — self-contained urllib + GET-confirm; orchestrator-sanctioned clear recipe: POST change-data+log clears, reload, clear again, sleep ~14s, GET-verify []/0 — close resurrecting tabs before final curl (§2 tab resurrection); re-join from a still-mounted EB after a data fix re-copies STALE in-memory monsters.json — full sequence: fetch cache:'reload' → page reload → re-select → re-join → re-stage; console 0 errors expected on clean rows; disk is truth over ticket prose (typo'd prose "rapie r"/"ofa"/"spel1" rides byte-identical cosmetic; RAW "takes 2" vs ticket "SRD 3" → disk wins); adjudication trichotomy: prose-only rider unexpressible = advisory PASS-subset; structured key absent but consumer-accessible = FAIL(b)/DATA; wrong-slot/over-grant/mis-sourced pool = FAIL(a); zero-affordance grep-confirmed no consumer = CODE-GAP (needs new automation.type+te+consumer), not one-field data. **Orchestration**: subagents NEVER write docs/automations-manifest.json (orchestrator set-status.mjs only; re-read the row after every mark; queue.txt array order is the ONLY dispatch source; recover adjudications only from live tool results). Subagent verdict contract: verdict-as-only-output, ~15-line cap — prose-without-verdict reports = failed attempts, row stays not-verified, re-dispatch. One shared SSE / no new EventSource (AGENTS.md) — app-wide `Subscriber`/`handleRuntimeEvent` fan-out; per-view handlers must key-gate (`event.key === \`map-activate-${campaign}\``) and self-state-gate.
- **Server/dev ops**: `npm run dev` runs node server.js WITHOUT nodemon — server-route edits need a child restart (kill + nohup npm run dev); Vite HMR reloads client only. Judge console-error fixes by the since-navigation counter (page header "Console: N errors"), not all:true session history. Dev-only HMR false-positive "change in the order of Hooks" on live-edited mounted components — re-verify fresh reload before filing. Known flaky test: mazeHandler.sp080-escape-concentration.test.js is unseeded, fails ~1-in-3 standalone — re-run before attributing. `rg -r` replace-flag misuse produced earlier "grep mangles" artifacts — plain `rg -n` only. **Server/dev ops addendum**: dev API is plain `node server.js` (no nodemon) — server-route edits are INVISIBLE until trio restart (confirm process start time > edit time before trusting a server-layer probe); restarting the trio can move Vite to **:5174** — read the port out of the dev log, don't assume 5173. `ps eww -p <pid>` recovers the live CAMPAIGN_LOCK verbatim before pkill+relaunch. Named CJS imports (`import { isEqual } from 'lodash'`) resolve UNDEFINED under plain-Node ESM — server-side shared modules need subpath default interop (`lodash/isEqual.js`).
- **Mock-surface growth (re-confirm + counts)**: ANY new export/import into a heavily-mocked module breaks every partial vi.mock in its graph — extend ALL mocks in one pass: changeData.js route imports; useRuntimeState partial mocks delegating to a Map need .has/.get; shared feature helpers get their OWN module (conditionEffects.js export breaks ~58 strict mocks; elfishLineageHandler mocked in ~15 files; spellValidation breaks 3 WizardStepSpells mocks; spellPreparationService export breaks ~12; loggedDiceRollUtils ~76; new barrel-in-modal ~50). `mockReturnValue([])` + consumer `.push` poisons shared arrays — use `mockReturnValueOnce` chains or `mockImplementation(=>[])`. Partial combatData mocks must extend when exports grow. Changing a hook's returned shape breaks all its Map-level partial mocks (add `flushSave: vi.fn()` to each); un-mocked methods throw AFTER routing asserts, masquerading as routing failures. Fake-timer batched-autosave tests: continuous strokes never fire the timer (each move restarts it) — mid-stroke saves need a >1s idle gap. `not.toHaveBeenCalled()` fetch asserts must scope method+URL once a component GETs more on mount. Route tests may run HAND-REPLICATED copies of the route — mirror route changes there or they verify stale logic. When `rulesFactory.getPlayerStats` is mocked, rules-layer speed folds never run — mock `speed` must carry the FOLDED total; invert stale render pins same pass. **Mock surface growth**: a NEW EXPORT in heavily-mocked loggedDiceRollUtils.js needs +1 vi.mock pin in ~76 sibling test files (batch-stage test folders); new barrel import in a modal breaks ~50 partial mocks — extend vi.mock (expirations etc.) same pass; adding an exported lane to contextBuilder-sync broke 8 partial mocks; makeDeps mocks must key-dispatch (key===MONSTER_RECHARGE_KEY) or uses-state cross-contaminates; store-as-Map keyed [characterKey][key] kills propertyName-mock blindness; complexity cap 15 forces helper hoisting (gateRecharge, summonPopupStay, selfBuffPopupBody, transport field hoist).
- **Char-JSON / campaign data safety**: characters are git-tracked — NEVER hand-restore tracked char JSON with git-show/string surgery; use Admin clear + reload or sanctioned PUT. Subclass-swap cleanup: close/deselect the sheet tab immediately after md5-restoring disk — an open tab holds the swapped config in memory and can re-PUSH full-store POSTs clobbering restored disk. Campaign rig drift between tickets: disk-check `class.subclass`/`race.subrace` before repro; PUT the bug-canonical shape for repro, RESTORE after. Wizard saves can silently re-home `class.fightingStyles` to None — re-check after subclass edits; class re-pick at step-6 CLEARS subclass (that IS the stale-major re-arm) — step-7 must re-pick before Save enables; step-7-only swap suffices when class.major null. 2024 feat PUT: string-form feats only — dict form breaks findFeat name.replace. Feat-less choice keys persist after untick+Save (inert) — clear via PUT. Disk spells[] ⊃ sheet rows (preparedSpells filtering) — pick probe spells from live DOM. Manifest handler/lane paths are frequently STALE (grep the real chain before trusting them; "Empowering Spell" was a mis-named real feature — rg the actual feature name before declaring fabrication). Enumerate class majors via node JSON walk (`majors[].features[]`, `class_levels[].features[]`) before dispatching name-uncertain rows; absent-from-all-data = STATIC-FAIL, no dispatch. **Wizard UI mechanics**: edits persist disk ~10-15s debounce; FEATURE ROWS rebuild from class data — reload after swap; wizard Save updates disk+runtime but NOT attack-cell renders — reload+re-select before judging cells. Spell selection = `.list-item-checkbox-trigger` click (row-body click only EXPANDS or no-ops); feat rows = `.feat-item .list-item-checkbox-trigger`; feat-cap "N of 5" is SOFT on some hosts, hard-blocks on others — deselect an owned feat if blocked; ASI chooser step-9 `.bg-ability-select`; skill/feat choice pool step-10 ("X of N", "Elevate" button = expertise face); Inventory step textarea: fill→Tab→Save (blur commits; `browser_type` wipes it; execCommand('insertText') for mastery equips), names with commas wrap in double quotes; click the AC-contribution popup away (blocks wizard nav). Spell-slot pip cells are TOGGLES (clicking a spent pip RESTORES) — click exactly `available` pips, GET-verify between rounds. Refusal popup at zero needs ALL eligible levels zero (getAvailableSpellSlotLevel loop). Resource tracker spinbuttons (`TrackedResourceInput`) fill+Enter refill stale 0 stamps. Sheet Long/Short Rest buttons click through while overlays linger.
- **Duplicate-name guard architecture (shipped behavior)**: quests/factions persist via GUID-keyed FULL-ARRAY POST (no per-entity PUT) — server guard = `validateList` option on `createJsonEntityCrud` rejecting case-insensitively-equal names (400 "A …with that name already exists"); rename-away+delete in one array passes (correct). Settlements/Sessions use custom PUT upsert routes that BYPASS validateList — they need their own PUT collision guard (npcs.js rename-guard pattern) AND validateList on the POST collection route. Client guard: hoisted `hasDuplicateName(list, editingId, name)` to module scope (component at eslint complexity cap 15); duplicate Save renders `.quests-form-error`/`.factions-form-error`/`.settlements-form-error` inline, modal stays open, NO POST hits the network. Dup-repro names must preserve whitespace when case-flipping ("qa dupa", not "qadupa"). Modal open on rejection intercepts list-row clicks — Cancel first. **Faction/Settlement UI lanes (first live CRUD ride XD 2026-11-16):** sidebar `Factions`/`Settlements` GM-only views; `New Faction`/`New Settlement` open INLINE editors with ids `#faction-name|#faction-description|#faction-goals|#faction-influence|#faction-notes` and `#settlement-name|#settlement-size (select)|#settlement-population|#settlement-government|#settlement-description|#settlement-atmosphere|#settlement-tags|#settlement-notes`; Save DISABLES on empty AND whitespace-only name; dup name disables Save WITH inline `…already exists…` copy beside the name field (edit leg lived; create-leg scoping unproven); row click opens the pre-filled editor; CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-16 (improve planner-delete-affordance): delete is now the shared role=dialog `DeleteConfirmModal` tier (`Delete Faction`/`Delete Settlement` + `Yes, Delete Permanently`) armed from EITHER a row-level icon chip `aria-label="Delete \"<name>\""` (`.factions-delete-btn`/`.settlements-delete-btn`, stopPropagation so the chip never opens the editor) OR the form footer `Delete` — the native `Delete Faction|Settlement "X"?` confirms are DEAD in both lanes; delete by id (factions) / name (settlements); create/edit persist within ~2 s (GUID id server-stamped); full create→edit→delete round-trips restore md5: factions.json `9ba33154…` (carries the pre-existing `qa-dup`/"The Iron Consortium" residue — leave it, it is baseline), settlements.json `ac5681f4…` (Ironhaven fixture only).
- **Cross-client turn-seam double-fire (FIXED 2026-11-08 5049bfad1 + LIVE FIX-VERIFIED run EO):** with TWO clients mounted on Initiative, turn-START automation used to fire once PER CLIENT (byte-identical `ability_use` rows; visitor POSTed `/FeyRanger` + `/__initiative__`). Guard = `createSseEventHandler` takes `isLocalhost` (fail-closed) and `applyTurnStartPass` early-returns for non-GM clients. EO re-ride: 5 seams × GM+LAN = exactly 1 row, visitor writes since mount = ZERO; **two-localhost-window control (GM↔GM twins, both pass the guard) also 1 row / 1 buff append — the dedupe ref/shared stamp already holds per-origin**. Witness rig: settled-page re-POST of a small hand board (§72), `Clear` nulls `dreadAmbushSpeedActive` itself (no PATCH needed post-Clear), DIFF `ability_use` rows by `timestamp > t0` marked before the re-POST. **Visitor drains the GM save-listener bucket (FILED run EO, `bug-visitor-drains-pending-save-listener-bucket`):** every GM-side `createSaveListener` save resolution makes the VISITOR's `useLoggedDiceRollEventHandlers.syncListenerPromptFilters` (zero isLocalhost guards in that file) attempt the drain-to-evict `DELETE /pendingSaveListenerPrompts` → 403 "Access denied: GM-only feature" (guard lives on `campaigns-character.js:268` DELETE `:file` wildcard which refuseUnlessGm BEFORE `next()`-ing non-`.json` — the change-data DELETE route itself has NO GM guard, containment is route-order) + a console error per LAN device per adjudicated save. Same window, the visitor also landed a SUCCEEDING full-store `POST /<Victim>` stale-mirror snapshot (`{activeConditions, pendingExpirations:[]}`) — character buckets are visitor-writable for delegation, so that face is a key-loss race hazard, not noise. Visitor-tab baseline law sharpened: mount stamp `POST /__campaign__` only; delegated-card rolls POSTing `/Goblin`/`/lastAttack`/`/<Target>`/`/log` are BY DESIGN — the regression witness is writes the visitor makes while IDLE mirroring someone else's resolution.
- **ST-guard must cover the SSE echo lane too (pitfall, 2026-11-09 fix 4dc24883b):** the `activeCreatureName` name echo runs `expireStaleEffects(newActive)` on the WRITING client *and* every mounted tab via `sseHandlers.js` — an ST-law fix that only guards navigationHandlers still materializes the phantom `{pendingExpirations:[]}` char bucket under the synthetic `(Second Turn)` label. The guard now lives in `applyEchoBoundaryPasses` (twin echoes skip `expireStaleEffects` + the turn-start pass); after both legs, a single-tab twin walk leaves zero `*Second Turn*` keys in change-data. Any future seam-law fix must audit BOTH the navigation writer and the echo boundary. **Victory banner floats above confirmation dialogs (FILED run EO, `bug-victory-banner-above-confirm-dialogs`):** `.victory-banner` z-1300 > `.modal-shell-overlay` z-1000 — an undismissed banner's `.victory-card` renders OVER a later-opened dialog and silently swallows the centered confirm button (Playwright: `victory-card … intercepts pointer events`, 30s click timeout; banner card click dismisses, so the SECOND attempt works). 29e67520e made the SCRIM click-through but the CARD tier above modals remains. Live proof recipe: Clear → (banner up) → Clear again → try `Yes, Clear Board` → hit-test names the intercepting subtree. **MutationObserver + cross-client floaters (run EO law):** React mounts the floater as a `.hp-feedback` WRAPPER whose added node's className lacks `hp-floater` — a className-only observer reads zero forever (two false-negatives this run). Scan addedNodes AND `node.querySelectorAll('.hp-floater')`. The real delegated-roll leg PASSES: visitor HIT (AC-12 token target for odds) → Done → visitor writes land → GM-TV observer captures `hp-floater hp-floater-damage −N`. Wrong-chip trap: `.mc-overlay .mc-dice-link` indices are saves(0–5)/skills(6)/attacks(7+) on the Goblin card — anchor by row text, not index.
- **`pendingOAs` CAMPAIGN_KEYS registration (FIXED 2026-11-09, `pendingOAs` added to the set — see `resolved/bug-pending-oas-key-not-registered-echo-misroutes.md`):** unregistered, the `change-<c>-pendingOAs` SSE echo failed `isCampaignKey` in `applyStoreUpdate` and dispatched to a ghost CHARACTER store named "pendingOAs", and the drain-to-evict null DELETE echo dropped — same misroute family as `lastCombatResult`/`pendingStarryChaliceHeal` (campaignKeys.js comments). Consequence for rigs (now cured): cross-tab/curl `pendingOAs` marker writes live-update settled tabs within the SSE tick (~2–3 s), object echo = full-bucket mirror, null echo = key REMOVE. CORRECTED wake claim: an unrelated registered-key echo does NOT wake a stale mirror (re-POSTing enforcement left the ghost store unread — `notify('campaign')` can't inject a value never stored campaign-level); pre-fix the only cure was a remount/reload seed. Rig budget note obsolete: no settle budget needed after campaign-select beyond normal seed hydration. **LAN map-view + fog parity (run EO):** visitor nav "Map"; zero active map = "Waiting for the GM to open a map…`Check Again`"; replacing the viewed map NEVER hijacks a mounted view (App.jsx:226 pinned-view contract) — the viewed map shows the honest advisory `GM deactivated this map — view continues read-only` and the visitor must navigate away/back to auto-open the NEW active map (zero reload). Fog sandbox (cp owner fixture, `revealed:[]`, wall row, 1 token, fogged-half npc placedItem → POST maps → activate): visitor DOM = `205 .fog-cell-player` + fogged token label ABSENT; GM = `205 .fog-cell` + label VISIBLE. `GridAndWalls` filters fogged WALLS by isLocalhost; player-side class is `fog-cell-player`.

## 18. Folded lane notes (maps/travel, AI authoring, campaign mgmt, monster/PC addenda)
Distilled from the 2026-10-10 → 2026-11-05 run logs; raw provenance + more detail in the archive.

Campaign mgmt / shell:
- **Select-screen stale-state law (live 2026-11-21 improve browser-title-campaign):** clicking "Campaigns" back to the select screen does NOT clear `campaignName`/`activeView` in `App.jsx` — they stay mounted with stale values while `showCampaignSelection` is true. Any title/affordance/derivation work must gate on `showCampaignSelection`, else stale campaign/character names leak into the select screen (this is how `getDocumentTitle` in `src/routes/config.js` guards its neutral `CharSheets` face; the tab title is `<campaign> — <view>` while mounted, the sheet view names the character).

Maps / hex / travel lane:
- **Indoor gridSize clamp law (shipped 2026-11-21 improve gridsize-save-clamp; asymmetry retired):** `clampGridSize` (5..100) now polices BOTH write lanes — create builders AND the whole-doc PUT (applied at the single store seam before `pruneRevealedToGrid`, so cells outside the clamped grid prune in the same pass and the SSE `map-data-*` echo carries the clamped value; live: PUT 110→GET 100, 2→5, 150→100, 20/5/100 verbatim). The clamp polices VALUES only: key-absent and explicit-`null` gridSize keep the erasure guard's presence protocol (omission never invents a field; explicit null is the sanctioned clear — pinned by `maps.whole-map-erasure-guard.test.js` + `maps.put-gridsize-clamp.test.js`). A whole-map PUT without `type` honestly 400s (`must include the "type" field`). - **`__map__` resurrection-race law (live 2026-11-17 fix opened-map-delete-leaves-ghost-active-map):** a live GM tab armed on a map re-stamps `__map__` on its next `activeMapName` transition (MapContextSync force-stamp), so an out-of-band activate→DELETE that races the SSE round-trip RESURRECTS the ghost even after an honest server clear — any server-side `__map__` clear must ALSO publish the `map-activate` null face (deactivate-route twin) so live tabs disarm, not just write the key. HIT-TEST NOTE: the hoisted row is topmost within its painted footprint, so chips grab presses there; token-body drags are unaffected (topmost-visible grab-surface doctrine intact). Ghost law re-stated at the layer: rows derive from the TRUE cell visibility (fogged/hidden truth cell = no row), so a `.token-ghost` (only ever painted where the client can't see the true cell) leaks zero chips.

Byte law: default/compact output replays byte-identical to pre-fix. Note: sprawling honestly packs fewer rooms into small grids (spacing eats capacity — fewer floors, not more).
- **Drag press-point law (live 2026-11-10 improve)**: on a settled board the svg-origin+`gx*40+20` recipe DRIFTS after the first drag (pan/zoom shift) — derive every press point from the token's own `getBoundingClientRect` (`svg.grid-svg circle.creature-circle`) and read the landing cell from the circle's `cx` attr (`(cx−20)/40 = gx`). SHARPENED same day (same-cell stacking improve): a press MISS on empty grid silently starts a MAP PAN and shifts every subsequent press in the tab — re-derive rects from the classed circles (`circle.creature-circle`/`circle.npc-circle`) after ANY crowd mutation, and beware the FIRST `circle` inside a token group is the `<defs><clipPath>` circle (zero-size rect) — always anchor on the classed circle. **Same-cell token stacking invariant (shipped 2026-11-10 improve same-cell-token-overlap)**: overlapping tokens fan out by paint order (player layer bottom → monster rows → npc rows top, `services/maps/tokenStackOffsets.js`, 10px steps capped at 2); buried groups carry `transform="translate(−10k,−10k)"`, stack tops and single tokens are attribute-absent, and crowded-cell placed tokens render `circle.placed-hit-area` instead of the 40×40 rect (painted hit = grab surface, paint order alone decides). Consequence for probes: `elementFromPoint` at a BURIED token's rect centre legitimately returns the token ABOVE it (that pixel shows the top token's art) — grab low tokens at their peek arc (~0.66r toward the fan direction).
- **Travel rigs**: travel tool state is per-mount LOCAL: opening an outdoor board with no live trip in the store does NOT restore a previously-pressed Travel tool — a fresh `[aria-label*="Travel mode"]` press is required per mount (laws bullet 10's zero-press auto-mount only applies when the store carries a live trip). Exhaustion grind = all-char buckets `POST /<Char> {value:{exhaustionLevel:N}}` + `POST /travel-accruedCost {value:10}` + `POST /travel-dailyBudget {value:2}` → exhausted banner without seeding dayExhausted/mode; Forced March adds +1 EX to every bucket EXCEPT self-restoration holders (live KV 2026-11-11: 12 of 13 PCs stamped, Disciplined_Monk honestly skipped by the `hasSelfRestoration` gate). Extreme-weather rig: POST throwaway outdoor map (`{name,type:"outdoor",gridSize:20}`, whole grid plays `plains`; CORRECTED IN PLACE live run KV 2026-11-11 re-verifying 1b28c3752: create DOES stamp a centred `partyPosition` (gridSize 20 → {q:20,r:10}) at writeFileSync — no create-then-PUT dance needed, GET-verify is immediate; the JT-era "client seeds defaults at editor-open" face is RETIRED; gridSize N ⇒ hexCols 2N × hexRows N) + PUT full body with `weather:{condition:'extreme',moveCostMod:null,budgetMod:0,…}`; advance refuses honestly (`travel/extreme_weather` row — CORRECTED IN PLACE: the row CARRIES the party hex + terrain (q29,r11 plains live KV; the old hex/terrain-NULL claim predates the 2026-10-27-G fix), plus panel message "Extreme weather makes travel impossible. Camp and wait it out." and a dedicated `.travel-panel-exhausted` camp row; dayExhausted UNREACHABLE under cost-null). Campaign-bucket reads use literal charKey `'campaign'` (`getRuntimeValue('campaign','travel-mode')`) — passing campaignName as charKey console-errors. **Travel console live-lane laws (run JT 2026-11-11, full ride):** trip keys seeded PRE-select adopt cleanly into the mounted console; a Travel-tool press on a fresh map WITHOUT tool-adoption runs `startPlanning` and REPLACES them (destination→null, path→[], new `travel-started-by` stamp). Honest plan lane = Travel tool + hex-click destination. Destination-click geometry (SHARPENED IN PLACE live run KV 2026-11-11, simplest proven lane): inside page.evaluate `const m = document.querySelector('.hex-grid-layer').getScreenCTM()` (the LAYER group's own CTM — the JT-era warning is about the SVG ROOT only) → `screen = m.a*x + m.c*y + m.e` with `x = 30*sqrt(3)*(q + r/2), y = 45*r` (HEX_SIZE 30, pointy-top axial) → `page.mouse.click`. GUARD mandatory: `document.elementFromPoint(x,y).tagName === 'path'` BEFORE clicking — the floating `.travel-panel` occupies the lower-left grid area and absorbs destination clicks (CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-11 improve travel-panel-click-feedback: a planning-await chrome click is no longer silent — it fires the `.travel-toast` "Select a destination hex on the map" via pointerup detection (not click — the header drag preventDefaults pointerdown); probes must not treat that toast as an arrival signal and must keep the elementFromPoint guard so a panel-absorbed click isn't misread as a failed arm). The centroid-affine two-rect law and the trusted `locator.nth(r*hexCols+q).click({force:true})` lane both remain valid alternatives. Arrival law GET-proved: `travel-destination/travel-path/travel-pathIndex` REMOVED (per-key DELETE, no null ghosts), `travel-started-by` → null (release is a null-POST — key PRESENT as null, not deleted; CANCEL also null-releases destination/mode rather than DELETE), day-state keys (`travel-accruedCost/-dailyBudget`) + `travel-lastMessage` SURVIVE arrival, party position lands in the MAP FILE, `.travel-toast[role=status]` "Arrived at destination — travel complete" (2.5 s) renders on the presser tab AND — since 5c403d502 — on every observer tab via the echo lane (live KV 2026-11-11: LAN isolated-tab toast appeared ~12 ms after the GM's arrival press; presser byte-unchanged), honest advance×N + arrived `travel` rows + `session_phase` pair. Whole-doc `weather` PUT adopts LIVE mid-session (panel row re-renders; per-hex cost = 0.75 plains base × moveCostMod: clear 0.75, rain 0.9375 GET-deltas) and `__hexmap3d.census` answers `weather:"<condition>"` GM + LAN — CORRECTED IN PLACE (115cd8f7f, live KV re-verify): census stamps the doc's verbatim condition, `clear` reads "clear"; null is reserved for no-weather. `travel-pathIndex` writes lag ~1–2 s — poll after EVERY advance; a 500 ms read false-stalls. Cross-client trap (filed + FIXED 2026-11-11 `bug-travel-cross-client-advance-replays-hex`): after an external advance echo, the adopting tab's stale `pathIndexRef` used to make its NEXT press replay the last hex (budget +cost, duplicate `travel`/`advance` row, pathIndex unchanged) — `useTravelManagement` now backfills the advance refs from the synced path/pathIndex echo (adoption-only, idempotent on local writes), so the adopting tab arrives on its first press; GET idx + accrued per press anyway before concluding cache. POI truth field is `label` (not `name`). POSTing ANY unregistered `travel-<name>` name creates a LITERAL change-data key (own `travel-weather` phantom, DELETE-swept) — §362 law again. **Event + day-state console laws (first live ride, run KV 2026-11-11, all faces GET/DOM-proved):** (1) An advance that triggers an event logs `travel`/`advance_with_event` (carries eventTitle), sets `travel-mode:'paused'` + `travel-pendingEvent` (object) + panel message `⚡ <title>`, and mounts `.event-dialog-overlay .event-dialog` (type/title/description/`.event-dialog-terrain` + type-colored header); Accept = `.event-btn-accept`, DECLINE = `.event-btn-skip`, `.event-btn-reroll` with `.reroll-count` `(N)` (title "Re-roll (N left today; resets when you Camp)"; at 0 disabled with "No re-rolls left today — Camp to start a new day"). Reroll decrements `travel-rerollsRemaining`, replaces pendingEvent with a FRESH random roll (duplicates possible), logs `event_reroll` naming the DISCARDED event. (2) SKIP mid-route resumes the interrupted mode (`traveling` — `modeBeforePauseRef`); ACCEPT keeps the legacy PLANNING restore on every type (documented in useTravelManagement :652-656 — do not fold); both clear lastMessage; non-combat accept logs exactly one `event_accept` row (eventType+eventTitle, battleMap null). `npc`-type dialogs carry the hint "Accepting only records this encounter — the GM adjudicates off-map." (3) COMBAT accept stages into the campaign's SINGLE reusable **"Travel Encounter Stage"** indoor map (30-grid, `parentHex`, wiped+reseeded per accept: 13 player fan-out tokens + the encounter's monsters as `type:'npc'` placedItems + decor), the `event_accept` row carries `battleMap:"Travel Encounter Stage"`, and the app NAVIGATES the GM to the stage board (panel unmounts, tool face follows on the hex view). With `enforcement.autoEncounters` OFF the lane writes ZERO initiative state (no combatSummary beyond the mount-merge 13-PC placeholder artifact — CV/CW law). CLEANUP: the stage map row appears in Maps-manager — Delete (role=dialog, confirm `Yes, Delete Permanently`) it at exit. (4) WEATHERCHANGE accept re-rolls the map-doc weather via handleGenerateWeather and re-derives `travel-dailyBudget` from the NEW roll (differential face: Fast budget 5 → 3 under storm budgetMod 0.75); plains-table weatherChange weight is 3/100 — do NOT chase it on the RNG: POST a synthetic `travel-pendingEvent` `{value:{type:'weatherChange',title,description,terrain}}` + `travel-mode:'paused'` onto a SETTLED tab — the reactive useSyncedState keys adopt live and the dialog mounts (sharpens §54: the "curl keys don't refresh a loaded tab" law belongs to NON-reactive getRuntimeValue consumers; every `travel-*` hook key renders live from the echo). (5) Exhausted-banner producer split: on a uniform-cost map the mount/echo MIRROR flags `travel-dayExhausted` the moment the budget is spent (accrued==budget) — NO lastMessage, NO `day_exhausted` row (banner copy "Travel budget exhausted — camp or forced march?" + `.travel-budget-hint` "X / N used today[ — carried from earlier trips today]"); the press-reject face (message + `day_exhausted` row) needs an unaffordable ROUTE next-hex with an affordable neighbor, unreachable on all-plains — judge the pressed-refuse family on the extreme-weather face instead. Camp (`.travel-btn-camp`, banner OR extreme row): re-rolls doc weather, accrued→0, budget re-derived from NEW roll × pace, rerolls→3, dayExhausted false, forcedMarchHours 0, PAUSED→PLANNING if paused, message "A new day dawns. Travel budget refreshed.", `camp` row (weather field = PRE-roll). (6) Forced March (`.travel-btn-march`): +1 exhaustionLevel to each PC bucket (self-restoration exempt), `travel-forcedMarchHours`+1, accrued 0, budget = floor(base×budgetMod×(5/6)^hours) — the penalty is BUDGET-ONLY: per-hex cost and "Next hex" time stay base (per-hex `applyExhaustionSpeedPenalty` RETIRED 2026-11-11 e2e-improve — copy, budget and per-hex numbers now tell one story), status row `.travel-panel-exhaustion` "N / 6 stacks Daily budget: 83%" (budget-framed copy, `.travel-exhaustion-budget` span; panel lastMessage "…Daily budget reduced to N%."), honest `forced_march` log row. (7) Max-EX gate (any PC exhaustionLevel ≥6): `.travel-panel-hint` "A party member is at maximum exhaustion — rest required" + Advance disabled with the same tooltip; over the exhausted banner the copy flips to "Travel budget exhausted — camp to rest." and `.travel-btn-march` disables with title "Cannot forced march — a party member has reached maximum exhaustion" — the gate is a RENDER-TIME read (`partyHasMaxExhaustion()` via non-reactive getRuntimeValue): a foreign bucket write flips it only when something re-renders the panel — a same-value chip press does NOT tick it, any real change (pace/frequency to a different value) does. (8) Pace chips (`.travel-pace-btn` Slow/Normal/Fast) re-derive `travel-dailyBudget` (floor(hexesPerHour×8): 2/4/5 ×weather budgetMod) + "Next hex" time (e.g. Fast 1h 8m vs Normal 1h 30m plains), clear dayExhausted + FM hours, KEEP accrued (same-day pool); horseback (`.travel-horseback-btn`) halves time ("1h 8m"→"34 min", label Walking↔Horseback + tooltips) without touching per-hex cost. (9) The travel console is 2D-ONLY: the 3D hex view renders the tube + party passively (no `.travel-panel`), `hex3dScene.js:1097` `void pathIndex` is reserved-for-v2 (NO behind/ahead colouring in 3D) and census `travelPath` is a presence flag 0/1 — cross-client advance/cancel adopt the mounted 3D scene live without remount (cancel flipped census travelPath 1→0 in KV). (10) Tool/panel parity (fd3b30cf5, re-verified ×4): opening a map whose store carries a live trip auto-mounts panel + pressed face with ZERO presses; arrival/cancel auto-unpresses (aria-pressed false, panel unmounted); re-entry is a single press into PLANNING with the honest "Select a destination" empty-path label.
- **HexMap.jsx statement/complexity ceiling (live 2026-11-11 improve travel-panel-click-feedback)**: the HexMap component body sits EXACTLY at the eslint max-statements(60)/complexity(15) ceiling — any new in-body derivation or `useCallback` tips it over; hoist to module-level helpers/consts instead.
- **Player waiting-pane**: `Waiting for the GM to open a map…` + `Check Again` renders even when `__map__` is non-null (auto-open fires only on NEW activation SSE events while blank; never-hijack for an already-OPEN map is PINNED by App.map-activate.test.jsx — neither is a bug; click Check Again). Self-heal rides server-truth auto-check (fixed 2026-11-05 maps-waiting-pane-self-heal). Visitor-face scoping law (live 2026-11-19 improve fog-ghost-memory-across-flips): a fresh visitor tab's sidebar header is `MAP` (singular — GM shows `Maps`), and the visitor Maps view AUTO-OPENS the active board on entry (no row-Open click needed — probe the board immediately after view entry).
- **Outdoor pan-clamp hides edge parties (live 2026-11-11 fix run)**: with `MIN_ZOOM=2` and `MARGIN_X=(hexRows−1)/2`, `clampPan` can pin the viewBox left edge PAST a low-q party — a party at (5,3) sat permanently off-screen and toolbar zoom-out clicks changed nothing visible. Recipe: seed the sandbox map's `partyPosition` near grid centre, or whole-doc PUT a new `partyPosition` — the settled board adopts via the map-data echo and the marker moves live. Related: Map-delete in the Maps manager is the role=dialog tier (confirm `Yes, Delete Permanently`, no native confirm), but a still-mounted board tab RE-STAMPS a deleted map ACTIVE (MapContextSync) — move that tab off the Map view before deleting. SHARPENED (live 2026-11-11 improve-run PT2 re-verify): the move-off cure is INSUFFICIENT after row **Open** — viewing a non-active map makes the client stamp `__map__.activeMapName` to the viewed map and that stamp survives a later DELETE even with the tab moved off first; close the tab (or clear `__map__`) and GET-verify before trusting activation state after deleting a map you opened. Row-button attribute selectors need the FULL quoted label (`button[aria-label='Deactivate "JV Insert Sandbox"']` — the bare unquoted form silently times out) and the label quotes the row's DISPLAYED name, not the file slug — a `Deactivate "slug"` selector no-ops whenever displayName≠slug (live 2026-11-11 improve marching-order-insert). **Cheapest map-history armer (live 2026-11-11 improve back-to-maps-label):** `handlePoiEnter` gates only on linkedMap *existence*, so an **outdoor→outdoor POI pair** (two throwaway outdoor maps with mutually linked POIs, party at `hexDistance===1` of the POI) arms the App map-history stack AND lands you on a HEX toolbar with the stack popped — no indoor handoff needed. The dynamic "Back to <map>" label (App `useMapHistory` → `backMapTarget` prop → both toolbars via `backToMapLabel`) reads `Back to <displayName||title-cased slug>` while armed, byte-identical `Back to maps` when the stack drains (reverts without remount).

AI authoring lane:
- **Referee/AI testing rigs**: deterministic proposal driving = `page.route('**/ai/referee*', r=>r.fulfill({ok:true, ruling:{…}}))` — everything downstream (card, picker gating, applyRuling, te writes, audit row) runs for real. But route-stubs answer AFTER server schema validation — to exercise invalid-output legs (retry→`needs_human`) fake the MODEL instead: keyless loopback OpenAI-compat server (e.g. :8123) steered by QA markers in the prompt. aiConfig rig: `POST /api/campaigns/:c/aiConfig {value:{mode,endpoint,model,provider:'openai'}}` — `provider:'local'` FAILS the validator; localhost endpoints only; restore = `DELETE` (GET then reports `{"value":null}`, configured:false). POST accepts unknown keys; merge-on-save preserves row-external keys; Decoding=probe REMOVES jsonSchema, blank Temperature REMOVES temperature. Admin AI badge/section SSE-live (no reload). **Grammar-echo fingerprint (oMLX + *-mtp builds)**: with aiConfig.jsonSchema=TRUE, refine + blueprint-repair faces return the input entity VERBATIM (server audits ok:true attempts:1 — never trust attempts alone); client `proposedNoChange()` echo-detect is the honest surface; server audits `ai_refine {noOp:true}` → Log row "… no change proposed — the model echoed the input". Keep jsonSchema:false for refine lanes.
- **AI audit log rows**: `ai_*` rows render prose CLIENT-SIDE via `AI_AUDIT_DESCRIBERS` in `src/components/log/log-utils.js` — server entries are structured-only; log-copy tickets belong there, not route files. `ai_ruling` (proposal, keeps model's original bad key) + `ai_ruling_applied` (GM-repaired key, `overridden:true`) pair is by design. Designer resume SUCCESS audits show `attempts:0` + created{} — machine truth = created counts + snapshot name, never attempts.
- **AI-Dress**: ops land as `placedItems` entries (type is a free string — "add wall" = wall-TYPE item; walls/paint/fog/image layers untouchable) — verify apply via placedItems array; `AiDressButton` toolbar trigger + dialog are mutually-exclusive render arms (mid-preview re-click structurally impossible); pre-disable when aiConfig unset/off (mode && mode!=='off', AiRefineButton.jsx precedent); no-config face (post-29e362837, live-verified CY): dock opens with `p.ai-instruction-hint[role=status]` "AI is not enabled for this campaign — configure it in Campaign Admin → AI Referee, then reopen this dialog." and the Dress map submit is DISABLED even with a typed instruction (`!aiLive` term in disabled expr, `aiLive = mode && mode!=='off'` read from runtime aiConfig live) — zero POST, zero 403, zero console error (the old fire-then-403 dance is retired). Without a model backend the dress POST honestly 200s an `AI_UNAVAILABLE` refusal — nothing saves. Applied POIs/tokens render live via SSE; dress fake-model loopback steered by a `dress`/`placedItems` marker in the prompt.
- **Encounters are ONE doc**: `data/encounters.json` `{encounters:[…]}`; AI drafts live there with `draft:true` (approve strips in place). Raw-API draft rigs MUST stamp `id: crypto.randomUUID()` — an id-less row can never be deleted/rejected by id. Faction full-array POST needs `{factions:[…]}` WRAPPER + echo ALL rows (partial array 409s). Banner-self-erase fix: `reload({keepError:true})` on SSE paths only. Mid-loop-erase batches order the FAILING row FIRST or the regression is vacuous.
- **EB save/load UI laws (CORRECTED IN PLACE live 2026-11-16 run XB, post `7f499c007`+`c10ab82fd`+`2007a3c03`+`24fff11c1`):** toolbar labels are now AT-CLEAN (`Save`/`Update`/`Load`/`Generate`/`Reset`/`Join Encounter`/`Refine with AI` textContent with NO leading space, aria-label OVERRIDES removed — the long copy moved to `title` ("Save encounter" etc.); `getByRole exact` on the bare words resolves — the WK-era DOM-filter workaround is retired; caveat: removing an override can collide toolbar `Save` with the modal's `Save` — scope modal clicks). Bestiary select is role=CHECKBOX `aria-label="Select Goblin"` (button-role probes time out); `Join Encounter` renders ONLY with ≥1 drafted monster; empty Save AND Update legs show inline `.encounter-modal-error` "Encounter has no monsters to save" WITHOUT opening the name modal (toast/popup probes false-negative); name modal `#encounter-name`: Save button now DISABLES while the name is blank/whitespace (blank Enter still pays the inline "Encounter name is required" backstop — the error copy STAYS after you then type a valid name: backlog); pool rows `button[aria-label='Load|Rename|Delete "name"']` — rename/delete icon-only buttons NOW carry `title` mirroring the aria verbatim (both 29/29 rows lived XB); rename reuses the modal with `#encounter-rename` pre-filled, cleared-name disables the Rename button, commit toast "Encounter renamed" closes the whole Load walk. LEGACY SHAPES DEAD: `encounters.json` migrated 2026-11-16 to 29 rows, every row `selectedMonsters`-carrying, ZERO `creatures[]` anywhere (test-encounter repaired to Goblin×2/effXP 100 and now loads a REAL draft — the "5 legacy rows load EMPTY" law is retired); rows are pre-stamped so loads fire ZERO effectiveXP-reconcile PUTs (md5 `dce8d33a` byte-stable through a live load); `Reset` detaches Update-mode back to create.
- **Forced-draft face without a model ride + designer forced-face rigs (live 2026-11-13 fix designer-kind-word-name):** `PUT /api/campaigns/:c/npcs/<name>` with `draft:true` in the body stores verbatim (+stamp) through the CRUD lane — instant on-disk draft row (entity files are read from disk each GET, no debounce); `DELETE /npcs/<name>` restores md5 byte-exact. PITFALL: the planted row POISONS `entitySchemas.test.js` (it validates the REAL test-campaign rows) — remove repro artifacts before running the suite. `POST /ai/designer/generate` takes `scope` directly (curl-driveable, no chat/plan walk), but the reasoning model will NOT reliably emit a kind-word name even when the brief demands it (it answered a name-"npc" ask with a compliant empty blueprint at attempts:1) — pin repair-turn/stop faces with the scripted-model rig (`designer.test.js` pattern, `cfg.schemaName === 'draft_npc'`), never model roulette. The kind-word/placeholder NAME LAW is server-ENFORCED since 2026-11-13 (`src/services/ai/entityNameLaw.js` → `validateEntity` + `approveDraft` refusal), superseding the 2026-10-31 "SOFT prompt fix only — validators untouched" ruling. **Drafts-panel decision faces + world-keeper kind laws (live 2026-11-13 explore run ZC):** Approve-guard refusals are CLIENT-side throws surfaced in the CampaignBuilder error banner (single: `Not approved — name "npc" is a kind word — …`; batch `Approve all npcs`: `N of M npcs rows failed — they were NOT saved: “<name>”: <law>`) — row stays `draft:true`, intentional console.error fires. `Reject` CORRECTED IN PLACE (live 2026-11-13 explore run AD, `5fb8a2236`): Reject now arms the shared `DeleteConfirmModal` role=dialog — title `Reject Draft`, body names the draft (`This will permanently reject the AI draft '<name>'…`), buttons `Cancel`/`Yes, Delete Permanently`; Cancel writes NOTHING (row stays `draft:true`, zero audit rows), confirm destroys + audits. Modal-family law (CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-13 improve modal-escape-dismiss, `3f41dc16b`): `DeleteConfirmModal` routes Escape and a PURE backdrop click to `onCancel` (backdrop needs mousedown+click fully on the overlay — a panel→backdrop drag-release is guard-inert); Cancel/Escape/backdrop all write nothing, the confirm button stays the sole destructive exit, Tab trap unchanged. `ai_draft_approved`/`ai_draft_rejected` rows fire ONLY on success (a race-loser's failed DELETE writes none). TWO-GM-TAB race rig: `page.context().newPage()` (same context = second localhost GM tab), navigate/select in its own bounded run; with the modal tier, arm BOTH modals first (locator clicks fine — SSE disable never reaches modal buttons), then `Promise.all` the two `Yes, Delete Permanently` clicks. Loser copy CORRECTED IN PLACE (AD, `32744761f`): the raw `NPC not found` is now mapped to `Draft "<name>" was approved or removed in another tab or session since you loaded it — the drafts list has already refreshed — nothing to do here.` (both panels resync empty; any other error still passes through verbatim). Deterministic single-tab face of the same 404 lane (AD): arm the Reject modal, externally `DELETE /npcs/<name>` while it sits open, confirm → same conflict copy — no second tab needed. Snapshot zips live at `public/campaigns/.snapshots/` (SIBLING of campaign dirs — `public/campaigns/<c>/.snapshots` never matches; dist has its own triplet — count "pre-existing" per folder). Designer chat `Send` button textContent is `" Send"` (leading icon space, `^Send$` misses — same family as sidebar headers); `Generate drafts` busy label appends `(this can take a while)`; full-set generate ~60–120 s on the reasoning rig, poll the banner text across bounded runs. PROVENANCE LAW (live 2026-11-13 explore run AD, `931ae7be8`): when the GM's edited plan totals diverge from the model's proposal, the generate `ai_designer` row's `description` reads `AI designer drafted N entities per the edited plan (N planned, model proposed M, restore point <zip>)` and the row stamps `planned`/`proposed`; the PLAN row always keeps the raw model-proposed total; agreement face keeps the simple copy. Live bait failure twice (ZC + AD): a compliant Qwen honors the EDITED counts at attempts:1 even when the chat brief explicitly demands a zero-count kind (faction-sheet ask, plan `factions:0` → drafted exactly the 3 planned npcs, zero off-plan rows) — the TRIM-DROPPED live face is model-roulette, rely on the unit pin. World keeper: narrative kinds (recap/rumor/event/npc_dialogue) `Log it` = log row ONLY (`applyWorldProposal.js` narrativeApply — zero entity dirt, swept by the exit log clear), receipt badge `Applied: <kind>`; quest_draft receipt (live AD, `dd18b5fc3`): `Add quest to ledger` → badge `Applied: quest '<name>'` + `ai_world_applied` description `… quest '<name>' added directly to the ledger` — restore `DELETE /quests/<FULL-uuid>` → md5 5055fb78; the keeper CONTEXTS on the campaign log — a logged rumor/event surfaces verbatim-ish in later proposals (cross-ride continuity). Post-`355ebed53` the `.ai-world-advisory` element is ABSENT (not empty) when all names ground — faction canon and `'s`-possessives (Consortium's, Ironhaven's) no longer flag; presence-with-names still legitimately means truly-unknown — but quest TITLES are out of the diffed ledger (npcs+settlements+factions only), so a quest_draft card honestly naming an existing quest ALWAYS carries an advisory (AD live face: `check spelling: Lost Artifact`) — never file it as a bug.
- **409-conflict rig (deterministic)**: `page.route('**/api/…/<kind>', hold POST ~700ms once)` + a Node-`fetch` write from inside run_code_unsafe (:80 bypasses page routes) makes stale-stamp conflicts 100% reproducible; conflict banners quote the entity name so body.innerText "row gone" waits self-match — scope to `.campaign-builder-row:has-text(…)` with `state:'detached'`. Two-tab same-ms race: `page.context().pages()` + one `Promise.all(pages.map(p=>p.evaluate(click)))`.

Campaign management / wizard / auth lanes:
- **Confirm-modal tiers**: Regenerate Adventure, friendly-fire arming, seed modals, map/POI/item deletes, snapshot rollback are role=dialog custom modals (`[role=dialog]`, `aria-modal`, `aria-labelledby` — shared `common/DeleteConfirmModal.jsx` chrome; friendly-fire = FriendlyFireConfirmModal; regenerate anchor `data-testid="regenerate-adventure-btn"` — checklist-step aria no longer contains "Regenerate"). Clear Change Data / Clear Log / Full Reset + the three Data-Management natives (handleClear/handleRemoveNpc/handleAddNpc in createCreatureHandlers.js) stay `window.confirm` — owner-blessed, do NOT migrate without an owner decision. Native tier EXTENDS into the planner/encounter lanes (live WK 2026-11-16): NPC delete `Delete NPC "X"?`, quest delete `Delete quest "X"?`, dirtied quest-form Cancel `Discard changes to "X"?`, EB Update-overwrite `Overwrite encounter "X"? This replaces the saved encounter.` (the EB confirm sits BEHIND the zero-draft `.encounter-modal-error` gate, so an empty draft never reaches it). CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-16 (improve planner-delete-affordance): the XD-era Faction `Delete Faction "X"?` + Settlement `Delete Settlement "X"?` natives are DEAD — both lanes ride the role=dialog `DeleteConfirmModal` tier with row-level `Delete "<name>"` chips (MapsManager pattern); natives survive in those lanes only for dirty-form discard (a form Cancel is SILENT once the edited name is restored to the original value — the dirty flag clears) and the settlement Generate overwrite.
