Monster lane addenda (not in §§5–6/§10):
- **Constrict ongoing-damage rider (MA-1842 template)**: `escape_dc:N` + `grapple_ongoing_damage:{dice,damage_type}` → parseGrappleOngoingDamage (structured-key-only) → handlePlainDamage registers te `constrict_grapple` {source,dc} + ONE rounds:100 backstop clock after the condition leg → constrictGrappleService turn-start tick runs PRE-playerStats-guard (PC+monster victims), untyped direct-HP loss at the VICTIM's turn-start while held; release = lazy strip when Grappled gone (escape badge / rest / GM removal) + `constrict_grapple_released` log; grappler-dead stops ticks. ATTACK-LANE-only — a save-lane grapple (via `save_effect:"Grappled"`) has NO ongoing-damage producer, and its grants carry no dc → no escape badge (accepted residual).
- **save-lane te transport**: row `save_target_effect:"<registered te>"` → parseSaveTargetEffect → grantSaveTargetEffectClause on FAIL face ONLY + ONE anchored addExpiration `expireOnCreatureName:"<Monster N>"` (anchor's next turn-start = RAW "until start of its next turn", expiryRounds:null). Do NOT co-author with the MA-0711 prose lane on one row (disjoint te grants need separate lanes; two clocks on DISTINCT keys = no race). Attacker-anchored; victim-anchored prose lane = `parseSlowedClauses(save_effect)` ("can't take Reactions" byte-exact → te no_reactions, /next turn/ fork → rounds:2).
- **hit_choice chooser (MA-0855 template)**: structured `hit_choice:{options,note}` on row → `buildHitChoiceOffer` → HIT-popup ChoiceOffer → `resolveHitChoiceSelection` grants ONLY the chosen via single merged setRuntimeObject (activeConditions+meta{source,dc:null}) + `hit_choice_selected`; `buildHitConditionClause` SUPPRESSES the hit_conditions auto-grant while hit_choice is armed; unpicked Done = zero grants + `hit_choice_undecided` advisory, NEVER over-grant both. Charmed-class grants can end same-hit via the engine-wide took-damage-clears rule — adjudicate at pick-time.
- **Save-lane "Failure or Success:" clauses**: `parseBothOutcomesClause` (MonsterCardHelpers) requires the byte-exact marker in `save_effect` AND is PICKER-only (SaveAttackAoeModal); single-target save path is fail-only — missing marker = condition never lands on success.
- **Caster fold (summons)**: `summonSpiritHandler.resolveMonsterActions` folds caster spellAbilities (attack_bonus backfill, WIS/spellcasting-mod AND "spell level"→slotLevel) into spawned combatants; `normalizeSigns` collapses "+N+-M"→total — any new token-fold seam MUST normalize signs. Single-variant summons auto-confirm with NO chooser. Draconic Spirit has its own 2024 spell entry.
- **5e cantrip damage lane**: 5e cantrips carry `damage_at_character_level` while `spellDamageFormulaAtLevel` reads `damage_at_slot_level` (2024 twins) — monster-card 5e cantrip spell-attacks refuse honestly "no damage formula"; read caster-level dice from spells.json when structuring legendary cantrip rows. Live findMonsterSpell loads 5e THEN 2024 fallback (2024-only spells cast fine for 5e-joined monsters; the ruleset-branch mock pitfall is in-test only).
- **Composite attack+save chips**: on `attack_bonus` rows, ANY rollable-dice token + the word "damage" inside `save_effect` flips `saveLegIsConditionRider` false → the standalone DC chip goes UNCLICKABLE or double-charges. Recurring/ongoing damage prose MUST stay in `description`; rider-only `save_effect` carries the canonical condition word + non-damage clauses with ZERO dice. **Legendary chips**: numeric legendary CHILDREN self-suppress the "Expend Legendary" chip (the +N/DC chip IS the gated press surface — absence ≠ dead economy); "Costs 2/3 Actions" in names is name-text (engine spends 1/press, advisory floor); Expend-Legendary chips can absorb 2+ clicks — diff counter/log per click.
- **Reaction addenda**: parry-class prose without `automation{effect:'parry'}` = inert DATA one-field fix; `usage:"1/Day"` STRING (not `uses`) renders no counter EVER (formatActionUsage reads `usage` dict only); absorb-class (concentration-target/check-based) reactions are advisory-floor — carry full RAW numerics inside `advisory_message`; `lastAttack.isSpellDamage:true` is cosmetic on non-spell gazes; external POST of lastAttack reaches client runtime only after reload (seedCampaignRuntimeData seeds once/load) — live reaction proofs need reload-per-event. **Save-conditions ARE live on EB-NPC victims** via saveProcessing.processNpcSave→applyDamagelessSaveConditions (older "statusEffects-only" note is superseded); compound attack+save rows resolve as TWO chips (attack=always-full damage, save chip=condition/semi-damage). The NPC-inline adjudicator IGNORES stamped cs/activeBuffs bonuses (even nat-20 may read "failed" — pre-existing defect): prove save-success legs via the PC `.sp-overlay` prompt seam; `save-damage` `total` may be POST-hALVE — judge `finalDamage==|hpΔ|`.
- **Auras (PC side)**: Corona of Light (`sunlight_aura`) is SAVE-lane ONLY (handleNpcSaveDamage/handlePlayerSaveDamage/aoeService/DivineSparkModal; attack-lane resolver removed — do not re-add); fires vs ANY Fire/Radiant save spell ANYONE casts (authored text is caster-agnostic; `skipRangeCheck` left the 60-ft "Bright Light" clause unmeasured until the 2026-11-04 measured fork). Aura utils with a skipRangeCheck escape hatch MUST keep the measured/lenient decision INSIDE the util (wolf/lion `accumulateAuraCounts` convention) — dropping the flag at callers silently DISABLES the aura gridless. Power of the Wilds Lion stamp = `{effect:'power_of_wilds_option',optionName:'Lion',range:'5 ft',duration:'while_raging'}` (byte = lionAuraUtils matcher); Path stances: Rage `{effect:'stance',duration:'until_next_turn_extendable'}`, lv3 modal `animal_rage_option`, radios em-dash-labelled in `.sp-modal`; stance rows are `b.clickable` with trailing colons on chooser rows. Monster-lane aura gaps see archive — UPDATE 2026-11-10 (GB fix run): the Lion "another Barbarian who has this option active" exemption is now IMPLEMENTED (`lionAuraUtils.js` pre-loop `targetIsExemptFromLionAuras` — a target carrying its own `optionName:'Lion'` stamp is exempt from EVERY bearer's aura; bearer-self skip retained; both lanes ride the one util so sheet-sync/monster-card parity is structural) — exempt faces flip `mode:"normal"` with NO "rage aura of <bearer>" row; non-holder targets still penalized. Two-holder face recipe (live-proved): full-store bucket stamps on two characters carrying the canonical Lion buff bytes above (e.g. ElderPaladin + DraconicDragon) + HP keys on every victim face + an UNPLACED EB-joined monster attacker (gridless ⇒ `resolveMonsterLionAura` skipRangeCheck ⇒ no map/tokens needed); enemy face = third stampless PC (control, disadvantage lands); judge by the roll-row `mode` + the "rage aura of <bearer>" automation row (phantom-second-die law: rows read rolls:[a,b] on every monster press — mode is the only truth). **Save-AoE picker route (sheet casts)**: Actions spell name cell → spell-detail popup → Cast Spell → (metamagic chooser) → SaveAttackAoeModal picker with `overlay-<id>` tokens armed from spell-overlay POST (pre-checks exactly overlay-hit tokens); `handleAoeDamage`/`processAoeNpcs` are NOT reached from this surface — route fixes belong in the shared `resolveAllSavesAndDamage` hub. Picker prompts persist as change-data `savePrompt-<Target>-<ts>` (payload carries disadvantage/saveDc/saveType/rawDamage); `mode` (NOT forcedMode) + `saveRawRolls[0]` = normal; unresolved `.sp-overlay` prompts block ALL lanes minutes later — drain queue in Z-ORDER: Leading-Evasion chooser (`.sp-overlay--evasion`) stacks ABOVE the save prompt and its full-viewport `.sp-modal` intercepts the prompt's Roll Save click (Playwright click times out at 30s — always dismiss evasion Skip FIRST) → Roll Save → Next Save → Done → Results Close; picker overlay stays mounted with live "(N)" confirm until Results closes. `mode` (NOT forcedMode) + `saveRawRolls` = machine truth; normal-mode saveRawRolls can carry 2 entries (both dice logged, one kept) — read `mode`+`saveRoll`, never dice-count. Spell-overlay POST takes campaign as QUERY param (`?campaign=`, body-only 400).
- **In-page service probe (Vite dev)**: `await import('/src/services/...js')` inside page.evaluate calls the exported pure function against the LIVE runtime store — perfect seam isolation to split "resolver broken" from "caller broken". PITFALL (live 2026-11-11 weather-visibility run): the probe serves the MODULE CACHE — after editing the module, a plain reload-or-import returns the OLD export; append a `?cb=<ts>` query to the dynamic import path to force Vite re-transform (no page reload needed).

Playwright / tooling addenda (on top of §12):
- **Current MCP API**: click/evaluate take `target` (snapshot ref or unique selector), NOT `ref`; refs gain frame prefixes after navigations — regex `[ref=[a-z0-9]*e\d+\]`; mandatory keys: `browser_console_messages.level`, `browser_network_requests.static`, `browser_take_screenshot.scale` (`"css"`); `browser_run_code_unsafe` code must be an `async (page) => {...}` expression (bare IIFE → `__fn__ is not a function`); `browser_select_option` is now safe on the friendly-fire lane. `locator.click()` defaults to 30s — pass `{timeout:5000}`.
- **Screenshots**: land them in gitignored `tests/e2e/screenshots/` (read tool denies external temp; relative paths otherwise drop PNGs into the repo).
- **Test-side vitest addenda**: Vitest 4 + oxc parse-errors on dense nested vi.fn one-liners inside vi.mock factories — extract named helpers; default reporter swallows console.log even with --silent=false (write proof to a temp file); `vi.useFakeTimers({toFake:['Date']})` for jsdom date tests (full fake timers freeze waitFor); narrow-mock a util that gains exports with importOriginal spread or you get a late unhandled "No export defined on mock" in a DIFFERENT file; runtime-store mocks for `__map__.activeMapName` must return `null` not `[]` ([] is truthy — measured branches leak into gridless tests); random-threshold boundary tests must use the EXACT float the shared formula computes (`0.12 + 0.1` sums to `0.22000000000000003` — a hand-written `0.22` fires instead of refusing); co-located encounter tests importing saveProcessing use `../../hooks/combat/`; saveProcessing tests must NOT import MonsterCardModal.jsx (barrel handle-export requirement); complex-15 arithmetic: `?.` and `||` count as branches, CampaignSelection/MonsterCardModal/Map.jsx are at cap — hoist child components (CampaignListArea/DeleteNotice precedent).
- **Ops addenda**: full-array faction POST strips `draft` cleanly and re-stamps; Sessions DELETE by URL-encoded name = 200 no dialog; `window.confirm = m=>{seen.push(m); return bool}` override + synchronous dispatch inside ONE evaluate drives deterministic confirm faces (restore after); music cleanup without admin clear = scoped `DELETE /<Char>`-style `DELETE /api/campaigns/:c/music` (close tab → wait past debounce → DELETE → two-pass verify); `GET /changeData` (one word) silently hits the `:key` wildcard → false-`{"value":null}` — the route is hyphenated `/change-data`; API restart resets in-memory change-data to `[]` even when disk had content (lazy hydration) — empty GET post-restart ≠ empty disk; app load seeds combatSummary/`__campaign__`/<Char> within ~2.5s — "nothing written" checks clear AFTER the browser leg.
- **savePrompt probe lane (EP 2026-11-08)**: raw `POST /api/campaigns/:c/savePrompt-<Target>-<promptId>` `{value:{targetName, saveType:'Constitution', saveDc:15, source, condition, promptId}}` lands the store key but the SAME tab's SavePromptModal stays shut (it scans the `change-<camp>-savePrompt-` localStorage mirror at MOUNT + storage events) — surface it via GM-tab reload/reselect (prompt survives); field is `saveDc` (payload `dc` renders "DC" blank). Drain-witness rig for the pendingSaveListenerPrompts lane: seed `pendingSaveListenerPrompts:['<id>']` via POST, resolve via Roll Save → GM fires `DELETE /pendingSaveListenerPrompts` (evict) and both keys evaporate; fixed-build visitor fires ZERO writes (565e07581 verified). Enforcement face: POST /enforcement `{value:{mode:'on'}}` arms the gate; sweep via DELETE.
- **3D last-known ghost lane + `__map3d` census law (SHIPPED 2026-11-19 improve 3d-ghost-parity-ruling + fog-ghost-memory-across-flips; supersedes the SK-era verdict in place):** DEV builds expose `globalThis.__map3d.census` (live scene traverse: `{monsters,npcs,players,avatars,fogCells,walls,lastKnown,lastKnownDimmed,lastKnownCells,…}`) — the only reliable 3D probe (tokens are meshes, not DOM; poll values, ≤5 s). With `lastKnownMode:true` and a creature in never-revealed fog, the visitor 3D now paints a MUTED memory token at the last-seen cell (grey `LAST_KNOWN` palette + `_setDimmed`/`HIDDEN_MARKER_OPACITY` idiom — no chips, never a truth mesh), fed by the SAME hoisted `ghostCells` Map.jsx computes above the viewMode conditional (`lastKnown` build key — named apart from the scene's fallback-prop "ghost" vocabulary; never a second tracker). FOG-BURIAL LAW: any additive lane for fogged-cell content MUST lift above `WALL_H` (80) — the opaque `FOG_PLAYER` cap renders above every token and buries ground-level meshes (census would count them blind). Memory survives 2D↔3D flips (Map-level cache) but still drops at genuine page boundaries and toggle OFF/GM (cull-honest never-seen creatures paint nothing in either view). 3D enter `button[aria-label="View map in 3D"]` (renders for visitors); EXIT face per host map (live TL 2026-11-20 indoor rider): the INDOOR toolbar exit is a plain button whose trimmed `textContent === '2D'` (evaluate-find + `.click()` landed both directions); the hex-map 3D back control is `Back to 2D map` (icon-prefixed there — `hasText:/^2D$/` is the hex-map failure face, not the indoor one). Creature-bucket move = plain whole-doc PUT of `placedItems` from a node-side fetch (localhost Host = GM); the GM tab keeps the live token + zero ghost chrome at the fogged cell (`ghostCells` arrives `null`). WORDING LAW (shipped 2026-11-20 improve visitor-3d-ghost-label-wording): a ghost's label text is `${placedItemLabel(item)} — last seen here` — byte-identical to the 2D twin's `<g title>` (PlacedItems.jsx `creatureGroupChrome`), nameless face included (`monster (8,2) — last seen here`, since `placedItemLabel` uses the item's REAL cell). Live token labels stay bare. The rendered text is stamped on `sprite.userData.labelText` by `_makeLabel` (the jsdom-assertable seam — canvas pixels are unreadable), and the same sprite carries `isTokenLabel` so the header Labels toggle governs ghost labels with no rebuild. SNUG-CANVAS LAW (re-dispatch fix 2026-11-20, round 2): the old FIXED 512×128 canvas + `sprite.scale.set(56,14)` CLIPPED every ` — last seen here` label symmetrically ("…n Rig — last seen…") — center-aligned text wider than the canvas is cut at both edges. `_makeLabel` now measures (`ctx.measureText` + 40px pad) and sizes the canvas to the string, keeping the legacy 14-world-unit height and px→world ratio (512px still lands on 56 wide); past the 1024px cap (112 world units ≈ 2.8 cells) the FONT shrinks (floor 24px) instead of the sprite ballooning. Test seam: `sprite.scale.x` under a mocked `measureText` (40px/char) — any new label lane's jsdom ctx stub MUST carry `measureText`. RE-FOG DANCE (live improve 3d-ghost-grounded-conditional-lift 2026-11-20, FULL EXPLORE-RIDE RE-DERIVED WW 2026-11-20 on a fresh sandbox): visitor fog = `!revealed && !current LOS`, so ghost grounded is the DEFAULT (memory cells are recorded visible). WW's three-face witness: token visible through an OPEN `door` item → moved to a wall-pocket cell (never visible) ⇒ ghost renders at the memory cell GROUNDED `y:0`; `door.open:false` PUT ⇒ the same ghost LIFTS to `y:96` (`WALL_H 80 + LAST_KNOWN_LIFT 16`, fogCells 2→7); reopen ⇒ back to `y:0`. Numbers are unreadable from the census — patch the prototype BEFORE the 3D mount (`const S=(await import('/src/components/map/Map3D/map3dScene.js')).Map3DScene`, wrap `_buildLastKnownTokens`) and read `mapGroup.children.filter(o=>o.userData.census?.kind==='lastKnown')` → `{cell, position.y, children[].userData.labelText}`; the label text sits on a CHILD sprite's `userData.labelText` (the group's own `userData.labelText` is undefined — a group-level read false-negatives the wording law). RIG LAWS (WW, cheaper than the improve-era two-PUT dance): (a) build the sandbox by API and NEVER open it in a GM tab — the GM mount stamps exploration memory and a revealed cell can NEVER re-fog, so no door will ever lift the ghost; the `revealed:[]` half of the improve-era recipe is only a repair for a board a GM already mounted; (b) `POST /maps` DROPS `lastKnownMode` from the create body (`buildIndoorMapData` whitelist) — follow with a whole-doc `PUT` carrying `type/gridSize/walls/placedItems` + `players` (the erasure guard) to stamp it; (c) LOS has NO radius, so one player token sees the entire open grid — the fogged pocket MUST come from a wall ring, and `closedDoors` (a `door` placedItem with `!item.open`) is the only cheap re-fog lever; (d) activate via `PUT …/activate` since visitors have no Maps manager (their sidebar `Map` opens the ACTIVE map). Snapshot `fogCells` AFTER the settle — never hand-threshold it (the re-fog delta landed an exact count, a `>60` wait one-shot'd). VISITOR NAV FACE: a LAN-origin tab's sidebar says `Map` (singular, opens the active map's board directly — a `Maps` textContent wait hangs); the 3D toolbar toggle chips (`Labels`, `Torch light`) are `<label>` elements, not `<button>`.
- **GM Screen lane (first lived WW 2026-11-20, read-only surface):** view button is the sidebar `GM Screen` (localhost-only — absent on a LAN tab); root `.gm-screen-view` with three `.gm-section` blocks: `Party` (one row per character carrying `computedStats`: Class/AC/Saves/Skills/HP/Speed/Res-Imm/In Play), `In Play` (`Combat — round N` + per-creature HP + condition badges + a `Unassigned effects` line) and `Quick Reference` (native `<details>`/`<summary>` accordions — `Conditions (15)` nested one `<details>` per condition, `Base Actions (13)`; ZERO `<button>`s, so a `button:has-text('Conditions')` locator finds nothing; open a body by setting `.parentElement.open = true`). ZONES LAW: the `No zones or markers on the table.` empty line reads the campaign `targetEffects` change-data key (`useRuntimeValue('campaign','targetEffects')` in GMScreen.jsx:25) — spell-overlay zones are a DIFFERENT store and never appear here (a live `/spell-overlay` add changed nothing; wildcard `POST /api/campaigns/:c/targetEffects {value:[{effect:'grease',target:…,source:…,dc:13}]}` grew `Unassigned effects → Grease (Zone)` and hid the empty line, `DELETE /targetEffects` reversed both, both live via SSE with no reload).
- **Honest-zero 3D census + zero-char ORIGIN matrix (WW 2026-11-20, post-93ec9d6ba/90f441e88):** (a) a visitor whose fog covers everything reads a bare `Nothing revealed yet` line (NOT an all-zero counts line — `buildCensusLine` only swaps the face when EVERY live-visible count is 0, so any `lastKnown` tail keeps the `N walls · … · N last known` form); the GM on the same never-revealed doc keeps full counts (`233 walls · 37 items · 0 players · 5 npcs · 0 monsters`), plurals correct. Probe: any innerText line containing `walls` or the exact string. (b) zero-character campaign entry has FOUR faces: LAN+seeded (`qa-starter`) = card copy + `Open the starter checklist` + 7-step checklist (the `Build your heroes`, Allies and Admin/Regenerate rows are `isLocalhost`-gated away); LAN+seedless (`qa-arena`) = card copy and ZERO buttons (`/starter-seed` answers `{seed:null}`); fresh localhost context = the offer-once wizard auto-mounts and stamps ITS OWN `character-wizard-offered:<name> = "1"`; shared localhost profile (keys persist across runs) = card + `Add your first character`, no auto-wizard. To witness the localhost auto-offer branch without burning the shared profile, open `browser_tabs {action:'new', url:'http://localhost:5173', isolatedContext:'<name>'}` — localStorage is per-origin PER-CONTEXT, so a fresh context starts with an empty offer store. The visitor checklist counts QUESTS LIVE (`Review the starter quests (N on the board)`, 944189479) — the `(N seeded)` seed-doc wording is DEAD; the DELETE leg is the witness (seed doc immutable, mounted face moves ~1 s).
